




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

vinayapiṭakai


mahāvaggapāḷi


1. mahākhandhakau

1. baudhikathā

1. udā॰ 1 ādayau taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā uruvailāyaṃ viharati naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai baudhirukkhamūlai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| atha khau bhagavā baudhirukkhamūlai sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina nisīdi vimuttisukhapaṭisaṃvaidī vimuttisukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidī (ka॰)| atha khau bhagavā rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ anulaumapaṭilaumaṃ manasākāsi – ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti – aivamaitassa  kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| ‘‘avij‍jāyatvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau, nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau, saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau, phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau, vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau, taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti – aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau  hautī’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yadā havai pātubhavanti dhammā|

ātāpinau jhāyatau brāhmaṇassa|

athassa kaṅkhā vapayanti sabbā|

yatau pajānāti sahaitudhamma’’nti||

2. udā॰ 2 atha khau bhagavā rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ anulaumapaṭilaumaṃ manasākāsi – ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ…pai॰…  aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī…pai॰… niraudhau hautī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yadā havai pātubhavanti dhammā|

ātāpinau jhāyatau brāhmaṇassa|

athassa kaṅkhā vapayanti sabbā|

yatau khayaṃ pac‍cayānaṃ avaidī’’ti||

3. udā॰ 3 atha khau bhagavā rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ anulaumapaṭilaumaṃ manasākāsi – ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ…pai॰…  aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti…pai॰… niraudhau hautī’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yadā havai pātubhavanti dhammā|

ātāpinau jhāyatau brāhmaṇassa|

vidhūpayaṃ tiṭṭhati mārasainaṃ|

sūriyauva suriyauva (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) aubhāsayamantalikkha’’nti||

baudhikathā niṭṭhitā|

2. ajapālakathā

4. udā॰ 4 atha  khau bhagavā sattāhassa ac‍cayaina tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhahitvā baudhirukkhamūlā yaina ajapālanigraudhau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā ajapālanigraudhamūlai sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina nisīdi vimuttisukhapaṭisaṃvaidī| atha khau añ‍ñatarau huṃhuṅkajātikau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami| upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ  sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau hauti, katamai ca pana brāhmaṇakaraṇā brāhmaṇakārakā (ka॰) brāhmaṇakarāṇā (?) dhammā’’ti? atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

naitti॰ 103 yau brāhmaṇau bāhitapāpadhammau|

nihuṃhuṅkau nik‍kasāvau yatattau|

vaidantagū vusitabrahmacariyau|

dhammaina sau brahmavādaṃ vadaiyya|

yassussadā natthi kuhiñ‍ci laukai’’ti||

ajapālakathā niṭṭhitā|

3. mucalindakathā

5. udā॰ 11 atha khau bhagavā sattāhassa ac‍cayaina tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhahitvā ajapālanigraudhamūlā  yaina mucalindau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā mucalindamūlai sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina nisīdi vimuttisukhapaṭisaṃvaidī| taina khau pana samayaina mahā akālamaighau udapādi, sattāhavaddalikā sītavātaduddinī| atha khau mucalindau nāgarājā sakabhavanā nikkhamitvā bhagavatau kāyaṃ sattakkhattuṃ bhaugaihi parikkhipitvā uparimuddhani  mahantaṃ phaṇaṃ karitvā aṭṭhāsi – ‘‘mā bhagavantaṃ sītaṃ, mā bhagavantaṃ uṇhaṃ, mā bhagavantaṃ ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassau’’ti …siriṃ sapa… (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)| atha  khau mucalindau nāgarājā sattāhassa ac‍cayaina viddhaṃ vigatavalāhakaṃ daivaṃ viditvā bhagavatau kāyā bhaugai vinivaiṭhaitvā sakavaṇṇaṃ paṭisaṃharitvā māṇavakavaṇṇaṃ abhinimminitvā bhagavatau puratau aṭṭhāsi pañ‍jalikau bhagavantaṃ namassamānau| atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

kathā॰ 338 kathāvatthupāḷiyampi‘‘sukhau vivaikau tuṭṭhassa, sutadhammassa passatau|

abyāpaj‍jaṃ sukhaṃ laukai, pāṇabhūtaisu saṃyamau||

kathā॰ 338 kathāvatthupāḷiyampi‘‘sukhā virāgatā laukai, kāmānaṃ samatik‍kamau|

asmimānassa yau vinayau, aitaṃ vai paramaṃ sukha’’nti||

mucalindakathā niṭṭhitā|

4. rājāyatanakathā

6. atha khau bhagavā sattāhassa ac‍cayaina tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhahitvā mucalindamūlā yaina rājāyatanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā rājāyatanamūlai sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina nisīdi vimuttisukhapaṭisaṃvaidī| taina khau pana  samayaina tapussa tapassu (sī॰) bhal‍likā vāṇijā uk‍kalā taṃ daisaṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| atha khau tapussabhal‍likānaṃ vāṇijānaṃ  ñātisālauhitā daivatā tapussabhal‍likai vāṇijai aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, mārisā, bhagavā rājāyatanamūlai viharati paṭhamābhisambuddhau; gacchatha taṃ bhagavantaṃ manthaina ca madhupiṇḍikāya ca patimānaitha; taṃ vau bhavissati dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| atha khau tapussabhal‍likā vāṇijā manthañ‍ca madhupiṇḍikañ‍ca ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau tapussabhal‍likā vāṇijā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘paṭiggaṇhātu nau, bhantai, bhagavā manthañ‍ca madhupiṇḍikañ‍ca, yaṃ amhākaṃ assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya  sukhāyā’’ti| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘na khau tathāgatā hatthaisu paṭiggaṇhanti| kimhi nu khau ahaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyyaṃ manthañ‍ca madhupiṇḍikañ‍cā’’ti? atha  khau cattārau mahārājānau bhagavatau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya catuddisā cattārau sailamayai pattai bhagavatau upanāmaisuṃ – ‘‘idha, bhantai, bhagavā paṭiggaṇhātu manthañ‍ca madhupiṇḍikañ‍cā’’ti| paṭiggahaisi bhagavā pac‍cagghai sailamayai pattai manthañ‍ca madhupiṇḍikañ‍ca, paṭiggahaitvā paribhuñ‍ji| atha khau tapussabhal‍likā vāṇijā bhagavantaṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ viditvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ (aunītapattapāṇiṃ viditvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ) ( ) sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu natthi aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘aitai mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāma dhammañ‍ca, upāsakai nau bhagavā dhāraitu  aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitai saraṇaṃ gatai’’ti| tai ca laukai paṭhamaṃ upāsakā ahaisuṃ dvaivācikā|

rājāyatanakathā niṭṭhitā|

5. brahmayācanakathā

7. ayaṃ brahmayācanakathā dī॰ ni॰ 2.64 ādayau; ma॰ ni॰ 1.281 ādayau; ma॰ ni॰ 2.336 ādayau; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.172 ādayau atha khau bhagavā sattāhassa ac‍cayaina tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhahitvā rājāyatanamūlā yaina ajapālanigraudhau tainupasaṅkami| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā ajapālanigraudhamūlai viharati| atha khau bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘adhigatau khau myāyaṃ dhammau gambhīrau duddasau duranubaudhau santau paṇītau atak‍kāvacarau nipuṇau paṇḍitavaidanīyau| ālayarāmā khau panāyaṃ pajā ālayaratā ālayasammuditā| ālayarāmāya khau pana pajāya ālayaratāya ālayasammuditāya duddasaṃ idaṃ ṭhānaṃ  yadidaṃ idappaccayatāpaṭiccasamuppādau; idampi khau ṭhānaṃ sududdasaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, parai ca mai na ājānaiyyuṃ, sau mamassa kilamathau, sā mamassa vihaisā’’ti| apissu bhagavantaṃ imā anacchariyā gāthāyau paṭibhaṃsu pubbai assutapubbā –

‘‘kicchaina  mai adhigataṃ, halaṃ dāni pakāsituṃ|

rāgadausaparaitaihi, nāyaṃ dhammau susambudhau||

‘‘paṭisautagāmiṃ  nipuṇaṃ, gambhīraṃ duddasaṃ aṇuṃ|

rāgarattā na dakkhanti, tamaukhandhaina āvuṭā āvaṭā (sī॰)’’ti||

itiha  bhagavatau paṭisañ‍cikkhatau appaussuk‍katāya cittaṃ namati, nau dhammadaisanāya|

8. atha khau brahmunau sahampatissa bhagavatau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya aitadahausi – ‘‘nassati vata bhau laukau, vinassati vata bhau laukau, yatra hi nāma tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa appaussuk‍katāya cittaṃ namati namissati (?), nau dhammadaisanāyā’’ti| atha khau brahmā sahampati – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva – brahmalaukai antarahitau bhagavatau puratau pāturahausi| atha khau brahmā sahampati aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā dakkhiṇajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ pathaviyaṃ nihantvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘daisaitu, bhantai, bhagavā dhammaṃ, daisaitu sugatau dhammaṃ| santi sattā apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti, bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārau’’ti| idamavauca brahmā sahampati, idaṃ vatvāna athāparaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘pāturahausi magadhaisu pubbai|

dhammau asuddhau samalaihi cintitau|

apāpuraitaṃ avāpuraitaṃ (sī॰) amatassa dvāraṃ|

suṇantu dhammaṃ vimalainānubuddhaṃ||

‘‘sailai yathā pabbatamuddhaniṭṭhitau|

yathāpi passai janataṃ samantatau|

tathūpamaṃ dhammamayaṃ sumaidha|

pāsādamāruyha samantacakkhu|

saukāvatiṇṇaṃ  janatamapaitasaukau|

avaikkhassu jātijarābhibhūtaṃ||

‘‘uṭṭhaihi  vīra vijitasaṅgāma|

satthavāha aṇaṇa anaṇa (ka॰) vicara laukai|

daisassu daisaitu (ka॰) bhagavā dhammaṃ|

añ‍ñātārau bhavissantī’’ti||

[ ] sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu natthi, mūlapaṇṇāsakaisu pāsarāsisutthai brahmayācanā sakiṃ yaiva āgatā [ aivaṃ  vuttai bhagavā brahmānaṃ sahampatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mayhampi khau, brahmai, aitadahausi – ‘adhigatau khau myāyaṃ dhammau gambhīrau duddasau duranubaudhau santau paṇītau atak‍kāvacarau nipuṇau paṇḍitavaidanīyau| ālayarāmā khau panāyaṃ pajā ālayaratā ālayasammuditā| ālayarāmāya khau pana pajāya ālayaratāya ālayasammuditāya duddasaṃ idaṃ ṭhānaṃ yadidaṃ idappac‍cayatāpaṭic‍casamuppādau; idampi khau ṭhānaṃ sududdasaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, parai ca mai na ājānaiyyuṃ, sau mamassa kilamathau, sā mamassa vihaisā’ti| apissu maṃ, brahmai, imā anacchariyā gāthāyau paṭibhaṃsu pubbai assutapubbā –

‘kicchaina mai adhigataṃ, halaṃ dāni pakāsituṃ|

rāgadausaparaitaihi, nāyaṃ dhammau susambudhau||

‘paṭisautagāmiṃ nipuṇaṃ, gambhīraṃ duddasaṃ aṇuṃ|

rāgarattā na dakkhanti, tamaukhandhaina āvuṭā’ti||

itiha mai, brahmai, paṭisañ‍cikkhatau appaussuk‍katāya cittaṃ namati nau dhammadaisanāyā’’ti|

dutiyampi khau brahmā sahampati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘daisaitu, bhantai, bhagavā dhammaṃ, daisaitu sugatau dhammaṃ; santi sattā apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti, bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārau’’ti| idamavauca brahmā sahampati, idaṃ vatvāna athāparaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘pāturahausi  magadhaisu pubbai|

dhammau asuddhau samalaihi cintitau|

apāpuraitaṃ amatassa dvāraṃ|

suṇantu dhammaṃ vimalainānubuddhaṃ||

‘‘sailai yathā pabbatamuddhaniṭṭhitau|

yathāpi passai janataṃ samantatau|

tathūpamaṃ  dhammamayaṃ sumaidha|

pāsādamāruyha samantacakkhu|

saukāvatiṇṇaṃ janatamapaitasaukau|

avaikkhassu jātijarābhibhūtaṃ||

‘‘uṭṭhaihi vīra vijitasaṅgāma|

satthavāha aṇaṇa vicara laukai|

daisassu bhagavā dhammaṃ|

añ‍ñātārau bhavissantī’’ti||

dutiyampi khau bhagavā brahmānaṃ sahampatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mayhampi khau, brahmai, aitadahausi – ‘adhigatau khau myāyaṃ dhammau gambhīrau duddasau duranubaudhau santau paṇītau atak‍kāvacarau nipuṇau paṇḍitavaidanīyau| ālayarāmā khau panāyaṃ pajā ālayaratā ālayasammuditā| ālayarāmāya khau pana pajāya ālayaratāya ālayasammuditāya duddasaṃ idaṃ ṭhānaṃ yadidaṃ idappac‍cayatāpaṭic‍casamuppādau; idampi khau ṭhānaṃ sududdasaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, parai ca mai na ājānaiyyuṃ, sau mamassa kilamathau, sā mamassa vihaisā’ti| apissu maṃ, brahmai, imā anacchariyā gāthāyau paṭibhaṃsu pubbai assutapubbā –

‘kicchaina mai adhigataṃ, halaṃ dāni pakāsituṃ|

rāgadausaparaitaihi, nāyaṃ dhammau susambudhau||

‘paṭisautagāmiṃ nipuṇaṃ, gambhīraṃ duddasaṃ aṇuṃ|

rāgarattā na dakkhanti, tamaukhandhaina āvuṭā’ti||

itiha  mai, brahmai, paṭisañ‍cikkhatau appaussuk‍katāya cittaṃ namati, nau dhammadaisanāyā’’ti|

tatiyampi khau brahmā sahampati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘daisaitu, bhantai, bhagavā dhammaṃ, daisaitu sugatau dhammaṃ| santi sattā apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti, bhavissanti  dhammassa añ‍ñātārau’’ti| idamavauca brahmā sahampati, idaṃ vatvāna athāparaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘pāturahausi magadhaisu pubbai|

dhammau asuddhau samalaihi cintitau|

apāpuraitaṃ amatassa dvāraṃ|

suṇantu dhammaṃ vimalainānubuddhaṃ||

‘‘sailai yathā pabbatamuddhaniṭṭhitau|

yathāpi passai janataṃ samantatau|

tathūpamaṃ dhammamayaṃ sumaidha|

pāsādamāruyha samantacakkhu|

saukāvatiṇṇaṃ janatamapaitasaukau|

avaikkhassu jātijarābhibhūtaṃ||

‘‘uṭṭhaihi vīra vijitasaṅgāma|

satthavāha aṇaṇa vicara laukai|

daisassu bhagavā dhammaṃ|

añ‍ñātārau bhavissantī’’ti||

9. atha  khau bhagavā brahmunau ca ajjhaisanaṃ viditvā sattaisu ca kāruñ‍ñataṃ paṭic‍ca buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaisi| addasā khau bhagavā buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaintau sattai apparajakkhai mahārajakkhai tikkhindriyai mudindriyai svākārai dvākārai suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai, appaikac‍cai paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai dassāvinau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) viharantai, appaikac‍cai na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai viharantai| saiyyathāpi nāma uppaliniyaṃ vā paduminiyaṃ vā puṇḍarīkiniyaṃ vā appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni antau nimuggapausīni, appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni samaudakaṃ ṭhitāni, appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakaṃ ac‍cuggamma ṭhitāni tiṭṭhanti (sī॰ syā॰) anupalittāni udakaina, aivamaivaṃ bhagavā buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaintau addasa  sattai apparajakkhai mahārajakkhai tikkhindriyai  mudindriyai svākārai dvākārai suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai, appaikac‍cai paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai viharantai, appaikac‍cai na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai viharantai; disvāna  brahmānaṃ sahampatiṃ gāthāya pac‍cabhāsi –

‘‘apārutā taisaṃ amatassa dvārā|

yai sautavantau pamuñ‍cantu saddhaṃ|

vihiṃsasañ‍ñī paguṇaṃ na bhāsiṃ|

dhammaṃ paṇītaṃ manujaisu brahmai’’ti||

atha khau brahmā sahampati ‘‘katāvakāsau khaumhi bhagavatā dhammadaisanāyā’’ti bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

brahmayācanakathā niṭṭhitā|

6. pañ‍cavaggiyakathā

10. ma॰ ni॰ 1.284 ādayau; ma॰ ni॰ 2.339 ādayau atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘kassa nu khau ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ? kau imaṃ dhammaṃ khippamaiva ājānissatī’’ti? atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āḷārau kālāmau paṇḍitau byattau maidhāvī dīgharattaṃ apparajakkhajātikau; yaṃnūnāhaṃ āḷārassa kālāmassa paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, sau imaṃ dhammaṃ khippamaiva ājānissatī’’ti| atha khau antarahitā daivatā bhagavatau āraucaisi – ‘‘sattāhakālaṅkatau, bhantai, āḷārau kālāmau’’ti| bhagavataupi khau ñāṇaṃ udapādi – ‘‘sattāhakālaṅkatau āḷārau  kālāmau’’ti| atha khau bhagavatau  aitadahausi – ‘‘mahājāniyau khau āḷārau kālāmau; sacai hi sau imaṃ dhammaṃ suṇaiyya, khippamaiva ājānaiyyā’’ti| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘kassa nu khau ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ? kau imaṃ dhammaṃ khippamaiva ājānissatī’’ti? atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau udakau uddakau (sī॰ syā॰) rāmaputtau paṇḍitau byattau maidhāvī dīgharattaṃ apparajakkhajātikau; yaṃnūnāhaṃ udakassa rāmaputtassa paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, sau imaṃ dhammaṃ khippamaiva ājānissatī’’ti| atha khau antarahitā daivatā bhagavatau āraucaisi – ‘‘abhidausakālaṅkatau, bhantai, udakau rāmaputtau’’ti| bhagavataupi khau ñāṇaṃ udapādi – ‘‘abhidausakālaṅkatau  udakau rāmaputtau’’ti| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘mahājāniyau khau udakau rāmaputtau; sacai hi sau imaṃ dhammaṃ suṇaiyya, khippamaiva ājānaiyyā’’ti

atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘kassa nu khau ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ? kau imaṃ dhammaṃ  khippamaiva ājānissatī’’ti? atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘bahukārā khau mai pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū, yai maṃ padhānapahitattaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃsu; yaṃnūnāhaṃ pañ‍cavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau aitarahi pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū viharantī’’ti? addasā khau bhagavā dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū bārāṇasiyaṃ viharantai  isipatanai migadāyai| atha khau bhagavā uruvailāyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina bārāṇasī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi|

11. addasā khau upakau ājīvakau bhagavantaṃ antarā ca gayaṃ antarā ca baudhiṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍naṃ, disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vippasan‍nāni khau tai, āvusau, indriyāni, parisuddhau chavivaṇṇau pariyaudātau| kaṃsi tvaṃ, āvusau, uddissa pabbajitau? kau vā tai satthā? kassa vā tvaṃ dhammaṃ raucaisī’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai bhagavā upakaṃ ājīvakaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

dha॰ pa॰ 353; kathā॰ 405 ‘‘sabbābhibhū  sabbavidūhamasmi,

sabbaisu dhammaisu anūpalittau|

sabbañ‍jahau taṇhākkhayai vimuttau,

sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya kamuddisaiyyaṃ||

mi॰ pa॰ 4.5.11 milindapañhaipi; kathā॰ 405 ‘‘na mai ācariyau atthi, sadisau mai na vij‍jati|

sadaivakasmiṃ laukasmiṃ, natthi mai paṭipuggalau||

kathā॰ 405 kathāvatthupāḷiyampi ‘‘ahañhi arahā laukai, ahaṃ satthā anuttarau|

aikaumhi sammāsambuddhau, sītibhūtausmi nibbutau||

kathā॰ 405 kathāvatthupāḷiyampi‘‘dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaituṃ, gacchāmi kāsinaṃ puraṃ|

andhībhūtasmiṃ laukasmiṃ, āhañchaṃ āhañ‍ñiṃ (ka॰) amatadundubhi’’nti||

yathā  khau tvaṃ, āvusau, paṭijānāsi, arahasi anantajinauti|

kathā॰ 405 kathāvatthupāḷiyampi ‘‘mādisā vai jinā haunti, yai pattā āsavakkhayaṃ|

jitā  mai pāpakā dhammā, tasmāhamupaka tasmāhamupakā (sī॰) jinau’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai upakau ājīvakau hupaiyyapāvusauti huvaiyyapāvusau (sī॰) huvaiyyāvusau (syā॰) vatvā sīsaṃ aukampaitvā ummaggaṃ gahaitvā pak‍kāmi|

12. atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina bārāṇasī isipatanaṃ migadāyau, yaina pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami| addasaṃsu khau pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ; disvāna añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ katikaṃ idaṃ padaṃ kaisuci natthi saṇṭhapaisuṃ – ‘‘ayaṃ, āvusau, samaṇau gautamau āgacchati, bāhul‍likau padhānavibbhantau  āvattau bāhul‍lāya| sau naiva abhivādaitabbau, na pac‍cuṭṭhātabbau, nāssa pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitabbaṃ; api ca khau āsanaṃ ṭhapaitabbaṃ, sacai sau ākaṅkhissati nisīdissatī’’ti| yathā yathā khau bhagavā pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū upasaṅkamati, tathā tathā tathā tathā tai (sī॰ syā॰) pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū nāsakkhiṃsu sakāya katikāya saṇṭhātuṃ| asaṇṭhahantā bhagavantaṃ pac‍cuggantvā aikau bhagavatau pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaisi, aikau āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi, aikau pādaudakaṃ, aikau pādapīṭhaṃ, aikau pādakaṭhalikaṃ upanikkhipi| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai; nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā pādai pakkhālaisi| apissu api ca khau (pāsarāsisuttha) bhagavantaṃ nāmaina ca āvusauvādaina ca samudācaranti| aivaṃ vuttai bhagavā pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘mā, bhikkhavai, tathāgataṃ nāmaina ca āvusauvādaina ca  samudācaratha samudācarittha (sī॰ syā॰)| arahaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau sammāsambuddhau, audahatha, bhikkhavai, sautaṃ, amatamadhigataṃ, ahamanusāsāmi, ahaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi| yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānā yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipaj‍jamānā (syā॰) nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘tāyapi khau tvaṃ, āvusau gautama, iriyāya cariyāya (syā॰), tāya paṭipadāya, tāya duk‍karakārikāya naivajjhagā uttari manussadhammā uttarimanussadhammaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ, kiṃ pana tvaṃ aitarahi, bāhul‍likau padhānavibbhantau āvattau bāhul‍lāya, adhigamissasi uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisa’’nti? aivaṃ vuttai bhagavā pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau bāhul‍likau, na padhānavibbhantau, na āvattau bāhul‍lāya; arahaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau sammāsambuddhau| audahatha, bhikkhavai, sautaṃ, amatamadhigataṃ, ahamanusāsāmi, ahaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi| yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānā nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ  – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaivadhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’’ti| dutiyampi khau pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ…pai॰…| dutiyampi khau bhagavā pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū aitadavauca…pai॰…| tatiyampi khau pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘tāyapi  khau tvaṃ, āvusau gautama, iriyāya, tāya paṭipadāya, tāya duk‍karakārikāya naivajjhagā uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ, kiṃ pana tvaṃ aitarahi, bāhul‍likau padhānavibbhantau  āvattau bāhul‍lāya, adhigamissasi uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisa’’nti? aivaṃ vuttai bhagavā pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘abhijānātha mai nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, itau pubbai aivarūpaṃ pabhāvitamaita’’nti bhāsitamaitanti (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) ṭīkāyau aulaukaitabbā? ‘‘nauhaitaṃ, bhantai’’| arahaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau sammāsambuddhau, audahatha, bhikkhavai, sautaṃ, amatamadhigataṃ, ahamanusāsāmi, ahaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi| yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānā nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃbrahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaivadhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathāti| asakkhi khau bhagavā pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū sañ‍ñāpaituṃ| atha khau pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ sussūsiṃsu, sautaṃ audahiṃsu, añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaisuṃ|

13. atha khau bhagavā pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1081 ādayau dvaimai, bhikkhavai, antā pabbajitaina na saivitabbā| katamai dvai idaṃ padadvayaṃ sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu natthi? yau cāyaṃ kāmaisu kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugau hīnau gammau pauthuj‍janikau anariyau anatthasaṃhitau, yau cāyaṃ attakilamathānuyaugau dukkhau anariyau anatthasaṃhitau| aitai khau, bhikkhavai, ubhau antai anupagamma, majjhimā paṭipadā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā, cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| katamā ca sā, bhikkhavai, majjhimā paṭipadā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā, cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau sā, bhikkhavai, majjhimā paṭipadā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā, cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati|

14. ‘‘idaṃ  khau pana, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ| jātipi dukkhā, jarāpi dukkhā, byādhipi dukkhau, maraṇampi dukkhaṃ, appiyaihi sampayaugau  dukkhau, piyaihi vippayaugau dukkhau, yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ| saṃkhittaina, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā  pañ‍cupādānakhandhāpi (ka) dukkhā| ‘‘idaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, dukkhasamudayaṃ aittha ‘‘idaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍canti ādīsu dukkhasamudayau dukkhaniraudhauti vattabbai dukkhasamudayaṃ dukkhaniraudhanti liṅgavipal‍lāsau tatau’’ti paṭisambhidāmaggaṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttaṃ| visuddhimaggaṭīkāyaṃ pana uppādau bhayantipāṭhavaṇṇanāyaṃ ‘‘satipi dvin‍naṃ padānaṃ samānādhikaraṇabhāvai liṅgabhaidau gahitau, yathā dukkhasamudayau ariyasac‍ca’’nti vuttaṃ| taisu dukkhasamudayau ariyasac‍ca’’nti sakaliṅgikapāṭhau ‘‘dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍ca’’nti pāḷiyā samaiti| ariyasac‍caṃ – yāyaṃ taṇhā paunaubbhavikā paunaubhavikā (ka॰) nandīrāgasahagatā nandirāgasahagatā (sī॰ syā॰) tatratatrābhinandinī, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmataṇhā, bhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhā|

‘‘idaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ – yau tassā yaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhau, cāgau, paṭinissaggau, mutti, anālayau| ‘‘idaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ – ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

15. ‘‘idaṃ  dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍canti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyanti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñātanti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘idaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍canti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pahātabbanti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pahīnanti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘idaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍canti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau  udapādi| taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ sacchikātabbanti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| taṃ  khau panidaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ sacchikatanti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘idaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍canti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ bhāvaitabbanti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ bhāvitanti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ  udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

16. ‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca mai, bhikkhavai, imaisu catūsu ariyasac‍caisu aivaṃ tiparivaṭṭaṃ dvādasākāraṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ na suvisuddhaṃ ahausi, naiva tāvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| yatau ca khau mai, bhikkhavai, imaisu catūsu ariyasac‍caisu aivaṃ tiparivaṭṭaṃ dvādasākāraṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ ahausi, athāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti abhisambuddhau (sī॰ syā॰) pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – akuppā mai vimutti, ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā attamanā pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti idamavauca…pai॰… abhinanduntivākyaṃ sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu natthi|

imasmiñ‍ca  pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai āyasmatau kauṇḍañ‍ñassa virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti|

17. pavattitai  ca pana bhagavatā dhammacak‍kai, bhummā daivā saddamanussāvaisuṃ – ‘‘aitaṃ bhagavatā bārāṇasiyaṃ isipatanai migadāyai  anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ, appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmi’’nti| bhummānaṃ daivānaṃ saddaṃ sutvā cātumahārājikā daivā saddamanussāvaisuṃ…pai॰… cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ saddaṃ sutvā tāvatiṃsā daivā…pai॰… yāmā daivā…pai॰… tusitā daivā…pai॰… nimmānaratī  daivā…pai॰… paranimmitavasavattī daivā…pai॰… brahmakāyikā daivā saddamanussāvaisuṃ – ‘‘aitaṃ bhagavatā bārāṇasiyaṃ isipatanai migadāyai anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmi’’nti| itiha, taina khaṇaina, taina layaina taina layaināti padadvayaṃ sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu natthi taina muhuttaina yāva brahmalaukā saddau abbhuggacchi| ayañ‍ca dasasahassilaukadhātu saṃkampi sampakampi sampavaidhi; appamāṇau ca uḷārau aubhāsau laukai pāturahausi, atik‍kamma daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| atha khau bhagavā imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘añ‍ñāsi vata, bhau kauṇḍañ‍ñau, añ‍ñāsi vata bhau kauṇḍañ‍ñau’’ti| iti hidaṃ āyasmatau kauṇḍañ‍ñassa ‘añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñau’ tvaiva nāmaṃ ahausi|

18. atha khau āyasmā añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñau diṭṭhadhammau pattadhammau viditadhammau pariyaugāḷhadhammau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti| ‘‘aihi bhikkhū’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘svākkhātau dhammau, cara brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| sāva tassa āyasmatau upasampadā ahausi|

19. atha khau bhagavā tadavasaisai bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya auvadi anusāsi| atha khau āyasmatau ca vappassa āyasmatau ca bhaddiyassa bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya auvadiyamānānaṃ anusāsiyamānānaṃ virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti|

tai  diṭṭhadhammā pattadhammā viditadhammā pariyaugāḷhadhammā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai  bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘labhaiyyāma mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyāma upasampada’’nti| ‘‘aitha bhikkhavau’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘svākkhātau dhammau, caratha  brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| sāva taisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ upasampadā ahausi|

atha khau bhagavā tadavasaisai bhikkhū nīhārabhattau dhammiyā kathāya auvadi anusāsi| yaṃ tayau bhikkhū piṇḍāya caritvā āharanti, taina chabbaggau yāpaiti| atha khau āyasmatau ca mahānāmassa āyasmatau ca assajissa bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya auvadiyamānānaṃ anusāsiyamānānaṃ virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti| tai diṭṭhadhammā pattadhammā viditadhammā pariyaugāḷhadhammā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘labhaiyyāma mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyāma upasampada’’nti| ‘‘aitha bhikkhavau’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘svākkhātau dhammau, caratha brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| sāva taisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ upasampadā ahausi|

20. atha khau bhagavā pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū āmantaisi –

saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.59 ādayau ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā| rūpañ‍ca hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, attā abhavissa, nayidaṃ rūpaṃ ābādhāya saṃvattaiyya, labbhaitha ca rūpai – ‘aivaṃ mai rūpaṃ  hautu, aivaṃ mai rūpaṃ mā ahausī’ti| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ anattā, tasmā rūpaṃ ābādhāya saṃvattati, na ca labbhati rūpai – ‘aivaṃ mai rūpaṃ hautu, aivaṃ mai rūpaṃ mā ahausī’ti| vaidanā, anattā| vaidanā ca hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, attā abhavissa, nayidaṃ vaidanā ābādhāya saṃvattaiyya, labbhaitha ca vaidanāya – ‘aivaṃ mai vaidanā hautu, aivaṃ mai vaidanā mā ahausī’ti| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, vaidanā anattā, tasmā vaidanā ābādhāya saṃvattati, na ca labbhati vaidanāya – ‘aivaṃ mai vaidanā hautu, aivaṃ mai vaidanā mā ahausī’ti| sañ‍ñā, anattā| sañ‍ñā ca hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, attā abhavissa, nayidaṃ sañ‍ñā ābādhāya saṃvattaiyya, labbhaitha  ca sañ‍ñāya – ‘aivaṃ mai sañ‍ñā hautu, aivaṃ mai sañ‍ñā mā ahausī’ti| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā anattā, tasmā sañ‍ñā ābādhāya saṃvattati, na ca labbhati sañ‍ñāya – ‘aivaṃ mai sañ‍ñā hautu, aivaṃ mai sañ‍ñā mā ahausī’ti| saṅkhārā, anattā| saṅkhārā ca hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, attā abhavissaṃsu, nayidaṃ nayimai (ka॰) saṅkhārā ābādhāya saṃvattaiyyuṃ, labbhaitha ca saṅkhāraisu – ‘aivaṃ mai saṅkhārā hauntu, aivaṃ mai saṅkhārā mā ahaisu’nti| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā anattā, tasmā saṅkhārā ābādhāya saṃvattanti, na ca labbhati saṅkhāraisu – ‘aivaṃ mai saṅkhārā hauntu, aivaṃ mai saṅkhārā mā ahaisu’nti| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, anattā| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, attā abhavissa, nayidaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ābādhāya saṃvattaiyya, labbhaitha ca viñ‍ñāṇai – ‘aivaṃ mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hautu, aivaṃ mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mā ahausī’ti| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā, tasmā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ābādhāya saṃvattati, na ca labbhati viñ‍ñāṇai – ‘aivaṃ mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hautu, aivaṃ mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mā ahausī’ti|

21. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vāti? anic‍caṃ, bhantai| yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vāti? dukkhaṃ, bhantai| yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti? nau haitaṃ, bhantai| vaidanā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vāti? anic‍cā, bhantai| yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vāti? dukkhaṃ, bhantai| yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti? nau haitaṃ, bhantai| sañ‍ñā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vāti? anic‍cā, bhantai| yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vāti? dukkhaṃ, bhantai| yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti? nau haitaṃ, bhantai| saṅkhārā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vāti? anic‍cā, bhantai| yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vāti? dukkhaṃ, bhantai| yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ  vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti? nau haitaṃ, bhantai| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vāti? anic‍caṃ, bhantai| yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vāti? dukkhaṃ, bhantai| yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti? nau haitaṃ, bhantai|

22. ‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai yaṃ dūrai vā (syā॰) santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ – naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attāti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| yā kāci vaidanā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikā vā sukhumā vā hīnā vā paṇītā vā yā dūrai santikai vā, sabbā vaidanā – naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attāti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| yā kāci sañ‍ñā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikā vā sukhumā vā hīnā vā paṇītā vā yā dūrai santikai vā, sabbā sañ‍ñā – naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attāti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| yai kaici saṅkhārā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikā vā sukhumā vā hīnā  vā paṇītā vā yai dūrai santikai vā, sabbai saṅkhārā – naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attāti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attāti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ|

23. ‘‘aivaṃ  passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati, sañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati, saṅkhāraisupi nibbindati, viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati; nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti, ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti|

24. idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti abhinanduṃ (syā॰)| imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai pañ‍cavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsu| taina khau pana samayaina cha laukai arahantau haunti|

pañ‍cavaggiyakathā niṭṭhitā|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau|

7. pabbaj‍jākathā

25. taina  khau pana samayaina bārāṇasiyaṃ yasau nāma kulaputtau saiṭṭhiputtau sukhumālau hauti| tassa tayau pāsādā haunti – aikau  haimantikau, aikau gimhikau, aikau vassikau| sau vassikai pāsādai cattārau māsai vassikai pāsādai vassikai cattārau māsai (sī॰) nippurisaihi tūriyaihi paricārayamānau na haiṭṭhāpāsādaṃ aurauhati| atha khau yasassa kulaputtassa pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitassa samaṅgībhūtassa paricārayamānassa paṭikac‍caiva paṭigac‍caiva (sī॰) niddā auk‍kami, parijanassapi niddā auk‍kami, sabbarattiyau ca tailapadīpau jhāyati| atha khau yasau kulaputtau paṭikac‍caiva pabujjhitvā addasa sakaṃ parijanaṃ supantaṃ – añ‍ñissā kacchai vīṇaṃ, añ‍ñissā kaṇṭhai mudiṅgaṃ, añ‍ñissā kacchai āḷambaraṃ, añ‍ñaṃ vikaisikaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ vikkhaiḷikaṃ, añ‍ñā vippalapantiyau, hatthappattaṃ susānaṃ mañ‍ñai| disvānassa ādīnavau pāturahausi, nibbidāya cittaṃ saṇṭhāsi| atha khau yasau kulaputtau udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘upaddutaṃ vata bhau, upassaṭṭhaṃ vata bhau’’ti|

atha khau yasau kulaputtau suvaṇṇapādukāyau ārauhitvā yaina nivaisanadvāraṃ tainupasaṅkami| amanussā dvāraṃ vivariṃsu – mā yasassa kulaputtassa kauci antarāyamakāsi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyāti| atha khau yasau kulaputtau yaina nagaradvāraṃ tainupasaṅkami| amanussā dvāraṃ  vivariṃsu – mā yasassa kulaputtassa kauci antarāyamakāsi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyāti| atha khau yasau kulaputtau yaina isipatanaṃ migadāyau tainupasaṅkami|

26. taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya ajjhaukāsai caṅkamati| addasā khau bhagavā yasaṃ kulaputtaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ, disvāna caṅkamā aurauhitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau yasau kulaputtau bhagavatau avidūrai udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘upaddutaṃ vata bhau, upassaṭṭhaṃ vata bhau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā yasaṃ kulaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ khau, yasa, anupaddutaṃ, idaṃ anupassaṭṭhaṃ| aihi yasa, nisīda, dhammaṃ tai daisaissāmī’’ti| atha khau yasau kulaputtau – idaṃ kira anupaddutaṃ, idaṃ anupassaṭṭhanti haṭṭhau udaggau suvaṇṇapādukāhi aurauhitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nassa khau yasassa kulaputtassa bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ, kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā bhagavā añ‍ñāsi  yasaṃ kulaputtaṃ kal‍lacittaṃ, muducittaṃ, vinīvaraṇacittaṃ, udaggacittaṃ, pasan‍nacittaṃ, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ, samudayaṃ, niraudhaṃ, maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi  nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, aivamaiva yasassa kulaputtassa tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti|

27. atha khau yasassa kulaputtassa mātā pāsādaṃ abhiruhitvā yasaṃ kulaputtaṃ apassantī yaina saiṭṭhi gahapati tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā saiṭṭhiṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘puttau tai, gahapati, yasau na dissatī’’ti| atha khau saiṭṭhi gahapati catuddisā assadūtai uyyaujaitvā sāmaṃyaiva yaina isipatanaṃ migadāyau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau saiṭṭhi gahapati suvaṇṇapādukānaṃ nikkhaipaṃ, disvāna taṃyaiva anugamāsi anugamā (sī॰ syā॰)| addasā khau bhagavā saiṭṭhiṃ gahapatiṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ, disvāna bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharaiyyaṃ yathā saiṭṭhi gahapati idha nisin‍nau idha nisin‍naṃ yasaṃ kulaputtaṃ na passaiyyā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharaisi| atha khau saiṭṭhi gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘api, bhantai, bhagavā yasaṃ kulaputtaṃ passaiyyā’’ti? taina hi, gahapati, nisīda, appaiva nāma idha nisin‍nau idha nisin‍naṃ yasaṃ kulaputtaṃ passaiyyāsīti| atha khau saiṭṭhi gahapati – idhaiva kirāhaṃ nisin‍nau idha nisin‍naṃ  yasaṃ kulaputtaṃ  passissāmīti haṭṭhau udaggau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nassa khau saiṭṭhissa gahapatissa bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ, kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ  pakāsaisi| yadā bhagavā añ‍ñāsi saiṭṭhiṃ gahapatiṃ kal‍lacittaṃ, muducittaṃ, vinīvaraṇacittaṃ, udaggacittaṃ, pasan‍nacittaṃ, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā, taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ, samudayaṃ, niraudhaṃ, maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya aivamaiva saiṭṭhissa gahapatissa tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti| atha khau saiṭṭhi gahapati diṭṭhadhammau pattadhammau viditadhammau pariyaugāḷhadhammau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ nikuj‍jitaṃ (ka॰) vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti – aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dhammañ‍ca, bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| sauva laukai paṭhamaṃ upāsakau ahausi taivācikau|

28. atha khau yasassa kulaputtassa pitunau dhammai daisiyamānai yathādiṭṭhaṃ yathāviditaṃ bhūmiṃ pac‍cavaikkhantassa anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍ci| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘yasassa khau kulaputtassa pitunau dhammai daisiyamānai yathādiṭṭhaṃ yathāviditaṃ bhūmiṃ pac‍cavaikkhantassa anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuttaṃ| abhabbau khau yasau kulaputtau hīnāyāvattitvā kāmai paribhuñ‍jituṃ, saiyyathāpi pubbai agārikabhūtau; yaṃnūnāhaṃ taṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ paṭippassambhaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā taṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ paṭippassambhaisi| addasā khau saiṭṭhi gahapati yasaṃ kulaputtaṃ nisin‍naṃ, disvāna yasaṃ kulaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mātā tai tāta, yasa, paridaiva paridaivī (ka॰) saukasamāpan‍nā, daihi mātuyā jīvita’’nti| atha khau yasau kulaputtau bhagavantaṃ ul‍laukaisi| atha khau bhagavā saiṭṭhiṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gahapati, yassa saikkhaina ñāṇaina saikkhaina dassanaina dhammau diṭṭhau viditau saiyyathāpi tayā? tassa yathādiṭṭhaṃ yathāviditaṃ bhūmiṃ pac‍cavaikkhantassa anupādāya  āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuttaṃ| bhabbau nu khau sau, gahapati, hīnāyāvattitvā kāmai paribhuñ‍jituṃ saiyyathāpi pubbai agārikabhūtau’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yasassa khau, gahapati, kulaputtassa saikkhaina ñāṇaina saikkhaina dassanaina  dhammau diṭṭhau viditau saiyyathāpi tayā| tassa yathādiṭṭhaṃ yathāviditaṃ bhūmiṃ pac‍cavaikkhantassa anupādāya āsavaihi  cittaṃ vimuttaṃ| abhabbau khau, gahapati, yasau kulaputtau hīnāyāvattitvā kāmai paribhuñ‍jituṃ saiyyathāpi pubbai agārikabhūtau’’ti| ‘‘lābhā, bhantai, yasassa kulaputtassa, suladdhaṃ, bhantai, yasassa kulaputtassa, yathā yasassa kulaputtassa anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuttaṃ| adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā aj‍jatanāya bhattaṃ yasaina kulaputtaina pacchāsamaṇainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau saiṭṭhi gahapati bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau yasau kulaputtau acirapak‍kantai saiṭṭhimhi gahapatimhi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti| ‘‘aihi bhikkhū’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘svākkhātau dhammau, cara brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| sāva  tassa āyasmatau upasampadā ahausi| taina khau pana samayaina satta laukai arahantau haunti|

yasassa pabbaj‍jā niṭṭhitā|

29. atha  khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya āyasmatā yasaina pacchāsamaṇaina yaina saiṭṭhissa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau āyasmatau yasassa mātā ca purāṇadutiyikā ca yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| tāsaṃ bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ, kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā tā bhagavā añ‍ñāsi kal‍lacittā, muducittā, vinīvaraṇacittā, udaggacittā, pasan‍nacittā, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ, samudayaṃ, niraudhaṃ, maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, aivamaiva tāsaṃ tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti| tā diṭṭhadhammā pattadhammā viditadhammā pariyaugāḷhadhammā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… aitā mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ  saraṇaṃ gacchāma, dhammañ‍ca, bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsikāyau nau bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitā saraṇaṃ gatā’’ti| tā ca laukai paṭhamaṃ upāsikā ahaisuṃ taivācikā|

atha khau āyasmatau yasassa mātā ca pitā ca purāṇadutiyikā ca bhagavantañ‍ca āyasmantañ‍ca yasaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā, bhagavantaṃ  bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ, aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| atha khau bhagavā āyasmatau yasassa mātarañ‍ca pitarañ‍ca purāṇadutiyikañ‍ca dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

30. assausuṃ khau āyasmatau yasassa cattārau gihisahāyakā bārāṇasiyaṃ saiṭṭhānusaiṭṭhīnaṃ kulānaṃ puttā – vimalau, subāhu, puṇṇaji, gavampati – yasau kira kulaputtau kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitauti| sutvāna naisaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘na hi nūna sau aurakau dhammavinayau, na sā aurakā pabbaj‍jā, yattha yasau kulaputtau kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau’’ti| tai tai cattārau janā (ka॰) yaināyasmā yasau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ yasaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| atha khau āyasmā yasau tai cattārau gihisahāyakai ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā yasau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imai mai, bhantai, cattārau gihisahāyakā bārāṇasiyaṃ saiṭṭhānusaiṭṭhīnaṃ kulānaṃ puttā – vimalau, subāhu, puṇṇaji, gavampati| imai imai cattārau (ka॰) bhagavā auvadatu anusāsatū’’ti| taisaṃ bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi, yadā tai bhagavā añ‍ñāsi kal‍lacittai muducittai vinīvaraṇacittai udaggacittai pasan‍nacittai, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā, taṃ pakāsaisi dukkhaṃ samudayaṃ niraudhaṃ maggaṃ, saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, aivamaiva taisaṃ tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti| tai diṭṭhadhammā pattadhammā viditadhammā pariyaugāḷhadhammā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘labhaiyyāma mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyāma upasampada’’nti| ‘‘aitha bhikkhavau’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘svākkhātau dhammau, caratha brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| sāva taisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ upasampadā ahausi| atha khau bhagavā tai bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya auvadi anusāsi| taisaṃ bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya auvadiyamānānaṃ anusāsiyamānānaṃ anupādāya āsavaihi  cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsu| taina khau pana samayaina aikādasa laukai arahantau haunti|

catugihisahāyakapabbaj‍jā niṭṭhitā|

31. assausuṃ  khau āyasmatau yasassa pañ‍ñāsamattā gihisahāyakā jānapadā pubbānupubbakānaṃ kulānaṃ puttā – yasau kira kulaputtau kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitauti| sutvāna naisaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘na hi nūna sau aurakau dhammavinayau, na sā aurakā pabbaj‍jā, yattha yasau kulaputtau kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau’’ti| tai yaināyasmā yasau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ yasaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| atha khau āyasmā yasau tai pañ‍ñāsamattai gihisahāyakai ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā yasau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imai mai, bhantai, pañ‍ñāsamattā gihisahāyakā jānapadā pubbānupubbakānaṃ kulānaṃ puttā| imai bhagavā auvadatu anusāsatū’’ti| taisaṃ bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā tai bhagavā añ‍ñāsi kal‍lacittai muducittai vinīvaraṇacittai udaggacittai pasan‍nacittai, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā  dhammadaisanā, taṃ pakāsaisi dukkhaṃ samudayaṃ niraudhaṃ maggaṃ, saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, aivamaiva taisaṃ tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti| tai diṭṭhadhammā pattadhammā viditadhammā pariyaugāḷhadhammā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘labhaiyyāma mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyāma upasampada’’nti| ‘‘aitha bhikkhavau’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘svākkhātau dhammau, caratha brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| sāva taisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ upasampadā ahausi| atha khau bhagavā tai bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya auvadi anusāsi| taisaṃ bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya auvadiyamānānaṃ anusāsiyamānānaṃ anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsu| taina khau pana samayaina aikasaṭṭhi laukai arahantau haunti|

pañ‍ñāsagihisahāyakapabbaj‍jā niṭṭhitā|

niṭṭhitā ca pabbaj‍jākathā|

8. mārakathā

32. atha khau bhagavā tai bhikkhū āmantaisi saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.141 mārasaṃyuttaipi – ‘‘muttāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sabbapāsaihi, yai  dibbā yai ca mānusā| tumhaipi, bhikkhavai, muttā sabbapāsaihi, yai dibbā yai ca mānusā| caratha, bhikkhavai, cārikaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ| mā aikaina dvai agamittha| daisaitha, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ  sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaitha| santi sattā apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti, bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārau| ahampi, bhikkhavai, yaina uruvailā sainānigamau tainupasaṅkamissāmi dhammadaisanāyā’’ti|

33. atha khau mārau pāpimā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘baddhausi sabbapāsaihi, yai dibbā yai ca mānusā|

mahābandhanabaddhausi, na mai samaṇa maukkhasī’’ti||

‘‘muttāhaṃ muttauhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) sabbapāsaihi, yai dibbā yai ca mānusā|

mahābandhanamuttaumhi, nihatau tvamasi antakāti||

saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.151 mārasaṃyuttaipi ‘‘antalikkhacarau pāsau, yvāyaṃ carati mānasau|

taina taṃ bādhayissāmi, na mai samaṇa maukkhasīti||

saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.1151 mārasaṃyuttaipi ‘‘rūpā saddā rasā gandhā, phauṭṭhabbā ca manauramā|

aittha mai vigatau chandau, nihatau tvamasi antakā’’ti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā – jānāti maṃ bhagavā, jānāti maṃ sugatauti dukkhī dummanau

tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

mārakathā niṭṭhitā|

9. pabbaj‍jūpasampadākathā

34. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū nānādisā nānājanapadā pabbaj‍jāpaikkhai ca upasampadāpaikkhai  ca ānainti – bhagavā nai pabbājaissati  upasampādaissatīti| tattha bhikkhū caiva kilamanti pabbaj‍jāpaikkhā ca upasampadāpaikkhā ca| atha khau bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘aitarahi khau bhikkhū nānādisā nānājanapadā pabbaj‍jāpaikkhai ca upasampadāpaikkhai ca ānainti – bhagavā nai pabbājaissati  upasampādaissatīti| tattha bhikkhū caiva kilamanti pabbaj‍jāpaikkhā ca upasampadāpaikkhā ca| yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anujānaiyyaṃ – tumhaiva dāni, bhikkhavai, tāsu tāsu disāsu taisu taisu janapadaisu pabbājaitha upasampādaithā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū  āmantaisi – ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘aitarahi khau bhikkhū nānādisā nānājanapadā pabbaj‍jāpaikkhai ca upasampadāpaikkhai ca ānainti bhagavā nai pabbājaissati upasampādaissatīti, tattha bhikkhū caiva kilamanti pabbaj‍jāpaikkhā ca upasampadāpaikkhā ca, yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anujānaiyyaṃ tumhaiva dāni, bhikkhavai, tāsu tāsu disāsu taisu taisu janapadaisu pabbājaitha upasampādaithā’’’ti, anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tumhaiva dāni tāsu tāsu disāsu taisu taisu janapadaisu pabbājaitha upasampādaitha| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, pabbājaitabbau upasampādaitabbau –

paṭhamaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhārāpaitvā  auhāraitvā (ka॰), kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādāpaitvā, aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ kārāpaitvā, bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vandāpaitvā, uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīdāpaitvā, añ‍jaliṃ paggaṇhāpaitvā, aivaṃ vadaihīti vattabbau – buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi; dutiyampi buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dutiyampi dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dutiyampi saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi; tatiyampi buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, tatiyampi dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, tatiyampi saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmī’’ti| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, imaihi tīhi saraṇagamanaihi pabbaj‍jaṃ upasampada’’nti|

tīhi saraṇagamanaihi upasampadākathā niṭṭhitā|

10. dutiyamārakathā

35. atha khau bhagavā vassaṃvuṭṭhau vassaṃvutthau (sī॰) bhikkhū āmantaisi saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.155 – ‘‘mayhaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasikārā yaunisau sammappadhānā anuttarā vimutti anuppattā, anuttarā vimutti sacchikatā| tumhaipi, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasikārā  yaunisau sammappadhānā anuttaraṃ vimuttiṃ anupāpuṇātha, anuttaraṃ vimuttiṃ sacchikarauthā’’ti| atha khau mārau pāpimā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘baddhausi mārapāsaihi, yai dibbā yai ca mānusā|

mahābandhanabaddhausi mārabandhanabaddhausi (sī॰ syā॰), na mai samaṇa maukkhasī’’ti||

‘‘muttāhaṃ mārapāsaihi, yai dibbā yai ca mānusā|

mahābandhanamuttaumhi  mārabandhanamuttaumhi (sī॰ syā॰), nihatau tvamasi antakā’’ti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā – jānāti maṃ bhagavā, jānāti maṃ sugatauti dukkhī dummanau

tatthaivantaradhāyi|

dutiyamārakathā niṭṭhitā|

11. bhaddavaggiyavatthu

36. atha  khau bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina uruvailā taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā maggā auk‍kamma yaina añ‍ñatarau vanasaṇḍau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisīdi| taina khau pana samayaina tiṃsamattā bhaddavaggiyā sahāyakā sapajāpatikā tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai paricārainti| aikassa pajāpati nāhausi; tassa atthāya vaisī ānītā ahausi| atha khau sā vaisī taisu pamattaisu paricāraintaisu bhaṇḍaṃ ādāya palāyittha| atha khau tai sahāyakā sahāyakassa vaiyyāvac‍caṃ karauntā, taṃ itthiṃ gavaisantā, taṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ āhiṇḍantā addasaṃsu bhagavantaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisin‍naṃ| disvāna yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘api, bhantai, bhagavā aikaṃ itthiṃ passaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘kiṃ pana vau, kumārā, itthiyā’’ti? ‘‘idha mayaṃ, bhantai, tiṃsamattā bhaddavaggiyā sahāyakā sapajāpatikā imasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai paricārimhā| aikassa pajāpati nāhausi; tassa atthāya vaisī ānītā ahausi| atha khau sā, bhantai, vaisī amhaisu pamattaisu paricāraintaisu bhaṇḍaṃ ādāya  palāyittha| tai mayaṃ, bhantai, sahāyakā sahāyakassa vaiyyāvac‍caṃ karauntā, taṃ itthiṃ gavaisantā, imaṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ āhiṇḍāmā’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha vau, kumārā, katamaṃ nu khau tumhākaṃ varaṃ  – yaṃ vā tumhai itthiṃ gavaisaiyyātha, yaṃ vā attānaṃ gavaisaiyyāthā’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, amhākaṃ varaṃ yaṃ mayaṃ attānaṃ gavaisaiyyāmā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi vau, kumārā, nisīdatha, dhammaṃ vau daisaissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhaddavaggiyā sahāyakā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| taisaṃ bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi, yadā tai bhagavā añ‍ñāsi kal‍lacittai muducittai vinīvaraṇacittai udaggacittai pasan‍nacittai, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā, taṃ pakāsaisi dukkhaṃ samudayaṃ niraudhaṃ maggaṃ, saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, aivamaiva taisaṃ tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti| tai diṭṭhadhammā pattadhammā viditadhammā pariyaugāḷhadhammā tiṇṇavicikicchā  vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘labhaiyyāma mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyāma upasampada’’nti| ‘‘aitha bhikkhavau’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘svākkhātau dhammau, caratha  brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| sāva taisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ upasampadā ahausi|

bhaddavaggiyasahāyakānaṃ vatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

dutiyabhāṇavārau|

12. uruvailapāṭihāriyakathā

37. atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina uruvailā tadavasari| taina khau pana samayaina uruvailāyaṃ tayau jaṭilā paṭivasanti – uruvailakassapau, nadīkassapau, gayākassapauti| taisu uruvailakassapau  jaṭilau pañ‍can‍naṃ jaṭilasatānaṃ nāyakau hauti, vināyakau aggau pamukhau pāmaukkhau| nadīkassapau jaṭilau tiṇṇaṃ jaṭilasatānaṃ nāyakau hauti, vināyakau aggau pamukhau pāmaukkhau| gayākassapau jaṭilau dvin‍naṃ jaṭilasatānaṃ nāyakau hauti, vināyakau aggau pamukhau pāmaukkhau| atha khau bhagavā yaina uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa assamau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā uruvailakassapaṃ jaṭilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai tai, kassapa, agaru, vasaiyyāma aikarattaṃ agyāgārai’’ti? ‘‘na khau mai, mahāsamaṇa, garu, caṇḍaittha nāgarājā iddhimā āsivisau  ghauravisau, sau taṃ mā vihaiṭhaisī’’ti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā uruvailakassapaṃ jaṭilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai tai, kassapa, agaru, vasaiyyāma aikarattaṃ agyāgārai’’ti? ‘‘na khau mai, mahāsamaṇa, garu, caṇḍaittha nāgarājā iddhimā āsivisau ghauravisau, sau taṃ mā vihaiṭhaisī’’ti| tatiyampi khau bhagavā uruvailakassapaṃ jaṭilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai tai, kassapa, agaru, vasaiyyāma aikarattaṃ agyāgārai’’ti? ‘‘na khau mai, mahāsamaṇa, garu, caṇḍaittha nāgarājā iddhimā āsivisau ghauravisau, sau taṃ mā vihaiṭhaisī’’ti| ‘‘appaiva maṃ na vihaiṭhaiyya, iṅgha tvaṃ, kassapa, anujānāhi agyāgāra’’nti| ‘‘vihara, mahāsamaṇa, yathāsukha’’nti| atha khau bhagavā agyāgāraṃ pavisitvā tiṇasanthārakaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā nisīdi pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā|

38. addasā khau sau nāgau bhagavantaṃ paviṭṭhaṃ, disvāna dummanau dukkhī dummanau (sī॰ syā॰) padhūpāyi pakhūpāsi (ka॰)| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ imassa nāgassa anupahac‍ca chaviñ‍ca  cammañ‍ca maṃsañ‍ca nhāruñ‍ca aṭṭhiñ‍ca aṭṭhimiñ‍jañ‍ca taijasā taijaṃ pariyādiyaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā padhūpāyi| atha khau sau nāgau makkhaṃ asahamānau paj‍jali| bhagavāpi taijaudhātuṃ samāpaj‍jitvā paj‍jali| ubhin‍naṃ sajautibhūtānaṃ agyāgāraṃ ādittaṃ viya hauti sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ| atha khau tai jaṭilā agyāgāraṃ parivāraitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘abhirūpau vata bhau mahāsamaṇau nāgaina vihaiṭhiyatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā tassā  rattiyā ac‍cayaina tassa nāgassa  anupahac‍ca chaviñ‍ca cammañ‍ca maṃsañ‍ca nhāruñ‍ca aṭṭhiñ‍ca aṭṭhimiñ‍jañ‍ca taijasā taijaṃ pariyādiyitvā pattai pakkhipitvā uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa dassaisi – ‘‘ayaṃ tai, kassapa, nāgau pariyādin‍nau pariyādiṇṇau (ka॰) assa taijasā taijau’’ti| atha khau uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahiddhikau khau mahāsamaṇau mahānubhāvau, yatra hi nāma caṇḍassa nāgarājassa iddhimatau āsivisassa ghauravisassa taijasā taijaṃ pariyādiyissati, natvaiva ca khau arahā yathā aha’’nti|

39.

 nairañ‍jarāyaṃ bhagavā, uruvailakassapaṃ jaṭilaṃ avauca|

‘‘sacai tai kassapa agaru, viharaimu aj‍jaṇhau aggisālamhī’’ti aggisaraṇamhīti (sī॰ syā॰)||

‘‘na khau mai mahāsamaṇa garu|

phāsukāmauva taṃ nivāraimi|

caṇḍaittha nāgarājā|

iddhimā āsivisau ghauravisau|

sau  taṃ mā vihaiṭhaisī’’ti||

‘‘appaiva maṃ na vihaiṭhaiyya|

iṅgha tvaṃ kassapa anujānāhi agyāgāra’’nti|

din‍nanti naṃ viditvā|

abhītau asambhītau (sī॰) pāvisi bhayamatītau||

disvā isiṃ paviṭṭhaṃ, ahināgau dummanau padhūpāyi|

sumanamanasau adhimanau avimanau (katthaci), navimanau (syā॰), manussanāgaupi tattha padhūpāyi||

makkhañ‍ca  asahamānau, ahināgau pāvakauva paj‍jali|

taijaudhātusu kusalau, manussanāgaupi tattha paj‍jali||

ubhin‍naṃ sajautibhūtānaṃ|

agyāgāraṃ ādittaṃ hauti sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ|

udiccharai jaṭilā|

‘‘abhirūpau vata bhau mahāsamaṇau|

nāgaina vihaiṭhiyatī’’ti bhaṇanti||

atha  tassā rattiyā atha rattiyā (sī॰ syā॰) ac‍cayaina|

hatā nāgassa ac‍ciyau haunti ahināgassa ac‍ciyau na haunti (sī॰ syā॰)|

iddhimatau pana ṭhitā iddhimatau panuṭṭhitā (sī॰)|

anaikavaṇṇā ac‍ciyau haunti||

nīlā atha lauhitikā|

mañ‍jiṭṭhā pītakā phalikavaṇṇāyau|

aṅgīrasassa kāyai|

anaikavaṇṇā ac‍ciyau haunti||

pattamhi  audahitvā|

ahināgaṃ brāhmaṇassa dassaisi|

‘‘ayaṃ tai kassapa nāgau|

pariyādin‍nau assa taijasā taijau’’ti||

atha khau uruvailakassapau jaṭilau bhagavatau iminā iddhipāṭihāriyaina abhippasan‍nau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhaiva, mahāsamaṇa, vihara, ahaṃ tai tai upaṭṭhāmi (itipi) dhuvabhattainā’’ti|

paṭhamaṃ pāṭihāriyaṃ|

40. atha  khau bhagavā uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa assamassa avidūrai  añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai vihāsi| atha khau cattārau mahārājānau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā catuddisā aṭṭhaṃsu saiyyathāpi mahantā aggikkhandhā| atha khau uruvailakassapau jaṭilau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kālau, mahāsamaṇa, niṭṭhitaṃ bhattaṃ| kai nu khau tai, mahāsamaṇa, abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina tvaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ abhivādaitvā catuddisā aṭṭhaṃsu ‘‘saiyyathāpi mahantā aggikkhandhā’’ti| ‘‘aitai khau, kassapa, cattārau mahārājānau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu dhammassavanāyā’’ti| atha khau uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahiddhikau khau mahāsamaṇau mahānubhāvau, yatra hi nāma cattāraupi mahārājānau upasaṅkamissanti dhammassavanāya, na tvaiva ca khau arahā yathā aha’’nti| atha khau bhagavā uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa bhattaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā tasmiṃyaiva vanasaṇḍai vihāsi|

dutiyaṃ pāṭihāriyaṃ|

41. atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi saiyyathāpi mahāaggikkhandhau, purimāhi vaṇṇanibhāhi abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| atha khau uruvailakassapau jaṭilau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina  yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kālau, mahāsamaṇa, niṭṭhitaṃ bhattaṃ| kau nu khau sau, mahāsamaṇa, abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina tvaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi saiyyathāpi mahāaggikkhandhau, purimāhi vaṇṇanibhāhi abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘aisau khau, kassapa, sak‍kau daivānamindau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami dhammassavanāyā’’ti| atha khau uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahiddhikau khau mahāsamaṇau mahānubhāvau, yatra hi nāma sak‍kaupi  daivānamindau upasaṅkamissati dhammassavanāya, na tvaiva ca khau arahā yathā aha’’nti| atha khau bhagavā uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa bhattaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā tasmiṃyaiva vanasaṇḍai vihāsi|

tatiyaṃ pāṭihāriyaṃ|

42. atha  khau brahmā sahampati abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi saiyyathāpi mahāaggikkhandhau, purimāhi vaṇṇanibhāhi abhik‍kantatarau ca  paṇītatarau ca| atha khau uruvailakassapau jaṭilau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kālau, mahāsamaṇa, niṭṭhitaṃ bhattaṃ| kau nu khau sau, mahāsamaṇa, abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina tvaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi saiyyathāpi mahāaggikkhandhau, purimāhi vaṇṇanibhāhi abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘aisau khau, kassapa, brahmā sahampati yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami dhammassavanāyā’’ti| atha khau uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahiddhikau khau mahāsamaṇau mahānubhāvau, yatra hi nāma brahmāpi sahampati upasaṅkamissati dhammassavanāya, na tvaiva ca khau arahā yathā aha’’nti| atha khau bhagavā uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa bhattaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā tasmiṃyaiva vanasaṇḍai vihāsi|

catutthaṃ pāṭihāriyaṃ|

43. taina khau pana samayaina uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa mahāyañ‍ñau pac‍cupaṭṭhitau hauti, kaivalakappā ca aṅgamagadhā pahūtaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ ādāya abhik‍kamitukāmā haunti| atha khau uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘aitarahi khau mai mahāyañ‍ñau pac‍cupaṭṭhitau, kaivalakappā ca aṅgamagadhā pahūtaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ ādāya abhik‍kamissanti| sacai mahāsamaṇau mahājanakāyai iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissati, mahāsamaṇassa lābhasak‍kārau abhivaḍḍhissati, mama lābhasak‍kārau parihāyissati| ahau nūna mahāsamaṇau svātanāya nāgacchaiyyā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā uruvailakassapassa  jaṭilassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya uttarakuruṃ gantvā tatau piṇḍapātaṃ āharitvā anautattadahai paribhuñ‍jitvā tatthaiva divāvihāraṃ akāsi| atha khau uruvailakassapau jaṭilau tassā  rattiyā ac‍cayaina yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kālau, mahāsamaṇa, niṭṭhitaṃ bhattaṃ| kiṃ nu khau, mahāsamaṇa, hiyyau nāgamāsi? api ca mayaṃ taṃ sarāma – kiṃ nu khau mahāsamaṇau nāgacchatīti? khādanīyassa ca bhaujanīyassa ca tai paṭivīsau paṭiviṃsau (sī॰), paṭivisau (syā॰) ṭhapitau’’ti| nanu tai, kassapa, aitadahausi – ‘‘‘aitarahi khau mai mahāyañ‍ñau pac‍cupaṭṭhitau, kaivalakappā ca aṅgamagadhā pahūtaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ ādāya abhik‍kamissanti, sacai mahāsamaṇau mahājanakāyai iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissati, mahāsamaṇassa lābhasak‍kārau abhivaḍḍhissati, mama lābhasak‍kārau parihāyissati, ahau nūna mahāsamaṇau svātanāya nāgacchaiyyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, kassapa, tava caitasā caitauparivitak‍kaṃ añ‍ñāya uttarakuruṃ gantvā tatau piṇḍapātaṃ āharitvā anautattadahai paribhuñ‍jitvā tatthaiva divāvihāraṃ akāsi’’nti| atha khau uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahiddhikau khau mahāsamaṇau mahānubhāvau, yatra hi nāma caitasāpi cittaṃ pajānissati, na tvaiva ca khau arahā yathā aha’’nti| atha khau bhagavā uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa bhattaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā tasmiṃyaiva vanasaṇḍai vihāsi|

pañ‍camaṃ pāṭihāriyaṃ|

44. taina khau pana samayaina bhagavatau paṃsukūlaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘kattha nu khau ahaṃ paṃsukūlaṃ dhauvaiyya’’nti? atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau bhagavatau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya pāṇinā paukkharaṇiṃ khaṇitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha, bhantai, bhagavā paṃsukūlaṃ dhauvatū’’ti| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘kimhi nu khau ahaṃ paṃsukūlaṃ parimaddaiyya’’nti? atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau bhagavatau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya mahatiṃ silaṃ upanikkhipi – idha, bhantai, bhagavā paṃsukūlaṃ parimaddatūti| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘kimhi nu khau ahaṃ ahaṃ paṃsukūlaṃ (ka॰) ālambitvā  uttaraiyya’’nti? atha khau kakudhai adhivatthā daivatā bhagavatau  caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya sākhaṃ aunāmaisi – idha, bhantai, bhagavā ālambitvā  uttaratūti| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘kimhi nu khau ahaṃ paṃsukūlaṃ vissaj‍jaiyya’’nti? atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau bhagavatau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya mahatiṃ silaṃ upanikkhipi – idha, bhantai, bhagavā paṃsukūlaṃ vissaj‍jaitūti| atha khau uruvailakassapau jaṭilau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kālau, mahāsamaṇa, niṭṭhitaṃ bhattaṃ| kiṃ nu khau, mahāsamaṇa, nāyaṃ pubbai idha paukkharaṇī, sāyaṃ idha paukkharaṇī| nayimā silā pubbai upanikkhittā| kainimā silā upanikkhittā? nayimassa kakudhassa pubbai sākhā aunatā, sāyaṃ sākhā aunatā’’ti| idha mai, kassapa, paṃsukūlaṃ uppan‍naṃ ahausi| tassa mayhaṃ, kassapa, aitadahausi – ‘‘kattha nu khau ahaṃ paṃsukūlaṃ dhauvaiyya’’nti? atha khau, kassapa, sak‍kau daivānamindau mama caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya pāṇinā paukkharaṇiṃ khaṇitvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha, bhantai, bhagavā paṃsukūlaṃ dhauvatū’’ti| sāyaṃ kassapa amanussaina pāṇinā khaṇitā paukkharaṇī| tassa mayhaṃ, kassapa, aitadahausi – ‘‘kimhi nu khau ahaṃ paṃsukūlaṃ parimaddaiyya’’nti? atha khau, kassapa, sak‍kau daivānamindau mama caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya mahatiṃ silaṃ upanikkhipi – ‘‘idha, bhantai, bhagavā paṃsukūlaṃ parimaddatū’’ti| sāyaṃ kassapa amanussaina upanikkhittā silā| tassa mayhaṃ, kassapa, aitadahausi – ‘‘kimhi nu khau ahaṃ ālambitvā uttaraiyya’’nti? atha khau, kassapa, kakudhai adhivatthā daivatā mama caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya sākhaṃ aunāmaisi – ‘‘idha, bhantai, bhagavā ālambitvā uttaratū’’ti| svāyaṃ āharahatthau kakudhau| tassa mayhaṃ, kassapa, aitadahausi – ‘‘kimhi nu khau ahaṃ paṃsukūlaṃ vissaj‍jaiyya’’nti? atha khau, kassapa, sak‍kau daivānamindau mama caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya mahatiṃ silaṃ upanikkhipi – ‘‘idha, bhantai, bhagavā paṃsukūlaṃ vissaj‍jaitū’’ti| sāyaṃ kassapa amanussaina upanikkhittā silāti| atha khau uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahiddhikau khau mahāsamaṇau mahānubhāvau, yatra hi nāma sak‍kaupi daivānamindau vaiyyāvac‍caṃ karissati, na tvaiva ca khau arahā yathā aha’’nti| atha khau bhagavā uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa bhattaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā tasmiṃyaiva vanasaṇḍai vihāsi|

atha  khau uruvailakassapau jaṭilau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau  kālaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘kālau, mahāsamaṇa, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| ‘‘gaccha tvaṃ, kassapa, āyāmaha’’nti uruvailakassapaṃ jaṭilaṃ uyyaujaitvā yāya jambuyā  ‘jambudīpau’ pañ‍ñāyati, tatau phalaṃ gahaitvā paṭhamataraṃ āgantvā agyāgārai nisīdi| addasā khau uruvailakassapau jaṭilau bhagavantaṃ agyāgārai nisin‍naṃ, disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katamaina tvaṃ, mahāsamaṇa, maggaina āgatau? ahaṃ tayā paṭhamataraṃ pak‍kantau, sau tvaṃ paṭhamataraṃ āgantvā agyāgārai nisin‍nau’’ti| ‘‘idhāhaṃ, kassapa, taṃ uyyaujaitvā yāya jambuyā ‘jambudīpau’ pañ‍ñāyati, tatau phalaṃ gahaitvā paṭhamataraṃ āgantvā agyāgārai nisin‍nau| idaṃ khau, kassapa, jambuphalaṃ vaṇṇasampan‍naṃ gandhasampan‍naṃ rasasampan‍naṃ| sacai ākaṅkhasi paribhuñ‍jā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, mahāsamaṇa, tvaṃyaiva taṃ arahasi, tvaṃyaiva taṃ tvaṃyaivaitaṃ āharasi, tvaṃyaivaitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) paribhuñ‍jāhī’’ti| atha khau uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahiddhikau khau mahāsamaṇau mahānubhāvau, yatra hi nāma maṃ paṭhamataraṃ uyyaujaitvā yāya jambuyā ‘jambudīpau’ pañ‍ñāyati, tatau phalaṃ gahaitvā paṭhamataraṃ āgantvā agyāgārai nisīdissati, na tvaiva ca khau arahā yathā aha’’nti| atha khau bhagavā uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa bhattaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā tasmiṃyaiva vanasaṇḍai vihāsi|

45. atha khau uruvailakassapau jaṭilau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘kālau, mahāsamaṇa, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| gaccha tvaṃ, kassapa, āyāmahanti uruvailakassapaṃ jaṭilaṃ uyyaujaitvā yāya jambuyā ‘jambudīpau’ pañ‍ñāyati, tassā avidūrai ambau…pai॰… tassā avidūrai āmalakī…pai॰… tassā avidūrai harītakī…pai॰… tāvatiṃsaṃ gantvā pāricchattakapupphaṃ gahaitvā paṭhamataraṃ āgantvā agyāgārai nisīdi| addasā khau uruvailakassapau jaṭilau bhagavantaṃ agyāgārai nisin‍naṃ, disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katamaina tvaṃ, mahāsamaṇa, maggaina āgatau? ahaṃ tayā paṭhamataraṃ pak‍kantau, sau tvaṃ paṭhamataraṃ āgantvā agyāgārai nisin‍nau’’ti| ‘‘idhāhaṃ, kassapa, taṃ uyyaujaitvā tāvatiṃsaṃ gantvā pāricchattakapupphaṃ gahaitvā paṭhamataraṃ āgantvā agyāgārai nisin‍nau| idaṃ khau, kassapa, pāricchattakapupphaṃ  vaṇṇasampan‍naṃ gandhasampan‍naṃ sugandhikaṃ (ka॰)| (sacai ākaṅkhasi gaṇhā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, mahāsamaṇa, tvaṃyaiva taṃ arahasi, tvaṃyaiva taṃ gaṇhā’’ti) ( ) sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu natthi|  atha khau uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahiddhikau khau mahāsamaṇau mahānubhāvau, yatra hi nāma maṃ paṭhamataraṃ uyyaujaitvā tāvatiṃsaṃ gantvā pāricchattakapupphaṃ gahaitvā paṭhamataraṃ āgantvā agyāgārai nisīdissati, na tvaiva ca khau arahā yathā aha’’nti|

46. taina khau pana samayaina tai jaṭilā aggiṃ paricaritukāmā na sak‍kaunti kaṭṭhāni phālaituṃ| atha khau taisaṃ jaṭilānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau mahāsamaṇassa iddhānubhāvau, yathā mayaṃ na sak‍kauma kaṭṭhāni phālaitu’’nti| atha khau bhagavā uruvailakassapaṃ jaṭilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘phāliyantu, kassapa, kaṭṭhānī’’ti| ‘‘phāliyantu, mahāsamaṇā’’ti| sakidaiva pañ‍ca kaṭṭhasatāni phāliyiṃsu| atha khau uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahiddhikau khau mahāsamaṇau mahānubhāvau, yatra hi nāma kaṭṭhānipi phāliyissanti, na tvaiva ca khau arahā yathā aha’’nti|

47. taina khau pana samayaina tai jaṭilā aggiṃ paricaritukāmā na sak‍kaunti aggiṃ uj‍jalaituṃ jālaituṃ (sī॰), uj‍jalituṃ (ka॰)| atha khau taisaṃ jaṭilānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau mahāsamaṇassa iddhānubhāvau, yathā mayaṃ na sak‍kauma aggiṃ  uj‍jalaitu’’nti| atha khau bhagavā uruvailakassapaṃ jaṭilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘uj‍jaliyantu, kassapa, aggī’’ti| ‘‘uj‍jaliyantu, mahāsamaṇā’’ti| sakidaiva pañ‍ca aggisatāni uj‍jaliyiṃsu| atha khau uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahiddhikau khau mahāsamaṇau mahānubhāvau, yatra hi nāma aggīpi uj‍jaliyissanti, na tvaiva ca khau arahā yathā aha’’nti|

48. taina khau pana samayaina tai jaṭilā aggiṃ paricaritvā na sak‍kaunti aggiṃ vijjhāpaituṃ| atha khau taisaṃ jaṭilānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau mahāsamaṇassa iddhānubhāvau, yathā mayaṃ na sak‍kauma aggiṃ vijjhāpaitu’’nti| atha khau bhagavā uruvailakassapaṃ jaṭilaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘vijjhāyantu, kassapa, aggī’’ti| ‘‘vijjhāyantu, mahāsamaṇā’’ti| sakidaiva pañ‍ca aggisatāni vijjhāyiṃsu| atha khau uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahiddhikau khau mahāsamaṇau mahānubhāvau, yatra hi nāma aggīpi vijjhāyissanti, na tvaiva ca khau arahā yathā aha’’nti|

49. taina khau pana samayaina tai jaṭilā sītāsu haimantikāsu rattīsu antaraṭṭhakāsu himapātasamayai naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya ummuj‍jantipi, nimuj‍jantipi, ummuj‍jananimuj‍janampi karaunti| atha khau bhagavā pañ‍camattāni mandāmukhisatāni abhinimmini, yattha tai jaṭilā uttaritvā visibbaisuṃ| atha khau taisaṃ jaṭilānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau  mahāsamaṇassa iddhānubhāvau, yathayimā mandāmukhiyau nimmitā’’ti| atha khau uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahiddhikau khau mahāsamaṇau mahānubhāvau, yatra hi nāma tāva bahū mandāmukhiyaupi abhinimminissati, na tvaiva ca khau arahā yathā aha’’nti|

50. taina  khau pana samayaina mahā akālamaighau pāvassi, mahā udakavāhakau sañ‍jāyi| yasmiṃ padaisai bhagavā viharati, sau padaisau udakaina na autthaṭau udakaina autthaṭau (sī॰ syā॰) hauti| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ samantā udakaṃ ussāraitvā majjhai raiṇuhatāya bhūmiyā caṅkamaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā samantā udakaṃ ussāraitvā majjhai raiṇuhatāya bhūmiyā caṅkami| atha khau uruvailakassapau jaṭilau – māhaiva khau mahāsamaṇau udakaina vūḷhau ahausīti nāvāya sambahulaihi jaṭilaihi saddhiṃ yasmiṃ padaisai bhagavā viharati taṃ padaisaṃ agamāsi| addasā khau uruvailakassapau jaṭilau bhagavantaṃ samantā udakaṃ ussāraitvā majjhai raiṇuhatāya bhūmiyā caṅkamantaṃ, disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ nu tvaṃ, mahāsamaṇā’’ti? ‘‘ayamahamasmi āma ahamasmi (syā॰), kassapā’’ti bhagavā vaihāsaṃ abbhuggantvā nāvāya pac‍cuṭṭhāsi| atha khau uruvailakassapassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahiddhikau khau mahāsamaṇau mahānubhāvau, yatra hi nāma udakampi na pavāhissati nappasahissati (sī॰), na  tvaiva ca khau arahā yathā aha’’nti|

51. atha  khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘cirampi khau imassa maughapurisassa aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘mahiddhikau khau mahāsamaṇau mahānubhāvau, na tvaiva ca khau arahā yathā aha’nti; yaṃnūnāhaṃ imaṃ jaṭilaṃ saṃvaijaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā uruvailakassapaṃ jaṭilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘naiva ca khau tvaṃ, kassapa, arahā, nāpi arahattamaggasamāpan‍nau| sāpi tai paṭipadā natthi, yāya tvaṃ arahā vā assasi, arahattamaggaṃ vā samāpan‍nau’’ti| atha khau uruvailakassapau jaṭilau bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti| tvaṃ khausi, kassapa, pañ‍can‍naṃ jaṭilasatānaṃ nāyakau vināyakau aggau pamukhau pāmaukkhau| taipi tāva apalaukaihi, yathā tai mañ‍ñissanti tathā tai karissantīti| atha khau uruvailakassapau jaṭilau yaina tai jaṭilā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā tai jaṭilai aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhau, mahāsamaṇai brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ, yathā bhavantau mañ‍ñanti tathā karauntū’’ti| ‘‘cirapaṭikā mayaṃ, bhau, mahāsamaṇai abhippasan‍nā, sacai bhavaṃ, mahāsamaṇai brahmacariyaṃ carissati, sabbaiva mayaṃ mahāsamaṇai brahmacariyaṃ carissāmā’’ti| atha khau tai jaṭilā kaisamissaṃ jaṭāmissaṃ khārikājamissaṃ aggihutamissaṃ udakai pavāhaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘labhaiyyāma  mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyāma upasampada’’nti| ‘‘aitha bhikkhavau’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘svākkhātau dhammau, caratha brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| sāva taisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ upasampadā ahausi|

52. addasā  khau nadīkassapau jaṭilau kaisamissaṃ jaṭāmissaṃ khārikājamissaṃ aggihutamissaṃ udakai vuyhamānai, disvānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘māhaiva mai bhātunau upasaggau ahausī’’ti| jaṭilai pāhaisi – gacchatha mai bhātaraṃ jānāthāti| sāmañ‍ca tīhi jaṭilasataihi saddhiṃ yaināyasmā uruvailakassapau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ uruvailakassapaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ nu khau, kassapa, saiyyau’’ti? ‘‘āmāvusau, idaṃ saiyyau’’ti| atha khau tai jaṭilā kaisamissaṃ jaṭāmissaṃ khārikājamissaṃ aggihutamissaṃ udakai pavāhaitvā yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘labhaiyyāma mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyāma upasampada’’nti| ‘‘aitha bhikkhavau’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘svākkhātau dhammau, caratha brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| sāva taisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ upasampadā ahausi|

53. addasā khau gayākassapau jaṭilau kaisamissaṃ jaṭāmissaṃ khārikājamissaṃ aggihutamissaṃ udakai vuyhamānai, disvānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘māhaiva mai bhātūnaṃ upasaggau ahausī’’ti| jaṭilai pāhaisi  – gacchatha mai bhātarau jānāthāti| sāmañ‍ca dvīhi jaṭilasataihi saddhiṃ yaināyasmā uruvailakassapau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ uruvailakassapaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ nu khau, kassapa, saiyyau’’ti? ‘‘āmāvusau, idaṃ saiyyau’’ti| atha khau tai jaṭilā kaisamissaṃ jaṭāmissaṃ khārikājamissaṃ aggihutamissaṃ udakai pavāhaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau pādaisu  sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘labhaiyyāma mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyāma upasampada’’nti| ‘‘aitha bhikkhavau’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘svākkhātau dhammau, caratha brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| sāva taisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ upasampadā ahausi|

bhagavatau adhiṭṭhānaina pañ‍ca kaṭṭhasatāni na phāliyiṃsu, phāliyiṃsu; aggī na uj‍jaliyiṃsu, uj‍jaliyiṃsu; na vijjhāyiṃsu, vijjhāyiṃsu; pañ‍camandāmukhisatāni abhinimmini| aitaina nayaina aḍḍhuḍḍhapāṭihāriyasahassāni haunti|

54. atha khau bhagavā uruvailāyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina gayāsīsaṃ taina pak‍kāmi mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ bhikkhusahassaina sabbaihaiva purāṇajaṭilaihi| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā gayāyaṃ viharati gayāsīsai saddhiṃ bhikkhusahassaina| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.29 ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādittaṃ| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ ādittaṃ? cakkhu  ādittaṃ, rūpā ādittā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ ādittaṃ, cakkhusamphassau ādittau, yamidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi ādittaṃ| kaina ādittaṃ?
