‘‘bhūmattharaṇaṃ autāpaitvā saudhaitvā papphauṭaitvā atiharitvā yathāpañ‍ñattaṃ pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ| mañ‍capaṭipādakā autāpaitvā pamaj‍jitvā atiharitvā yathāṭhānai ṭhapaitabbā| mañ‍cau autāpaitvā saudhaitvā papphauṭaitvā nīcaṃ katvā sādhukaṃ appaṭighaṃsantaina, asaṅghaṭṭaintaina kavāṭapiṭṭhaṃ, atiharitvā yathāpañ‍ñattaṃ pañ‍ñapaitabbau| pīṭhaṃ autāpaitvā saudhaitvā papphauṭaitvā nīcaṃ katvā sādhukaṃ appaṭighaṃsantaina, asaṅghaṭṭaintaina kavāṭapiṭṭhaṃ, atiharitvā yathāpañ‍ñattaṃ pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ| bhisibibbauhanaṃ autāpaitvā saudhaitvā papphauṭaitvā atiharitvā yathāpañ‍ñattaṃ pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ| nisīdanapac‍cattharaṇaṃ autāpaitvā saudhaitvā papphauṭaitvā atiharitvā yathāpañ‍ñattaṃ pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ| khaiḷamal‍lakau autāpaitvā pamaj‍jitvā atiharitvā yathāṭhānai ṭhapaitabbau| apassainaphalakaṃ autāpaitvā pamaj‍jitvā atiharitvā yathāṭhānai ṭhapaitabbaṃ| pattacīvaraṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ| pattaṃ nikkhipantaina aikaina hatthaina pattaṃ gahaitvā aikaina hatthaina haiṭṭhāmañ‍caṃ vā haiṭṭhāpīṭhaṃ vā parāmasitvā pattau nikkhipitabbau| na ca anantarahitāya bhūmiyā pattau nikkhipitabbau| cīvaraṃ nikkhipantaina aikaina hatthaina cīvaraṃ gahaitvā aikaina hatthaina cīvaravaṃsaṃ vā cīvararaj‍juṃ vā pamaj‍jitvā pāratau antaṃ auratau bhaugaṃ katvā cīvaraṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ|

‘‘sacai  puratthimā sarajā vātā vāyanti, puratthimā vātapānā  thakaitabbā| sacai pacchimā sarajā vātā vāyanti, pacchimā vātapānā thakaitabbā| sacai uttarā sarajā vātā vāyanti, uttarā vātapānā thakaitabbā| sacai dakkhiṇā sarajā vātā vāyanti, dakkhiṇā vātapānā thakaitabbā| sacai sītakālau hauti, divā vātapānā vivaritabbā, rattiṃ thakaitabbā| sacai uṇhakālau hauti, divā vātapānā thakaitabbā, rattiṃ vivaritabbā|

‘‘sacai  parivaiṇaṃ uk‍lāpaṃ hauti, parivaiṇaṃ sammaj‍jitabbaṃ| sacai kauṭṭhakau uk‍lāpau hauti, kauṭṭhakau sammaj‍jitabbau| sacai upaṭṭhānasālā uk‍lāpā hauti, upaṭṭhānasālā sammaj‍jitabbā| sacai aggisālā uk‍lāpā hauti, aggisālā sammaj‍jitabbā| sacai vac‍cakuṭi uk‍lāpā hauti, vac‍cakuṭi sammaj‍jitabbā| sacai pānīyaṃ na hauti, pānīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitabbaṃ| sacai paribhaujanīyaṃ na hauti, paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitabbaṃ| sacai ācamanakumbhiyā udakaṃ na hauti, ācamanakumbhiyā udakaṃ āsiñ‍citabbaṃ|

‘‘sacai antaivāsikassa anabhirati uppan‍nā hauti, ācariyaina vūpakāsaitabbau, vūpakāsāpaitabbau, dhammakathā vāssa kātabbā| sacai antaivāsikassa kuk‍kuc‍caṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti, ācariyaina vinaudaitabbaṃ, vinaudāpaitabbaṃ, dhammakathā vāssa kātabbā| sacai antaivāsikassa diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti, ācariyaina vivaicaitabbaṃ, vivaicāpaitabbaṃ, dhammakathā vāssa kātabbā| sacai antaivāsikau garudhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau hauti parivāsārahau, ācariyaina ussuk‍kaṃ  kātabbaṃ – kinti nu khau saṅghau, antaivāsikassa parivāsaṃ dadaiyyāti| sacai antaivāsikau mūlāya paṭikassanārahau hauti, ācariyaina ussuk‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ – kinti nu khau saṅghau antaivāsikaṃ mūlāya paṭikassaiyyāti| sacai antaivāsikau mānattārahau hauti, ācariyaina ussuk‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ – kinti nu khau saṅghau antaivāsikassa mānattaṃ dadaiyyāti| sacai antaivāsikau abbhānārahau hauti, ācariyaina ussuk‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ – kinti nu khau saṅghau antaivāsikaṃ abbhaiyyāti| sacai saṅghau antaivāsikassa kammaṃ kattukāmau hauti, taj‍janīyaṃ vā niyassaṃ vā pabbājanīyaṃ vā paṭisāraṇīyaṃ vā ukkhaipanīyaṃ  vā, ācariyaina ussuk‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ – kinti nu khau saṅghau antaivāsikassa kammaṃ na karaiyya, lahukāya vā pariṇāmaiyyāti| kataṃ vā panassa hauti saṅghaina kammaṃ, taj‍janīyaṃ vā niyassaṃ vā pabbājanīyaṃ vā paṭisāraṇīyaṃ vā ukkhaipanīyaṃ vā, ācariyaina ussuk‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ – kinti nu khau antaivāsikau sammā vattaiyya, laumaṃ pātaiyya, naitthāraṃ vattaiyya, saṅghau taṃ kammaṃ paṭippassambhaiyyāti|

‘‘sacai antaivāsikassa cīvaraṃ dhauvitabbaṃ hauti, ācariyaina ācikkhitabbaṃ – ‘aivaṃ dhauvaiyyāsī’ti, ussuk‍kaṃ vā kātabbaṃ – kinti nu khau antaivāsikassa cīvaraṃ dhauviyaithāti| sacai antaivāsikassa cīvaraṃ kātabbaṃ hauti, ācariyaina ācikkhitabbaṃ – ‘aivaṃ karaiyyāsī’ti, ussuk‍kaṃ vā kātabbaṃ – kinti nu khau antaivāsikassa cīvaraṃ kariyaithāti| sacai  antaivāsikassa rajanaṃ pacitabbaṃ hauti, ācariyaina ācikkhitabbaṃ – ‘aivaṃ pacaiyyāsī’ti, ussuk‍kaṃ vā kātabbaṃ – kinti nu khau antaivāsikassa rajanaṃ paciyaithāti| sacai  antaivāsikassa cīvaraṃ rajitabbaṃ hauti, ācariyaina ācikkhitabbaṃ – ‘aivaṃ rajaiyyāsī’ti, ussuk‍kaṃ vā kātabbaṃ – kinti nu khau antaivāsikassa cīvaraṃ rajiyaithāti| cīvaraṃ rajantaina sādhukaṃ samparivattakaṃ samparivattakaṃ rajitabbaṃ, na ca acchin‍nai thaivai pak‍kamitabbaṃ| sacai antaivāsikau gilānau hauti, yāvajīvaṃ upaṭṭhātabbau, vuṭṭhānamassa āgamaitabba’’nti|

antaivāsikavattaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

chaṭṭhabhāṇavārau|

20. paṇāmanā khamāpanā

80. taina khau pana samayaina antaivāsikā ācariyaisu na sammā vattanti…pai॰… bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, antaivāsikaina ācariyamhi na sammā vattitabbaṃ| yau na sammā vattaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti| naiva sammā vattanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, asammāvattantaṃ paṇāmaituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, paṇāmaitabbau – paṇāmaimi tanti vā, māyidha paṭik‍kamīti  vā, nīhara tai pattacīvaranti vā, nāhaṃ tayā upaṭṭhātabbauti vā| kāyaina viñ‍ñāpaiti, vācāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, kāyaina vācāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, paṇāmitau hauti antaivāsikau; na kāyaina viñ‍ñāpaiti, na vācāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, na kāyaina vācāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, na paṇāmitau hauti antaivāsikauti|

taina khau pana samayaina  antaivāsikā paṇāmitā na khamāpainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, khamāpaitunti| naiva khamāpainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, paṇāmitaina na khamāpaitabbau| yau na khamāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina ācariyā khamāpiyamānā na khamanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, khamitunti| naiva khamanti| antaivāsikā pak‍kamantipi vibbhamantipi titthiyaisupi saṅkamanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, khamāpiyamānaina na khamitabbaṃ| yau na khamaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina  khau pana samayaina ācariyā sammāvattantaṃ paṇāmainti, asammāvattantaṃ na paṇāmainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, sammāvattantau paṇāmaitabbau| yau paṇāmaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, asammāvattantau na paṇāmaitabbau| yau na paṇāmaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

81. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau antaivāsikau paṇāmaitabbau| ācariyamhi nādhimattaṃ paimaṃ hauti, nādhimattau pasādau hauti, nādhimattā hirī hauti, nādhimattau gāravau hauti, nādhimattā bhāvanā hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau antaivāsikau paṇāmaitabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau antaivāsikau na paṇāmaitabbau| ācariyamhi adhimattaṃ paimaṃ hauti, adhimattau pasādau hauti, adhimattā  hirī hauti, adhimattau gāravau hauti, adhimattā bhāvanā hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau antaivāsikau na paṇāmaitabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau antaivāsikau alaṃ paṇāmaituṃ| ācariyamhi nādhimattaṃ paimaṃ hauti, nādhimattau pasādau hauti, nādhimattā hirī hauti, nādhimattau gāravau hauti, nādhimattā bhāvanā hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau antaivāsikau alaṃ paṇāmaituṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau antaivāsikau nālaṃ paṇāmaituṃ| ācariyamhi adhimattaṃ paimaṃ hauti, adhimattau pasādau hauti, adhimattā hirī hauti, adhimattau gāravau hauti, adhimattā bhāvanā hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau antaivāsikau nālaṃ paṇāmaituṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ antaivāsikaṃ appaṇāmaintau ācariyau sātisārau hauti, paṇāmaintau anatisārau hauti| ācariyamhi nādhimattaṃ paimaṃ hauti, nādhimattau pasādau hauti, nādhimattā hirī hauti, nādhimattau gāravau hauti, nādhimattā bhāvanā hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ antaivāsikaṃ appaṇāmaintau ācariyau sātisārau hauti, paṇāmaintau anatisārau hauti|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ antaivāsikaṃ paṇāmaintau ācariyau sātisārau hauti, appaṇāmaintau anatisārau hauti| ācariyamhi adhimattaṃ paimaṃ hauti, adhimattau pasādau hauti, adhimattā  hirī hauti, adhimattau gāravau hauti, adhimattā bhāvanā hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ antaivāsikaṃ paṇāmaintau ācariyau sātisārau hauti, appaṇāmaintau anatisārau hautī’’ti|

paṇāmanā khamāpanā niṭṭhitā|

21. bālaabyattavatthu

82. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū, dasavassamhā dasavassamhāti, bālā abyattā nissayaṃ dainti| dissanti ācariyā bālā, antaivāsikā paṇḍitā| dissanti ācariyā abyattā, antaivāsikā byattā| dissanti ācariyā appassutā, antaivāsikā bahussutā| dissanti ācariyā duppañ‍ñā, antaivāsikā pañ‍ñavantau| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰…  tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū, dasavassamhā dasavassamhāti, bālā abyattā nissayaṃ dassanti| dissanti ācariyā bālā antaivāsikā paṇḍitā, dissanti ācariyā abyattā antaivāsikā byattā, dissanti ācariyā appassutā antaivāsikā bahussutā, dissanti ācariyā duppañ‍ñā antaivāsikā pañ‍ñavantau’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū, dasavassamhā dasavassamhāti, bālā abyattā nissayaṃ dainti…pai॰… sac‍caṃ, bhagavāti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bālaina abyattaina nissayau dātabbau| yau dadaiyya, āpatti  duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina dasavassaina vā atiraikadasavassaina vā nissayaṃ dātu’’nti|

bālaabyattavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

22. nissayapaṭippassaddhikathā

83. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū ācariyupajjhāyaisu pak‍kantaisupi vibbhantaisupi kālaṅkataisupi  pakkhasaṅkantaisupi nissayapaṭippassaddhiyau na jānanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cimā, bhikkhavai, nissayapaṭippassaddhiyau upajjhāyamhā – upajjhāyau pak‍kantau vā hauti, vibbhantau vā, kālaṅkatau vā, pakkhasaṅkantau vā, āṇattiyaiva pañ‍camī| imā khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca nissayapaṭippassaddhiyau upajjhāyamhā|

‘‘chayimā, bhikkhavai, nissayapaṭippassaddhiyau ācariyamhā – ācariyau pak‍kantau vā hauti, vibbhantau vā, kālaṅkatau vā, pakkhasaṅkantau vā, āṇattiyaiva pañ‍camī, upajjhāyaina vā samaudhānagatau hauti| imā khau, bhikkhavai, cha nissayapaṭippassaddhiyau ācariyamhā’’|

nissapaṭippassaddhikathā niṭṭhitā|

23. upasampādaitabbapañ‍cakaṃ

84. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| na asaikkhaina na asaikhaina (ka॰) sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi  saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā  upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau  dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| attanā na asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na paraṃ asaikkhai sīlakkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā na asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na paraṃ asaikkhai samādhikkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā na asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na paraṃ asaikkhai pañ‍ñākkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā na asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na paraṃ asaikkhai vimuttikkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā na asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na paraṃ asaikkhai vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhai samādapaitā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na  nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| attanā asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai sīlakkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai samādhikkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai pañ‍ñākkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai vimuttikkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhai samādapaitā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| assaddhau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti, kusītau hauti, muṭṭhassati hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| saddhau hauti, hirimā hauti, auttappī hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, upaṭṭhitassati hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti, appassutau hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| na adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, na ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, na atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti, bahussutau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| na paṭibalau hauti antaivāsiṃ vā saddhivihāriṃ vā  gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ vā upaṭṭhāpaituṃ vā, anabhirataṃ anabhiratiṃ (syā॰), uppan‍naṃ anabhiratiṃ (ka॰) vūpakāsaituṃ vā vūpakāsāpaituṃ vā, uppan‍naṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ dhammatau vinaudaituṃ vinaudaituṃ vā vinaudāpaituṃ vā (sabbattha, vimativinaudanī ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā) āpattiṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā vuṭṭhānaṃ na jānāti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| paṭibalau hauti antaivāsiṃ vā saddhivihāriṃ vā gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ vā upaṭṭhāpaituṃ vā, anabhirataṃ vūpakāsaituṃ vā vūpakāsāpaituṃ vā, uppan‍naṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ dhammatau vinaudaituṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, āpattiyā vuṭṭhānaṃ jānāti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| na paṭibalau hauti antaivāsiṃ vā saddhivihāriṃ vā abhisamācārikāya sikkhāya sikkhāpaituṃ, ādibrahmacariyakāya sikkhāya vinaituṃ, abhidhammai vinaituṃ, abhivinayai vinaituṃ, uppan‍naṃ diṭṭhigataṃ dhammatau vivaicaituṃ – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na  nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| paṭibalau hauti antaivāsiṃ vā saddhivihāriṃ vā abhisamācārikāya sikkhāya sikkhāpaituṃ, ādibrahmacariyakāya sikkhāya vinaituṃ, abhidhammai vinaituṃ, abhivinayai vinaituṃ, uppan‍naṃ diṭṭhigataṃ dhammatau  vivaicaituṃ – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| āpattiṃ na jānāti, anāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina na svāgatāni haunti na suvibhattāni na suppavattīni na suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau  – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| āpattiṃ na jānāti, anāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, ūnadasavassau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ  jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, dasavassau vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau’’ti|

upasampādaitabbapañ‍cakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

24. upasampādaitabbachak‍kaṃ

85. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| na asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina  saman‍nāgatau hauti, ūnadasavassau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, dasavassau vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| attanā na asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na paraṃ asaikkhai sīlakkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā na asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau  hauti, na paraṃ asaikkhai samādhikkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā  na asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na paraṃ asaikkhai pañ‍ñākkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā na asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na paraṃ asaikkhai vimuttikkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā na asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na paraṃ asaikkhai vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhai  samādapaitā; ūnadasavassau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| attanā asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai sīlakkhandhai samādapaitā attanā asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai samādhikkhandhai samādapaitā| attanā asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai pañ‍ñākkhandhai samādapaitā| attanā asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai vimuttikkhandhai samādapaitā| attanā asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhai samādapaitā; dasavassau vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| assaddhau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti, kusītau hauti, muṭṭhassati hauti, ūnadasavassau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau  upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| saddhau hauti, hirimā hauti, auttappī hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, upaṭṭhitassati hauti, dasavassau vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā – imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti, appassutau hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti, ūnadasavassau  hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| na adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, na ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, na atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti, bahussutau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti, dasavassau vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina  bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| na paṭibalau hauti antaivāsiṃ vā saddhivihāriṃ vā gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ vā upaṭṭhāpaituṃ vā, anabhirataṃ vūpakāsaituṃ vā vūpakāsāpaituṃ vā, uppan‍naṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ dhammatau vinaudaituṃ, āpattiṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā vuṭṭhānaṃ na jānāti, ūnadasavassau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| paṭibalau hauti antaivāsiṃ vā saddhivihāriṃ vā gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ vā upaṭṭhāpaituṃ vā, anabhirataṃ vūpakāsaituṃ vā vūpakāsāpaituṃ vā, uppan‍naṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ dhammatau vinaudaituṃ, āpattiṃ jānāti, āpattiyā vuṭṭhānaṃ jānāti, dasavassau  vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| na paṭibalau hauti antaivāsiṃ vā saddhivihāriṃ vā abhisamācārikāya sikkhāya sikkhāpaituṃ, ādibrahmacariyakāya sikkhāya vinaituṃ, abhidhammai vinaituṃ, abhivinayai vinaituṃ, uppan‍naṃ diṭṭhigataṃ dhammatau vivaicaituṃ, ūnadasavassau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| paṭibalau hauti antaivāsiṃ vā saddhivihāriṃ vā abhisamācārikāya sikkhāya sikkhāpaituṃ ādibrahmacariyakāya sikkhāya vinaituṃ, abhidhammai vinaituṃ, abhivinayai vinaituṃ, uppan‍naṃ diṭṭhigataṃ dhammatau vivaicaituṃ, dasavassau vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| āpattiṃ na jānāti, anāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina na svāgatāni haunti na suvibhattāni na suppavattīni na suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau, ūnadasavassau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau, dasavassau vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau’’ti|

upasampādaitabbachak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

25. añ‍ñatitthiyapubbakathā

86. taina  khau pana samayaina yau sau añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau yau sau pasuraparibbājakau añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau (ka॰) pajjhāyaina sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānau upajjhāyassa vādaṃ āraupaitvā taṃyaiva titthāyatanaṃ saṅkami| sau puna pac‍cāgantvā bhikkhū upasampadaṃ yāci| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| yau sau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau upajjhāyaina sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānau upajjhāyassa vādaṃ āraupaitvā taṃyaiva titthāyatanaṃ  saṅkantau, sau āgatau na upasampādaitabbau| yau sau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaupi añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhati pabbaj‍jaṃ, ākaṅkhati upasampadaṃ, tassa cattārau māsai parivāsau dātabbau| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbau – paṭhamaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhārāpaitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādāpaitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ kārāpaitvā bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vandāpaitvā  uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīdāpaitvā añ‍jaliṃ paggaṇhāpaitvā aivaṃ vadaihīti vattabbau – ‘‘buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi; dutiyampi buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dutiyampi dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dutiyampi saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi; tatiyampi buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, tatiyampi dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, tatiyampi saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmī’’ti|

taina, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbaina saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau imasmiṃ  dhammavinayai ākaṅkhāmi upasampadaṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ cattārau māsai parivāsaṃ yācāmī’’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhati upasampadaṃ| sau saṅghaṃ cattārau māsai parivāsaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa añ‍ñatitthiyapubbassa cattārau māsai parivāsaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhati upasampadaṃ| sau saṅghaṃ cattārau māsai parivāsaṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa añ‍ñatitthiyapubbassa cattārau māsai parivāsaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa añ‍ñatitthiyapubbassa  cattārau māsai parivāsassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa añ‍ñatitthiyapubbassa cattārau māsai parivāsau| khamati  saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

87. ‘‘aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau ārādhakau hauti, aivaṃ anārādhakau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau anārādhakau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau atikālaina  gāmaṃ pavisati, atidivā paṭik‍kamati| aivampi, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau anārādhakau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau vaisiyāgaucarau vā hauti, vidhavāgaucarau vā hauti, thul‍lakumārikāgaucarau vā hauti, paṇḍakagaucarau vā hauti, bhikkhunigaucarau vā hauti| aivampi, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau anārādhakau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ uc‍cāvacāni karaṇīyāni, tattha na dakkhau hauti, na analasau, na tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatau, na alaṃ kātuṃ, na alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ| aivampi, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau anārādhakau hauti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau na tibbacchandau hauti uddaisai, paripucchāya, adhisīlai, adhicittai, adhipañ‍ñāya| aivampi, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau anārādhakau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau yassa titthāyatanā saṅkantau hauti, tassa satthunau tassa diṭṭhiyā tassa khantiyā tassa ruciyā tassa ādāyassa  avaṇṇai bhañ‍ñamānai kupitau hauti anattamanau anabhiraddhau, buddhassa vā dhammassa vā saṅghassa vā avaṇṇai bhañ‍ñamānai attamanau hauti udaggau abhiraddhau| yassa vā pana titthāyatanā saṅkantau hauti, tassa satthunau tassa diṭṭhiyā tassa khantiyā tassa ruciyā tassa ādāyassa vaṇṇai bhañ‍ñamānai attamanau hauti udaggau abhiraddhau, buddhassa vā dhammassa vā saṅghassa vā vaṇṇai bhañ‍ñamānai kupitau hauti anattamanau anabhiraddhau| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṅghātanikaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyapubbassa anārādhanīyasmiṃ| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau anārādhakau hauti| aivaṃ anārādhakau khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau āgatau na upasampādaitabbau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau ārādhakau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau nātikālaina gāmaṃ pavisati nātidivā paṭik‍kamati| aivampi, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau ārādhakau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau na vaisiyāgaucarau hauti, na vidhavāgaucarau hauti, na thul‍lakumārikāgaucarau hauti, na paṇḍakagaucarau hauti, na bhikkhunigaucarau hauti| aivampi, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau  ārādhakau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ uc‍cāvacāni karaṇīyāni, tattha dakkhau hauti, analasau, tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatau, alaṃ kātuṃ, alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ| aivampi, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau ārādhakau hauti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau tibbacchandau hauti uddaisai, paripucchāya, adhisīlai, adhicittai, adhipañ‍ñāya| aivampi, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau ārādhakau hauti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau yassa titthāyatanā saṅkantau hauti, tassa satthunau tassa diṭṭhiyā tassa khantiyā tassa ruciyā tassa ādāyassa avaṇṇai bhañ‍ñamānai attamanau hauti udaggau abhiraddhau, buddhassa vā dhammassa vā saṅghassa vā avaṇṇai bhañ‍ñamānai kupitau hauti anattamanau anabhiraddhau| yassa vā pana titthāyatanā saṅkantau hauti, tassa satthunau tassa diṭṭhiyā tassa khantiyā tassa ruciyā tassa ādāyassa vaṇṇai bhañ‍ñamānai kupitau hauti anattamanau anabhiraddhau, buddhassa vā dhammassa vā saṅghassa vā vaṇṇai bhañ‍ñamānai attamanau hauti udaggau abhiraddhau| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṅghātanikaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyapubbassa ārādhanīyasmiṃ| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau ārādhakau hauti| aivaṃ ārādhakau khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau āgatau upasampādaitabbau|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau naggau āgacchati, upajjhāyamūlakaṃ cīvaraṃ pariyaisitabbaṃ| sacai acchin‍nakaisau āgacchati, saṅghau apalaukaitabbau bhaṇḍukammāya| yai tai, bhikkhavai, aggikā jaṭilakā, tai āgatā upasampādaitabbā, na taisaṃ parivāsau dātabbau| taṃ kissa haitu? kammavādinau aitai, bhikkhavai, kiriyavādinau| sacai, bhikkhavai, jātiyā sākiyau añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau āgacchati, sau āgatau upasampādaitabbau, na tassa parivāsau dātabbau| imāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ñātīnaṃ āvaiṇikaṃ parihāraṃ dammī’’ti|

añ‍ñatitthiyapubbakathā niṭṭhitā|

sattamabhāṇavārau|

26. pañ‍cābādhavatthu

88. taina  khau pana samayaina magadhaisu pañ‍ca ābādhā ussan‍nā haunti – kuṭṭhaṃ, gaṇḍau, kilāsau, sausau, apamārau| manussā pañ‍cahi ābādhaihi phuṭṭhā jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘sādhu nau, ācariya, tikicchāhī’’ti| ‘‘ahaṃ khvayyau, bahukic‍cau bahukaraṇīyau; rājā ca mai māgadhau sainiyau  bimbisārau upaṭṭhātabbau  itthāgārañ‍ca buddhappamukhau ca bhikkhusaṅghau; nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi tikicchitu’’nti| ‘‘sabbaṃ sāpataiyyañ‍ca tai, ācariya, hautu; mayañ‍ca tai dāsā; sādhu, nau, ācariya, tikicchāhī’’ti| ‘‘ahaṃ khvayyau, bahukic‍cau bahukaraṇīyau rājā ca mai māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau upaṭṭhātabbau itthāgārañ‍ca buddhappamukhau ca bhikkhusaṅghau; nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi tikicchitu’’nti| atha khau taisaṃ manussānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘imai khau samaṇā sakyaputtiyā sukhasīlā sukhasamācārā, subhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā nivātaisu sayanaisu sayanti| yaṃnūna mayaṃ samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajaiyyāma| tattha bhikkhū caiva upaṭṭhahissanti, jīvakau ca kaumārabhac‍cau tikicchissatī’’ti| atha khau tai manussā bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ yāciṃsu| tai bhikkhū pabbājaisuṃ, upasampādaisuṃ| tai bhikkhū caiva upaṭṭhahiṃsu jīvakau ca kaumārabhac‍cau tikicchi| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū bahū gilānai bhikkhū upaṭṭhahantā yācanabahulā viñ‍ñattibahulā viharanti – gilānabhattaṃ daitha, gilānupaṭṭhākabhattaṃ daitha, gilānabhaisaj‍jaṃ daithāti| jīvakaupi kaumārabhac‍cau bahū gilānai bhikkhū tikicchantau añ‍ñataraṃ rājakic‍caṃ parihāpaisi|

89. añ‍ñataraupi purisau pañ‍cahi ābādhaihi phuṭṭhau jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu maṃ, ācariya, tikicchāhī’’ti| ‘‘ahaṃ khvayyau, bahukic‍cau, bahukaraṇīyau, rājā ca mai māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau upaṭṭhātabbau itthāgārañ‍ca buddhappamukhau ca bhikkhusaṅghau; nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi tikicchitu’’nti| ‘‘sabbaṃ sāpataiyyañ‍ca tai, ācariya, hautu, ahañ‍ca tai dāsau; sādhu maṃ, ācariya, tikicchāhī’’ti| ‘‘ahaṃ khvayyau, bahukic‍cau bahukaraṇīyau, rājā ca mai māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau upaṭṭhātabbau itthāgārañ‍ca buddhappamukhau ca bhikkhusaṅghau, nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi tikicchitu’’nti| atha khau tassa purisassa aitadahausi – ‘‘imai khau samaṇā sakyaputtiyā sukhasīlā sukhasamācārā, subhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā nivātaisu sayanaisu sayanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajaiyyaṃ| tattha bhikkhū caiva upaṭṭhahissanti, jīvakau ca kaumārabhac‍cau tikicchissati| saumhi sauhaṃ (bahūsu, vimativinaudanīṭīkā aulaukaitabbā) araugau vibbhamissāmī’’ti| atha khau sau  purisau bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā  pabbaj‍jaṃ yāci| taṃ bhikkhū pabbājaisuṃ, upasampādaisuṃ| taṃ bhikkhū caiva upaṭṭhahiṃsu, jīvakau ca kaumārabhac‍cau tikicchi| sau araugau vibbhami| addasā khau jīvakau  kaumārabhac‍cau taṃ purisaṃ vibbhantaṃ, disvāna taṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nanu tvaṃ, ayyau, bhikkhūsu pabbajitau ahausī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, ācariyā’’ti| ‘‘kissa pana tvaṃ, ayyau, aivarūpamakāsī’’ti? atha khau sau purisau jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhadantā bhaddantā (ka॰) pañ‍cahi ābādhaihi phuṭṭhaṃ pabbājaissantī’’ti| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu, bhantai, ayyā pañ‍cahi ābādhaihi phuṭṭhaṃ na pabbājaiyyu’’nti| atha khau bhagavā jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi ābādhaihi phuṭṭhau pabbājaitabbau| yau pabbājaiyya, āpatti  duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

pañ‍cābādhavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

27. rājabhaṭavatthu

90. taina khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa pac‍cantau kupitau hauti| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau sainānāyakai mahāmattai āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gacchatha, bhaṇai, pac‍cantaṃ uc‍cinathā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau sainānāyakā mahāmattā rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa pac‍cassausuṃ| atha khau abhiñ‍ñātānaṃ abhiñ‍ñātānaṃ yaudhānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘mayaṃ khau yuddhābhinandinau gacchantā pāpañ‍ca karauma, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavāma| kaina nu khau mayaṃ upāyaina pāpā ca viramaiyyāma kalyāṇañ‍ca karaiyyāmā’’ti? atha khau taisaṃ yaudhānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘imai khau  samaṇā sakyaputtiyā dhammacārinau samacārinau brahmacārinau sac‍cavādinau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā| sacai khau mayaṃ samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajaiyyāma, aivaṃ mayaṃ pāpā ca viramaiyyāma kalyāṇañ‍ca karaiyyāmā’’ti| atha khau tai yaudhā bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ yāciṃsu| tai bhikkhū pabbājaisuṃ, upasampādaisuṃ| sainānāyakā mahāmattā rājabhaṭai  pucchiṃsu – ‘‘kiṃ nu  khau, bhaṇai, itthan‍nāmau ca itthan‍nāmau ca yaudhā na dissantī’’ti? ‘‘itthan‍nāmau ca itthan‍nāmau ca, sāmi, yaudhā bhikkhūsu pabbajitā’’ti| sainānāyakā mahāmattā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā rājabhaṭaṃ pabbājaissantī’’ti| sainānāyakā mahāmattā rañ‍ñau  māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau vauhārikai mahāmattai pucchi – ‘‘yau, bhaṇai, rājabhaṭaṃ pabbājaiti, kiṃ sau pasavatī’’ti? ‘‘upajjhāyassa, daiva, sīsaṃ chaitabbaṃ, anussāvakassa anusāvakassa (ka॰) jivhā uddharitabbā, gaṇassa upaḍḍhaphāsukā bhañ‍jitabbā’’ti| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘santi, bhantai, rājānau assaddhā appasan‍nā| tai appamattakainapi bhikkhū vihaiṭhaiyyuṃ| sādhu, bhantai, ayyā rājabhaṭaṃ na pabbājaiyyu’’nti| atha khau bhagavā rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, rājabhaṭau pabbājaitabbau| yau pabbājaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

rājabhaṭavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

28. aṅgulimālacauravatthu

91. taina khau pana samayaina caurau aṅgulimālau bhikkhūsu pabbajitau hauti| manussā passitvā ubbij‍jantipi, uttasantipi, palāyantipi, añ‍ñainapi gacchanti, añ‍ñainapi mukhaṃ karaunti, dvārampi thakainti| manussā ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā dhajabandhaṃ cauraṃ pabbājaissantī’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, dhajabandhau caurau pabbājaitabbau| yau pabbājaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

aṅgulimālacauravatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

29. kārabhaidakacauravatthu

92. taina  khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñā māgadhaina sainiyaina bimbisāraina  anuñ‍ñātaṃ hauti – ‘‘yai samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajanti, na tai labbhā kiñ‍ci kātuṃ; svākkhātau dhammau, carantu brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau purisau caurikaṃ katvā kārāya baddhau hauti| sau kāraṃ bhinditvā palāyitvā bhikkhūsu pabbajitau hauti| manussā passitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘ayaṃ sau kārabhaidakau caurau| handa, naṃ naimā’’ti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘māyyau, aivaṃ avacuttha| anuñ‍ñātaṃ rañ‍ñā māgadhaina sainiyaina bimbisāraina – ‘‘yai samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajanti, na tai labbhā kiñ‍ci kātuṃ; svākkhātau dhammau, carantu brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘abhayūvarā imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā, nayimai  labbhā kiñ‍ci kātuṃ| kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā kārabhaidakaṃ cauraṃ pabbājaissantī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, kārabhaidakau caurau pabbājaitabbau| yau pabbājaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

kārabhaidakacauravatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

30. likhitakacauravatthu

93. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau purisau caurikaṃ katvā palāyitvā bhikkhūsu pabbajitau hauti| sau ca rañ‍ñau antaipurai likhitau hauti – yattha passati, tattha hantabbauti| manussā passitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘ayaṃ sau likhitakau caurau| handa, naṃ hanāmā’’ti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘māyyau, aivaṃ avacuttha| anuñ‍ñātaṃ rañ‍ñā māgadhaina sainiyaina bimbisāraina ‘‘yai samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajanti, na tai labbhā kiñ‍ci kātuṃ, svākkhātau dhammau, carantu brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘abhayūvarā  imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā, nayimai labbhā kiñ‍ci kātuṃ| kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā likhitakaṃ cauraṃ pabbājaissantī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, likhitakau caurau pabbājaitabbau| yau pabbājaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

likhitakacauravatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

31. kasāhatavatthu

94. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau purisau kasāhatau katadaṇḍakammau bhikkhūsu pabbajitau hauti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā kasāhataṃ katadaṇḍakammaṃ pabbājaissantī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, kasāhatau katadaṇḍakammau pabbājaitabbau| yau pabbājaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

kasāhatavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

32. lakkhaṇāhatavatthu

95. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau purisau lakkhaṇāhatau katadaṇḍakammau bhikkhūsu pabbajitau hauti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā lakkhaṇāhataṃ katadaṇḍakammaṃ pabbājaissantī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, lakkhaṇāhatau katadaṇḍakammau pabbājaitabbau| yau pabbājaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

lakkhaṇāhatavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

33. iṇāyikavatthu

96. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau purisau iṇāyikau palāyitvā bhikkhūsu pabbajitau hauti| dhaniyā passitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘ayaṃ sau amhākaṃ iṇāyikau| handa, naṃ naimā’’ti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘māyyau, aivaṃ avacuttha| anuñ‍ñātaṃ rañ‍ñā māgadhaina sainiyaina bimbisāraina – ‘‘yai samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajanti, na tai labbhā kiñ‍ci kātuṃ; svākkhātau dhammau, carantu brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘abhayūvarā imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā| nayimai labbhā kiñ‍ci kātuṃ| kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā iṇāyikaṃ pabbājaissantī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, iṇāyikau pabbājaitabbau| yau pabbājaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

iṇāyikavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

34. dāsavatthu

97. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau dāsau palāyitvā bhikkhūsu  pabbajitau hauti| ayyakā ayyikā (ka॰), ayirakā (sī॰) passitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘ayaṃ sau amhākaṃ dāsau| handa, naṃ naimā’’ti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘māyyau, aivaṃ avacuttha, anuñ‍ñātaṃ rañ‍ñā māgadhaina sainiyaina bimbisāraina ‘‘yai samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajanti, na tai labbhā kiñ‍ci kātuṃ, svākkhātau dhammau, carantu brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘abhayūvarā imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā, nayimai labbhā kiñ‍ci kātuṃ| kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā dāsaṃ pabbājaissantī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, dāsau pabbājaitabbau| yau pabbājaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

dāsavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

35. kammārabhaṇḍuvatthu

98. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau kammārabhaṇḍu mātāpitūhi saddhiṃ bhaṇḍitvā ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūsu pabbajitau hauti| atha khau tassa kammārabhaṇḍussa mātāpitarau taṃ kammārabhaṇḍuṃ vicinantā ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhū pucchiṃsu – ‘‘api, bhantai, aivarūpaṃ dārakaṃ passaiyyāthā’’ti? bhikkhū ajānaṃyaiva āhaṃsu – ‘‘na jānāmā’’ti, apassaṃyaiva āhaṃsu – ‘‘na passāmā’’ti| atha khau tassa kammārabhaṇḍussa mātāpitarau taṃ kammārabhaṇḍuṃ vicinantā  bhikkhūsu pabbajitaṃ disvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘alaj‍jinau imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā, dussīlā musāvādinau| jānaṃyaiva āhaṃsu – ‘na jānāmā’ti, passaṃyaiva āhaṃsu – ‘na passāmā’ti| ayaṃ dārakau bhikkhūsu  pabbajitau’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa kammārabhaṇḍussa mātāpitūnaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū  bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṅghaṃ apalaukaituṃ bhaṇḍukammāyāti|

kammārabhaṇḍuvatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

36. upālidārakavatthu

99. idaṃ vatthu pāci॰ 402 ādayau taina  khau pana samayaina rājagahai sattarasavaggiyā dārakā sahāyakā haunti| upālidārakau taisaṃ pāmaukkhau hauti| atha khau upālissa mātāpitūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau upāyaina upāli amhākaṃ ac‍cayaina sukhañ‍ca jīvaiyya, na ca kilamaiyyā’’ti? atha khau upālissa mātāpitūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘sacai khau upāli laikhaṃ sikkhaiyya, aivaṃ khau upāli amhākaṃ ac‍cayaina sukhañ‍ca jīvaiyya, na ca kilamaiyyā’’ti| atha khau upālissa mātāpitūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘sacai khau upāli laikhaṃ sikkhissati, aṅguliyau dukkhā bhavissanti| sacai khau upāli gaṇanaṃ sikkhaiyya, aivaṃ khau upāli amhākaṃ ac‍cayaina sukhañ‍ca jīvaiyya, na ca kilamaiyyā’’ti| atha khau upālissa mātāpitūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘sacai khau upāli gaṇanaṃ sikkhissati, urassa dukkhau bhavissati| sacai khau upāli rūpaṃ sikkhaiyya, aivaṃ khau upāli amhākaṃ ac‍cayaina sukhañ‍ca jīvaiyya, na ca kilamaiyyā’’ti| atha khau upālissa mātāpitūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘sacai khau upāli rūpaṃ sikkhissati, akkhīni dukkhā bhavissanti| imai khau samaṇā sakyaputtiyā sukhasīlā sukhasamācārā, subhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā nivātaisu sayanaisu sayanti| sacai khau upāli samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajaiyya, aivaṃ khau upāli amhākaṃ ac‍cayaina sukhañ‍ca jīvaiyya, na ca kilamaiyyā’’ti|

assausi khau upālidārakau mātāpitūnaṃ imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ| atha khau upālidārakau yaina tai dārakā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā tai dārakai aitadavauca – ‘‘aitha mayaṃ, ayyā, samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajissāmā’’ti| ‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, ayya, pabbajissasi, aivaṃ mayampi pabbajissāmā’’ti| atha khau tai dārakā aikamaikassa mātāpitarau upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘anujānātha maṃ agārasmā anāgāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti| atha khau taisaṃ dārakānaṃ  mātāpitarau – ‘‘sabbaipimai dārakā samānacchandā kalyāṇādhippāyā’’ti – anujāniṃsu| tai bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ yāciṃsu| tai bhikkhū pabbājaisuṃ upasampādaisuṃ| tai rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya raudanti – ‘‘yāguṃ daitha, bhattaṃ daitha, khādanīyaṃ daithā’’ti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘āgamaitha, āvusau, yāva ratti vibhāyati| sacai yāgu bhavissati  pivissatha, sacai bhattaṃ bhavissati bhuñ‍jissatha, sacai khādanīyaṃ bhavissati khādissatha; nau cai bhavissati yāgu vā bhattaṃ vā khādanīyaṃ vā, piṇḍāya caritvā bhuñ‍jissathā’’ti| aivampi khau tai bhikkhū bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānā raudantiyaiva ‘‘yāguṃ daitha, bhattaṃ daitha, khādanīyaṃ daithā’’ti; saināsanaṃ uhadantipi ummihantipi|

assausi khau bhagavā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ  pac‍cuṭṭhāya dārakasaddaṃ| sutvāna āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau sau, ānanda, dārakasaddau’’ti? atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū jānaṃ ūnavīsativassaṃ puggalaṃ upasampādaintī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā jānaṃ ūnavīsativassaṃ puggalaṃ upasampādaissanti| ūnavīsativassau, bhikkhavai, puggalau akkhamau hauti sītassa uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ anadhivāsakajātikau hauti| vīsativassauva khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau khamau hauti sītassa uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ, uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātikau hauti| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya, pasan‍nānaṃ vā bhiyyaubhāvāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, jānaṃ ūnavīsativassau puggalau upasampādaitabbau| yau upasampādaiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau’’ti|

upālidārakavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

37. ahivātakaraugavatthu

100. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraṃ kulaṃ ahivātakaraugaina kālaṅkataṃ hauti| tassa pitāputtakā saisā haunti| tai bhikkhūsu pabbajitvā  aikatauva piṇḍāya caranti| atha khau sau dārakau pitunau bhikkhāya din‍nāya upadhāvitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘mayhampi, tāta, daihi; mayhampi, tāta, daihī’’ti| manussā ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘abrahmacārinau imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā| ayampi dārakau bhikkhuniyā jātau’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ  khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, ūnapan‍narasavassau dārakau pabbājaitabbau| yau pabbājaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau ānandassa upaṭṭhākakulaṃ saddhaṃ pasan‍naṃ ahivātakaraugaina kālaṅkataṃ hauti, dvai ca dārakā saisā haunti| tai paurāṇakaina āciṇṇakappaina bhikkhū passitvā upadhāvanti| bhikkhū apasādainti| tai bhikkhūhi apasādiyamānā raudanti| atha khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘na ūnapan‍narasavassau dārakau pabbājaitabbau’ti| imai ca dārakā ūnapan‍narasavassā| kaina nu khau upāyaina imai dārakā na vinassaiyyu’’nti? atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ussahanti pana tai, ānanda, dārakā kākai uḍḍāpaitunti? ussahanti, bhagavāti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ūnapan‍narasavassaṃ dārakaṃ kākuḍḍaipakaṃ pabbājaitu’’nti|

ahivātakaraugavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

38. kaṇṭakavatthu

101. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa dvai sāmaṇairā haunti – kaṇṭakau ca mahakau ca| tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ dūsaisuṃ| bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma sāmaṇairā aivarūpaṃ anācāraṃ ācarissantī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, aikaina dvai sāmaṇairā upaṭṭhāpaitabbā| yau upaṭṭhāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

kaṇṭakavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

39. āhundarikavatthu

102. taina  khau pana samayaina bhagavā tatthaiva rājagahai vassaṃ vasi, tattha haimantaṃ, tattha gimhaṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘āhundarikā samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ disā andhakārā, na imaisaṃ disā pakkhāyantī’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi  – ‘‘gacchānanda, avāpuraṇaṃ apāpuraṇaṃ (ka॰) ādāya  anuparivaiṇiyaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaihi – ‘‘icchatāvusau bhagavā dakkhiṇāgiriṃ cārikaṃ pak‍kamituṃ| yassāyasmatau atthau, sau āgacchatū’’ti| aivaṃ, bhantai, ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā avāpuraṇaṃ ādāya anuparivaiṇiyaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘icchatāvusau bhagavā dakkhiṇāgiriṃ cārikaṃ pak‍kamituṃ| yassāyasmatau atthau, sau āgacchatū’’’ti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘bhagavatā, āvusau ānanda, pañ‍ñattaṃ dasavassāni nissāya vatthuṃ, dasavassaina  nissayaṃ dātuṃ| tattha ca nau gantabbaṃ bhavissati, nissayau ca gahaitabbau bhavissati, ittarau ca vāsau bhavissati, puna ca pac‍cāgantabbaṃ bhavissati, puna ca nissayau gahaitabbau bhavissati| sacai amhākaṃ ācariyupajjhāyā gamissanti, mayampi gamissāma; nau cai amhākaṃ ācariyupajjhāyā gamissanti, mayampi na gamissāma| lahucittakatā nau, āvusau ānanda, pañ‍ñāyissatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā augaṇaina bhikkhusaṅghaina dakkhiṇāgiriṃ cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi|

āhundarikavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

40. nissayamuc‍canakakathā

103. atha khau bhagavā dakkhiṇāgirismiṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā punadaiva rājagahaṃ pac‍cāgacchi| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, ānanda, tathāgatau augaṇaina bhikkhusaṅghaina dakkhiṇāgiriṃ cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’’ti? atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina pañ‍cavassāni nissāya vatthuṃ, abyattaina yāvajīvaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| na asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti na asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina, na asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina na asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina na asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau  hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina| asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina… asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina… asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| assaddhau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti, kusītau hauti, muṭṭhassati hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| saddhau hauti, hirimā hauti, auttappī hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, upaṭṭhitassati hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti, appassutau hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā  anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| na adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, na ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, na atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti, bahussutau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| āpattiṃ na jānāti, anāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina na svāgatāni haunti na suvibhattāni na suppavattīni na suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| āpattiṃ na jānāti, anāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, ūnapañ‍cavassau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, pañ‍cavassau vā hauti atiraika pañ‍cavassau vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

nissayamuc‍canakakathā niṭṭhitā|

pañ‍cakadasavārau niṭṭhitau|

104. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| na asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina, na asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina, na asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina, na asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau  hauti, ūnapañ‍cavassau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina, asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina, asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina, asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, pañ‍cavassau vā hauti atiraikapañ‍cavassau vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| assaddhau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti, kusītau  hauti, muṭṭhassati hauti, ūnapañ‍cavassau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| saddhau hauti, hirimā hauti, auttappī hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, upaṭṭhitassati hauti, pañ‍cavassau vā hauti atiraikapañ‍cavassau vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti, appassutau hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti, ūnapañ‍cavassau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| na adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, na ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, na atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti, bahussutau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti, pañ‍cavassau vā hauti atiraikapañ‍cavassau vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| āpattiṃ  na jānāti, anāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina na svāgatāni  haunti na suvibhattāni na suppavattīni na suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau, ūnapañ‍cavassau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau  anubyañ‍janasau, pañ‍cavassau vā hauti atiraikapañ‍cavassau vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabba’’nti|

abhayūvarabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau aṭṭhamau|

aṭṭhamabhāṇavārau|

41. rāhulavatthu

105. atha  khau bhagavā rājagahai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina kapilavatthu taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina kapilavatthu tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina suddhaudanassa sak‍kassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau rāhulamātā daivī rāhulaṃ kumāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aisau tai, rāhula, pitā| gacchassu gacchassa (syā॰), dāyaj‍jaṃ yācāhī’’ti| atha khau rāhulau kumārau yaina  bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau puratau, aṭṭhāsi – ‘‘sukhā tai, samaṇa, chāyā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau rāhulau kumārau bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhi – ‘‘dāyaj‍jaṃ mai, samaṇa, daihi; dāyaj‍jaṃ mai, samaṇa, daihī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, sāriputta, rāhulaṃ kumāraṃ pabbājaihī’’ti| ‘‘kathāhaṃ, bhantai, rāhulaṃ kumāraṃ pabbājaimī’’ti? atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tīhi saraṇagamanaihi sāmaṇairapabbaj‍jaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, pabbājaitabbau – paṭhamaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhārāpaitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādāpaitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ kārāpaitvā bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vandāpaitvā  uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīdāpaitvā añ‍jaliṃ paggaṇhāpaitvā aivaṃ vadaihīti vattabbau – buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi; dutiyampi buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dutiyampi dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dutiyampi saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi; tatiyampi buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, tatiyampi  dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, tatiyampi saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmīti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, imaihi tīhi saraṇagamanaihi sāmaṇairapabbaj‍ja’’nti| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau rāhulaṃ kumāraṃ pabbājaisi|

atha khau suddhaudanau sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau suddhaudanau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aikāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ varaṃ yācāmī’’ti| ‘‘atik‍kantavarā khau, gautama, tathāgatā’’ti| ‘‘yañ‍ca, bhantai, kappati, yañ‍ca anavaj‍ja’’nti| ‘‘vadaihi, gautamā’’ti| ‘‘bhagavati mai, bhantai, pabbajitai anappakaṃ dukkhaṃ ahausi, tathā nandai, adhimattaṃ rāhulai| puttapaimaṃ, bhantai, chaviṃ chindati, chaviṃ chaitvā cammaṃ chindati, cammaṃ chaitvā maṃsaṃ chindati, maṃsaṃ chaitvā nhāruṃ chindati, nhāruṃ chaitvā aṭṭhiṃ chindati, aṭṭhiṃ chaitvā aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati| sādhu, bhantai, ayyā ananuñ‍ñātaṃ mātāpitūhi puttaṃ na pabbājaiyyu’’nti| atha khau bhagavā suddhaudanaṃ sak‍kaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau suddhaudanau sak‍kau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, ananuñ‍ñātau mātāpitūhi puttau pabbājaitabbau| yau pabbājaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā kapilavatthusmiṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina sāvatthi taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina sāvatthi tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmatau sāriputtassa upaṭṭhākakulaṃ āyasmatau sāriputtassa santikai dārakaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘imaṃ dārakaṃ thairau pabbājaitū’’ti| atha khau āyasmatau sāriputtassa aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘na aikaina dvai sāmaṇairā upaṭṭhāpaitabbā’ti| ayañ‍ca mai rāhulau sāmaṇairau| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, byattaina bhikkhunā  paṭibalaina aikaina dvai sāmaṇairai upaṭṭhāpaituṃ, yāvatakai  vā pana ussahati auvadituṃ anusāsituṃ tāvatakai upaṭṭhāpaitunti|

rāhulavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

42. sikkhāpadakathā

106. atha khau sāmaṇairānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kati nu khau amhākaṃ sikkhāpadāni, kattha ca amhaihi sikkhitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sāmaṇairānaṃ dasa sikkhāpadāni, taisu ca sāmaṇairaihi sikkhituṃ – pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī vairamaṇi, vairamaṇiṃ (ka॰), adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī, abrahmacariyā vairamaṇī, musāvādā vairamaṇī, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī, vikālabhaujanā vairamaṇī, nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanā vairamaṇī, mālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī, uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā vairamaṇī, jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā vairamaṇī| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sāmaṇairānaṃ imāni dasa sikkhāpadāni, imaisu ca sāmaṇairaihi sikkhitunti|

sikkhāpadakathā niṭṭhitā|

43. daṇḍakammavatthu

107. taina khau pana samayaina sāmaṇairā bhikkhūsu agāravā appatissā  asabhāgavuttikā viharanti| bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma sāmaṇairā bhikkhūsu agāravā appatissā asabhāgavuttikā viharissantī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa sāmaṇairassa daṇḍakammaṃ kātuṃ| bhikkhūnaṃ alābhāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhūnaṃ anatthāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhūnaṃ avāsāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhū ak‍kausati paribhāsati, bhikkhū bhikkhūhi bhaidaiti – anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, imaihi pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa sāmaṇairassa daṇḍakammaṃ kātunti|

atha  khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau daṇḍakammaṃ kātabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇaṃ kātunti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū sāmaṇairānaṃ sabbaṃ saṅghārāmaṃ āvaraṇaṃ karaunti| sāmaṇairā ārāmaṃ pavisituṃ alabhamānā pak‍kamantipi, vibbhamantipi, titthiyaisupi saṅkamanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, sabbau saṅghārāmau āvaraṇaṃ kātabbau| yau karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yattha vā vasati, yattha vā paṭik‍kamati, tattha āvaraṇaṃ kātunti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū sāmaṇairānaṃ mukhadvārikaṃ āhāraṃ āvaraṇaṃ karaunti| manussā yāgupānampi saṅghabhattampi karauntā sāmaṇairai aivaṃ vadainti – ‘‘aitha, bhantai, yāguṃ pivatha; aitha, bhantai, bhattaṃ bhuñ‍jathā’’ti| sāmaṇairā aivaṃ  vadainti – ‘‘nāvusau, labbhā| bhikkhūhi āvaraṇaṃ kata’’nti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhadantā sāmaṇairānaṃ mukhadvārikaṃ āhāraṃ āvaraṇaṃ karissantī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, mukhadvārikau āhārau āvaraṇaṃ kātabbau| yau karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

daṇḍakammavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

44. anāpucchāvaraṇavatthu

108. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū upajjhāyai  anāpucchā sāmaṇairānaṃ āvaraṇaṃ karaunti| upajjhāyā gavaisanti – kathaṃ kahaṃ (ka॰) nu khau amhākaṃ sāmaṇairā na dissantīti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘chabbaggiyaihi, āvusau, bhikkhūhi āvaraṇaṃ kata’’nti| upajjhāyā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū amhai anāpucchā amhākaṃ sāmaṇairānaṃ āvaraṇaṃ karissantī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, upajjhāyai anāpucchā āvaraṇaṃ kātabbaṃ| yau karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

anāpucchāvaraṇavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

45. apalāḷanavatthu

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sāmaṇairai apalāḷainti| thairā sāmaṃ dantakaṭṭhampi mukhaudakampi gaṇhantā kilamanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñassa parisā apalāḷaitabbā| yau apalāḷaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā  ti|

apalāḷanavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

46. kaṇṭakasāmaṇairavatthu

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa kaṇṭakau  nāma sāmaṇairau kaṇṭakiṃ nāma bhikkhuniṃ dūsaisi| bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma sāmaṇairau aivarūpaṃ anācāraṃ ācarissatī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dasahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ sāmaṇairaṃ nāsaituṃ| pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, abrahmacārī hauti, musāvādī hauti, maj‍japāyī hauti, buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, bhikkhunidūsakau hauti – anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, imaihi dasahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ sāmaṇairaṃ nāsaitunti|

47. paṇḍakavatthu

109. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau paṇḍakau bhikkhūsu pabbajitau hauti| sau daharai daharai bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘aitha, maṃ āyasmantau dūsaithā’’ti| bhikkhū apasādainti – ‘‘nassa, paṇḍaka, vinassa, paṇḍaka, kau tayā atthau’’ti| sau bhikkhūhi apasāditau mahantai mahantai mauḷigal‍lai sāmaṇairai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘aitha, maṃ āvusau dūsaithā’’ti| sāmaṇairā apasādainti – ‘‘nassa, paṇḍaka, vinassa, paṇḍaka, kau tayā atthau’’ti| sau sāmaṇairaihi apasāditau hatthibhaṇḍai assabhaṇḍai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘aitha, maṃ, āvusau, dūsaithā’’ti| hatthibhaṇḍā assabhaṇḍā dūsaisuṃ| tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘paṇḍakā  imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā| yaipi imaisaṃ na paṇḍakā, taipi imai paṇḍakai dūsainti| aivaṃ imai sabbaiva abrahmacārinau’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ hatthibhaṇḍānaṃ  assabhaṇḍānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| paṇḍakau, bhikkhavai, anupasampan‍nau na upasampādaitabbau, upasampan‍nau nāsaitabbauti|

48. thaiyyasaṃvāsakavatthu

110. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau purāṇakulaputtau khīṇakaulañ‍ñau sukhumālau hauti| atha khau tassa purāṇakulaputtassa khīṇakaulañ‍ñassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ahaṃ khau sukhumālau, na paṭibalau anadhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ adhigantuṃ, adhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ| kaina nu khau ahaṃ upāyaina sukhañ‍ca jīvaiyyaṃ, na ca kilamaiyya’’nti? atha khau tassa purāṇakulaputtassa khīṇakaulañ‍ñassa aitadahausi – ‘‘imai khau samaṇā sakyaputtiyā sukhasīlā sukhasamācārā, subhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā nivātaisu sayanaisu sayanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ sāmaṃ pattacīvaraṃ paṭiyādaitvā kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvasaiyya’’nti| atha khau sau purāṇakulaputtau khīṇakaulañ‍ñau sāmaṃ pattacīvaraṃ paṭiyādaitvā kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhū abhivādaiti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kativassausi tvaṃ, āvusau’’ti? kiṃ aitaṃ, āvusau, kativassau nāmāti? kau pana tai, āvusau, upajjhāyauti? kiṃ aitaṃ, āvusau, upajjhāyau nāmāti? bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘iṅghāvusau upāli, imaṃ pabbajitaṃ anuyuñ‍jāhī’’ti| atha khau sau purāṇakulaputtau khīṇakaulañ‍ñau āyasmatā upālinā anuyuñ‍jiyamānau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| āyasmā upāli bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| thaiyyasaṃvāsakau, bhikkhavai, anupasampan‍nau na upasampādaitabbau, upasampan‍nau nāsaitabbauti| titthiyapak‍kantakau, bhikkhavai, anupasampan‍nau na upasampādaitabbau, upasampan‍nau nāsaitabbauti|

49. tiracchānagatavatthu

111. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau nāgau nāgayauniyā aṭṭīyati  harāyati jigucchati| atha khau tassa nāgassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau ahaṃ upāyaina nāgayauniyā ca parimuc‍caiyyaṃ khippañ‍ca manussattaṃ paṭilabhaiyya’’nti| atha khau tassa nāgassa aitadahausi – ‘‘imai khau samaṇā sakyaputtiyā dhammacārinau samacārinau brahmacārinau sac‍cavādinau  sīlavantau  kalyāṇadhammā| sacai khau ahaṃ samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajaiyyaṃ, aivāhaṃ nāgayauniyā ca parimuc‍caiyyaṃ, khippañ‍ca manussattaṃ paṭilabhaiyya’’nti| atha khau sau nāgau māṇavakavaṇṇaina bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ yāci| taṃ bhikkhū pabbājaisuṃ, upasampādaisuṃ| taina khau pana samayaina sau nāgau añ‍ñataraina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ pac‍cantimai vihārai paṭivasati| atha khau sau bhikkhu rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya ajjhaukāsai caṅkamati| atha khau sau  nāgau tassa bhikkhunau nikkhantai vissaṭṭhau niddaṃ auk‍kami| sabbau vihārau ahinā puṇṇau, vātapānaihi bhaugā nikkhantā haunti| atha khau sau bhikkhu vihāraṃ pavisissāmīti kavāṭaṃ paṇāmaintau addasa sabbaṃ vihāraṃ ahinā puṇṇaṃ, vātapānaihi bhaugai nikkhantai, disvāna bhītau vissaramakāsi| bhikkhū upadhāvitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, āvusau, vissaramakāsī’’ti? ‘‘ayaṃ, āvusau, sabbau vihārau ahinā puṇṇau, vātapānaihi bhaugā nikkhantā’’ti| atha khau sau nāgau taina saddaina paṭibujjhitvā sakai āsanai nisīdi| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kausi tvaṃ, āvusau’’ti? ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, nāgau’’ti| ‘‘kissa pana tvaṃ, āvusau, aivarūpaṃ akāsī’’ti? atha khau sau nāgau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā taṃ nāgaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tumhai khauttha nāgā aviruḷhidhammā imasmiṃ dhammavinayai| gaccha tvaṃ, nāga, tatthaiva cātuddasai pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa upausathaṃ upavasa, aivaṃ tvaṃ nāgayauniyā ca parimuc‍cissasi, khippañ‍ca manussattaṃ paṭilabhissasī’’ti| atha khau sau nāgau aviruḷhidhammau kirāhaṃ imasmiṃ dhammavinayaiti dukkhī dummanau assūni pavattayamānau vissaraṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘dvaimai, bhikkhavai, pac‍cayā nāgassa sabhāvapātukammāya| yadā ca sajātiyā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ  paṭisaivati, yadā ca vissaṭṭhau niddaṃ auk‍kamati – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dvai pac‍cayā nāgassa sabhāvapātukammāya| tiracchānagatau, bhikkhavai, anupasampan‍nau na upasampādaitabbau, upasampan‍nau nāsaitabbau’’ti|

50. mātughātakavatthu

112. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau māṇavakau mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaisi| sau taina pāpakaina kammaina aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati| atha khau tassa māṇavakassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau ahaṃ upāyaina imassa pāpakassa kammassa nikkhantiṃ karaiyya’’nti? atha khau tassa māṇavakassa aitadahausi – ‘‘imai khau samaṇā sakyaputtiyā dhammacārinau  samacārinau brahmacārinau sac‍cavādinau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā| sacai khau ahaṃ samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajaiyyaṃ, aivāhaṃ imassa pāpakassa kammassa nikkhantiṃ karaiyya’’nti| atha khau sau māṇavakau bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ yāci| bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pubbaipi khau, āvusau upāli, nāgau māṇavakavaṇṇaina bhikkhūsu pabbajitau| iṅghāvusau upāli, imaṃ māṇavakaṃ anuyuñ‍jāhī’’ti| atha khau sau māṇavakau āyasmatā upālinā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| āyasmā upāli bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… mātughātakau, bhikkhavai, anupasampan‍nau na upasampādaitabbau, upasampan‍nau nāsaitabbauti|

51. pitughātakavatthu

113. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau māṇavakau pitaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaisi| sau taina pāpakaina kammaina aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati| atha khau tassa māṇavakassa aitadahausi ‘‘kaina nu khau ahaṃ upāyaina imassa pāpakassa kammassa nikkhantiṃ karaiyya’’nti| atha khau tassa māṇavakassa aitadahausi ‘‘imai khau samaṇā sakyaputtiyā dhammacārinau samacārinau brahmacārinau sac‍cavādinau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā, sacai khau ahaṃ samaṇaisu sakyaputtiyaisu pabbajaiyyaṃ, aivāhaṃ imassa pāpakassa kammassa nikkhantiṃ karaiyya’’nti| atha khau sau māṇavakau bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ yāci| bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pubbaipi khau, āvusau upāli, nāgau māṇavakavaṇṇaina bhikkhūsu pabbajitau, iṅghāvusau, upāli, imaṃ māṇavakaṃ anuyuñ‍jāhī’’ti| atha khau sau māṇavakau āyasmatā upālinā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| āyasmā upāli bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| pitughātakau, bhikkhavai, anupasampan‍nau na upasampādaitabbau, upasampan‍nau nāsaitabbauti|

52. arahantaghātakavatthu

114. taina  khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū sākaitā sāvatthiṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| antarāmaggai caurā nikkhamitvā aikac‍cai bhikkhū acchindiṃsu, aikac‍cai bhikkhū haniṃsu| sāvatthiyā rājabhaṭā nikkhamitvā aikac‍cai caurai aggahaisuṃ, aikac‍cai  caurā palāyiṃsu| yai tai palāyiṃsu tai bhikkhūsu pabbajiṃsu, yai tai gahitā tai vadhāya auniyyanti| addasaṃsu khau tai palāyitvā pabbajitā tai caurai vadhāya auniyyamānai, disvāna aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘sādhu khau mayaṃ palāyimhā, sacā ca sacai ca, sacaj‍ja (aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pāṭhantarā) mayaṃ gayhaiyyāma gaṇhaiyyāma (ka॰), mayampi aivamaiva hañ‍ñaiyyāmā’’ti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau, akatthā’’ti? atha khau tai pabbajitā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| arahantau aitai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū| arahantaghātakau, bhikkhavai, anupasampan‍nau na upasampādaitabbau, upasampan‍nau nāsaitabbauti|

53. bhikkhunīdūsakavatthu

115. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhuniyau sākaitā sāvatthiṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| antarāmaggai caurā nikkhamitvā aikac‍cā bhikkhuniyau acchindiṃsu, aikac‍cā bhikkhuniyau dūsaisuṃ| sāvatthiyā rājabhaṭā nikkhamitvā aikac‍cai caurai aggahaisuṃ, aikac‍cai caurā palāyiṃsu| yai tai palāyiṃsu, tai bhikkhūsu pabbajiṃsu| yai tai gahitā, tai vadhāya auniyyanti| addasaṃsu khau tai palāyitvā pabbajitā tai caurai vadhāya auniyyamānai, disvāna aivamāhaṃsu ‘‘sādhu khau mayaṃ palāyimhā, sacā ca mayaṃ gayhaiyyāma, mayampi aivamaiva hañ‍ñaiyyāmā’’ti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu ‘‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau, akatthā’’ti| atha khau tai pabbajitā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhunidūsakau, bhikkhavai, anupasampan‍nau na upasampādaitabbau, upasampan‍nau nāsaitabbauti| saṅghabhaidakau, bhikkhavai, anupasampan‍nau na upasampādaitabbau, upasampan‍nau nāsaitabbauti| lauhituppādakau, bhikkhavai, anupasampan‍nau na upasampādaitabbau, upasampan‍nau nāsaitabbauti|

54. ubhataubyañ‍janakavatthu

116. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau ubhataubyañ‍janakau bhikkhūsu pabbajitau hauti| sau karautipi kārāpaitipi| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ubhataubyañ‍janakau, bhikkhavai, anupasampan‍nau na upasampādaitabbau, upasampan‍nau nāsaitabbauti|

55. anupajjhāyakādivatthūni

117. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū anupajjhāyakaṃ upasampādainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, anupajjhāyakau upasampādaitabbau| yau upasampādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū saṅghaina upajjhāyaina upasampādainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, saṅghaina upajjhāyaina upasampādaitabbau| yau upasampādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū gaṇaina upajjhāyaina upasampādainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, gaṇaina upajjhāyaina upasampādaitabbau| yau upasampādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū paṇḍakupajjhāyaina upasampādainti…pai॰… thaiyyasaṃvāsakupajjhāyaina upasampādainti…pai॰… titthiyapak‍kantakupajjhāyaina upasampādainti …pai॰… tiracchānagatupajjhāyaina  upasampādainti…pai॰… mātughātakupajjhāyaina upasampādainti…pai॰… pitughātakupajjhāyaina upasampādainti…pai॰… arahantaghātakupajjhāyaina upasampādainti…pai॰… bhikkhunidūsakupajjhāyaina upasampādainti…pai॰… saṅghabhaidakupajjhāyaina upasampādainti…pai॰… lauhituppādakupajjhāyaina upasampādainti…pai॰… ubhataubyañ‍janakupajjhāyaina upasampādainti bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, paṇḍakupajjhāyaina upasampādaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, thaiyyasaṃvāsakupajjhāyaina upasampādaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, titthiyapak‍kantakupajjhāyaina upasampādaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatupajjhāyaina upasampādaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, mātughātakupajjhāyaina upasampādaitabbau …pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, pitughātakupajjhāyaina upasampādaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, arahantaghātakupajjhāyaina upasampādaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunidūsakupajjhāyaina upasampādaitabbau …pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, saṅghabhaidakupajjhāyaina upasampādaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, lauhituppādakupajjhāyaina upasampādaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, ubhataubyañ‍janakupajjhāyaina upasampādaitabbau| yau upasampādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

56. apattakādivatthu

118. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū apattakaṃ upasampādainti| hatthaisu piṇḍāya caranti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi titthiyāti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, apattakau upasampādaitabbau| yau upasampādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū acīvarakaṃ upasampādainti| naggā piṇḍāya caranti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi titthiyāti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, acīvarakau upasampādaitabbau| yau upasampādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū apattacīvarakaṃ upasampādainti| naggā hatthaisu piṇḍāya caranti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi titthiyāti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, apattacīvarakau upasampādaitabbau| yau upasampādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū yācitakaina pattaina upasampādainti| upasampan‍nai pattaṃ paṭiharanti| hatthaisu piṇḍāya caranti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi titthiyāti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, yācitakaina pattaina upasampādaitabbau| yau upasampādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|
