taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū yācitakaina cīvaraina upasampādainti| upasampan‍nai cīvaraṃ paṭiharanti| naggā piṇḍāya caranti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi titthiyāti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, yācitakaina cīvaraina upasampādaitabbau| yau upasampādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū yācitakaina pattacīvaraina upasampādainti| upasampan‍nai pattacīvaraṃ  paṭiharanti| naggā hatthaisu  piṇḍāya caranti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti  – saiyyathāpi titthiyāti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, yācitakaina pattacīvaraina upasampādaitabbau| yau upasampādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

naupasampādaitabbaikavīsativārau niṭṭhitau|

57. napabbājaitabbadvattiṃsavārau

119. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū hatthacchin‍naṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… pādacchin‍naṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… hatthapādacchin‍naṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… kaṇṇacchin‍naṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… nāsacchin‍naṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… kaṇṇanāsacchin‍naṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… aṅgulicchin‍naṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… aḷacchin‍naṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… kaṇḍaracchin‍naṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… phaṇahatthakaṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… khuj‍jaṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… vāmanaṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… galagaṇḍiṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… lakkhaṇāhataṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… kasāhataṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… likhitakaṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… sīpadiṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… pāparaugiṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… parisadūsakaṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… kāṇaṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… kuṇiṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… khañ‍jaṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… pakkhahataṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… chin‍niriyāpathaṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… jarādubbalaṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… andhaṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… mūgaṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… badhiraṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… andhamūgaṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… andhabadhiraṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… mūgabadhiraṃ pabbājainti…pai॰… andhamūgabadhiraṃ pabbājainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, hatthacchin‍nau  pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, pādacchin‍nau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, hatthapādacchin‍nau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, kaṇṇacchin‍nau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, nāsacchin‍nau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, kaṇṇanāsacchin‍nau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, aṅgulicchin‍nau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, aḷacchin‍nau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, kaṇḍaracchin‍nau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, phaṇahatthakau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, khuj‍jau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, vāmanau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, galagaṇḍī pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, lakkhaṇāhatau  pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, kasāhatau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, likhitakau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, sīpadī pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, pāparaugī pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, parisadūsakau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, kāṇau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, kuṇī pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, khañ‍jau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, pakkhahatau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, chin‍niriyāpathau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, jarādubbalau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, andhau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, mūgau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, badhirau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, andhamūgau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, andhabadhirau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, mūgabadhirau pabbājaitabbau…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, andhamūgabadhirau pabbājaitabbau| yau pabbājaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

napabbājaitabbadvattiṃsavārau niṭṭhitau|

dāyaj‍jabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau navamau|

58. alaj‍jīnissayavatthūni

120. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū alaj‍jīnaṃ nissayaṃ  dainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, alaj‍jīnaṃ nissayau dātabbau| yau dadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū alaj‍jīnaṃ nissāya vasanti| taipi nacirassaiva alaj‍jinau haunti pāpakābhikkhū| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, alaj‍jīnaṃ nissāya vatthabbaṃ| yau vasaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘na alaj‍jīnaṃ nissayau dātabbau, na alaj‍jīnaṃ nissāya vatthabba’nti| kathaṃ nu khau mayaṃ jānaiyyāma laj‍jiṃ vā alaj‍jiṃ vā’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, catūhapañ‍cāhaṃ āgamaituṃ yāva bhikkhusabhāgataṃ jānāmīti|

59. gamikādinissayavatthūni

121. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kausalaisu janapadai addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau hauti| atha khau tassa bhikkhunau aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘na anissitaina  vatthabba’nti| ahañ‍camhi nissayakaraṇīyau addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau, kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, addhānamaggappaṭipan‍naina bhikkhunā nissayaṃ alabhamānaina anissitaina vatthunti|

taina khau pana samayaina dvai bhikkhū kausalaisu janapadai addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| tai añ‍ñataraṃ āvāsaṃ upagacchiṃsu| tattha aikau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| atha khau tassa gilānassa bhikkhunau aitadahausi  – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘na anissitaina vatthabba’nti| ahañ‍camhi nissayakaraṇīyau gilānau, kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānaina bhikkhunā nissayaṃ alabhamānaina anissitaina vatthunti|

atha khau tassa gilānupaṭṭhākassa bhikkhunau aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘na anissitaina vatthabba’nti| ahañ‍camhi nissayakaraṇīyau, ayañ‍ca bhikkhu gilānau, kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānupaṭṭhākaina bhikkhunā nissayaṃ alabhamānaina yāciyamānaina anissitaina vatthunti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu arañ‍ñai viharati| tassa ca tasmiṃ saināsanai phāsu hauti| atha khau tassa bhikkhunau aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘na anissitaina vatthabba’nti| ahañ‍camhi nissayakaraṇīyau arañ‍ñai viharāmi, mayhañ‍ca imasmiṃ saināsanai phāsu hauti, kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā phāsuvihāraṃ sal‍lakkhaintaina nissayaṃ alabhamānaina anissitaina vatthuṃ – yadā patirūpau nissayadāyakau āgacchissati, tadā tassa nissāya vasissāmīti|

60. gauttaina anussāvanānujānanā

122. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmatau mahākassapassa upasampadāpaikkhau hauti| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmatau ānandassa santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – āgacchatu ānandau imaṃ anussāvaissatūti  anussāvaissatīti (syā॰)| āyasmā ānandau aivamāha – ‘‘nāhaṃ ussahāmi thairassa nāmaṃ  gahaituṃ, garu mai thairau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gauttainapi anussāvaitunti|

61. dvaiupasampadāpaikkhādivatthu

123. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau mahākassapassa dvai upasampadāpaikkhā haunti| tai vivadanti – ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ upasampaj‍jissāmi, ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ upasampaj‍jissāmīti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dvai aikānussāvanai kātunti|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulānaṃ thairānaṃ upasampadāpaikkhā haunti| tai vivadanti – ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ upasampaj‍jissāmi, ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ upasampaj‍jissāmīti| thairā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘handa, mayaṃ, āvusau, sabbaiva aikānussāvanai karaumā’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dvai tayau aikānussāvanai kātuṃ, tañ‍ca khau aikaina upajjhāyaina, na tvaiva nānupajjhāyaināti|

62. gabbhavīsūpasampadānujānanā

124. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā kumārakassapau gabbhavīsau upasampan‍nau ahausi| atha khau āyasmatau kumārakassapassa aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘na ūnavīsativassau puggalau upasampādaitabbau’ti| ahañ‍camhi gabbhavīsau upasampan‍nau| upasampan‍nau nu khaumhi, nanu khau upasampan‍nau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, mātukucchismiṃ paṭhamaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ, paṭhamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pātubhūtaṃ, tadupādāya sāvassa jāti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gabbhavīsaṃ upasampādaitunti|

63. upasampadāvidhi

125. taina  khau pana samayaina upasampan‍nā dissanti kuṭṭhikāpi gaṇḍikāpi kilāsikāpi sausikāpi apamārikāpi| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, upasampādaintaina tairasa tassa (ka॰) antarāyikai dhammai pucchituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, pucchitabbau – ‘‘santi tai aivarūpā ābādhā – kuṭṭhaṃ, gaṇḍau, kilāsau, sausau, apamārau? manussausi? purisausi? bhujissausi? aṇaṇausi? nasi rājabhaṭau? anuñ‍ñātausi mātāpitūhi? paripuṇṇavīsativassausi? paripuṇṇaṃ tai pattacīvaraṃ? kiṃnāmausi? kaunāmau tai upajjhāyau’’ti?

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū ananusiṭṭhai upasampadāpaikkhai antarāyikai dhammai pucchanti| upasampadāpaikkhā vitthāyanti, maṅkū haunti, na sak‍kaunti vissaj‍jaituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ anusāsitvā pacchā antarāyikai  dhammai pucchitunti|

tatthaiva saṅghamajjhai anusāsanti| upasampadāpaikkhā tathaiva vitthāyanti, maṅkū haunti, na sak‍kaunti vissaj‍jaituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aikamantaṃ anusāsitvā saṅghamajjhai antarāyikai dhammai pucchituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, anusāsitabbau –

126. paṭhamaṃ upajjhaṃ gāhāpaitabbau| upajjhaṃ gāhāpaitvā  pattacīvaraṃ ācikkhitabbaṃ – ayaṃ tai pattau, ayaṃ saṅghāṭi, ayaṃ uttarāsaṅgau, ayaṃ antaravāsakau| gaccha, amumhi aukāsai tiṭṭhāhīti|

bālā abyattā anusāsanti| duranusiṭṭhā upasampadāpaikkhā vitthāyanti, maṅkū haunti, na sak‍kaunti vissaj‍jaituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, bālaina abyattaina anusāsitabbau| yau anusāsaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina anusāsitunti|

asammatā anusāsanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, asammataina anusāsitabbau| yau anusāsaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sammataina anusāsituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau sammanitabbau (ka॰) – attanā vā attanāva (syā॰) attānaṃ samman‍nitabbaṃ, paraina vā parau samman‍nitabbau|

kathañ‍ca  attanāva attānaṃ samman‍nitabbaṃ? byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau – ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmassa āyasmatau upasampadāpaikkhau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ itthan‍nāmaṃ anusāsaiyya’’nti| aivaṃ attanāva attānaṃ samman‍nitabbaṃ|

kathañ‍ca pana paraina parau samman‍nitabbau? byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau – ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmassa āyasmatau upasampadāpaikkhau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmaṃ anusāsaiyyā’’ti| aivaṃ paraina parau samman‍nitabbau|

taina sammataina bhikkhunā upasampadāpaikkhau upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘suṇasi, itthan‍nāma, ayaṃ tai sac‍cakālau bhūtakālau| yaṃ jātaṃ taṃ saṅghamajjhai pucchantai santaṃ atthīti vattabbaṃ, asantaṃ natthī’’ti vattabbaṃ| mā khau vitthāyi, mā khau maṅku ahausi| aivaṃ taṃ pucchissanti – ‘‘santi tai aivarūpā ābādhā – kuṭṭhaṃ, gaṇḍau, kilāsau, sausau, apamārau? manussausi? purisausi? bhujissausi? aṇaṇausi? nasi rājabhaṭau? anuñ‍ñātausi mātāpitūhi? paripuṇṇavīsativassausi? paripuṇṇaṃ tai pattacīvaraṃ? kiṃnāmausi? kaunāmau tai upajjhāyau’’ti?

aikatau āgacchanti| na, bhikkhavai, aikatau āgantabbaṃ| anusāsakaina paṭhamataraṃ āgantvā saṅghau ñāpaitabbau – ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmassa āyasmatau upasampadāpaikkhau| anusiṭṭhau sau mayā| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, itthan‍nāmau āgacchaiyyā’’ti| āgacchāhīti vattabbau|

aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ kārāpaitvā bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vandāpaitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīdāpaitvā añ‍jaliṃ paggaṇhāpaitvā upasampadaṃ yācāpaitabbau – ‘‘saṅghaṃ, bhantai, upasampadaṃ yācāmi| ul‍lumpatu maṃ, bhantai, saṅghau anukampaṃ upādāya| dutiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācāmi| ul‍lumpatu maṃ, bhantai, saṅghau anukampaṃ  upādāya| tatiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācāmi| ul‍lumpatu maṃ, bhantai, saṅghau anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmassa āyasmatau upasampadāpaikkhau| yadi  saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ itthan‍nāmaṃ antarāyikai dhammai pucchaiyya’’nti? suṇasi, itthan‍nāma, ayaṃ tai sac‍cakālau bhūtakālau| yaṃ jātaṃ taṃ pucchāmi| santaṃ atthīti vattabbaṃ, asantaṃ natthīti vattabbaṃ| santi tai aivarūpā ābādhā – kuṭṭhaṃ gaṇḍau kilaisau sausau apamārau, manussausi, purisausi, bhujissausi, aṇaṇausi, nasi rājabhaṭau, anuñ‍ñātausi mātāpitūhi, paripuṇṇavīsativassausi, paripuṇṇaṃ tai pattacīvaraṃ, kiṃnāmausi, kaunāmau tai upajjhāyauti? byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

127. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmassa āyasmatau upasampadāpaikkhau, parisuddhau antarāyikaihi dhammaihi, paripuṇṇassa pattacīvaraṃ| itthan‍nāmau saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati itthan‍nāmaina upajjhāyaina| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upasampādaiyya itthan‍nāmaina upajjhāyaina| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmassa āyasmatau upasampadāpaikkhau, parisuddhau antarāyikaihi dhammaihi, paripuṇṇassa pattacīvaraṃ| itthan‍nāmau saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati itthan‍nāmaina upajjhāyaina| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upasampādaiti itthan‍nāmaina upajjhāyaina| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa upasampadā itthan‍nāmaina upajjhāyaina, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmassa āyasmatau upasampadāpaikkhau, parisuddhau antarāyikaihi dhammaihi, paripuṇṇassa pattacīvaraṃ| itthan‍nāmau saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati itthan‍nāmaina upajjhāyaina| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upasampādaiti itthan‍nāmaina upajjhāyaina| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa upasampadā itthan‍nāmaina upajjhāyaina, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘tatiyampi  aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmassa āyasmatau upasampadāpaikkhau, parisuddhau antarāyikaihi dhammaihi, paripuṇṇassa pattacīvaraṃ| itthan‍nāmau saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati itthan‍nāmaina upajjhāyaina| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upasampādaiti itthan‍nāmaina upajjhāyaina| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa upasampadā itthan‍nāmaina upajjhāyaina, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘upasampan‍nau  saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmaina upajjhāyaina| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

upasampadākammaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

64. cattārau nissayā

128. tāvadaiva chāyā maitabbā, utuppamāṇaṃ ācikkhitabbaṃ, divasabhāgau  ācikkhitabbau, saṅgīti ācikkhitabbā, cattārau nissayā ācikkhitabbā ācikkhitabbā, cattāri akaraṇīyāni ācikkhitabbāni| (ka॰) –

‘‘piṇḍiyālaupabhaujanaṃ nissāya pabbaj‍jā| tattha tai yāvajīvaṃ ussāhau karaṇīyau| atiraikalābhau – saṅghabhattaṃ, uddaisabhattaṃ, nimantanaṃ, salākabhattaṃ, pakkhikaṃ, upausathikaṃ, pāṭipadikaṃ|

‘‘paṃsukūlacīvaraṃ nissāya pabbaj‍jā| tattha tai yāvajīvaṃ ussāhau karaṇīyau| atiraikalābhau – khaumaṃ, kappāsikaṃ, kausaiyyaṃ, kambalaṃ, sāṇaṃ, bhaṅgaṃ|

‘‘rukkhamūlasaināsanaṃ nissāya pabbaj‍jā| tattha tai yāvajīvaṃ ussāhau karaṇīyau| atiraikalābhau – vihārau, aḍḍhayaugau, pāsādau, hammiyaṃ, guhā|

‘‘pūtimuttabhaisaj‍jaṃ nissāya pabbaj‍jā| tattha tai yāvajīvaṃ ussāhau karaṇīyau| atiraikalābhau – sappi, navanītaṃ, tailaṃ, madhu, phāṇita’’nti|

cattārau nissayā niṭṭhitā|

65. cattāri akaraṇīyāni

129. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ upasampādaitvā aikakaṃ auhāya pak‍kamiṃsu| sau pacchā aikakauva āgacchantau antarāmaggai purāṇadutiyikāya samāgañchi| sā aivamāha – ‘‘kiṃdāni pabbajitausī’’ti? ‘‘āma, pabbajitaumhī’’ti| ‘‘dul‍labhau khau pabbajitānaṃ maithunau dhammau; aihi, maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivā’’ti| sau tassā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ  paṭisaivitvā ciraina agamāsi| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ ciraṃ akāsī’’ti? atha khau sau bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, upasampādaitvā dutiyaṃ dātuṃ, cattāri ca akaraṇīyāni ācikkhituṃ –

‘‘upasampan‍naina bhikkhunā maithunau dhammau na paṭisaivitabbau, antamasau tiracchānagatāyapi| yau bhikkhu maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati, assamaṇau hauti asakyaputtiyau| saiyyathāpi nāma purisau sīsacchin‍nau abhabbau taina sarīrabandhanaina jīvituṃ, aivamaiva bhikkhu maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivitvā assamaṇau hauti asakyaputtiyau| taṃ tai yāvajīvaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ|

‘‘upasampan‍naina bhikkhunā adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ na ādātabbaṃ, antamasau tiṇasalākaṃ upādāya| yau bhikkhu pādaṃ vā pādārahaṃ vā atiraikapādaṃ vā adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyati, assamaṇau hauti asakyaputtiyau| saiyyathāpi nāma paṇḍupalāsau bandhanā pamuttau abhabbau haritatthāya, aivamaiva bhikkhu pādaṃ vā pādārahaṃ vā atiraikapādaṃ vā adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyitvā assamaṇau hauti asakyaputtiyau| taṃ tai yāvajīvaṃ  akaraṇīyaṃ|

‘‘upasampan‍naina bhikkhunā sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇau jīvitā na vauraupaitabbau, antamasau kunthakipil‍likaṃ upādāya| yau bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiti, antamasau gabbhapātanaṃ upādāya, assamaṇau hauti asakyaputtiyau| saiyyathāpi nāma puthusilā dvaidhā bhin‍nā appaṭisandhikā hauti, aivamaiva bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupaitvā assamaṇau hauti asakyaputtiyau| taṃ tai yāvajīvaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ|

‘‘upasampan‍naina  bhikkhunā uttarimanussadhammau na ul‍lapitabbau, antamasau ‘suñ‍ñāgārai abhiramāmī’ti| yau bhikkhu pāpicchau icchāpakatau asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapati jhānaṃ vā vimaukkhaṃ vā samādhiṃ vā samāpattiṃ vā maggaṃ vā phalaṃ vā, assamaṇau hauti asakyaputtiyau| saiyyathāpi nāma tālau matthakacchin‍nau abhabbau puna viruḷhiyā, aivamaiva bhikkhu pāpicchau icchāpakatau asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapitvā assamaṇau hauti asakyaputtiyau| taṃ tai yāvajīvaṃ akaraṇīya’’nti|

cattāri akaraṇīyāni niṭṭhitāni|

66. āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakavatthūni

130. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakau vibbhami| sau puna pac‍cāgantvā bhikkhū upasampadaṃ yāci| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakau vibbhamati| sau puna pac‍cāgantvā bhikkhū upasampadaṃ yācati| sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘passissasi taṃ āpatti’’nti? sacāhaṃ passissāmīti, pabbājaitabbau| sacāhaṃ na passissāmīti, na pabbājaitabbau| pabbājaitvā vattabbau – ‘‘passissasi taṃ āpatti’’nti? sacāhaṃ passissāmīti, upasampādaitabbau| sacāhaṃ na passissāmīti, na upasampādaitabbau| upasampādaitvā vattabbau – ‘‘passissasi taṃ āpatti’’nti? sacāhaṃ passissāmīti, ausāraitabbau| sacāhaṃ na passissāmīti, na ausāraitabbau| ausāraitvā vattabbau – ‘‘passasi passāhi (sī॰) taṃ āpatti’’nti? sacai passati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai passati, labbhamānāya sāmaggiyā puna ukkhipitabbau| alabbhamānāya sāmaggiyā anāpatti sambhaugai saṃvāsai|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhittakau vibbhamati| sau puna pac‍cāgantvā bhikkhū upasampadaṃ yācati| sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘paṭikarissasi taṃ āpatti’’nti? sacāhaṃ paṭikarissāmīti, pabbājaitabbau| sacāhaṃ na paṭikarissāmīti, na pabbājaitabbau| pabbājaitvā vattabbau – ‘‘paṭikarissasi  taṃ āpatti’’nti? sacāhaṃ paṭikarissāmīti, upasampādaitabbau| sacāhaṃ na paṭikarissāmīti, na upasampādaitabbau| upasampādaitvā vattabbau – ‘‘paṭikarissasi taṃ āpatti’’nti? sacāhaṃ paṭikarissāmīti, ausāraitabbau| sacāhaṃ na paṭikarissāmīti, na ausāraitabbau| ausāraitvā vattabbau – ‘‘paṭikarauhi taṃ āpatti’’nti| sacai paṭikarauti, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai paṭikarauti labbhamānāya sāmaggiyā puna ukkhipitabbau| alabbhamānāya sāmaggiyā anāpatti sambhaugai saṃvāsai|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhittakau vibbhamati| sau puna pac‍cāgantvā bhikkhū upasampadaṃ yācati| sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘paṭinissaj‍jissasi taṃ pāpikaṃ diṭṭhi’’nti? sacāhaṃ paṭinissaj‍jissāmīti, pabbājaitabbau| sacāhaṃ na paṭinissaj‍jissāmīti, na pabbājaitabbau| pabbājaitvā vattabbau – ‘‘paṭinissaj‍jissasi  taṃ  pāpikaṃ diṭṭhi’’nti? sacāhaṃ paṭinissaj‍jissāmīti, upasampādaitabbau| sacāhaṃ na paṭinissaj‍jissāmīti, na upasampādaitabbau| upasampādaitvā vattabbau – ‘‘paṭinissaj‍jissasi taṃ pāpikaṃ diṭṭhi’’nti? sacāhaṃ paṭinissaj‍jissāmīti, ausāraitabbau| sacāhaṃ na paṭinissaj‍jissāmīti, na ausāraitabbau| ausāraitvā vattabbau – ‘‘paṭinissaj‍jaihi taṃ pāpikaṃ diṭṭhi’’nti| sacai paṭinissaj‍jati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai paṭinissaj‍jati, labbhamānāya sāmaggiyā puna ukkhipitabbau| alabbhamānāya sāmaggiyā anāpatti sambhaugai saṃvāsaiti|

mahākhandhakau paṭhamau|

67. tassuddānaṃ

131.

 vinayamhi mahatthaisu, paisalānaṃ sukhāvahai|

niggahānañ‍ca pāpicchai, laj‍jīnaṃ paggahaisu ca||

sāsanādhāraṇai caiva, sabbañ‍ñujinagaucarai|

anañ‍ñavisayai khaimai, supañ‍ñattai asaṃsayai||

khandhakai vinayai caiva, parivārai ca mātikai|

yathātthakārī kusalau, paṭipaj‍jati yaunisau||

yau  gavaṃ na vijānāti, na sau rakkhati gaugaṇaṃ|

aivaṃ  sīlaṃ ajānantau, kiṃ sau rakkhaiyya saṃvaraṃ||

pamuṭṭhamhi ca suttantai, abhidhammai ca tāvadai|

vinayai  avinaṭṭhamhi, puna tiṭṭhati sāsanaṃ||

tasmā saṅgāhaṇāhaituṃ saṅgāhanāhaituṃ (ka॰), uddānaṃ anupubbasau|

pavakkhāmi yathāñāyaṃ, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

vatthu  nidānaṃ āpatti, nayā paiyyālamaiva ca|

duk‍karaṃ taṃ asaisaituṃ, nayatau taṃ vijānathāti||

baudhi rājāyatanañ‍ca, ajapālau sahampati|

brahmā āḷārau udakau, bhikkhu ca upakau isi||

kauṇḍañ‍ñau vappau bhaddiyau, mahānāmau ca assaji|

yasau cattārau pañ‍ñāsa, sabbai paisaisi sau disā||

vatthu māraihi tiṃsā ca, uruvailaṃ tayau jaṭī|

agyāgāraṃ mahārājā, sak‍kau brahmā ca kaivalā||

paṃsukūlaṃ paukkharaṇī, silā ca kakudhau silā|

jambu  ambau ca āmalau, pāripupphañ‍ca āhari||

phāliyantu uj‍jalantu, vijjhāyantu ca kassapa|

nimuj‍janti mukhī maighau, gayā laṭṭhi ca māgadhau||

upatissau kaulitau ca, abhiñ‍ñātā ca pabbajuṃ|

dun‍nivatthā paṇāmanā, kisau lūkhau ca brāhmaṇau||

anācāraṃ ācarati, udaraṃ māṇavau gaṇau|

vassaṃ bālaihi pak‍kantau, dasa vassāni nissayau||

na vattanti paṇāmaituṃ, bālā passaddhi pañ‍ca cha|

yau sau añ‍ñau ca naggau ca, acchin‍najaṭilasākiyau||

magadhaisu pañ‍cābādhā, aikau rājā bhaṭau caurau (syā॰) ca aṅguli|

māgadhau ca anuñ‍ñāsi, kārā likhi kasāhatau||

lakkhaṇā  iṇā dāsau ca, bhaṇḍukau upāli ahi|

saddhaṃ kulaṃ kaṇṭakau ca, āhundarikamaiva ca||

vatthumhi dārakau sikkhā, viharanti ca kiṃ nu khau|

sabbaṃ mukhaṃ upajjhāyai, apalāḷana kaṇṭakau||

paṇḍakau thaiyyapak‍kantau, ahi ca mātarī pitā|

arahantabhikkhunībhaidā, ruhiraina ca byañ‍janaṃ||

anupajjhāyasaṅghaina, gaṇapaṇḍakapattakau|

acīvaraṃ  tadubhayaṃ, yācitainapi yai tayau||

hatthā pādā hatthapādā, kaṇṇā nāsā tadūbhayaṃ|

aṅguliaḷakaṇḍaraṃ, phaṇaṃ khuj‍jañ‍ca vāmanaṃ||

galagaṇḍī lakkhaṇā caiva, kasā likhitasīpadī|

pāpaparisadūsī ca, kāṇaṃ kuṇi tathaiva ca||

khañ‍jaṃ  pakkhahatañ‍caiva, sacchin‍nairiyāpathaṃ|

jarāndhamūgabadhiraṃ, andhamūgañ‍ca yaṃ tahiṃ||

andhabadhiraṃ yaṃ vuttaṃ, mūgabadhiramaiva ca|

andhamūgabadhirañ‍ca, alaj‍jīnañ‍ca nissayaṃ||

vatthabbañ‍ca tathāddhānaṃ, yācamānaina lakkhaṇā paikkhanā (sabbattha)|

āgacchatu vivadanti, aikupajjhāyaina kassapau||

dissanti upasampan‍nā, ābādhaihi ca pīḷitā|

ananusiṭṭhā vitthainti, tatthaiva anusāsanā||

saṅghaipi  ca athau bālā, asammatā ca aikatau|

ul‍lumpatupasampadā, nissayau aikakau tayauti||

imamhi khandhakai vatthūni aikasatañ‍ca dvāsattati|

mahākhandhakau niṭṭhitau|



2. upausathakkhandhakau

68. san‍nipātānujānanā

132. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā cātuddasai pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa san‍nipatitvā dhammaṃ bhāsanti| tai manussā upasaṅkamanti dhammassavanāya| tai labhanti añ‍ñatitthiyaisu paribbājakaisu paimaṃ, labhanti pasādaṃ, labhanti añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā pakkhaṃ| atha khau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘aitarahi khau añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā cātuddasai pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa san‍nipatitvā dhammaṃ bhāsanti| tai manussā upasaṅkamanti dhammassavanāya| tai labhanti añ‍ñatitthiyaisu paribbājakaisu paimaṃ, labhanti pasādaṃ, labhanti añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā pakkhaṃ| yaṃnūna ayyāpi cātuddasai pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa san‍nipataiyyu’’nti| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi ‘aitarahi khau añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā cātuddasai pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa san‍nipatitvā dhammaṃ bhāsanti| tai manussā  upasaṅkamanti dhammassavanāya| tai labhanti añ‍ñatitthiyaisu paribbājakaisu paimaṃ, labhanti pasādaṃ, labhanti añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā pakkhaṃ| yaṃnūna ayyāpi cātuddasai pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa san‍nipataiyyu’nti| sādhu, bhantai, ayyāpi cātuddasai pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa san‍nipataiyyu’’nti| atha khau bhagavā rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā  padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi  – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cātuddasai pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa san‍nipatitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū – bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātā cātuddasai pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa san‍nipatitunti – cātuddasai pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa san‍nipatitvā tuṇhī nisīdanti| tai manussā upasaṅkamanti dhammassavanāya| tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā cātuddasai pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa san‍nipatitvā tuṇhī nisīdissanti, saiyyathāpi mūgasūkarā| nanu nāma san‍nipatitaihi dhammau bhāsitabbau’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ  vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cātuddasai pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa san‍nipatitvā dhammaṃ bhāsitu’’nti|

69. pātimaukkhuddaisānujānanā

133. atha khau bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ yāni mayā bhikkhūnaṃ pañ‍ñattāni sikkhāpadāni, tāni naisaṃ pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ anujānaiyyaṃ| sau naisaṃ bhavissati upausathakamma’’nti| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – idha mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ yāni mayā bhikkhūnaṃ pañ‍ñattāni sikkhāpadāni, tāni naisaṃ pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ anujānaiyyaṃ| sau naisaṃ bhavissati upausathakamma’nti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, uddisitabbaṃ| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

134. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa  pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau upausathaṃ karaiyya, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisaiyya| kiṃ saṅghassa pubbakic‍caṃ? pārisuddhiṃ āyasmantau āraucaitha| pātimaukkhaṃ uddisissāmi| taṃ sabbaiva santā sādhukaṃ suṇauma manasi karauma| yassa siyā āpatti, sau āvikaraiyya| asantiyā āpattiyā tuṇhī bhavitabbaṃ| tuṇhībhāvaina khau panāyasmantai  parisuddhāti vaidissāmi| yathā khau pana pac‍caikapuṭṭhassa vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti, aivamaivaṃ aivamaiva (ka) aivarūpāya parisāya yāvatatiyaṃ anussāvitaṃ hauti| yau pana bhikkhu yāvatatiyaṃ anussāviyamānai saramānau santiṃ āpattiṃ nāvikaraiyya, sampajānamusāvādassa hauti| sampajānamusāvādau khau panāyasmantau antarāyikau dhammau vuttau bhagavatā| tasmā, saramānaina bhikkhunā āpan‍naina visuddhāpaikkhaina santī āpatti āvikātabbā; āvikatā hissa phāsu hautī’’ti|

135. pātimaukkhanti ādimaitaṃ mukhamaitaṃ pamukhamaitaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ| taina vuc‍cati pātimaukkhanti| āyasmantauti piyavacanamaitaṃ garuvacanamaitaṃ sagāravasappatissādhivacanamaitaṃ āyasmantauti| uddisissāmīti ācikkhissāmi daisaissāmi pañ‍ñapaissāmi paṭṭhapaissāmi vivarissāmi vibhajissāmi  uttāniṃ karissāmi uttānī karissāmi (sī॰ syā॰) pakāsaissāmi| tanti pātimaukkhaṃ vuc‍cati| sabbaiva santāti yāvatikā tassā parisāya thairā ca navā ca majjhimā ca, aitai vuc‍canti sabbaiva santāti| sādhukaṃ suṇaumāti aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi katvā sabbacaitasā sabbaṃ caitasā (syā॰ ka॰) saman‍nāharāma| manasi karaumāti aikaggacittā avikkhittacittā avisāhaṭacittā nisāmaima| yassa siyā āpattīti thairassa vā navassa vā majjhimassa vā, pañ‍can‍naṃ vā āpattikkhandhānaṃ añ‍ñatarā āpatti, sattan‍naṃ vā āpattikkhandhānaṃ añ‍ñatarā āpatti| sau āvikaraiyyāti sau daisaiyya, sau vivaraiyya, sau uttāniṃ karaiyya, sau pakāsaiyya saṅghamajjhai vā gaṇamajjhai vā aikapuggalai vā| asantī nāma āpatti anajjhāpan‍nā vā hauti, āpaj‍jitvā vā vuṭṭhitā| tuṇhī bhavitabbanti adhivāsaitabbaṃ na byāharitabbaṃ| parisuddhāti vaidissāmīti jānissāmi dhāraissāmi| yathā khau pana pac‍caikapuṭṭhassa vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hautīti yathā aikaina aikau puṭṭhau byākaraiyya, aivamaiva tassā parisāya jānitabbaṃ maṃ pucchatīti| aivarūpā nāma parisā  bhikkhuparisā vuc‍cati| yāvatatiyaṃ anussāvitaṃ hautīti sakimpi anussāvitaṃ hauti, dutiyampi anussāvitaṃ hauti, tatiyampi anussāvitaṃ  hauti| saramānauti jānamānau sañ‍jānamānau| santī nāma āpatti ajjhāpan‍nā vā hauti, āpaj‍jitvā vā avuṭṭhitā| nāvikaraiyyāti na daisaiyya, na vivaraiyya, na uttāniṃ karaiyya, na pakāsaiyya saṅghamajjhai  vā gaṇamajjhai vā aikapuggalai vā| sampajānamusāvādassa hautīti| sampajānamusāvādai kiṃ hauti? duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti| antarāyikau dhammau vuttau bhagavatāti| kissa antarāyikau? paṭhamassa jhānassa adhigamāya antarāyikau, dutiyassa jhānassa adhigamāya antarāyikau, tatiyassa jhānassa adhigamāya antarāyikau, catutthassa jhānassa adhigamāya antarāyikau, jhānānaṃ vimaukkhānaṃ samādhīnaṃ samāpattīnaṃ naikkhammānaṃ nissaraṇānaṃ pavivaikānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ adhigamāya  antarāyikau| tasmāti taṅkāraṇā| saramānaināti jānamānaina sañ‍jānamānaina| visuddhāpaikkhaināti vuṭṭhātukāmaina visujjhitukāmaina| santī nāma āpatti ajjhāpan‍nā vā hauti, āpaj‍jitvā vā avuṭṭhitā| āvikātabbāti āvikātabbā saṅghamajjhai vā gaṇamajjhai vā aikapuggalai vā| āvikatā hissa phāsu hautīti| kissa phāsu hauti? paṭhamassa jhānassa adhigamāya phāsu hauti, dutiyassa jhānassa adhigamāya phāsu hauti, tatiyassa jhānassa  adhigamāya phāsu hauti, catutthassa jhānassa adhigamāya phāsu hauti, jhānānaṃ vimaukkhānaṃ samādhīnaṃ samāpattīnaṃ naikkhammānaṃ nissaraṇānaṃ pavivaikānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ adhigamāya phāsu hautīti|

136. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū – bhagavatā pātimaukkhuddaisau anuñ‍ñātauti – daivasikaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, daivasikaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbaṃ| yau uddisaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, upausathai pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū – bhagavatā upausathai pātimaukkhuddaisau anuñ‍ñātauti – pakkhassa tikkhattuṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisanti, cātuddasai pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, pakkhassa tikkhattuṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbaṃ| yau uddisaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sakiṃ pakkhassa cātuddasai vā pan‍narasai vā pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitunti|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū yathāparisāya pātimaukkhaṃ uddisanti sakāya sakāya parisāya| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, yathāparisāya  pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbaṃ sakāya sakāya parisāya| yau uddisaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, samaggānaṃ upausathakammanti|

atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘samaggānaṃ upausathakamma’nti| kittāvatā  nu khau sāmaggī hauti, yāvatā aikāvāsau, udāhu sabbā pathavī’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aittāvatā sāmaggī yāvatā aikāvāsauti|

70. mahākappinavatthu

137. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā mahākappinau rājagahai viharati maddakucchimhi migadāyai| atha khau āyasmatau mahākappinassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘gacchaiyyaṃ vāhaṃ upausathaṃ na vā gacchaiyyaṃ, gacchaiyyaṃ vāhaṃ saṅghakammaṃ na vā gacchaiyyaṃ, atha khvāhaṃ visuddhau paramāya visuddhiyā’’ti? atha khau bhagavā āyasmatau mahākappinassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – gijjhakūṭai pabbatai antarahitau maddakucchimhi migadāyai āyasmatau mahākappinassa sammukhai pāturahausi| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| āyasmāpi khau mahākappinau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ mahākappinaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘nanu tai, kappina, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – gacchaiyyaṃ vāhaṃ upausathaṃ na vā gacchaiyyaṃ, gacchaiyyaṃ vāhaṃ saṅghakammaṃ na vā gacchaiyyaṃ, atha khvāhaṃ visuddhau paramāya visuddhiyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘tumhai cai brāhmaṇā upausathaṃ na sak‍karissatha na  garukarissatha na garuṃ karissatha (ka॰) na mānaissatha na pūjaissatha, atha kau carahi upausathaṃ sak‍karissati garukarissati mānaissati pūjaissati? gaccha tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, upausathaṃ, mā nau agamāsi| gaccha tvaṃ saṅghakammaṃ, mā nau agamāsī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā mahākappinau  bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ mahākappinaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – maddakucchimhi migadāyai āyasmatau mahākappinassa sammukhai antarahitau gijjhakūṭai pabbatai pāturahausi|

71. sīmānujānanā

138. atha  khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘aittāvatā sāmaggī yāvatā aikāvāsau’ti, kittāvatā nu khau aikāvāsau hautī’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sīmaṃ samman‍nituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbā – paṭhamaṃ nimittā kittaitabbā – pabbatanimittaṃ, pāsāṇanimittaṃ, vananimittaṃ, rukkhanimittaṃ, magganimittaṃ, vammikanimittaṃ, nadīnimittaṃ, udakanimittaṃ| nimittai kittaitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

139. ‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yāvatā samantā nimittā kittitā| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau aitaihi nimittaihi sīmaṃ samman‍naiyya samānasaṃvāsaṃ aikupausathaṃ aikūpausathaṃ (ka॰)| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yāvatā samantā nimittā kittitā| saṅghau aitaihi nimittaihi sīmaṃ samman‍nati samānasaṃvāsaṃ aikupausathaṃ| yassāyasmatau khamati aitaihi nimittaihi sīmāya sammuti sammati (syā॰) samānasaṃvāsāya aikupausathāya, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya| sammatā sīmā saṅghaina aitaihi nimittaihi samānasaṃvāsā aikupausathā| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

140. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū – bhagavatā sīmāsammuti anuñ‍ñātāti – atimahatiyau sīmāyau samman‍nanti, catuyaujanikāpi pañ‍cayaujanikāpi chayaujanikāpi| bhikkhū upausathaṃ āgacchantā uddissamānaipi pātimaukkhai āgacchanti, uddiṭṭhamattaipi āgacchanti, antarāpi  parivasanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, atimahatī sīmā samman‍nitabbā, catuyaujanikā vā pañ‍cayaujanikā vā chayaujanikā vā| yau samman‍naiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tiyaujanaparamaṃ sīmaṃ samman‍nitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū nadīpārasīmaṃ nadīpāraṃ sīmaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) samman‍nanti| upausathaṃ āgacchantā bhikkhūpi vuyhanti, pattāpi vuyhanti, cīvarānipi vuyhanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, nadīpārasīmā samman‍nitabbā| yau samman‍naiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yatthassa dhuvanāvā vā dhuvasaitu vā, aivarūpaṃ nadīpārasīmaṃ samman‍nitunti|

72. upausathāgārakathā

141. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū anuparivaiṇiyaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ  uddisanti asaṅkaitaina| āgantukā bhikkhū na jānanti – ‘‘kattha vā aj‍jupausathau karīyissatī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, anuparivaiṇiyaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbaṃ asaṅkaitaina| yau uddisaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, upausathāgāraṃ samman‍nitvā upausathaṃ kātuṃ, yaṃ saṅghau ākaṅkhati vihāraṃ vā aḍḍhayaugaṃ vā pāsādaṃ vā hammiyaṃ vā  guhaṃ vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbaṃ| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ vihāraṃ upausathāgāraṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ vihāraṃ upausathāgāraṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa vihārassa upausathāgārassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya| sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau vihārau upausathāgāraṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai dvai upausathāgārāni sammatāni haunti| bhikkhū ubhayattha san‍nipatanti – ‘‘idha upausathau karīyissati, idha upausathau karīyissatī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, aikasmiṃ āvāsai dvai upausathāgārāni samman‍nitabbāni| yau samman‍naiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aikaṃ samūhanitvā samuhanitvā (ka॰) aikattha upausathaṃ kātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samūhantabbaṃ| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upausathāgāraṃ samūhanaiyya samuhanaiyya (ka॰)| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upausathāgāraṃ samūhanati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa upausathāgārassa samugghātau, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya| samūhataṃ saṅghaina itthan‍nāmaṃ upausathāgāraṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

73. upausathappamukhānujānanā

142. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai atikhuddakaṃ upausathāgāraṃ sammataṃ hauti, tadahupausathai mahābhikkhusaṅghau san‍nipatitau hauti| bhikkhū asammatāya bhūmiyā nisin‍nā pātimaukkhaṃ  assausuṃ| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘upausathāgāraṃ samman‍nitvā  upausathau kātabbau’ti, mayañ‍camhā asammatāya bhūmiyā nisin‍nau pātimaukkhaṃ assumhā, katau nu khau amhākaṃ upausathau, akatau  nu khau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| sammatāya vā, bhikkhavai, bhūmiyā nisin‍nā asammatāya vā yatau pātimaukkhaṃ suṇāti, katauvassa upausathau| taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau yāva mahantaṃ upausathappamukhaṃ upausathamukhaṃ (syā॰) ākaṅkhati, tāva mahantaṃ upausathappamukhaṃ samman‍natu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbaṃ| paṭhamaṃ nimittā kittaitabbā| nimittai kittaitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yāvatā samantā nimittā kittitā| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau aitaihi nimittaihi upausathappamukhaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yāvatā samantā nimittā kittitā| saṅghau aitaihi nimittaihi upausathappamukhaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau  khamati aitaihi nimittaihi upausathappamukhassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya| sammataṃ saṅghaina aitaihi nimittaihi upausathappamukhaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai tadahupausathai navakā bhikkhū paṭhamataraṃ san‍nipatitvā – ‘‘na tāva thairā āgacchantī’’ti – pak‍kamiṃsu| upausathau vikālai ahausi| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tadahupausathai thairaihi bhikkhūhi paṭhamataraṃ san‍nipatitunti|

taina  khau pana samayaina rājagahai sambahulā āvāsā samānasīmā haunti| tattha bhikkhū vivadanti – ‘‘amhākaṃ āvāsai upausathau karīyatu, amhākaṃ āvāsai upausathau karīyatū’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| idha pana, bhikkhavai, sambahulā āvāsā samānasīmā haunti| tattha bhikkhū vivadanti – ‘‘amhākaṃ āvāsai upausathau karīyatu, amhākaṃ āvāsai upausathau karīyatū’’ti| taihi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi sabbaihaiva aikajjhaṃ san‍nipatitvā upausathau kātabbau| yattha vā pana thairau bhikkhu viharati, tattha san‍nipatitvā upausathau kātabbau, na tvaiva vaggaina saṅghaina upausathau kātabbau| yau karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

74. avippavāsasīmānujānanā

143. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā mahākassapau andhakavindā rājagahaṃ upausathaṃ āgacchantau antarāmaggai nadiṃ tarantau manaṃ vūḷhau ahausi, cīvarānissa taina cīvarānissa (ka॰) al‍lāni| bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tai, āvusau, cīvarāni al‍lānī’’ti? ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, andhakavindā rājagahaṃ upausathaṃ āgacchantau antarāmaggai nadiṃ tarantau manamhi vūḷhau| taina mai cīvarāni al‍lānī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| yā sā, bhikkhavai, saṅghaina sīmā sammatā samānasaṃvāsā aikupausathā, saṅghau taṃ sīmaṃ ticīvaraina avippavāsaṃ samman‍natu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbā| byattaina  bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yā sā saṅghaina sīmā sammatā samānasaṃvāsā aikupausathā, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ saṅghau taṃ sīmaṃ ticīvaraina avippavāsaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yā sā saṅghaina sīmā sammatā samānasaṃvāsā aikupausathā, saṅghau taṃ sīmaṃ ticīvaraina avippavāsaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati aitissā sīmāya ticīvaraina avippavāsāya avippavāsassa (syā॰) sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya| sammatā sā sīmā saṅghaina ticīvaraina avippavāsā avippavāsau (syā॰)| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū bhagavatā ticīvaraina avippavāsasammuti anuñ‍ñātāti antaragharai cīvarāni nikkhipanti| tāni cīvarāni nassantipi ḍayhantipi undūraihipi khaj‍janti| bhikkhū duc‍cauḷā haunti lūkhacīvarā| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kissa tumhai, āvusau, duc‍cauḷā lūkhacīvarā’’ti? ‘‘idha mayaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā ticīvaraina avippavāsasammuti anuñ‍ñātāti antaragharai cīvarāni nikkhipimhā| tāni cīvarāni naṭṭhānipi daḍḍhānipi, undūraihipi khāyitāni, taina mayaṃ duc‍cauḷā lūkhacīvarā’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| yā sā, bhikkhavai, saṅghaina sīmā sammatā samānasaṃvāsā aikupausathā, saṅghau taṃ sīmaṃ ticīvaraina avippavāsaṃ samman‍natu, ṭhapaitvā gāmañ‍ca gāmūpacārañ‍ca| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

144. ‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yā sā saṅghaina sīmā sammatā samānasaṃvāsā aikupausathā yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau taṃ sīmaṃ ticīvaraina avippavāsaṃ samman‍naiyya, ṭhapaitvā gāmañ‍ca  gāmūpacārañ‍ca| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yā sā saṅghaina sīmā sammatā samānasaṃvāsā aikupausathā, saṅghau taṃ sīmaṃ ticīvaraina avippavāsaṃ samman‍nati, ṭhapaitvā gāmañ‍ca gāmūpacārañ‍ca| yassāyasmatau khamati aitissā sīmāya ticīvaraina avippavāsāya avippavāsassa (syā॰) sammuti, ṭhapaitvā gāmañ‍ca gāmūpacārañ‍ca, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya| sammatā sā  sīmā saṅghaina ticīvaraina avippavāsā avippavāsau (syā॰), ṭhapaitvā gāmañ‍ca gāmūpacārañ‍ca| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

75. sīmāsamūhanana

‘‘sīmaṃ, bhikkhavai, samman‍nantaina paṭhamaṃ samānasaṃvāsasīmā samānasaṃvāsā sīmā (syā॰) samman‍nitabbā, pacchā ticīvaraina avippavāsau samman‍nitabbau| sīmaṃ, bhikkhavai, samūhanantaina paṭhamaṃ ticīvaraina avippavāsau samūhantabbau, pacchā samānasaṃvāsasīmā samūhantabbā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, ticīvaraina avippavāsau samūhantabbau| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

145. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yau sau saṅghaina ticīvaraina avippavāsau sammatau, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau taṃ ticīvaraina avippavāsaṃ samūhanaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yau sau saṅghaina ticīvaraina avippavāsau sammatau, saṅghau taṃ ticīvaraina avippavāsaṃ samūhanati| yassāyasmatau khamati aitassa ticīvaraina avippavāsassa samugghātau, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya| samūhatau sau saṅghaina ticīvaraina avippavāsau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, sīmā samānasaṃvāsā sīmā (syā॰)| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

146. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yā sā saṅghaina sīmā sammatā samānasaṃvāsā aikupausathā, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau taṃ sīmaṃ samūhanaiyya samānasaṃvāsaṃ aikupausathaṃ| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yā sā saṅghaina sīmā sammatā samānasaṃvāsā aikupausathā, saṅghau taṃ  sīmaṃ samūhanati samānasaṃvāsaṃ aikupausathaṃ| yassāyasmatau khamati aitissā sīmāya samānasaṃvāsāya aikupausathāya samugghātau, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya| samūhatā sā sīmā saṅghaina samānasaṃvāsā aikupausathā| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

76. gāmasīmādi

147. asammatāya, bhikkhavai, sīmāya aṭṭhapitāya, yaṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharati, yā tassa vā gāmassa gāmasīmā, nigamassa  vā nigamasīmā, ayaṃ tattha samānasaṃvāsā  aikupausathā| agāmakai cai, bhikkhavai, arañ‍ñai samantā sattabbhantarā, ayaṃ tattha samānasaṃvāsā aikupausathā| sabbā, bhikkhavai, nadī asīmā; sabbau samuddau asīmau; sabbau jātassarau asīmau| nadiyā vā, bhikkhavai, samuddai vā jātassarai vā yaṃ majjhimassa purisassa samantā udakukkhaipā, ayaṃ tattha samānasaṃvāsā aikupausathāti|

148. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sīmāya sīmaṃ sambhindanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| yaisaṃ, bhikkhavai, sīmā paṭhamaṃ sammatā taisaṃ taṃ kammaṃ dhammikaṃ akuppaṃ ṭhānārahaṃ| yaisaṃ, bhikkhavai, sīmā pacchā sammatā taisaṃ taṃ kammaṃ adhammikaṃ kuppaṃ aṭṭhānārahaṃ| na, bhikkhavai, sīmāya sīmā sambhinditabbā| yau sambhindaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sīmāya sīmaṃ ajjhauttharanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| yaisaṃ, bhikkhavai, sīmā paṭhamaṃ sammatā taisaṃ taṃ kammaṃ dhammikaṃ akuppaṃ ṭhānārahaṃ| yaisaṃ, bhikkhavai, sīmā pacchā sammatā taisaṃ taṃ kammaṃ adhammikaṃ kuppaṃ aṭṭhānārahaṃ| na, bhikkhavai, sīmāya sīmā ajjhauttharitabbā| yau ajjhauttharaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sīmaṃ samman‍nantaina sīmantarikaṃ ṭhapaitvā sīmaṃ samman‍nitunti|

77. upausathabhaidādi

149. atha  khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kati nu khau upausathā’’ti? bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| dvaimai, bhikkhavai, upausathā – cātuddasikau ca pan‍narasikau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, dvai upausathāti|

atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kati nu khau upausathakammānī’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, upausathakammāni  – adhammaina vaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ, dhammaina samaggaṃ upausathakammanti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yadidaṃ adhammaina vaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ, na, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaṃ upausathakammaṃ, kātabbaṃ| na ca mayā aivarūpaṃ upausathakammaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ| tatra, bhikkhavai, yadidaṃ adhammaina samaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ, na, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaṃ  upausathakammaṃ kātabbaṃ| na ca mayā aivarūpaṃ upausathakammaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ| tatra, bhikkhavai, yadidaṃ dhammaina vaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ, na, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaṃ upausathakammaṃ kātabbaṃ| na ca mayā aivarūpaṃ upausathakammaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ| tatra, bhikkhavai, yadidaṃ dhammaina samaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ, aivarūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, upausathakammaṃ kātabbaṃ, aivarūpañ‍ca mayā upausathakammaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaṃ upausathakammaṃ karissāma yadidaṃ dhammaina samagganti – aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbanti|

78. saṃkhittaina pātimaukkhuddaisādi

150. atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kati nu khau pātimaukkhuddaisā’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, pātimaukkhuddaisā – nidānaṃ uddisitvā avasaisaṃ sutaina sāvaitabbaṃ| ayaṃ  paṭhamau pātimaukkhuddaisau| nidānaṃ uddisitvā cattāri pārājikāni uddisitvā avasaisaṃ sutaina sāvaitabbaṃ| ayaṃ dutiyau pātimaukkhuddaisau| nidānaṃ uddisitvā cattāri pārājikāni uddisitvā tairasa saṅghādisaisai uddisitvā avasaisaṃ sutaina sāvaitabbaṃ| ayaṃ tatiyau pātimaukkhuddaisau| nidānaṃ uddisitvā cattāri pārājikāni uddisitvā tairasa saṅghādisaisai uddisitvā dvai aniyatai uddisitvā avasaisaṃ sutaina sāvaitabbaṃ| ayaṃ catutthau pātimaukkhuddaisau| vitthārainaiva pañ‍camau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca pātimaukkhuddaisāti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū – bhagavatā saṃkhittaina pātimaukkhuddaisau anuñ‍ñātauti – sabbakālaṃ saṃkhittaina pātimaukkhaṃ uddisanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, saṃkhittaina pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbaṃ| yau uddisaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina  khau pana samayaina kausalaisu janapadai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai tadahupausathai savarabhayaṃ saṃcarabhayaṃ (syā॰) ahausi| bhikkhū nāsakkhiṃsu vitthāraina pātimaukkhaṃ uddisituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sati antarāyai saṃkhittaina pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū  asatipi antarāyai saṃkhittaina pātimaukkhaṃ uddisanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, asati antarāyai saṃkhittaina pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbaṃ| yau uddisaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sati antarāyai saṃkhittaina pātimaukkhaṃ uddisituṃ| tatrimai antarāyā – rājantarāyau, caurantarāyau, agyantarāyau, udakantarāyau, manussantarāyau, amanussantarāyau, vāḷantarāyau, sarīsapantarāyau, jīvitantarāyau, brahmacariyantarāyauti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaisu antarāyaisu saṃkhittaina pātimaukkhaṃ uddisituṃ, asati antarāyai vitthāraināti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū saṅghamajjhai anajjhiṭṭhā dhammaṃ bhāsanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai anajjhiṭṭhaina dhammau bhāsitabbau| yau bhāsaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, thairaina bhikkhunā sāmaṃ vā dhammaṃ bhāsituṃ paraṃ vā ajjhaisitunti|

79. vinayapucchanakathā

151. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū saṅghamajjhai asammatā vinayaṃ pucchanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai asammataina vinayau pucchitabbau| yau pucchaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai sammataina vinayaṃ pucchituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau – attanā vā attanāva (syā॰) attānaṃ samman‍nitabbaṃ, paraina vā parau samman‍nitabbau| kathañ‍ca  attanāva attānaṃ samman‍nitabbaṃ? byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ itthan‍nāmaṃ vinayaṃ pucchaiyya’’nti| aivaṃ attanāva attānaṃ samman‍nitabbaṃ|

kathañ‍ca paraina parau samman‍nitabbau? byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmaṃ vinayaṃ pucchaiyyā’’ti| aivaṃ paraina parau samman‍nitabbauti|

taina khau pana samayaina paisalā bhikkhū saṅghamajjhai sammatā vinayaṃ pucchanti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū labhanti āghātaṃ, labhanti appac‍cayaṃ, vadhaina taj‍jainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai sammatainapi parisaṃ aulaukaitvā puggalaṃ tulayitvā vinayaṃ pucchitunti|

80. vinayavissaj‍janakathā

152. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū saṅghamajjhai asammatā vinayaṃ vissaj‍jainti vissaj‍janti (ka॰)| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai asammataina vinayau vissaj‍jaitabbau| yau vissaj‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai sammataina vinayaṃ vissaj‍jaituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbaṃ| attanā vā attanāva (syā॰) attānaṃ samman‍nitabbaṃ, paraina vā parau samman‍nitabbau| kathañ‍ca  attanāva attānaṃ samman‍nitabbaṃ? byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ  itthan‍nāmaina vinayaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiyya’’nti| aivaṃ attanāva attānaṃ samman‍nitabbaṃ|

kathañ‍ca paraina parau samman‍nitabbau? byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, itthan‍nāmau itthan‍nāmaina vinayaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiyyā’’ti| aivaṃ paraina parau samman‍nitabbauti|

taina  khau pana samayaina paisalā bhikkhū saṅghamajjhai sammatā vinayaṃ vissaj‍jainti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū labhanti āghātaṃ, labhanti appac‍cayaṃ, vadhaina taj‍jainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai sammatainapi parisaṃ aulaukaitvā puggalaṃ tulayitvā vinayaṃ vissaj‍jaitunti|

81. caudanākathā

153. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū anaukāsakataṃ bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā caudainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, anaukāsakatau bhikkhu āpattiyā caudaitabbau| yau caudaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā āpattiyā caudaituṃ – karautu āyasmā aukāsaṃ, ahaṃ taṃ vattukāmauti|

taina khau pana samayaina paisalā bhikkhū chabbaggiyai bhikkhū aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā āpattiyā caudainti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū labhanti āghātaṃ, labhanti appac‍cayaṃ, vadhaina taj‍jainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kataipi aukāsai puggalaṃ tulayitvā āpattiyā caudaitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā  bhikkhū – puramhākaṃ paisalā bhikkhū aukāsaṃ kārāpaintīti – paṭikac‍caiva suddhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anāpattikānaṃ avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai aukāsaṃ kārāpainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, suddhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anāpattikānaṃ avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai aukāsau kārāpaitabbau| yau kārāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, puggalaṃ tulayitvā aukāsaṃ kātu kārāpaituṃ (syā॰) nti|

82. adhammakammapaṭik‍kausanādi

154. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū saṅghamajjhai adhammakammaṃ karaunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, adhammakammaṃ kātabbaṃ| yau karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti| karauntiyaiva adhammakammaṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, adhammakammai kayiramānai paṭik‍kausitunti|

taina  khau pana samayaina paisalā bhikkhū chabbaggiyaihi bhikkhūhi adhammakammai kayiramānai paṭik‍kausanti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū labhanti āghātaṃ, labhanti appac‍cayaṃ, vadhaina taj‍jainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhimpi āvikātunti| taisaṃyaiva santikai diṭṭhiṃ āvikaraunti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū labhanti āghātaṃ, labhanti appac‍cayaṃ, vadhaina taj‍jainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, catūhi pañ‍cahi paṭik‍kausituṃ, dvīhi tīhi diṭṭhiṃ āvikātuṃ, aikaina adhiṭṭhātuṃ – ‘na maitaṃ khamatī’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū saṅghamajjhai pātimaukkhaṃ  uddisamānā sañ‍cic‍ca na sāvainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, pātimaukkhuddaisakaina sañ‍cic‍ca na sāvaitabbaṃ| yau na sāvaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī saṅghassa pātimaukkhuddaisakau hauti kākassarakau| atha khau āyasmatau udāyissa aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘pātimaukkhuddaisakaina sāvaitabba’nti, ahañ‍camhi kākassarakau, kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pātimaukkhuddaisakaina vāyamituṃ – ‘kathaṃ sāvaiyya’nti| vāyamantassa anāpattīti|

taina khau pana samayaina daivadattau sagahaṭṭhāya parisāya pātimaukkhaṃ uddisati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, sagahaṭṭhāya parisāya pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbaṃ| yau uddisaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū saṅghamajjhai anajjhiṭṭhā pātimaukkhaṃ uddisanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai anajjhiṭṭhaina pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbaṃ| yau uddisaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, thairādhikaṃ thairādhaiyyaṃ (aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pāṭhantaraṃ) pātimaukkhanti|

añ‍ñatitthiyabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau paṭhamau aikādasamau (ka॰)|

83. pātimaukkhuddaisakaajjhaisanādi

155. atha  khau bhagavā rājagahai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina caudanāvatthu taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina caudanāvatthu tadavasari| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai sambahulā bhikkhū viharanti| tattha thairau bhikkhu bālau hauti abyattau| sau na jānāti upausathaṃ vā upausathakammaṃ vā, pātimaukkhaṃ vā pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ vā| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘thairādhikaṃ pātimaukkha’nti, ayañ‍ca amhākaṃ thairau bālau abyattau, na jānāti upausathaṃ vā upausathakammaṃ vā, pātimaukkhaṃ vā pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ vā| kathaṃ nu khau amhaihi paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yau tattha bhikkhu byattau paṭibalau tassādhaiyyaṃ pātimaukkhanti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai tadahupausathai sambahulā bhikkhū viharanti bālā abyattā| tai na jānanti upausathaṃ vā upausathakammaṃ vā, pātimaukkhaṃ vā pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ vā| tai thairaṃ ajjhaisiṃsu – ‘‘uddisatu, bhantai, thairau pātimaukkha’’nti| sau aivamāha – ‘‘na mai, āvusau, vattatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ thairaṃ ajjhaisiṃsu – ‘‘uddisatu, bhantai, thairau pātimaukkha’’nti| saupi aivamāha – ‘‘na mai, āvusau, vattatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ thairaṃ ajjhaisiṃsu – ‘‘uddisatu, bhantai, thairau pātimaukkha’’nti| saupi aivamāha – ‘‘na mai, āvusau, vattatī’’ti| aitainaiva upāyaina yāva saṅghanavakaṃ ajjhaisiṃsu – ‘‘uddisatu āyasmā pātimaukkha’’nti| saupi aivamāha – ‘‘na mai, bhantai, vattatī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai tadahupausathai sambahulā bhikkhū viharanti bālā abyattā| tai na jānanti upausathaṃ vā upausathakammaṃ vā, pātimaukkhaṃ vā pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ vā| tai thairaṃ ajjhaisanti – ‘‘uddisatu, bhantai, thairau pātimaukkha’’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘na mai, āvusau, vattatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ thairaṃ ajjhaisanti – ‘‘uddisatu, bhantai, thairau pātimaukkha’’nti| saupi aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘na mai, āvusau, vattatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ  thairaṃ ajjhaisanti – ‘‘uddisatu, bhantai, thairau pātimaukkha’’nti| saupi aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘na mai, āvusau, vattatī’’ti| aitainaiva upāyaina yāva saṅghanavakaṃ ajjhaisanti – ‘‘uddisatu āyasmā pātimaukkha’’nti| saupi aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘na mai, bhantai, vattatī’’ti| taihi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi  aikau bhikkhu sāmantā āvāsā saj‍jukaṃ pāhaitabbau – gacchāvusau, saṃkhittaina vā vitthāraina vā pātimaukkhaṃ pariyāpuṇitvāna āgacchāhīti|

atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau pāhaitabbau’’ti? bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, thairaina bhikkhunā navaṃ bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaitunti| thairaina āṇattā navā bhikkhū na gacchanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, thairaina  āṇattaina agilānaina na gantabbaṃ| yau na gacchaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

84. pakkhagaṇanādiuggahaṇānujānanā

156. atha khau bhagavā caudanāvatthusmiṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā punadaiva rājagahaṃ pac‍cāgañchi|

taina khau pana samayaina manussā bhikkhū piṇḍāya carantai pucchanti – ‘‘katimī, bhantai, pakkhassā’’ti? bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na khau mayaṃ, āvusau, jānāmā’’ti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘pakkhagaṇanamattamampimai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā na jānanti, kiṃ panimai añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍ci kalyāṇaṃ jānissantī’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pakkhagaṇanaṃ uggahaitunti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau pakkhagaṇanā uggahaitabbā’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sabbaihaiva pakkhagaṇanaṃ uggahaitunti|

157. taina khau pana samayaina manussā bhikkhū piṇḍāya carantai pucchanti – ‘‘kīvatikā, bhantai, bhikkhū’’ti? bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na khau mayaṃ, āvusau, jānāmā’’ti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘añ‍ñamañ‍ñampimai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā na jānanti, kiṃ panimai añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍ci  kalyāṇaṃ jānissantī’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū gaṇaitunti|

atha  khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kadā nu khau bhikkhū gaṇaitabbā’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tadahupausathai nāmaggaina nāmamattaina (syā॰), gaṇamaggaina (ka॰) gaṇaituṃ, salākaṃ vā gāhaitunti|

158. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū ajānantā aj‍jupausathauti dūraṃ gāmaṃ piṇḍāya caranti| tai uddissamānaipi pātimaukkhai āgacchanti, uddiṭṭhamattaipi āgacchanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āraucaituṃ ‘aj‍jupausathau’ti|

atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau āraucaitabbau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, thairaina bhikkhunā kālavatau āraucaitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau thairau kālavatau nassarati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhattakālaipi āraucaitunti|

bhattakālaipi nassarati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yaṃ kālaṃ sarati, taṃ kālaṃ āraucaitunti|

85. pubbakaraṇānujānanā

159. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai upausathāgāraṃ uk‍lāpaṃ hauti| āgantukā bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi  nāma āvāsikā bhikkhū upausathāgāraṃ na sammaj‍jissantī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, upausathāgāraṃ sammaj‍jitunti|

atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau upausathāgāraṃ sammaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, thairaina bhikkhunā navaṃ bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaitunti|

thairaina āṇattā navā bhikkhū na sammaj‍janti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, thairaina āṇattaina agilānaina na sammaj‍jitabbaṃ| yau na sammaj‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

160. taina  khau pana samayaina upausathāgārai āsanaṃ apañ‍ñattaṃ hauti| bhikkhū chamāyaṃ  nisīdanti, gattānipi cīvarānipi paṃsukitāni haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, upausathāgārai āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitunti|

atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau upausathāgārai āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, thairaina bhikkhunā navaṃ bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaitunti|

thairaina āṇattā navā bhikkhū na pañ‍ñapainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, thairaina āṇattaina agilānaina na pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ| yau na pañ‍ñapaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

161. taina khau pana samayaina upausathāgārai padīpau na hauti| bhikkhū andhakārai kāyampi cīvarampi ak‍kamanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, upausathāgārai padīpaṃ  kātunti|

atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau upausathāgārai padīpau kātabbau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, thairaina bhikkhunā navaṃ bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaitunti|

thairaina āṇattā navā bhikkhū na padīpainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, thairaina āṇattaina agilānaina na padīpaitabbau| yau na padīpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

162. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai āvāsikā bhikkhū naiva pānīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpainti, na paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpainti| āgantukā bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āvāsikā bhikkhū naiva pānīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpaissanti, na paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpaissantī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pānīyaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitunti|

atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau pānīyaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, thairaina bhikkhunā navaṃ bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaitunti|

thairaina  āṇattā navā bhikkhū na upaṭṭhāpainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, thairaina āṇattaina agilānaina na upaṭṭhāpaitabbaṃ| yau na upaṭṭhāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

86. disaṃgamikādivatthu

163. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū bālā abyattā disaṃgamikā  ācariyupajjhāyai na āpucchiṃsu na āpucchiṃsu (ka॰)| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, sambahulā bhikkhū bālā abyattā disaṃgamikā ācariyupajjhāyai na āpucchanti na āpucchanti (ka॰)| tai taihi (ka॰), bhikkhavai, ācariyupajjhāyaihi pucchitabbā – ‘‘kahaṃ gamissatha, kaina saddhiṃ gamissathā’’ti? tai cai, bhikkhavai, bālā abyattā añ‍ñai bālai abyattai apadisaiyyuṃ, na, bhikkhavai, ācariyupajjhāyaihi anujānitabbā| anujānaiyyuṃ cai, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tai ca, bhikkhavai, bālā abyattā ananuñ‍ñātā ācariyupajjhāyaihi gacchaiyyuṃ cai, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai sambahulā bhikkhū viharanti bālā abyattā| tai na jānanti upausathaṃ vā upausathakammaṃ vā, pātimaukkhaṃ vā pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ vā| tattha añ‍ñau bhikkhu āgacchati bahussutau āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau paṇḍitau byattau maidhāvī laj‍jī kuk‍kuc‍cakau sikkhākāmau| taihi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi sau bhikkhu saṅgahaitabbau anuggahaitabbau upalāpaitabbau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau cuṇṇaina mattikāya dantakaṭṭhaina mukhaudakaina| nau cai saṅgaṇhaiyyuṃ anuggaṇhaiyyuṃ upalāpaiyyuṃ upaṭṭhāpaiyyuṃ cuṇṇaina mattikāya dantakaṭṭhaina mukhaudakaina, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai tadahupausathai sambahulā bhikkhū  viharanti bālā abyattā| tai na jānanti upausathaṃ vā upausathakammaṃ vā, pātimaukkhaṃ vā pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ vā| taihi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi aikau bhikkhu sāmantā āvāsā saj‍jukaṃ pāhaitabbau  – ‘‘gacchāvusau, saṃkhittaina vā vitthāraina vā pātimaukkhaṃ pariyāpuṇitvā āgacchā’’ti| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, taihi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi  sabbaihaiva yattha jānanti upausathaṃ vā upausathakammaṃ vā pātimaukkhaṃ vā pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ vā, sau āvāsau gantabbau| nau cai gacchaiyyuṃ, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai sambahulā bhikkhū vassaṃ vasanti bālā abyattā| tai na jānanti upausathaṃ vā upausathakammaṃ vā pātimaukkhaṃ vā pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ vā| taihi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi aikau bhikkhu sāmantā āvāsā saj‍jukaṃ pāhaitabbau – ‘‘gacchāvusau, saṃkhittaina vā vitthāraina vā pātimaukkhaṃ pariyāpuṇitvā āgacchā’’ti| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, aikau bhikkhu sattāhakālikaṃ pāhaitabbau – ‘‘gacchāvusau, saṃkhittaina vā vitthāraina vā pātimaukkhaṃ pariyāpuṇitvā āgacchā’’ti| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, na, bhikkhavai, taihi bhikkhūhi tasmiṃ āvāsai vassaṃ vasitabbaṃ| vasaiyyuṃ cai, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

87. pārisuddhidānakathā

164. atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘san‍nipatatha, bhikkhavai, saṅghau upausathaṃ karissatī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘atthi, bhantai, bhikkhu gilānau, sau anāgatau’’ti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānaina bhikkhunā pārisuddhiṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbā – taina gilānaina bhikkhunā aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘pārisuddhiṃ dammi, pārisuddhiṃ mai hara, pārisuddhiṃ mai āraucaihī’’ti| kāyaina viñ‍ñāpaiti, vācāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, kāyaina vācāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, din‍nā hauti pārisuddhi| na kāyaina viñ‍ñāpaiti, na vācāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, na kāyaina vācāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, na din‍nā hauti pārisuddhi| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, sau, bhikkhavai, gilānau bhikkhu mañ‍caina vā pīṭhaina vā saṅghamajjhai ānaitvā upausathau kātabbau| sacai, bhikkhavai, gilānupaṭṭhākānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘sacai khau mayaṃ gilānaṃ ṭhānā cāvaissāma, ābādhau vā abhivaḍḍhissati kālaṃkiriyā vā bhavissatī’’ti, na, bhikkhavai, gilānau bhikkhu ṭhānā cāvaitabbau| saṅghaina tattha gantvā upausathau  kātabbau| na tvaiva vaggaina saṅghaina upausathau kātabbau| karaiyya cai, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

pārisuddhihārakau  cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nāya pārisuddhiyā tatthaiva pak‍kamati, añ‍ñassa dātabbā pārisuddhi| pārisuddhihārakau cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nāya pārisuddhiyā tatthaiva vibbhamati,…pai॰… kālaṃ karauti – sāmaṇairau paṭijānāti  – sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakau paṭijānāti – antimavatthuṃ ajjhāpan‍nakau  paṭijānāti – ummattakau paṭijānāti – khittacittau paṭijānāti – vaidanāṭṭau paṭijānāti – āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti – āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti – pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti – paṇḍakau paṭijānāti – thaiyyasaṃvāsakau paṭijānāti – titthiyapak‍kantakau paṭijānāti – tiracchānagatau paṭijānāti – mātughātakau paṭijānāti – pitughātakau paṭijānāti – arahantaghātakau paṭijānāti – bhikkhunidūsakau paṭijānāti – saṅghabhaidakau paṭijānāti – lauhituppādakau paṭijānāti – ubhataubyañ‍janakau paṭijānāti, añ‍ñassa dātabbā pārisuddhi|

pārisuddhihārakau cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nāya pārisuddhiyā antarāmaggai pak‍kamati, anāhaṭā hauti pārisuddhi| pārisuddhihārakau cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nāya pārisuddhiyā antarāmaggai vibbhamati,…pai॰… ubhataubyañ‍janakau paṭijānāti, anāhaṭā hauti pārisuddhi|

pārisuddhihārakau cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nāya pārisuddhiyā saṅghappattau pak‍kamati, āhaṭā hauti pārisuddhi| pārisuddhihārakau cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nāya pārisuddhiyā saṅghappattau vibbhamati,…pai॰… ubhataubyañ‍janakau paṭijānāti, āhaṭā hauti pārisuddhi|

pārisuddhihārakau cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nāya pārisuddhiyā saṅghappattau suttau na āraucaiti, pamattau na āraucaiti, samāpan‍nau na āraucaiti, āhaṭā hauti pārisuddhi| pārisuddhihārakassa anāpatti|

pārisuddhihārakau cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nāya pārisuddhiyā saṅghappattau sañ‍cic‍ca na āraucaiti, āhaṭā hauti pārisuddhi| pārisuddhihārakassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

88. chandadānakathā

165. atha  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘san‍nipatatha, bhikkhavai, saṅghau kammaṃ karissatī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi, bhantai, bhikkhu gilānau, sau anāgatau’’ti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānaina bhikkhunā chandaṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbau| taina gilānaina bhikkhunā aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘chandaṃ dammi, chandaṃ mai hara, chandaṃ mai āraucaihī’’ti| kāyaina viñ‍ñāpaiti, vācāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, kāyaina vācāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, din‍nau hauti chandau| na kāyaina viñ‍ñāpaiti, na vācāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, na kāyaina vācāya viñ‍ñāpaiti, na din‍nau hauti chandau| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, sau, bhikkhavai, gilānau bhikkhu mañ‍caina vā pīṭhaina vā saṅghamajjhai ānaitvā kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| sacai, bhikkhavai, gilānupaṭṭhākānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘sacai khau mayaṃ gilānaṃ ṭhānā cāvaissāma, ābādhau vā abhivaḍḍhissati kālaṃkiriyā vā bhavissatī’’ti, na, bhikkhavai, gilānau bhikkhu ṭhānā cāvaitabbau| saṅghaina tattha gantvā kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| na tvaiva vaggaina saṅghaina kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| karaiyya cai, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

chandahārakau  cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nai chandai tatthaiva pak‍kamati, añ‍ñassa dātabbau chandau| chandahārakau cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nai chandai tatthaiva vibbhamati…pai॰… kālaṃkarauti – sāmaṇairau paṭijānāti – sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakau paṭijānāti – antimavatthuṃ ajjhāpan‍nakau paṭijānāti – ummattakau paṭijānāti – khittacittau paṭijānāti – vaidanāṭṭau paṭijānāti – āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti – āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti – pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti – paṇḍakau paṭijānāti – thaiyyasaṃvāsakau paṭijānāti – titthiyapak‍kantakau paṭijānāti – tiracchānagatau paṭijānāti – mātughātakau paṭijānāti – pitughātakau paṭijānāti – arahantaghātakau paṭijānāti – bhikkhunidūsakau paṭijānāti – saṅghabhaidakau paṭijānāti – lauhituppādakau paṭijānāti – ubhataubyañ‍janakau paṭijānāti, añ‍ñassa dātabbau chandau|

chandahārakau  cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nai chandai antarāmaggai pak‍kamati, anāhaṭau hauti chandau| chandahārakau cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nai chandai antarāmaggai vibbhamati…pai॰… ubhataubyañ‍janakau paṭijānāti, anāhaṭau hauti chandau|

chandahārakau cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nai chandai saṅghappattau pak‍kamati, āhaṭau hauti chandau| chandahārakau  cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nai chandai saṅghappattau vibbhamati…pai॰… ubhataubyañ‍janakau paṭijānāti, āhaṭau hauti chandau|

chandahārakau cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nai  chandai saṅghappattau suttau na āraucaiti, pamattau na āraucaiti, samāpan‍nau na āraucaiti, āhaṭau hauti chandau| chandahārakassa anāpatti|

chandahārakau cai, bhikkhavai, din‍nai chandai saṅghappattau sañ‍cic‍ca na āraucaiti, āhaṭau hauti chandau| chandahārakassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tadahupausathai pārisuddhiṃ daintaina chandampi dātuṃ, santi saṅghassa karaṇīyanti|

89. ñātakādiggahaṇakathā

166. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ tadahupausathai ñātakā gaṇhiṃsuṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ tadahupausathai ñātakā gaṇhanti| tai ñātakā bhikkhūhi aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘‘iṅgha, tumhai āyasmantau imaṃ bhikkhuṃ muhuttaṃ muñ‍catha, yāvāyaṃ bhikkhu upausathaṃ karautī’’ti| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, tai ñātakā bhikkhūhi aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘‘iṅgha, tumhai āyasmantau muhuttaṃ aikamantaṃ hautha, yāvāyaṃ bhikkhu pārisuddhiṃ daitī’’ti| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, tai ñātakā bhikkhūhi aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘‘iṅgha, tumhai āyasmantau imaṃ bhikkhuṃ muhuttaṃ nissīmaṃ naitha, yāva saṅghau upausathaṃ karautī’’ti| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, na tvaiva vaggaina saṅghaina upausathau kātabbau| karaiyya cai, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ tadahupausathai rājānau gaṇhanti,…pai॰… caurā gaṇhanti – dhuttā  gaṇhanti – bhikkhupac‍catthikā gaṇhanti, tai bhikkhupac‍catthikā bhikkhūhi aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘‘iṅgha, tumhai āyasmantau imaṃ bhikkhuṃ muhuttaṃ muñ‍catha, yāvāyaṃ bhikkhu upausathaṃ karautī’’ti| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, tai bhikkhupac‍catthikā bhikkhūhi aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘‘iṅgha, tumhai āyasmantau muhuttaṃ aikamantaṃ hautha, yāvāyaṃ bhikkhu pārisuddhiṃ daitī’’ti| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, tai bhikkhupac‍catthikā  bhikkhūhi aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘‘iṅgha, tumhai āyasmantau imaṃ bhikkhuṃ muhuttaṃ nissīmaṃ naitha, yāva saṅghau upausathaṃ karautī’’ti| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, na tvaiva vaggaina saṅghaina upausathau kātabbau| karaiyya cai, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

90. ummattakasammuti

167. atha  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘san‍nipatatha, bhikkhavai, atthi saṅghassa karaṇīya’’nti| aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi, bhantai, gaggau nāma bhikkhu ummattakau, sau anāgatau’’ti|

‘‘dvaimai, bhikkhavai, ummattakā – atthi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ummattakau saratipi upausathaṃ napi sarati, saratipi saṅghakammaṃ napi sarati, atthi naiva sarati; āgacchatipi upausathaṃ napi āgacchati, āgacchatipi saṅghakammaṃ napi āgacchati, atthi naiva āgacchati| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ ummattakau saratipi upausathaṃ napi sarati, saratipi saṅghakammaṃ napi sarati, āgacchatipi upausathaṃ napi āgacchati, āgacchatipi saṅghakammaṃ  napi āgacchati, anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aivarūpassa ummattakassa ummattakasammuttiṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| gaggau bhikkhu ummattakau – saratipi upausathaṃ napi sarati, saratipi saṅghakammaṃ napi sarati, āgacchatipi upausathaṃ napi āgacchati, āgacchatipi saṅghakammaṃ napi āgacchati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau gaggassa bhikkhunau ummattakassa ummattakasammutiṃ  dadaiyya| saraiyya vā gaggau bhikkhu upausathaṃ na vā saraiyya, saraiyya vā saṅghakammaṃ na vā saraiyya, āgacchaiyya vā upausathaṃ na vā āgacchaiyya, āgacchaiyya vā saṅghakammaṃ na vā āgacchaiyya, saṅghau saha vā gaggaina vinā vā gaggaina upausathaṃ karaiyya, saṅghakammaṃ karaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| gaggau bhikkhu ummattakau – saratipi upausathaṃ napi sarati, saratipi saṅghakammaṃ napi sarati, āgacchatipi upausathaṃ napi āgacchati, āgacchatipi saṅghakammaṃ napi āgacchati| saṅghau gaggassa bhikkhunau ummattakassa ummattakasammutiṃ  daiti| saraiyya vā gaggau bhikkhu upausathaṃ na vā saraiyya, saraiyya vā saṅghakammaṃ na vā saraiyyaṃ, āgacchaiyya vā upausathaṃ na vā āgacchaiyya, āgacchaiyya vā saṅghakammaṃ na vā āgacchaiyya, saṅghau saha vā gaggaina, vinā vā gaggaina upausathaṃ karissati, saṅghakammaṃ karissati| yassāyasmatau khamati gaggassa bhikkhunau  ummattakassa ummattakasammutiyā dānaṃ – saraiyya vā gaggau bhikkhu upausathaṃ na vā saraiyya, saraiyya vā saṅghakammaṃ na vā saraiyya, āgacchaiyya vā upausathaṃ na vā āgacchaiyya, āgacchaiyya vā saṅghakammaṃ na vā āgacchaiyya, saṅghau saha vā gaggaina, vinā vā gaggaina upausathaṃ karissati, saṅghakammaṃ karissati, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nā saṅghaina gaggassa bhikkhunau ummattakassa ummattakasammuti| saraiyya vā gaggau bhikkhu upausathaṃ na vā saraiyya, saraiyya vā saṅghakammaṃ na vā saraiyya, āgacchaiyya vā upausathaṃ na vā āgacchaiyya, āgacchaiyya vā saṅghakammaṃ na vā āgacchaiyya, saṅghau saha vā gaggaina vinā vā gaggaina upausathaṃ karissati, saṅghakammaṃ karissati| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

91. saṅghupausathādippabhaidaṃ

168. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai tadahupausathai cattārau bhikkhū viharanti| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘upausathau kātabbau’ti, mayañ‍camhā cattārau janā, kathaṃ nu khau amhaihi upausathau kātabbau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai tadahupausathai tayau bhikkhū viharanti| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ catun‍naṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisituṃ, mayañ‍camhā tayau  janā, kathaṃ nu khau amhaihi upausathau kātabbau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ pārisuddhiupausathaṃ kātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, kātabbau| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina tai bhikkhū ñāpaitabbā –

‘‘suṇantu mai āyasmantā| aj‍jupausathau pan‍narasau| yadāyasmantānaṃ pattakal‍laṃ, mayaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ pārisuddhiupausathaṃ karaiyyāmā’’ti|

thairaina  bhikkhunā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā tai bhikkhū aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘‘parisuddhau ahaṃ, āvusau; parisuddhauti maṃ dhāraitha| parisuddhau ahaṃ, āvusau; parisuddhauti maṃ dhāraitha| parisuddhau ahaṃ, āvusau; parisuddhauti maṃ dhāraithā’’ti|

navakaina bhikkhunā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā tai bhikkhū aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘‘parisuddhau ahaṃ, bhantai; parisuddhauti maṃ dhāraitha| parisuddhau ahaṃ, bhantai; parisuddhauti maṃ dhāraitha| parisuddhau ahaṃ, bhantai; parisuddhauti maṃ dhāraithā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai tadahupausathai dvai bhikkhū viharanti| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ catun‍naṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisituṃ, tiṇṇan‍naṃ pārisuddhiupausathaṃ kātuṃ| mayañ‍camhā dvai janā| kathaṃ nu khau amhaihi upausathau kātabbau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dvin‍naṃ pārisuddhiupausathaṃ kātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, kātabbau| thairaina bhikkhunā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā navau bhikkhu aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘parisuddhau ahaṃ, āvusau; parisuddhauti maṃ dhāraihi| parisuddhau ahaṃ, āvusau; parisuddhauti  maṃ dhāraihi| parisuddhau ahaṃ, āvusau; parisuddhauti maṃ dhāraihī’’ti|

navakaina  bhikkhunā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā thairau bhikkhu aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘parisuddhau ahaṃ, bhantai; parisuddhauti maṃ dhāraitha| parisuddhau ahaṃ, bhantai; parisuddhauti maṃ dhāraitha| parisuddhau ahaṃ, bhantai; parisuddhauti maṃ dhāraithā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai tadahupausathai aikau bhikkhu viharati| atha khau tassa bhikkhunau aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ catun‍naṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisituṃ, tiṇṇan‍naṃ pārisuddhiupausathaṃ kātuṃ, dvin‍naṃ pārisuddhiupausathaṃ kātuṃ| ahañ‍camhi aikakau| kathaṃ nu khau mayā upausathau kātabbau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| idha pana, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarasmiṃ āvāsai tadahupausathai aikau bhikkhu viharati| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā yattha bhikkhū paṭik‍kamanti upaṭṭhānasālāya vā, maṇḍapai vā, rukkhamūlai vā, sau daisau sammaj‍jitvā  pānīyaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā āsanaṃ  pañ‍ñapaitvā padīpaṃ katvā nisīditabbaṃ| sacai añ‍ñai bhikkhū āgacchanti, taihi saddhiṃ upausathau kātabbau| nau cai āgacchanti, aj‍ja mai upausathauti adhiṭṭhātabbau| nau cai adhiṭṭhahaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

tatra, bhikkhavai, yattha cattārau bhikkhū viharanti, na aikassa pārisuddhiṃ āharitvā tīhi pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbaṃ| uddisaiyyuṃ cai, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tatra, bhikkhavai, yattha tayau bhikkhū viharanti, na aikassa pārisuddhiṃ āharitvā dvīhi pārisuddhiupausathau kātabbau| karaiyyuṃ cai, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| tatra, bhikkhavai, yattha dvai bhikkhū viharanti, na aikassa pārisuddhiṃ āharitvā aikaina adhiṭṭhātabbau| adhiṭṭhahaiyya cai, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

92. āpattipaṭikammavidhi
