atha khau tāni asīti gāmikasahassāni yaina gijjhakūṭau pabbatau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā sāgatau bhagavatau upaṭṭhākau hauti| atha khau tāni asīti gāmikasahassāni yaināyasmā sāgatau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ  sāgataṃ aitadavaucuṃ  – ‘‘imāni, bhantai, asīti gāmikasahassāni idhūpasaṅkantāni bhagavantaṃ dassanāya; sādhu mayaṃ, bhantai, labhaiyyāma bhagavantaṃ dassanāyā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi tumhai āyasmantau muhuttaṃ idhaiva tāva hautha, yāvāhaṃ bhagavantaṃ paṭivaidaimī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā sāgatau taisaṃ asītiyā gāmikasahassānaṃ puratau paikkhamānānaṃ pāṭikāya nimuj‍jitvā bhagavatau puratau ummuj‍jitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imāni, bhantai, asīti gāmikasahassāni idhūpasaṅkantāni bhagavantaṃ dassanāya; yassa dāni, bhantai, bhagavā kālaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, sāgata, vihārapacchāyāyaṃ āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā sāgatau  bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā pīṭhaṃ gahaitvā bhagavatau puratau nimuj‍jitvā taisaṃ asītiyā gāmikasahassānaṃ puratau paikkhamānānaṃ pāṭikāya ummuj‍jitvā vihārapacchāyāyaṃ āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| atha khau bhagavā vihārā nikkhamitvā vihārapacchāyāyaṃ pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau tāni asīti gāmikasahassāni yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| atha khau tāni asīti gāmikasahassāni āyasmantaṃyaiva sāgataṃ saman‍nāharanti, nau tathā bhagavantaṃ| atha khau bhagavā taisaṃ asītiyā gāmikasahassānaṃ caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya āyasmantaṃ sāgataṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, sāgata, bhiyyausaumattāya uttarimanussadhammaṃ iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā sāgatau bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā vaihāsaṃ abbhuggantvā ākāsai antalikkhai caṅkamatipi, tiṭṭhatipi, nisīdatipi, saiyyampi kappaiti, dhūmāyatipi dhūpāyatipi (sī॰), padhūpāyatipi (syā॰) paj‍jalatipi, antaradhāyatipi| atha khau āyasmā sāgatau ākāsai antalikkhai anaikavihitaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassaitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā mai, bhantai, bhagavā; sāvakauhamasmi| satthā mai, bhantai, bhagavā; sāvakauhamasmī’’ti| atha khau tāni asīti gāmikasahassāni ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau! abbhutaṃ vata bhau! sāvakaupi nāma aivaṃ mahiddhikau bhavissati, aivaṃ mahānubhāvau, ahau nūna satthā’’ti bhagavantaṃyaiva saman‍nāharanti, nau  tathā āyasmantaṃ sāgataṃ|

atha  khau bhagavā taisaṃ asītiyā gāmikasahassānaṃ caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ, kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā tai bhagavā añ‍ñāsi kal‍lacittai, muducittai, vinīvaraṇacittai, udaggacittai, pasan‍nacittai, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā, taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ, samudayaṃ, niraudhaṃ, maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, aivamaivaṃ taisaṃ asītiyā gāmikasahassānaṃ tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ  vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘‘yaṃkiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti| tai diṭṭhadhammā pattadhammā viditadhammā pariyaugāḷhadhammā tiṇṇavicikicchā vigatakathaṃkathā vaisāraj‍jappattā aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – ‘‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’’ti, aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aitai mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāma| dhammañ‍ca, bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakai nau bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitai saraṇaṃ gatai’’ti|

sauṇassa pabbaj‍jā

243. atha khau sauṇassa kauḷivisassa aitadahausi ‘‘yathā yathā khau ahaṃ  bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ; yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’’nti| atha khau tāni asīti gāmikasahassāni bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kamisuṃ| atha khau sauṇau kauḷivisau acirapak‍kantaisu taisu asītiyā gāmikasahassaisu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sauṇau kauḷivisau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yathā yathāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā  kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ| pabbājaitu maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā’’ti| alattha khau sauṇau kauḷivisau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirupasampan‍nau ca  panāyasmā sauṇau sītavanai viharati| tassa ac‍cāraddhavīriyassa caṅkamatau pādā bhij‍jiṃsu| caṅkamau lauhitaina phuṭau hauti, saiyyathāpi gavāghātanaṃ| itau paraṃ yāva imassa vatthussa avasānaṃ tāva pāṭhau a॰ ni॰ 6.55 ādayau atha khau āyasmatau sauṇassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘yai  khau kaici bhagavatau sāvakā āraddhavīriyā viharanti, ahaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau| atha ca pana mai nānupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| saṃvij‍janti khau pana mai kulai bhaugā; sak‍kā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jituṃ, puñ‍ñāni ca kātuṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jaiyyaṃ, puñ‍ñāni ca karaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmatau sauṇassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi  nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva – gijjhakūṭai pabbatai antarahitau sītavanai pāturahausi| atha khau bhagavā sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saināsanacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantau yaināyasmatau sauṇassa caṅkamau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau bhagavā āyasmatau sauṇassa caṅkamaṃ lauhitaina phuṭaṃ, disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kassa nvāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, caṅkamau lauhitaina phuṭau, saiyyathāpi gavāghātana’’nti? ‘‘āyasmatau, bhantai, sauṇassa ac‍cāraddhavīriyassa caṅkamatau pādā bhij‍jiṃsu| tassāyaṃ caṅkamau lauhitaina phuṭau, saiyyathāpi gavāghātana’’nti|

atha khau bhagavā yaināyasmatau sauṇassa vihārau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| āyasmāpi khau sauṇau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ sauṇaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘nanu tai, sauṇa, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘yai khau kaici bhagavatau sāvakā āraddhavīriyā viharanti, ahaṃ  taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau| atha ca pana mai nānupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| saṃvij‍janti khau pana mai kulai bhaugā; sak‍kā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jituṃ, puñ‍ñāni ca kātuṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jaiyyaṃ, puñ‍ñāni ca karaiyya’’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sauṇa, kusalau tvaṃ pubbai agārikabhūtau vīṇāya tantissarai’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sauṇa, yadā tai vīṇāya tantiyau ac‍cāyatā haunti, api nu tai vīṇā tasmiṃ samayai saravatī vā hauti, kammañ‍ñā vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sauṇa, yadā tai vīṇāya tantiyau atisithilā haunti, api nu tai vīṇā tasmiṃ samayai saravatī vā hauti, kammañ‍ñā vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sauṇa, yadā tai vīṇāya tantiyau naiva ac‍cāyatā haunti nātisithilā, samai guṇai patiṭṭhitā, api nu tai vīṇā tasmiṃ samayai saravatī vā hauti, kammañ‍ñā vā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, sauṇa, ac‍cāraddhavīriyaṃ uddhac‍cāya saṃvattati, atilīnavīriyaṃ kausaj‍jāya saṃvattati| tasmātiha tvaṃ, sauṇa, vīriyasamataṃ adhiṭṭhaha, indriyānañ‍ca samataṃ paṭivijjha, tattha ca nimittaṃ gaṇhāhī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā sauṇau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sauṇaṃ iminā auvādaina auvaditvā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau  sammiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva – sītavanai āyasmatau sauṇassa sammukhai antarahitau gijjhakūṭai pabbatai pāturahausi| atha khau āyasmā sauṇau aparaina samayaina vīriyasamataṃ adhiṭṭhāsi, indriyānañ‍ca samataṃ paṭivijjhi, tattha ca nimittaṃ  aggahaisi| atha khau āyasmā sauṇau, aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau, na cirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti – tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abhiñ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā sauṇau arahataṃ ahausi|

244. atha khau āyasmatau sauṇassa arahattappattassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhagavatau santikai añ‍ñaṃ byākaraiyya’’nti| atha khau āyasmā sauṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sauṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – yau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasañ‍ñaujanau  sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau, sau chaṭṭhānāni adhimuttau hauti – naikkhammādhimuttau hauti, pavivaikādhimuttau hauti, abyāpaj‍jādhimuttau hauti, upādānakkhayādhimuttau hauti, taṇhakkhayādhimuttau hauti, asammauhādhimuttau hauti|

‘‘siyā khau pana, bhantai, idhaikac‍cassa āyasmatau aivamassa – ‘kaivalaṃ saddhāmattakaṃ nūna ayamāyasmā nissāya naikkhammādhimuttau’ti, na khau panaitaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| khīṇāsavau, bhantai, bhikkhu, vusitavā, katakaraṇīyau, karaṇīyamattānaṃ asamanupassantau katassa vā paṭicayaṃ khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā naikkhammādhimuttau hauti, khayā dausassa vītadausattā naikkhammādhimuttau hauti, khayā mauhassa vītamauhattā naikkhammādhimuttau hauti|

‘‘siyā khau pana, bhantai, idhaikac‍cassa āyasmatau aivamassa – ‘lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ nūna ayamāyasmā nikāmayamānau  pavivaikādhimuttau’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| khīṇāsavau, bhantai, bhikkhu, vusitavā, katakaraṇīyau, karaṇīyamattānaṃ karaṇīyaṃ attanau (aṅguttarapāḷiyaṃ) asamanupassantau katassa vā paṭicayaṃ, khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā pavivaikādhimuttau hauti, khayā dausassa vītadausattā pavivaikādhimuttau hauti, khayā mauhassa vītamauhattā pavivaikādhimuttau hauti|

‘‘siyā khau pana, bhantai, idhaikac‍cassa āyasmatau aivamassa – ‘sīlabbataparāmāsaṃ nūna ayamāyasmā sāratau pac‍cāgacchantau abyāpaj‍jādhimuttau’ti| na khau panaitaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| khīṇāsavau, bhantai, bhikkhu, vusitavā, katakaraṇīyau, karaṇīyamattānaṃ asamanupassantau  katassa vā paṭicayaṃ, khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā abyāpaj‍jādhimuttau hauti, khayā dausassa vītadausattā abyāpaj‍jādhimuttau hauti, khayā mauhassa vītamauhattā abyāpaj‍jādhimuttau  hauti|

‘‘khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā upādānakkhayādhimuttau hauti, khayā dausassa vītadausattā upādānakkhayādhimuttau hauti, khayā mauhassa vītamauhattā upādānakkhayādhimuttau hauti|

‘‘khayā  rāgassa vītarāgattā taṇhakkhayādhimuttau hauti, khayā dausassa vītadausattā taṇhakkhayādhimuttau hauti, khayā mauhassa vītamauhattā taṇhakkhayādhimuttau hauti|

‘‘khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā asammauhādhimuttau hauti, khayā dausassa vītadausattā asammauhādhimuttau hauti, khayā mauhassa vītamauhattā asammauhādhimuttau hauti|

‘‘aivaṃ sammā vimuttacittassa, bhantai, bhikkhunau bhusā caipi cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā cakkhussa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti| amissīkatamaivassa cittaṃ hauti, ṭhitaṃ, ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ, vayañ‍cassānupassati| bhusā caipi sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā… manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā manassa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti; amissīkatamaivassa cittaṃ hauti, ṭhitaṃ, ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ, vayañ‍cassānupassati| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, sailau pabbatau acchiddau asusirau aikagghanau, puratthimāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi, naiva naṃ saṅkampaiyya na sampakampaiyya na sampavaidhaiyya; pacchimāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya bhusā  vātavuṭṭhi…pai॰… uttarāya caipi disāya…pai॰… dakkhiṇāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi, naiva naṃ saṅkampaiyya na sampakampaiyya na sampavaidhaiyya, aivamaiva khau, bhantai, aivaṃ sammā vimuttacittassa bhikkhunau bhusā caipi cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā cakkhussa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti; amissīkatamaivassa cittaṃ hauti, ṭhitaṃ, ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ, vayañ‍cassānupassati| bhusā caipi sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā… manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā manassa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti; amissīkatamaivassa cittaṃ hauti, ṭhitaṃ, ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ, vayañ‍cassānupassatī’’ti|

naikkhammaṃ  adhimuttassa, pavivaikañ‍ca caitasau|

abyāpaj‍jādhimuttassa, upādānakkhayassa ca||

taṇhakkhayādhimuttassa, asammauhañ‍ca caitasau|

disvā āyatanuppādaṃ, sammā cittaṃ vimuc‍cati||

tassa  sammāvimuttassa, santacittassa bhikkhunau|

katassa paṭicayau natthi, karaṇīyaṃ na vij‍jati||

sailau yathā aikagghanau, vātaina na samīrati|

aivaṃ rūpā rasā saddā, gandhā phassā ca kaivalā||

iṭṭhā dhammā aniṭṭhā ca, na pavaidhainti tādinau|

ṭhitaṃ  cittaṃ vippamuttaṃ, vayañ‍cassānupassatīti||

sauṇakauḷivisavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

148. diguṇādiupāhanapaṭikkhaipau

245. atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kulaputtā añ‍ñaṃ byākaraunti, atthau ca vuttau, attā ca anupanītau| atha ca, panidhaikac‍cai maughapurisā hasamānakaṃ, mañ‍ñai, añ‍ñaṃ byākaraunti, tai pacchā vighātaṃ āpaj‍jantī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sauṇaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘tvaṃ khausi, sauṇa, sukhumālau| anujānāmi tai, sauṇa, aikapalāsikaṃ upāhana’’nti| ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, asītisakaṭavāhai hirañ‍ñaṃ auhāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau, sattahatthikañ‍ca anīkaṃ| athāhaṃ bhantai aikapalāsikaṃ cai upāhanaṃ pariharissāmi, tassa mai bhavissanti vattārau ‘sauṇau kauḷivisau asītisakaṭavāhai hirañ‍ñaṃ auhāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau, sattahatthikañ‍ca anīkaṃ| sau dānāyaṃ aikapalāsikāsu upāhanāsu sattau’ti| sacai bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghassa anujānissati ahampi paribhuñ‍jissāmi; nau cai bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghassa anujānissati, ahampi na paribhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū  āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aikapalāsikaṃ upāhanaṃ| na, bhikkhavai, diguṇā upāhanā dhāraitabbā| na tiguṇā upāhanā dhāraitabbā| na guṇaṅguṇūpāhanā gaṇaṅgaṇūpāhanā (bahūsu) dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

diguṇādiupāhanapaṭikkhaipau niṭṭhitau|

149. sabbanīlikādipaṭikkhaipau

246. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sabbanīlikā upāhanāyau dhārainti…pai॰… sabbapītikā upāhanāyau dhārainti… sabbalauhitikā upāhanāyau dhārainti… sabbamañ‍jiṭṭhikā  sabbamañ‍jaiṭṭhikā (ka॰) upāhanāyau dhārainti … sabbakaṇhā upāhanāyau dhārainti… sabbamahāraṅgarattā upāhanāyau dhārainti… sabbamahānāmarattā upāhanāyau dhārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti, ‘‘saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, sabbanīlikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā…pai॰… na sabbapītikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na sabbalauhitikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na sabbamañ‍jiṭṭhikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na sabbakaṇhā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na sabbamahāraṅgarattā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na sabbamahānāmarattā upāhanā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā  bhikkhū nīlakavaddhikā vaṭṭikā (sī॰) upāhanāyau dhārainti, pītakavaddhikā upāhanāyau dhārainti, lauhitakavaddhikā upāhanāyau dhārainti, mañ‍jiṭṭhikavaddhikā upāhanāyau dhārainti, kaṇhavaddhikā upāhanāyau dhārainti, mahāraṅgarattavaddhikā upāhanāyau dhārainti, mahānāmarattavaddhikā upāhanāyau dhārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti, ‘‘saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, nīlakavaddhikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā…pai॰… na pītakavaddhikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na lauhitakavaddhikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na mañ‍jiṭṭhikavaddhikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na  kaṇhavaddhikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na mahāraṅgarattavaddhikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na mahānāmarattavaddhikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū khal‍lakabaddhā …bandhā (ka॰) upāhanāyau dhārainti…pai॰… puṭabaddhā upāhanāyau dhārainti, pāliguṇṭhimā upāhanāyau dhārainti, tūlapuṇṇikā upāhanāyau dhārainti, tittirapattikā upāhanāyau dhārainti, maiṇḍavisāṇavaddhikā upāhanāyau dhārainti, ajavisāṇavaddhikā upāhanāyau dhārainti, vicchikāḷikā upāhanāyau dhārainti, maurapiñcha maurapiñ‍ja (sī॰ syā॰) parisibbitā upāhanāyau dhārainti, citrā upāhanāyau dhārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti, ‘‘saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, khal‍lakabaddhā upāhanā dhāraitabbā…pai॰… na puṭabaddhā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na pāliguṇṭhimā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na tūlapuṇṇikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na tittirapattikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na maiṇḍavisāṇavaddhikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na ajavisāṇavaddhikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na vicchikāḷikā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na maurapiñchaparisibbitā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na citrā upāhanā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sīhacammaparikkhaṭā upāhanāyau dhārainti…pai॰… byagghacammaparikkhaṭā upāhanāyau  dhārainti, dīpicammaparikkhaṭā upāhanāyau dhārainti, ajinacammaparikkhaṭā upāhanāyau dhārainti, uddacammaparikkhaṭā upāhanāyau dhārainti, maj‍jāracammaparikkhaṭā upāhanāyau dhārainti, kāḷakacammaparikkhaṭā upāhanāyau dhārainti, luvakacammaparikkhaṭā ulūkacammaparikkhaṭā (yaujanā) upāhanāyau dhārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti, ‘‘saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, sīhacammaparikkhaṭā upāhanā dhāraitabbā…pai॰… na byagghacammaparikkhaṭā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na dīpicammaparikkhaṭā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na ajinacammaparikkhaṭā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na uddacammaparikkhaṭā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na maj‍jāracammaparikkhaṭā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na kāḷakacammaparikkhaṭā upāhanā dhāraitabbā, na luvakacammaparikkhaṭā upāhanā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

sabbanīlikādipaṭikkhaipau niṭṭhitau|

150. aumuk‍kaguṇaṅguṇūpāhanānujānanā

247. atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi, añ‍ñataraina bhikkhunā pacchāsamaṇaina| atha khau sau bhikkhu khañ‍jamānau bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhi| addasā khau añ‍ñatarau upāsakau guṇaṅguṇūpāhanā ārauhitvā bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ; disvā upāhanā ārauhitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādaitvā  aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa, bhantai, ayyau khañ‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘pādā mai, āvusau, phalitā’’ti phālitāti (ka॰)| ‘‘handa, bhantai, upāhanāyau’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau, paṭikkhittā bhagavatā guṇaṅguṇūpāhanā’’ti| ‘‘gaṇhāhaitā, bhikkhu, upāhanāyau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aumuk‍kaṃ guṇaṅguṇūpāhanaṃ| na, bhikkhavai, navā guṇaṅguṇūpāhanā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

aumuk‍kaguṇaṅguṇūpāhanānujānanā niṭṭhitā|

151. ajjhārāmai upāhanapaṭikkhaipau

248. taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā ajjhaukāsai anupāhanau caṅkamati| satthā anupāhanau caṅkamatīti, thairāpi bhikkhū anupāhanā caṅkamanti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū, satthari anupāhanai caṅkamamānai, thairaisupi bhikkhūsu anupāhanaisu caṅkamamānaisu, saupāhanā caṅkamanti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū, satthari anupāhanai caṅkamamānai, thairaisupi bhikkhūsu anupāhanaisu caṅkamamānaisu, saupāhanā caṅkamissantī’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū, satthari anupāhanai caṅkamamānai, thairaisupi bhikkhūsu anupāhanaisu caṅkamamānaisu, saupāhanā caṅkamantī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā, satthari anupāhanai caṅkamamānai, thairaisupi bhikkhūsu anupāhanaisu caṅkamamānaisu, saupāhanā caṅkamissanti| imai hi nāma, bhikkhavai, gihī audātavatthavasanakā abhijīvanikassa sippassa kāraṇā ācariyaisu sagāravā sappatissā sabhāgavuttikā viharissanti| idha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, saubhaitha, yaṃ tumhai aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitā samānā ācariyaisu ācariyamattaisu upajjhāyaisu upajjhāyamattaisu agāravā appatissā asabhāgavuttikā sagāravā saggatissā sabhāgavuttikā (ka॰) viharaiyyātha| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, ācariyaisu ācariyamattaisu upajjhāyaisu upajjhāyamattaisu anupāhanaisu caṅkamamānaisu saupāhanaina caṅkamitabbaṃ| yau caṅkamaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, ajjhārāmai upāhanā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

249. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau pādakhilābādhau hauti| taṃ bhikkhū pariggahaitvā uc‍cārampi passāvampi nikkhāmainti| addasā khau bhagavā saināsanacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantau tai bhikkhū taṃ bhikkhuṃ pariggahaitvā uc‍cārampi  passāvampi nikkhāmaintai, disvāna yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ imassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ābādhau’’ti? ‘‘imassa, bhantai, āyasmatau pādakhilābādhau; imaṃ mayaṃ pariggahaitvā uc‍cārampi passāvampi nikkhāmaimā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ  katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yassa pādā vā dukkhā, pādā vā phalitā, pādakhilau vā ābādhau pādakhilābādhau vā (syā॰) upāhanaṃ dhāraitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū adhautaihi pādaihi mañ‍campi pīṭhampi abhiruhanti; cīvarampi saināsanampi dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ‘idāni mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā abhiruhissāmī’’ti upāhanaṃ dhāraitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū rattiyā upausathaggampi san‍nisaj‍jampi gacchantā andhakārai khāṇumpi kaṇṭakampi ak‍kamanti; pādā dukkhā haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ajjhārāmai upāhanaṃ dhāraituṃ, uk‍kaṃ, padīpaṃ, kattaradaṇḍanti|

ajjhārāmai upāhanapaṭikkhaipau niṭṭhitau|

152. kaṭṭhapādukādipaṭikkhaipau

250. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya kaṭṭhapādukāyau abhiruhitvā ajjhaukāsai caṅkamanti, uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā khaṭakhaṭasaddā, anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaintā, saiyyathidaṃ imā tiracchānakathāyau pāci॰ 508; dī॰ ni॰ 1.7; ma॰ ni॰ 2.223; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1080; a॰ ni॰ 10.69 ādayau – rājakathaṃ, caurakathaṃ, mahāmattakathaṃ, sainākathaṃ, bhayakathaṃ, yuddhakathaṃ, an‍nakathaṃ, pānakathaṃ, vatthakathaṃ, sayanakathaṃ, mālākathaṃ, gandhakathaṃ, ñātikathaṃ, yānakathaṃ, gāmakathaṃ, nigamakathaṃ, nagarakathaṃ, janapadakathaṃ, itthikathaṃ itthikathaṃ purisakathaṃ (ka॰), sūrakathaṃ, visikhākathaṃ, kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ, pubbapaitakathaṃ, nānattakathaṃ, laukakkhāyikaṃ, samuddakkhāyikaṃ, itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā; kīṭakampi ak‍kamitvā mārainti, bhikkhūpi samādhimhā cāvainti| yai  tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū rattiyā  pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya kaṭṭhapādukāyau abhiruhitvā ajjhaukāsai caṅkamissanti, uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā khaṭakhaṭasaddā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaintā, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ, caurakathaṃ…pai॰… itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā, kīṭakampi ak‍kamitvā māraissanti, bhikkhūpi samādhimhā cāvaissantī’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya kaṭṭhapādukāyau abhiruhitvā ajjhaukāsai caṅkamanti, uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā khaṭakhaṭasaddā, anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaintā, saiyyathidaṃ, – rājakathaṃ, caurakathaṃ…pai॰… itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā, kīṭakampi ak‍kamitvā mārainti, bhikkhūpi samādhimhā  cāvaintī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, kaṭṭhapādukā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā rājagahai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina bārāṇasī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina bārāṇasī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati isipatanai migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū – bhagavatā kaṭṭhapādukā paṭikkhittāti – tālataruṇai chaidāpaitvā tālapattapādukāyau dhārainti; tāni tālataruṇāni chin‍nāni milāyanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā  tālataruṇai chaidāpaitvā tālapattapādukāyau dhāraissanti; tāni tālataruṇāni chin‍nāni milāyanti; aikindriyaṃ samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jīvaṃ vihaiṭhaintī’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū tālataruṇai chaidāpaitvā tālapattapādukāyau dhārainti; tāni tālataruṇāni chin‍nāni milāyantī’’ti? sac‍caṃ bhagavāti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā tālataruṇai chaidāpaitvā tālapattapādukāyau dhāraissanti; tāni tālataruṇāni chin‍nāni milāyanti| jīvasañ‍ñinau hi, bhikkhavai, manussā rukkhasmiṃ| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ  vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, tālapattapādukā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ‘bhagavatā tālapattapādukā paṭikkhittā’ti vaiḷutaruṇai chaidāpaitvā vaiḷupattapādukāyau dhārainti| tāni vaiḷutaruṇāni chin‍nāni milāyanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā vaiḷutaruṇai chaidāpaitvā vaiḷupattapādukāyau dhāraissanti| tāni vaiḷutaruṇāni chin‍nāni milāyanti| aikindriyaṃ samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jīvaṃ vihaiṭhaintī’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ  vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… jīvasañ‍ñinau hi, bhikkhavai, manussā rukkhasmiṃ…pai॰… na, bhikkhavai, vaiḷupattapādukā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

251. atha khau bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina bhaddiyaṃ taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina bhaddiyaṃ tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā bhaddiyai viharati jātiyā vanai| taina khau  pana samayaina bhaddiyā bhikkhū anaikavihitaṃ pādukamaṇḍanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti, tiṇapādukaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, muñ‍japādukaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, pabbajapādukaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, hintālapādukaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, kamalapādukaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, kambalapādukaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, riñ‍canti uddaisaṃ paripucchaṃ adhisīlaṃ adhicittaṃ adhipañ‍ñaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhaddiyā bhikkhū anaikavihitaṃ pādukamaṇḍanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharissanti, tiṇapādukaṃ karissantipi kārāpaissantipi, muñ‍japādukaṃ karissantipi kārāpaissantipi, pabbajapādukaṃ karissantipi kārāpaissantipi, hintālapādukaṃ karissantipi kārāpaissantipi, kamalapādukaṃ karissantipi kārāpaissantipi, kambalapādukaṃ karissantipi kārāpaissantipi, riñ‍cissanti uddaisaṃ paripucchaṃ adhisīlaṃ adhicittaṃ adhipañ‍ña’’nti| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhaddiyā bhikkhū anaikavihitaṃ pādukamaṇḍanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti, tiṇapādukaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi…pai॰… riñ‍canti uddaisaṃ paripucchaṃ adhisīlaṃ adhicittaṃ  adhipañ‍ña’’nti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā anaikavihitaṃ pādukamaṇḍanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharissanti, tiṇapādukaṃ karissantipi kārāpaissantipi…pai॰… riñ‍cissanti uddaisaṃ paripucchaṃ adhisīlaṃ adhicittaṃ adhipañ‍ñaṃ| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, tiṇapādukā dhāraitabbā, na muñ‍japādukā dhāraitabbā, na pabbajapādukā dhāraitabbā, na hintālapādukā dhāraitabbā, na kamalapādukā dhāraitabbā, na kambalapādukā dhāraitabbā, na sauvaṇṇamayā pādukā dhāraitabbā, na rūpiyamayā pādukā dhāraitabbā, na maṇimayā pādukā dhāraitabbā, na vaiḷuriyamayā pādukā dhāraitabbā, na  phalikamayā pādukā dhāraitabbā, na kaṃsamayā pādukā dhāraitabbā, na kācamayā pādukā dhāraitabbā, na tipumayā pādukā dhāraitabbā, na sīsamayā pādukā dhāraitabbā, na tambalauhamayā pādukā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, kāci saṅkamaniyā pādukā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tissau pādukā dhuvaṭṭhāniyā asaṅkamaniyāyau – vac‍capādukaṃ, passāvapādukaṃ, ācamanapāduka’’nti|

252. atha khau bhagavā bhaddiyai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina sāvatthi taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina sāvatthi tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū  aciravatiyā nadiyā gāvīnaṃ tarantīnaṃ visāṇaisupi gaṇhanti, kaṇṇaisupi gaṇhanti, gīvāyapi gaṇhanti, chaippāpi gaṇhanti, piṭṭhimpi abhiruhanti, rattacittāpi aṅgajātaṃ chupanti, vacchatarimpi augāhaitvā mārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā gāvīnaṃ tarantīnaṃ visāṇaisupi gahaissanti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ  vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai,…pai॰… sac‍caṃ bhagavāti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, gāvīnaṃ visāṇaisu gahaitabbaṃ, na kaṇṇaisu gahaitabbaṃ, na gīvāya gahaitabbaṃ, na chaippāya gahaitabbaṃ, na piṭṭhi abhiruhitabbā| yau abhiruhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, rattacittaina aṅgajātaṃ chupitabbaṃ| yau chupaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| na vacchatarī māraitabbā| yau māraiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau’’ti|

kaṭṭhapādukādipaṭikkhaipau niṭṭhitau|

153. yānādipaṭikkhaipau

253. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū yānaina yāyanti, itthiyuttainapi purisantaraina, purisayuttainapi itthantaraina| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘saiyyathāpi gaṅgāmahiyāyā’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, yānaina yāyitabbaṃ| yau yāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kausalaisu janapadai sāvatthiṃ gacchantau bhagavantaṃ dassanāya antarāmaggai gilānau hauti| atha khau sau bhikkhu maggā auk‍kamma añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisīdi| manussā taṃ bhikkhuṃ disvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kahaṃ, bhantai, ayyau gamissatī’’ti? ‘‘sāvatthiṃ khau ahaṃ, āvusau, gamissāmi bhagavantaṃ dassanāyā’’ti| ‘‘aihi, bhantai, gamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘nāhaṃ, āvusau, sak‍kaumi, gilānaumhī’’ti| ‘‘aihi, bhantai, yānaṃ abhiruhā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau, paṭikkhittaṃ bhagavatā yāna’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantau yānaṃ nābhiruhi| atha khau sau bhikkhu sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānassa yānanti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘itthiyuttaṃ nu khau purisayuttaṃ nu khau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, purisayuttaṃ hatthavaṭṭakanti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau yānugghātaina bāḷhataraṃ aphāsu ahausi| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sivikaṃ pāṭaṅkinti|

yānādipaṭikkhaipau niṭṭhitau|

154. uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanapaṭikkhaipau

254. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanāni dhārainti, saiyyathidaṃ – āsandiṃ, pal‍laṅkaṃ, gaunakaṃ, cittakaṃ, paṭikaṃ, paṭalikaṃ, tūlikaṃ, vikatikaṃ, uddhalaumiṃ undalaumiṃ (ka॰), uddalaumiṃ (ka॰), aikantalaumiṃ, kaṭṭissaṃ, kausaiyyaṃ, kuttakaṃ, hatthattharaṃ, assattharaṃ, rathattharaṃ, ajinapavaiṇiṃ, kadalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇaṃ, sauttaracchadaṃ, ubhataulauhitakūpadhānanti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanāni dhāraitabbāni, saiyyathidaṃ – āsandi, pal‍laṅkau, gaunakau, cittakau, paṭikā, paṭalikā, tūlikā, vikatikā, uddhalaumi, aikantalaumi, kaṭṭissaṃ, kausaiyyaṃ, kuttakaṃ, hatthattharaṃ, assattharaṃ, rathattharaṃ, ajinapavaiṇi, kadalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇaṃ, sauttaracchadaṃ, ubhataulauhitakūpadhānaṃ| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanapaṭikkhaipau niṭṭhitau|

155. sabbacammapaṭikkhaipau

255. taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū – bhagavatā uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanāni paṭikkhittānīti – mahācammāni dhārainti, sīhacammaṃ byagghacammaṃ dīpicammaṃ| tāni mañ‍cappamāṇainapi chin‍nāni haunti, pīṭhappamāṇainapi chin‍nāni haunti, antaupi mañ‍cai pañ‍ñattāni haunti, bahipi mañ‍cai pañ‍ñattāni haunti, antaupi pīṭhai pañ‍ñattāni haunti, bahipi pīṭhai pañ‍ñattāni haunti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, mahācammāni dhāraitabbāni, sīhacammaṃ byagghacammaṃ dīpicammaṃ| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū – bhagavatā mahācammāni paṭikkhittānīti – gaucammāni dhārainti| tāni mañ‍cappamāṇainapi chin‍nāni haunti, pīṭhappamāṇainapi chin‍nāni haunti, antaupi mañ‍cai pañ‍ñattāni haunti, bahipi mañ‍cai pañ‍ñattāni haunti, antaupi pīṭhai pañ‍ñattāni haunti, bahipi pīṭhai pañ‍ñattāni haunti| añ‍ñataraupi pāpabhikkhu añ‍ñatarassa pāpupāsakassa kulūpakau hauti| atha khau sau pāpabhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina tassa pāpupāsakassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau sau  pāpupāsakau yaina sau pāpabhikkhu tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ pāpabhikkhuṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| taina khau pana samayaina tassa pāpupāsakassa vacchakau hauti taruṇakau abhirūpau dassanīyau  pāsādikau citrau, saiyyathāpi dīpicchāpau| atha khau sau pāpabhikkhu taṃ vacchakaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ upanijjhāyati| atha khau sau pāpupāsakau taṃ pāpabhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa, bhantai, ayyau imaṃ vacchakaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ upanijjhāyatī’’ti? ‘‘atthau mai, āvusau, imassa vacchakassa cammainā’’ti| atha khau sau pāpupāsakau taṃ vacchakaṃ vadhitvā cammaṃ vidhunitvā tassa pāpabhikkhunau pādāsi| atha khau sau pāpabhikkhu taṃ cammaṃ saṅghāṭiyā paṭicchādaitvā agamāsi| atha khau sā gāvī vacchagiddhinī taṃ pāpabhikkhuṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhi| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kissa tyāyaṃ, āvusau, gāvī piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhī’’ti? ‘‘ahampi khau, āvusau, na jānāmi kaina kainaci (ka॰) myāyaṃ gāvī piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhī’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina tassa pāpabhikkhunau saṅghāṭi lauhitaina makkhitā hauti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘ayaṃ pana tai, āvusau, saṅghāṭi kiṃ katā’’ti? atha khau sau pāpabhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, āvusau, pāṇātipātai samādapaisī’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu pāṇātipātai samādapaissati, nanu bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina pāṇātipātau garahitau, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī pasatthā’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

atha  khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā taṃ pāpabhikkhuṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, pāṇātipātai samādapaisī’’ti? sac‍caṃ bhagavāti…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, pāṇātipātai samādapaissasi, nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina pāṇātipātau garahitau, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī pasatthā| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pāṇātipātai samādapaitabbaṃ| yau samādapaiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau| na, bhikkhavai, gaucammaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, kiñ‍ci cammaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

sabbacammapaṭikkhaipau niṭṭhitau|

156. gihivikatānuñ‍ñātādi

256. taina  khau pana samayaina manussānaṃ mañ‍campi pīṭhampi cammaunaddhāni haunti, cammavinaddhāni| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nābhinisīdanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gihivikataṃ abhinisīdituṃ, na tvaiva abhinipaj‍jitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina vihārā cammavaddhaihi augumphiyanti augumbhiyanti (ka॰)| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nābhinisīdanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bandhanamattaṃ abhinisīditunti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū saupāhanā gāmaṃ pavisanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, saupāhanaina  gāmau pavisitabbau| yau pavisaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti, na sak‍kauti vinā upāhanaina gāmaṃ pavisituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānaina bhikkhunā saupāhanaina gāmaṃ pavisitunti|

gihivikatānuñ‍ñātādi niṭṭhitā|

157. sauṇakuṭikaṇṇavatthu

257. udā॰ 46 saukasuttaina saṃsanditvā passitabbaṃ taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā mahākac‍cānau avantīsu viharati kuraragharai kururagharai (ka॰) papatakai papātai (sī॰ syā॰) pavatthai (udā॰ 46) pabbatai| taina khau pana samayaina sauṇau upāsakau kuṭikaṇṇau āyasmatau mahākac‍cānassa upaṭṭhākau hauti| atha khau sauṇau upāsakau kuṭikaṇṇau yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sauṇau upāsakau kuṭikaṇṇau āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yathā yathāhaṃ, bhantai, ayyaina mahākac‍cānaina dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ| pabbājaitu maṃ, bhantai, ayyau mahākac‍cānau’’ti| ( ) (aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau sauṇaṃ upāsakaṃ kuṭikaṇṇaṃ aitadavauca) (syā॰ udā॰ 46) ‘‘duk‍karaṃ khau, sauṇa, yāvajīvaṃ aikasaiyyaṃ aikabhattaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| iṅgha, tvaṃ, sauṇa, tatthaiva agārikabhūtau buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ anuyuñ‍ja, kālayuttaṃ aikasaiyyaṃ aikabhattaṃ brahmacariya’’nti| atha khau sauṇassa upāsakassa kuṭikaṇṇassa yau ahausi pabbaj‍jābhisaṅkhārau sau paṭippassambhi| dutiyampi khau sauṇau upāsakau kuṭikaṇṇau …pai॰… tatiyampi khau sauṇau upāsakau kuṭikaṇṇau yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sauṇau upāsakau kuṭikaṇṇau āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yathā yathāhaṃ, bhantai, ayyaina mahākac‍cānaina dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ| pabbājaitu maṃ, bhantai, ayyau mahākac‍cānau’’ti| atha khau āyasmā  mahākac‍cānau sauṇaṃ upāsakaṃ kuṭikaṇṇaṃ pabbājaisi| taina khau pana samayaina avantidakkhiṇāpathau appabhikkhukau hauti| atha khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau tiṇṇaṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina kicchaina kasiraina tatau tatau dasavaggaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā āyasmantaṃ sauṇaṃ upasampādaisi|

sauṇakuṭikaṇṇavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

158. mahākac‍cānassa pañ‍cavaraparidassanā

atha khau āyasmatau sauṇassa vassaṃvuṭṭhassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘sutauyaiva khau mai sau bhagavā aidisau ca aidisau cāti, na ca mayā sammukhā  diṭṭhau, gacchaiyyāhaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ, sacai maṃ upajjhāyau anujānaiyyā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā sauṇau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sauṇau āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, rahaugatassa  paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘sutau yaiva khau mai sau bhagavā aidisau ca aidisau cāti, na ca mayā sammukhā diṭṭhau, gacchaiyyāhaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ, sacai maṃ upajjhāyau anujānaiyyā’ti; gacchaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ, sacai maṃ upajjhāyau anujānātī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sauṇa| gaccha tvaṃ, sauṇa, taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ| dakkhissasi tvaṃ, sauṇa, taṃ bhagavantaṃ pāsādikaṃ pasādanīyaṃ santindriyaṃ santamānasaṃ uttamadamathasamathaṃ amanuppattaṃ dantaṃ guttaṃ yatindriyaṃ nāgaṃ| taina hi tvaṃ, sauṇa, mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vanda – ‘upajjhāyau mai, bhantai, āyasmā mahākac‍cānau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandatī’’’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘‘avantidakkhiṇāpathau, bhantai, appabhikkhukau, tiṇṇaṃ mai vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina kicchaina kasiraina tatau tatau dasavaggaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā upasampadaṃ alatthaṃ; appaiva nāma bhagavā avantidakkhiṇāpathai  appataraina gaṇaina upasampadaṃ anujānaiyya| avantidakkhiṇāpathai, bhantai, kaṇhuttarā bhūmi kharā gaukaṇṭakahatā; appaiva nāma bhagavā avantidakkhiṇāpathai  guṇaṅguṇūpāhanaṃ anujānaiyya| avantidakkhiṇāpathai, bhantai, nahānagarukā manussā udakasuddhikā; appaiva nāma bhagavā avantidakkhiṇāpathai dhuvanahānaṃ anujānaiyya| avantidakkhiṇāpathai, bhantai, cammāni attharaṇāni, aiḷakacammaṃ  ajacammaṃ migacammaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, majjhimaisu janapadaisu airagū mauragū maj‍jārū majjhārū (ka॰) jantū, aivamaiva khau, bhantai, avantidakkhiṇāpathai cammāni attharaṇāni, aiḷakacammaṃ ajacammaṃ migacammaṃ; appaiva nāma bhagavā avantidakkhiṇāpathai cammāni attharaṇāni anujānaiyya, aiḷakacammaṃ ajacammaṃ migacammaṃ| aitarahi, bhantai, manussā nissīmagatānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ cīvaraṃ dainti – ‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ itthan‍nāmassa daimā’’’ti| tai āgantvā āraucainti – ‘itthan‍nāmaihi tai, āvusau, manussaihi cīvaraṃ din‍na’nti tai kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na sādiyanti – ‘mā nau nissaggiyaṃ ahausī’ti; appaiva nāma bhagavā cīvarai pariyāyaṃ ācikkhaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā sauṇau āyasmatau mahākac‍cānassa paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya  yaina sāvatthi taina pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina yaina sāvatthi jaitavanaṃ anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘imassa, ānanda, āgantukassa bhikkhunau saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaihī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau – ‘‘yassa khau maṃ bhagavā āṇāpaiti, ‘imassa, ānanda, āgantukassa bhikkhunau saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaihī’ti, icchati bhagavā taina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikavihārai vatthuṃ, icchati bhagavā āyasmatā sauṇaina saddhiṃ aikavihārai vatthu’’nti – yasmiṃ vihārai bhagavā viharati tasmiṃ vihārai āyasmatau sauṇassa saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaisi|

258. atha khau bhagavā bahudaiva rattiṃ ajjhaukāsai vītināmaitvā vihāraṃ pāvisi| āyasmāpi khau sauṇau bahudaiva rattiṃ ajjhaukāsai vītināmaitvā vihāraṃ pāvisi| atha khau bhagavā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya āyasmantaṃ sauṇaṃ ajjhaisi – ‘‘paṭibhātu taṃ, bhikkhu, dhammau bhāsitu’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā sauṇau bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā sabbānaiva aṭṭhakavaggikāni saraina abhāsi| atha khau bhagavā āyasmatau sauṇassa sarabhañ‍ñapariyausānai abbhānumaudi – ‘‘sādhu, sādhu, bhikkhu| suggahitāni khau tai, bhikkhu, aṭṭhakavaggikāni, sumanasikatāni sūpadhāritāni| kalyāṇiyāpi vācāya saman‍nāgatau, vissaṭṭhāya, anailagalāya anaiḷagalāya (ka॰), atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā| kativassausi tvaṃ, bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘aikavassauhaṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘kissa pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, aivaṃ ciraṃ akāsī’’ti? ‘‘ciraṃ diṭṭhau mai, bhantai, kāmaisu ādīnavau, api ca sambādhā  gharāvāsā bahukic‍cā bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

udā॰ 46 udānaipi ‘‘disvā  ādīnavaṃ laukai, ñatvā dhammaṃ nirūpadhiṃ|

ariyau na ramatī pāpai, pāpai na ramatī sucī’’ti||

atha khau āyasmā sauṇau – paṭisammaudati khau maṃ bhagavā, ayaṃ khvassa kālau yaṃ mai upajjhāyau paridassīti – uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘upajjhāyau mai, bhantai, āyasmā mahākac‍cānau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati, aivañ‍ca vadaiti avantidakkhiṇāpathau, bhantai, appabhikkhukau| tiṇṇaṃ mai vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina kicchaina kasiraina tatau tatau dasavaggaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā upasampadaṃ alatthaṃ, appaiva nāma bhagavā avantidakkhiṇāpathai appataraina gaṇaina upasampadaṃ anujānaiyya| avantidakkhiṇāpathai, bhantai, kaṇhuttarā bhūmi kharā gaukaṇṭakahatā; appaiva nāma bhagavā avantidakkhiṇāpathai guṇaṅguṇūpāhanaṃ anujānaiyya| avantidakkhiṇāpathai, bhantai, nahānagarukā manussā udakasuddhikā, appaiva nāma bhagavā avantidakkhiṇāpathai dhuvanahānaṃ anujānaiyya| avantidakkhiṇāpathai, bhantai, cammāni attharaṇāni, aiḷakacammaṃ ajacammaṃ migacammaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, majjhimaisu janapadaisu airagū mauragū maj‍jārū jantū, aivamaiva khau, bhantai, avantidakkhiṇāpathai cammāni attharaṇāni, aiḷakacammaṃ ajacammaṃ migacammaṃ; appaiva nāma bhagavā avantidakkhiṇāpathai cammāni attharaṇāni anujānaiyya, aiḷakacammaṃ ajacammaṃ migacammaṃ| aitarahi, bhantai, manussā nissīmagatānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ cīvaraṃ dainti – ‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ itthan‍nāmassa daimā’ti| tai āgantvā āraucainti – ‘itthan‍nāmaihi tai, āvusau, manussaihi cīvaraṃ din‍na’nti| tai kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na sādiyanti – ‘mā nau nissaggiyaṃ ahausī’ti; appaiva nāma bhagavā cīvarai pariyāyaṃ ācikkhaiyyā’’ti|

259. atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘avantidakkhiṇāpathau, bhikkhavai, appabhikkhukau| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sabbapac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu vinayadharapañ‍camaina gaṇaina upasampadaṃ| tatrimai pac‍cantimā janapadā – puratthimāya disāya gajaṅgalaṃ kajaṅgalaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) nāma nigamau, tassa paraina mahāsālā, tatau parā pac‍cantimā janapadā, auratau majjhai; puratthimadakkhiṇāya disāya sal‍lavatī salalavatī (sī॰) nāma nadī, tatau parā pac‍cantimā janapadā, auratau majjhai; dakkhiṇāya disāya saitakaṇṇikaṃ nāma nigamau, tatau parā pac‍cantimā janapadā, auratau majjhai; pacchimāya disāya thūṇaṃ nāma brāhmaṇagāmau, tatau parā pac‍cantimā janapadā, auratau majjhai; uttarāya  disāya usīraddhajau nāma pabbatau, tatau parā pac‍cantimā janapadā, auratau majjhai| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaisu pac‍cantimaisu  janapadaisu vinayadharapañ‍camaina gaṇaina upasampadaṃ| avantidakkhiṇāpathai, bhikkhavai, kaṇhuttarā bhūmi kharā gaukaṇṭakahatā| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sabbapac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu guṇaṅguṇūpāhanaṃ| avantidakkhiṇāpathai, bhikkhavai, nahānagarukā manussā udakasuddhikā| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sabbapac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu dhuvanahānaṃ| avantidakkhiṇāpathai, bhikkhavai, cammāni attharaṇāni, aiḷakacammaṃ ajacammaṃ  migacammaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, majjhimaisu janapadaisu airagū mauragū maj‍jārū jantū, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, avantidakkhiṇāpathai cammāni attharaṇāni, aiḷakacammaṃ ajacammaṃ migacammaṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sabbapac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu cammāni attharaṇāni, aiḷakacammaṃ ajacammaṃ migacammaṃ| idha pana, bhikkhavai, manussā nissīmagatānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ cīvaraṃ dainti – ‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ itthan‍nāmassa daimā’ti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sādituṃ, na tāva taṃ gaṇanūpagaṃ yāva na hatthaṃ gacchatī’’ti|

mahākac‍cānassa pañ‍cavaraparidassanā niṭṭhitā|

cammakkhandhakau pañ‍camau|

159. tassuddānaṃ

rājā ca māgadhau sauṇau, asītisahassissarau|

sāgatau gijjhakūṭasmiṃ, bahuṃ dassaiti uttariṃ||

pabbaj‍jāraddhabhij‍jiṃsu, vīṇaṃ aikapalāsikaṃ|

nīlā pītā lauhitikā, mañ‍jiṭṭhā kaṇhamaiva ca||

mahāraṅgamahānāmā, vaddhikā ca paṭikkhipi|

khal‍lakā puṭapāli ca, tūlatittiramaiṇḍajā||

vicchikā mauracitrā ca, sīhabyagghā ca dīpikā|

ajinuddā maj‍jārī ca, kāḷaluvakaparikkhaṭā||

phalitupāhanā  khilā, dhautakhāṇukhaṭakhaṭā|

tālavaiḷutiṇaṃ caiva, muñ‍japabbajahintālā||

kamalakambalasauvaṇṇā, rūpikā maṇivaiḷuriyā|

phalikā kaṃsakācā ca, tipusīsañ‍ca tambakā||

gāvī yānaṃ gilānau ca, purisāyuttasivikā|

sayanāni mahācammā, gaucammaihi ca pāpakau||

gihīnaṃ cammavaddhaihi, pavisanti gilāyanau|

mahākac‍cāyanau sauṇau, saraina aṭṭhakavaggikaṃ||

upasampadaṃ pañ‍cahi, guṇaṅguṇā dhuvasinā|

cammattharaṇānuñ‍ñāsi, na tāva gaṇanūpagaṃ|

adāsi mai varai pañ‍ca, sauṇatthairassa nāyakauti||

imamhi khandhakai vatthūni taisaṭṭhi|

cammakkhandhakau niṭṭhitau|



6. bhaisaj‍jakkhandhakau

160. pañ‍cabhaisaj‍jakathā

260. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhūnaṃ sāradikaina ābādhaina phuṭṭhānaṃ yāgupi pītā uggacchati, bhattampi bhuttaṃ uggacchati| tai taina kisā haunti, lūkhā, dubbaṇṇā, uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātā, dhamanisanthatagattā| addasā khau bhagavā tai bhikkhū kisai lūkhai dubbaṇṇai uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātai dhamanisanthatagattai, disvāna āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, ānanda, aitarahi bhikkhū kisā, lūkhā, dubbaṇṇā, uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātā, dhamanisanthatagattā’’ti? ‘‘aitarahi, bhantai, bhikkhūnaṃ sāradikaina ābādhaina phuṭṭhānaṃ yāgupi pītā uggacchati, bhattampi bhuttaṃ uggacchati| tai taina kisā haunti, lūkhā, dubbaṇṇā, uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātā, dhamanisanthatagattā’’ti| atha khau bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘aitarahi khau bhikkhūnaṃ sāradikaina ābādhaina phuṭṭhānaṃ yāgupi pītā uggacchati, bhattampi bhuttaṃ uggacchati| tai taina kisā haunti, lūkhā, dubbaṇṇā, uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātā, dhamanisanthatagattā| kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ anujānaiyyaṃ, yaṃ bhaisaj‍jañ‍caiva assa bhaisaj‍jasammatañ‍ca laukassa, āhāratthañ‍ca pharaiyya, na ca auḷārikau āhārau pañ‍ñāyaiyyā’’ti? atha khau bhagavatau  aitadahausi – ‘‘imāni khau pañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jāni, saiyyathidaṃ – sappi, navanītaṃ, tailaṃ, madhu, phāṇitaṃ; bhaisaj‍jāni caiva bhaisaj‍jasammatāni ca laukassa, āhāratthañ‍ca pharanti, na ca auḷārikau āhārau pañ‍ñāyati| yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ imāni pañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jāni anujānaiyyaṃ, kālai paṭiggahaitvā kālai paribhuñ‍jitu’’nti| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘aitarahi khau bhikkhūnaṃ sāradikaina ābādhaina  phuṭṭhānaṃ yāgupi pītā uggacchati, bhattampi bhuttaṃ uggacchati| tai taina kisā haunti, lūkhā, dubbaṇṇā, uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātā, dhamanisanthatagattā| kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ anujānaiyyaṃ, yaṃ bhaisaj‍jañ‍caiva assa bhaisaj‍jasammatañ‍ca laukassa, āhāratthañ‍ca  pharaiyya, na ca auḷārikau āhārau pañ‍ñāyaiyyā’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi ‘imāni khau pañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jāni, saiyyathidaṃ – sappi, navanītaṃ, tailaṃ, madhu, phāṇitaṃ; bhaisaj‍jāni caiva bhaisaj‍jasammatāni ca laukassa, āhāratthañ‍ca pharanti, na ca auḷārikau āhārau pañ‍ñāyati| yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ imāni pañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jāni anujānaiyyaṃ, kālai paṭiggahaitvā kālai paribhuñ‍jitu’nti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tāni pañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jāni kālai paṭiggahaitvā kālai paribhuñ‍jitu’’nti|

261. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū tāni pañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jāni kālai paṭiggahaitvā kālai paribhuñ‍janti| taisaṃ yānipi tāni pākatikāni lūkhāni bhaujanāni tānipi nacchādainti, pagaiva sainaisitāni sainaisikāni (sī॰ syā॰), sainaihikāni (yaujanā)| tai taina caiva sāradikaina ābādhaina phuṭṭhā, iminā ca bhattācchādakaina bhattācchan‍nakaina (ka॰), tadubhayaina bhiyyausaumattāya kisā haunti, lūkhā, dubbaṇṇā, uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātā, dhamanisanthatagattā| addasā khau bhagavā tai bhikkhū bhiyyausaumattāya kisai lūkhai dubbaṇṇai uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātai dhamanisanthatagattai, disvāna āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, ānanda, aitarahi bhikkhū bhiyyausaumattāya kisā, lūkhā, dubbaṇṇā, uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātā, dhamanisanthatagattā’’ti? ‘‘aitarahi, bhantai, bhikkhū tāni ca pañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jāni kālai paṭiggahaitvā kālai paribhuñ‍janti| taisaṃ yānipi tāni pākatikāni lūkhāni bhaujanāni tānipi nacchādainti, pagaiva sainaisikāni| tai taina caiva sāradikaina ābādhaina phuṭṭhā, iminā ca bhattācchādakaina, tadubhayaina bhiyyausaumattāya kisā, lūkhā, dubbaṇṇā, uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātā, dhamanisanthatagattā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tāni pañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jāni paṭiggahaitvā kālaipi vikālaipi paribhuñ‍jitu’’nti|

262. taina  khau pana samayaina gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vasaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vasāni bhaisaj‍jāni – acchavasaṃ, macchavasaṃ, susukāvasaṃ, sūkaravasaṃ, gadrabhavasaṃ – kālai paṭiggahitaṃ kālai nippak‍kaṃ nipak‍kaṃ (ka॰) kālai saṃsaṭṭhaṃ tailaparibhaugaina paribhuñ‍jituṃ| vikālai cai, bhikkhavai, paṭiggahitaṃ vikālai nippak‍kaṃ vikālai saṃsaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ cai paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti tiṇṇaṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ| kālai cai, bhikkhavai, paṭiggahitaṃ vikālai nippak‍kaṃ vikālai saṃsaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ cai paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ| kālai cai, bhikkhavai, paṭiggahitaṃ  kālai nippak‍kaṃ vikālai saṃsaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ cai paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| kālai cai, bhikkhavai, paṭiggahitaṃ kālai nippak‍kaṃ kālai saṃsaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ cai paribhuñ‍jaiyya, anāpattīti|

pañ‍cabhaisaj‍jakathā niṭṭhitā|

161. mūlādibhaisaj‍jakathā

263. taina khau pana samayaina gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ mūlaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mūlāni bhaisaj‍jāni – haliddiṃ, siṅgivairaṃ, vacaṃ, vacatthaṃ vacatthaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), ativisaṃ, kaṭukarauhiṇiṃ, usīraṃ, bhaddamuttakaṃ, yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi atthi mūlāni bhaisaj‍jāni, naiva khādanīyai khādanīyatthaṃ pharanti, na bhaujanīyai bhaujanīyatthaṃ pharanti, tāni – paṭiggahaitvā yāvajīvaṃ pariharituṃ; sati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jituṃ| asati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana  samayaina gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ mūlaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi piṭṭhaihi atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, nisadaṃ nisadapautakanti|

taina khau pana samayaina gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kasāvaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kasāvāni kasāvabhaisaj‍jāni (ka॰) bhaisaj‍jāni – nimbakasāvaṃ, kuṭajakasāvaṃ, paṭaulakasāvaṃ, phaggavakasāvaṃ, nattamālakasāvaṃ, yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi atthi kasāvāni bhaisaj‍jāni naiva khādanīyai khādanīyatthaṃ pharanti, na bhaujanīyai bhaujanīyatthaṃ pharanti, tāni – paṭiggahaitvā yāvajīvaṃ pariharituṃ; sati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jituṃ| asati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṇṇaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paṇṇāni bhaisaj‍jāni – nimbapaṇṇaṃ, kuṭajapaṇṇaṃ, paṭaulapaṇṇaṃ, sulasipaṇṇaṃ, kappāsapaṇṇaṃ, yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi atthi paṇṇāni bhaisaj‍jāni, naiva khādanīyai khādanīyatthaṃ pharanti, na bhaujanīyai bhaujanīyatthaṃ pharanti…pai॰…|

taina  khau pana samayaina gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phalaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, phalāni  bhaisaj‍jāni – bilaṅgaṃ, pippaliṃ, maricaṃ, harītakaṃ, vibhītakaṃ, āmalakaṃ, gauṭṭhaphalaṃ gauṭhaphalaṃ (syā॰), kauṭṭhaphalaṃ (ka॰), yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi atthi phalāni bhaisaj‍jāni, naiva khādanīyai khādanīyatthaṃ pharanti, na bhaujanīyai bhaujanīyatthaṃ pharanti…pai॰…|

taina khau pana samayaina gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ jatūhi bhaisaj‍jaihi atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, jatūni bhaisaj‍jāni – hiṅguṃ, hiṅgujatuṃ, hiṅgusipāṭikaṃ, takaṃ, takapattiṃ, takapaṇṇiṃ, saj‍julasaṃ, yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi atthi jatūni bhaisaj‍jāni, naiva khādanīyai khādanīyatthaṃ pharanti…pai॰…|

taina khau pana samayaina gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ lauṇaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, lauṇāni bhaisaj‍jāni – sāmuddaṃ, kāḷalauṇaṃ, sindhavaṃ, ubbhidaṃ ubbhiraṃ (ka॰), bilaṃ biḷālaṃ (sī॰), yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi atthi lauṇāni bhaisaj‍jāni, naiva khādanīyai khādanīyatthaṃ pharanti, na bhaujanīyai bhaujanīyatthaṃ pharanti, tāni – paṭiggahaitvā yāvajīvaṃ pariharituṃ; sati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jituṃ| asati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

264. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau ānandassa upajjhāyassa  āyasmatau bailaṭṭhasīsassa thul‍lakacchābādhau hauti| tassa lasikāya cīvarāni kāyai lagganti, tāni bhikkhū udakaina taimaitvā taimaitvā apakaḍḍhanti| addasā khau bhagavā saināsanacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantau tai bhikkhū tāni cīvarāni udakaina taimaitvā taimaitvā apakaḍḍhantai, disvāna yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ imassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ābādhau’’ti? ‘‘imassa, bhantai, āyasmatau thul‍lakacchābādhau, lasikāya cīvarāni kāyai lagganti, tāni mayaṃ udakaina taimaitvā taimaitvā apakaḍḍhāmā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yassa kaṇḍu vā, piḷakā vā, assāvau vā, thul‍lakacchu vā ābādhau, kāyau vā duggandhau, cuṇṇāni bhaisaj‍jāni; agilānassa chakaṇaṃ mattikaṃ rajananippak‍kaṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udukkhalaṃ musala’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ cuṇṇaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi cālitaihi atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cuṇṇacālininti| saṇhaihi atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dussacālininti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau amanussikābādhau hauti| taṃ ācariyupajjhāyā upaṭṭhahantā nāsakkhiṃsu araugaṃ kātuṃ| sau sūkarasūnaṃ gantvā āmakamaṃsaṃ khādi, āmakalauhitaṃ pivi| tassa sau amanussikābādhau paṭippassambhi| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, amanussikābādhai āmakamaṃsaṃ āmakalauhitanti|

265. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau cakkhuraugābādhau hauti| taṃ bhikkhū pariggahaitvā uc‍cārampi passāvampi nikkhāmainti| addasā khau bhagavā saināsanacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantau tai bhikkhū taṃ bhikkhuṃ pariggahaitvā uc‍cārampi passāvampi nikkhāmaintai, disvāna yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ imassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ābādhau’’ti? ‘‘imassa, bhantai, āyasmatau cakkhuraugābādhau| imaṃ mayaṃ pariggahaitvā uc‍cārampi passāvampi nikkhāmaimā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, añ‍janaṃ – kāḷañ‍janaṃ, rasañ‍janaṃ, sautañ‍janaṃ, gairukaṃ, kapal‍la’’nti| añ‍janūpapisanaihi atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, candanaṃ, tagaraṃ, kāḷānusāriyaṃ, tālīsaṃ, bhaddamuttakanti| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū piṭṭhāni añ‍janāni carukaisupi thālakaisupi (sī॰ syā॰) sarāvakaisupi nikkhipanti; tiṇacuṇṇaihipi paṃsukaihipi aukiriyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, añ‍janinti|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāvacā añ‍janiyau dhārainti – sauvaṇṇamayaṃ, rūpiyamayaṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, uc‍cāvacā añ‍janī dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhimayaṃ, dantamayaṃ, visāṇamayaṃ, naḷamayaṃ, vaiḷumayaṃ, kaṭṭhamayaṃ, jatumayaṃ, phalamayaṃ, lauhamayaṃ, saṅkhanābhimayanti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍janiyau apārutā haunti, tiṇacuṇṇaihipi paṃsukaihipi aukiriyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, apidhānanti| apidhānaṃ nipatati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, suttakaina bandhitvā añ‍janiyā bandhitunti| añ‍janī phalati nipatati (ka॰)| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, suttakaina sibbaitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū aṅguliyā añ‍janti, akkhīni dukkhāni haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, añ‍janisalākanti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāvacā añ‍janisalākāyau dhārainti – sauvaṇṇamayaṃ rūpiyamayaṃ| manussā  ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti, ‘‘saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, uc‍cāvacā añ‍janisalākā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhimayaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhanābhimayanti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍janisalākā bhūmiyaṃ patitā pharusā hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, salākaṭhāniyanti salākaudhāniyanti (sī॰ syā॰)|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū añ‍janimpi añ‍janisalākampi hatthaina pariharanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, añ‍janitthavikanti| aṃsabaddhakau na hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṃsabaddhakaṃ bandhanasuttakanti|

266. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmatau pilindavacchassa sīsābhitāpau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, muddhani tailakanti| nakkhamaniyau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, natthukammanti| natthu galati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, natthukaraṇinti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāvacā natthukaraṇiyau dhārainti – sauvaṇṇamayaṃ rūpiyamayaṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti, ‘‘saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, uc‍cāvacā natthukaraṇī dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhimayaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhanābhimayanti| natthuṃ visamaṃ āsiñ‍canti natthu visamaṃ āsiñ‍ciyati (sī॰ syā॰)| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yamakanatthukaraṇinti| nakkhamaniyau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dhūmaṃ pātunti| tañ‍ñaiva vaṭṭiṃ ālimpaitvā pivanti, kaṇṭhau dahati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dhūmanaittanti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāvacāni dhūmanaittāni dhārainti – sauvaṇṇamayaṃ rūpiyamayaṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, uc‍cāvacāni dhūmanaittāni dhāraitabbāni| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhimayaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhanābhimayanti|

taina khau pana samayaina dhūmanaittāni apārutāni haunti, pāṇakā pavisanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, apidhānanti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū dhūmanaittāni hatthaina pariharanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dhūmanaittathavikanti| aikatau ghaṃsiyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yamakathavikanti| aṃsabaddhakau na hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṃsabaddhakaṃ bandhanasuttakanti|

267. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmatau pilindavacchassa vātābādhau  hauti| vaij‍jā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘tailaṃ pacitabba’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tailapākanti| tasmiṃ khau pana tailapākai maj‍jaṃ pakkhipitabbaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tailapākai maj‍jaṃ pakkhipitunti|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū atipakkhittamaj‍jāni atikhittamaj‍jāni (ka॰) tailāni pacanti, tāni pivitvā maj‍janti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, atipakkhittamaj‍jaṃ tailaṃ pātabbaṃ| yau pivaiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ tailapākai maj‍jassa na vaṇṇau na gandhau na rasau pañ‍ñāyati, aivarūpaṃ maj‍japakkhittaṃ tailaṃ pātunti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhūnaṃ bahuṃ atipakkhittamaj‍jaṃ tailaṃ pak‍kaṃ hauti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ  aitadahausi – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau atipakkhittamaj‍jai tailai paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, abbhañ‍janaṃ adhiṭṭhātunti|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau pilindavacchassa bahutaraṃ tailaṃ pak‍kaṃ hauti, tailabhājanaṃ na vij‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tīṇi tumbāni – lauhatumbaṃ, kaṭṭhatumbaṃ, phalatumbanti|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau pilindavacchassa aṅgavātau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saidakammanti| nakkhamaniyau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sambhārasaidanti| nakkhamaniyau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mahāsaidanti| nakkhamaniyau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhaṅgaudakanti| nakkhamaniyau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakakauṭṭhakanti|

taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmatau pilindavacchassa pabbavātau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, lauhitaṃ  maucaitunti| nakkhamaniyau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, lauhitaṃ maucaitvā visāṇaina gāhaitunti gahaitunti (sī॰ syā॰)|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau pilindavacchassa pādā phalitā phālitā (ka॰) haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pādabbhañ‍jananti| nakkhamaniyau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paj‍jaṃ abhisaṅkharitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau gaṇḍābādhau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, satthakammanti| kasāvaudakaina atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kasāvaudakanti| tilakak‍kaina atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tilakak‍kanti| kabaḷikāya atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kabaḷikanti| vaṇabandhanacauḷaina atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vaṇabandhanacauḷanti| vaṇau kaṇḍuvati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sāsapakuṭṭaina sāsapakuḍḍaina (sī॰ syā॰) phausitunti| vaṇau kilij‍jittha| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dhūmaṃ  kātunti| vaḍḍhamaṃsaṃ vuṭṭhāti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, lauṇasakkharikāya chinditunti| vaṇau na ruhati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vaṇatailanti| tailaṃ galati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vikāsikaṃ sabbaṃ vaṇapaṭikammanti|

268. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu ahinā daṭṭhau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cattāri mahāvikaṭāni dātuṃ – gūthaṃ, muttaṃ, chārikaṃ, mattikanti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘appaṭiggahitāni nu khau udāhu paṭiggahaitabbānī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sati kappiyakārakai paṭiggahāpaituṃ, asati kappiyakārakai sāmaṃ gahaitvā paribhuñ‍jitunti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraina bhikkhunā visaṃ pītaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi bhikkhavai gūthaṃ pāyaitunti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘appaṭiggahitaṃ nu khau udāhu paṭiggahaitabbau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yaṃ karauntau paṭiggaṇhāti, svaiva paṭiggahau katau, na puna katau pana (?) paṭiggahaitabbauti|

269. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau gharadin‍nakābādhau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sītālauḷiṃ pāyaitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu duṭṭhagahaṇikau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āmisakhāraṃ pāyaitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau paṇḍuraugābādhau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, muttaharītakaṃ pāyaitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau chavidausābādhau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gandhālaipaṃ kātunti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu abhisan‍nakāyau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, viraicanaṃ pātunti| acchakañ‍jiyā atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, acchakañ‍jinti| akaṭayūsaina atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, akaṭayūsanti| kaṭākaṭaina atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kaṭākaṭanti| paṭicchādanīyaina atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paṭicchādanīyanti|

mūlādibhaisaj‍jakathā niṭṭhitā|

162. pilindavacchavatthu

270. idaṃ vatthu pārā॰ 618 ādayau taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā pilindavacchau rājagahai pabbhāraṃ saudhāpaiti laiṇaṃ kattukāmau| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau  bimbisārau yaināyasmā pilindavacchau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ pilindavacchaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau āyasmantaṃ pilindavacchaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, thairau kārāpaitī’’ti? ‘‘pabbhāraṃ, mahārāja, saudhāpaimi, laiṇaṃ kattukāmau’’ti| ‘‘atthau, bhantai, ayyassa  ārāmikainā’’ti? ‘‘na khau, mahārāja, bhagavatā ārāmikau anuñ‍ñātau’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ paṭipucchitvā mama āraucaiyyāthā’’ti| ‘aivaṃ, mahārājā’ti khau āyasmā pilindavacchau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa pac‍cassausi| atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi, samādapaisi, samuttaijaisi, sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau āyasmatā pilindavacchaina dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā āyasmantaṃ pilindavacchaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau bhagavatau santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘rājā, bhantai, māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau ārāmikaṃ dātukāmau| kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ārāmika’’nti| dutiyampi khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau yaināyasmā pilindavacchau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ pilindavacchaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau āyasmantaṃ pilindavacchaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘anuñ‍ñātau, bhantai, bhagavatā ārāmikau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, mahārājā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhantai, ayyassa ārāmikaṃ dammī’’ti| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau  āyasmatau pilindavacchassa ārāmikaṃ paṭissutvā, vissaritvā, ciraina satiṃ paṭilabhitvā, añ‍ñataraṃ sabbatthakaṃ mahāmattaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘yau mayā, bhaṇai, ayyassa ārāmikau paṭissutau, din‍nau sau ārāmikau’’ti? ‘‘na khau, daiva, ayyassa ārāmikau din‍nau’’ti| ‘‘kīva ciraṃ nu khau, bhaṇai, itau itau ratti (syā॰) hi taṃ hautī’’ti? atha khau sau  mahāmattau rattiyau gaṇaitvā vigaṇaitvā (sī॰) rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pañ‍ca, daiva, rattisatānī’’ti| taina hi, bhaṇai, ayyassa pañ‍ca ārāmikasatāni daihīti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau sau mahāmattau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa paṭissutvā āyasmatau pilindavacchassa pañ‍ca ārāmikasatāni pādāsi, pāṭiyaik‍kau gāmau nivisi| ‘ārāmikagāmakauti’pi naṃ āhaṃsu, ‘pilindagāmakau’tipi naṃ āhaṃsu|

271. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā pilindavacchau tasmiṃ gāmakai kulūpakau hauti| atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya pilindagāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| taina khau pana samayaina tasmiṃ gāmakai ussavau hauti| dārakā alaṅkatā mālākitā kīḷanti| atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau pilindagāmakai  sapadānaṃ piṇḍāya caramānau yaina añ‍ñatarassa ārāmikassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| taina khau pana samayaina tassā ārāmikiniyā dhītā añ‍ñai dārakai alaṅkatai mālākitai passitvā raudati – ‘mālaṃ mai daitha, alaṅkāraṃ mai daithā’ti| atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau taṃ ārāmikiniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissāyaṃ dārikā raudatī’’ti? ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, dārikā añ‍ñai dārakai alaṅkatai mālākitai passitvā raudati – ‘mālaṃ mai daitha, alaṅkāraṃ mai daithā’ti| kutau amhākaṃ duggatānaṃ mālā, kutau alaṅkārau’’ti? atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau añ‍ñataraṃ tiṇaṇḍupakaṃ gahaitvā taṃ ārāmikiniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘handimaṃ tiṇaṇḍupakaṃ tassā dārikāya sīsai paṭimuñ‍cā’’ti| atha khau sā ārāmikinī taṃ tiṇaṇḍupakaṃ gahaitvā tassā dārikāya sīsai paṭimuñ‍ci| sā ahausi suvaṇṇamālā abhirūpā, dassanīyā, pāsādikā; natthi tādisā rañ‍ñaupi antaipurai suvaṇṇamālā| manussā rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa āraucaisuṃ – ‘‘amukassa, daiva, ārāmikassa gharai suvaṇṇamālā abhirūpā, dassanīyā, pāsādikā; natthi tādisā daivassapi  antaipurai suvaṇṇamālā; kutau tassa duggatassa? nissaṃsayaṃ caurikāya ābhatā’’ti|

atha  khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau taṃ ārāmikakulaṃ bandhāpaisi| dutiyampi  khau āyasmā pilindavacchau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya pilindagāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| pilindagāmakai sapadānaṃ piṇḍāya caramānau yaina tassa ārāmikassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā paṭivissakai pucchi – ‘‘kahaṃ imaṃ ārāmikakulaṃ gata’’nti? ‘‘aitissā, bhantai, suvaṇṇamālāya kāraṇā rañ‍ñā bandhāpita’’nti| atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau yaina rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau yaināyasmā pilindavacchau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ pilindavacchaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau  rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ āyasmā pilindavacchau aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa, mahārāja, ārāmikakulaṃ bandhāpita’’nti? ‘‘tassa, bhantai, ārāmikassa gharai suvaṇṇamālā abhirūpā, dassanīyā, pāsādikā; natthi tādisā amhākampi antaipurai suvaṇṇamālā; kutau tassa duggatassa? nissaṃsayaṃ caurikāya ābhatā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā pilindavacchau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa pāsādaṃ suvaṇṇanti adhimuc‍ci; sau ahausi sabbasauvaṇṇamayau| ‘‘idaṃ pana tai, mahārāja, tāva bahuṃ suvaṇṇaṃ kutau’’ti? ‘añ‍ñātaṃ, bhantai, ayyassaivaisau  iddhānubhāvau’ti taṃ ārāmikakulaṃ muñ‍cāpaisi|

manussā ‘‘ayyaina kira pilindavacchaina sarājikāya parisāya uttarimanussadhammaṃ iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassita’’nti attamanā abhippasan‍nā āyasmatau pilindavacchassa pañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jāni abhihariṃsu, saiyyathidaṃ – sappiṃ, navanītaṃ, tailaṃ, madhuṃ sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu (ka॰), phāṇitaṃ| pakatiyāpi ca āyasmā pilindavacchau lābhī hauti pañ‍can‍naṃ bhaisaj‍jānaṃ; laddhaṃ laddhaṃ parisāya vissaj‍jaiti| parisā cassa hauti bāhul‍likā; laddhaṃ laddhaṃ kaulambaipi kauḷumbaipi (ka॰), ghaṭaipi, pūraitvā paṭisāmaiti; parissāvanānipi, thavikāyaupi, pūraitvā vātapānaisu laggaiti| tāni aulīnavilīnāni tiṭṭhanti| undūraihipi vihārā aukiṇṇavikiṇṇā haunti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘antaukauṭṭhāgārikā imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā, saiyyathāpi rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā, tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti  vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū aivarūpāya bāhul‍lāya caitaissantī’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū tai anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū aivarūpāya bāhul‍lāya caitaintī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavāti…pai॰… vigarahitvā dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yāni khau pana tāni gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ  paṭisāyanīyāni bhaisaj‍jāni, saiyyathidaṃ – sappi, navanītaṃ, tailaṃ, madhu, phāṇitaṃ, tāni paṭiggahaitvā sattāhaparamaṃ san‍nidhikārakaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbāni| taṃ atik‍kāmayatau yathādhammau kāraitabbau’’ti|

pilindavacchavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

bhaisaj‍jānuñ‍ñātabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau paṭhamau|

163. guḷādianujānanā

272. atha khau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā  yaina rājagahaṃ taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| addasā khau āyasmā kaṅkhāraivatau antarāmaggai guḷakaraṇaṃ, auk‍kamitvā guḷai piṭṭhampi chārikampi pakkhipantai, disvāna ‘‘akappiyau guḷau sāmisau, na kappati guḷau vikālai paribhuñ‍jitu’’nti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantau saparisau guḷaṃ na paribhuñ‍jati| yaipissa sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti, taipi guḷaṃ na paribhuñ‍janti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| kimatthāya kimatthiyā (ka॰), bhikkhavai, guḷai piṭṭhampi chārikampi pakkhipantīti? thaddhatthāya bandhanatthāya (sī॰ syā॰) bhagavāti| sacai, bhikkhavai, thaddhatthāya guḷai piṭṭhampi chārikampi pakkhipanti, sau ca guḷautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yathāsukhaṃ guḷaṃ paribhuñ‍jitunti|

addasā khau āyasmā kaṅkhāraivatau antarāmaggai vac‍cai muggaṃ jātaṃ, passitvā ‘‘akappiyā muggā; pak‍kāpi muggā jāyantīti’’ kuk‍kuc‍cāyantau saparisau muggaṃ na paribhuñ‍jati| yaipissa sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti, taipi muggaṃ na paribhuñ‍janti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| sacai sacaipi (?), bhikkhavai, pak‍kāpi muggā jāyanti, anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yathāsukhaṃ muggaṃ paribhuñ‍jitunti|

273. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau udaravātābādhau hauti| sau lauṇasauvīrakaṃ apāyi| tassa sau udaravātābādhau paṭippassambhi| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānassa lauṇasauvīrakaṃ; agilānassa udakasambhin‍naṃ pānaparibhaugaina paribhuñ‍jitunti|

guḷādianujānanā niṭṭhitā|

164. antauvuṭṭhādipaṭikkhaipakathā

274. atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina rājagahaṃ tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavatau udaravātābādhau hauti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau – ‘pubbaipi bhagavatau udaravātābādhau taikaṭulayāguyā phāsu hautī’ti – sāmaṃ tilampi, taṇḍulampi, muggampi viñ‍ñāpaitvā, antau vāsaitvā, antau sāmaṃ pacitvā bhagavatau upanāmaisi – ‘‘pivatu bhagavā taikaṭulayāgu’’nti| jānantāpi tathāgatā pucchanti, jānantāpi na pucchanti; kālaṃ viditvā pucchanti, kālaṃ viditvā na pucchanti; atthasaṃhitaṃ tathāgatā pucchanti, nau anatthasaṃhitaṃ| anatthasaṃhitai saitughātau tathāgatānaṃ| dvīhi ākāraihi buddhā bhagavantau bhikkhū paṭipucchanti – dhammaṃ vā daisaissāma, sāvakānaṃ vā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmāti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ  ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kutāyaṃ, ānanda, yāgū’’ti? atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘ananucchavikaṃ, ānanda, ananulaumikaṃ, appatirūpaṃ, assāmaṇakaṃ, akappiyaṃ, akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, ānanda, aivarūpāya bāhul‍lāya caitaissasi| yadapi, ānanda, antau vuṭṭhaṃ vutthaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) tadapi akappiyaṃ; yadapi antau pak‍kaṃ tadapi akappiyaṃ; yadapi sāmaṃ pak‍kaṃ, tadapi akappiyaṃ| naitaṃ, ānanda, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, antau vuṭṭhaṃ, antau pak‍kaṃ, sāmaṃ pak‍kaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau paribhuñ‍jaiya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| antau cai, bhikkhavai, vuṭṭhaṃ, antau pak‍kaṃ, sāmaṃ pak‍kaṃ tañ‍cai paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti tiṇṇaṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ| antau cai, bhikkhavai, vuṭṭhaṃ, antau pak‍kaṃ, añ‍ñaihi pak‍kaṃ, tañ‍cai paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ| antau  cai, bhikkhavai, vuṭṭhaṃ, bahi pak‍kaṃ, sāmaṃ pak‍kaṃ, tañ‍cai paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ| bahi cai, bhikkhavai, vuṭṭhaṃ, antau pak‍kaṃ, sāmaṃ pak‍kaṃ, tañ‍cai paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti dvin‍naṃ duk‍kaṭānaṃ| antau cai, bhikkhavai, vuṭṭhaṃ, bahi pak‍kaṃ, añ‍ñaihi pak‍kaṃ, tañ‍cai paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bahi cai, bhikkhavai, vuṭṭhaṃ, antau pak‍kaṃ, añ‍ñaihi pak‍kaṃ, tañ‍cai paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bahi cai, bhikkhavai, vuṭṭhaṃ, bahi pak‍kaṃ, sāmaṃ pak‍kaṃ, tañ‍cai paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bahi cai, bhikkhavai, vuṭṭhaṃ, bahi pak‍kaṃ, añ‍ñaihi pak‍kaṃ, tañ‍cai paribhuñ‍jaiyya, anāpattī’’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū ‘‘bhagavatā sāmaṃpākau paṭikkhittau’’ti puna pākai kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, puna pākaṃ pacitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina rājagahaṃ dubbhikkhaṃ hauti| manussā lauṇampi, tailampi, taṇḍulampi, khādanīyampi ārāmaṃ āharanti| tāni bhikkhū bahi vāsainti; uk‍kapiṇḍakāpi khādanti, caurāpi haranti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, antau vāsaitunti| antau vāsaitvā bahi pācainti| damakā parivārainti| bhikkhū avissaṭṭhā paribhuñ‍janti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, antau pacitunti| dubbhikkhai kappiyakārakā bahutaraṃ haranti, appataraṃ bhikkhūnaṃ dainti| bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sāmaṃ pacituṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, antau vuṭṭhaṃ, antau pak‍kaṃ, sāmaṃ pak‍kanti|

antauvuṭṭhādipaṭikkhaipakathā niṭṭhitā|

165. uggahitapaṭiggahaṇā
