275. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū kāsīsu vassaṃvuṭṭhā rājagahaṃ gacchantā bhagavantaṃ dassanāya antarāmaggai na labhiṃsu lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ; bahuñ‍ca phalakhādanīyaṃ  ahausi; kappiyakārakau ca na ahausi| atha khau tai bhikkhū kilantarūpā yaina rājagahaṃ vaiḷuvanaṃ kalandakanivāpau, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| āciṇṇaṃ khau panaitaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ āgantukaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ paṭisammaudituṃ| atha khau bhagavā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhikkhavai, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍cittha appakilamathaina addhānaṃ āgatā; kutau ca tumhai, bhikkhavai, āgacchathā’’ti? ‘‘khamanīyaṃ bhagavā, yāpanīyaṃ bhagavā| idha mayaṃ, bhantai, kāsīsu vassaṃvuṭṭhā rājagahaṃ āgacchantā bhagavantaṃ dassanāya antarāmaggai na labhimhā lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ; bahuñ‍ca phalakhādanīyaṃ  ahausi; kappiyakārakau ca na ahausi; taina mayaṃ kilantarūpā addhānaṃ āgatā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yattha phalakhādanīyaṃ passati, kappiyakārakau ca na hauti, sāmaṃ gahaitvā, haritvā, kappiyakārakai passitvā, bhūmiyaṃ nikkhipitvā, paṭiggahāpaitvā paribhuñ‍jituṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uggahitaṃ paṭiggahitu’’nti|

276. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa brāhmaṇassa navā ca tilā navañ‍ca madhu uppan‍nā haunti| atha khau tassa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi  – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ navai ca tilai navañ‍ca madhuṃ buddhappamukhassa bhikkhusaṅghassa dadaiyya’’nti| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ paṭisammaudi, sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ, saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhau gautama, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina tassa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi, saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā, samādapaitvā, samuttaijaitvā, sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

atha  khau tassa brāhmaṇassa acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘yaisaṃ khau mayā atthāya buddhappamukhau bhikkhusaṅghau nimantitau, ‘navai ca tilai navañ‍ca madhuṃ dassāmī’ti, tai mayā pamuṭṭhā dātuṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ navai ca tilai navañ‍ca madhuṃ kaulambaihi ca ghaṭaihi ca ārāmaṃ harāpaiyya’’nti| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau navai ca tilai navañ‍ca madhuṃ kaulambaihi ca ghaṭaihi ca ārāmaṃ harāpaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yaisaṃ khau mayā, bhau gautama, atthāya buddhappamukhau bhikkhusaṅghau nimantitau, ‘navai ca tilai navañ‍ca madhuṃ dassāmī’ti, tai mayā pamuṭṭhā dātuṃ| paṭiggaṇhātu mai bhavaṃ gautamau navai ca tilai navañ‍ca madhu’’nti| taina hi, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhūnaṃ daihīti| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū dubbhikkhai appamattakaipi pavārainti, paṭisaṅkhāpi paṭikkhipanti, sabbau ca saṅghau pavāritau hauti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na paṭiggaṇhanti| paṭiggaṇhatha, bhikkhavai, paribhuñ‍jatha| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tatau nīhaṭaṃ bhuttāvinā pavāritaina anatirittaṃ paribhuñ‍jitunti|

uggahitapaṭiggahaṇā niṭṭhitā|

166. paṭiggahitādianujānanā

277. pāci॰ 295 taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa upaṭṭhākakulaṃ saṅghassatthāya khādanīyaṃ pāhaisi – ayyassa upanandassa dassaitvā saṅghassa dātabbanti| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau  gāmaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhau hauti| atha khau tai manussā ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhū pucchiṃsu – ‘‘kahaṃ, bhantai, ayyau upanandau’’ti? ‘‘aisāvusau, āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau gāmaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhau’’ti| ‘‘idaṃ, bhantai, khādanīyaṃ ayyassa upanandassa dassaitvā saṅghassa dātabba’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| taina hi, bhikkhavai, paṭiggahaitvā nikkhipatha yāva upanandau āgacchatīti| atha khau āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau puraibhattaṃ kulāni payirupāsitvā divā āgacchati| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū dubbhikkhai appamattakaipi pavārainti, paṭisaṅkhāpi paṭikkhipanti, sabbau ca saṅghau pavāritau hauti, bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na paṭiggaṇhanti| paṭiggaṇhatha, bhikkhavai, paribhuñ‍jatha| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, puraibhattaṃ paṭiggahitaṃ bhuttāvinā pavāritaina anatirittaṃ paribhuñ‍jitunti|

278. atha  khau bhagavā rājagahai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina sāvatthi taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina sāvatthi tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau sāriputtassa kāyaḍāhābādhau hauti| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pubbai tai, āvusau sāriputta, kāyaḍāhābādhau kaina phāsu hautī’’ti? ‘‘bhisaihi ca mai, āvusau, muḷālikāhi cā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau sammiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva jaitavanai antarahitau mandākiniyā paukkharaṇiyā tīrai pāturahausi| addasā khau añ‍ñatarau nāgau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ dūratauva  āgacchantaṃ, disvāna āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitu khau, bhantai, ayyau mahāmauggal‍lānau| svāgataṃ, bhantai, ayyassa mahāmauggal‍lānassa| kaina, bhantai, ayyassa atthau; kiṃ dammī’’ti? ‘‘bhisaihi ca mai, āvusau, atthau, muḷālikāhi cā’’ti| atha khau sau nāgau añ‍ñataraṃ nāgaṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhaṇai, ayyassa bhisai ca muḷālikāyau ca yāvadatthaṃ daihī’’ti| atha khau sau nāgau mandākiniṃ paukkharaṇiṃ augāhaitvā, sauṇḍāya bhisañ‍ca muḷālikañ‍ca abbāhitvā, suvikkhālitaṃ  vikkhālaitvā, bhaṇḍikaṃ bandhitvā yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – mandākiniyā paukkharaṇiyā tīrai antarahitau jaitavanai pāturahausi| saupi khau nāgau mandākiniyā paukkharaṇiyā tīrai antarahitau jaitavanai pāturahausi| atha khau sau nāgau āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa bhisai ca muḷālikāyau  ca paṭiggahāpaitvā jaitavanai antarahitau mandākiniyā paukkharaṇiyā tīrai pāturahausi| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhisai ca muḷālikāyau ca upanāmaisi| atha khau āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhisai ca muḷālikāyau ca bhuttassa kāyaḍāhābādhau paṭippassambhi| bahū bhisā ca  muḷālikāyau ca avasiṭṭhā haunti| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū dubbhikkhai appamattakaipi pavārainti, paṭisaṅkhāpi paṭikkhipanti, sabbau ca saṅghau pavāritau hauti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na paṭiggaṇhanti| paṭiggaṇhatha, bhikkhavai, paribhuñ‍jatha| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vanaṭṭhaṃ paukkharaṭṭhaṃ bhuttāvinā pavāritaina anatirittaṃ paribhuñ‍jitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ bahuṃ phalakhādanīyaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti, kappiyakārakau ca na hauti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā phalaṃ na paribhuñ‍janti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, abījaṃ nibbattabījaṃ nibbaṭṭabījaṃ (sī॰), nibbaṭabījaṃ (syā॰), nippaṭṭabījaṃ (ka॰) akatakappaṃ phalaṃ paribhuñ‍jitunti|

paṭiggahitādi anujānanā niṭṭhitā|

167. satthakammapaṭikkhaipakathā

279. atha khau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina rājagahaṃ taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina rājagahaṃ tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau bhagandalābādhau  hauti| ākāsagauttau vaij‍jau satthakammaṃ  karauti| atha khau bhagavā saināsanacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantau yaina tassa bhikkhunau vihārau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau ākāsagauttau vaij‍jau bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ, disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āgacchatu bhavaṃ gautamau, imassa bhikkhunau vac‍camaggaṃ passatu, saiyyathāpi gaudhāmukha’’nti| atha khau bhagavā – ‘‘sau maṃ khvāyaṃ maughapurisau uppaṇḍaitī’’ti – tatauva paṭinivattitvā, aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā, bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘atthi kira, bhikkhavai, amukasmiṃ vihārai bhikkhu gilānau’’ti? ‘‘atthi bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ābādhau’’ti? ‘‘tassa, bhantai, āyasmatau bhagandalābādhau, ākāsagauttau vaij‍jau satthakammaṃ karautī’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, bhikkhavai, tassa maughapurisassa, ananulaumikaṃ, appatirūpaṃ, assāmaṇakaṃ, akappiyaṃ, akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma sau, bhikkhavai, maughapurisau sambādhai satthakammaṃ kārāpaissati| sambādhai, bhikkhavai, sukhumā chavi, duraupayau vaṇau, dupparihāraṃ satthaṃ| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, sambādhai satthakammaṃ kārāpaitabbaṃ| yau kārāpaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū – bhagavatā satthakammaṃ paṭikkhittanti – vatthikammaṃ kārāpainti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā, tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma  chabbaggiyā bhikkhū vatthikammaṃ kārāpaissantī’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū vatthikammaṃ kārāpaintī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, sambādhassa sāmantā dvaṅgulā satthakammaṃ vā vatthikammaṃ vā kārāpaitabbaṃ| yau kārāpaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

satthakammapaṭikkhaipakathā niṭṭhitā|

168. manussamaṃsapaṭikkhaipakathā

280. atha khau bhagavā rājagahai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina bārāṇasī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina bārāṇasī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati isipatanai migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina bārāṇasiyaṃ suppiyau ca upāsakau suppiyā  ca upāsikā ubhataupasan‍nā haunti, dāyakā, kārakā, saṅghupaṭṭhākā| atha khau suppiyā upāsikā ārāmaṃ gantvā vihāraina vihāraṃ parivaiṇaina parivaiṇaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhū pucchati – ‘‘kau, bhantai, gilānau, kassa kiṃ āhariyatū’’ti? taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraina bhikkhunā viraicanaṃ  pītaṃ hauti| atha khau sau bhikkhu suppiyaṃ upāsikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mayā khau, bhagini, viraicanaṃ pītaṃ| atthau mai paṭicchādanīyainā’’ti| ‘‘suṭṭhu, ayya, āhariyissatī’’ti gharaṃ gantvā antaivāsiṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha, bhaṇai, pavattamaṃsaṃ jānāhī’’ti| aivaṃ, ayyaiti khau sau purisau suppiyāya  upāsikāya paṭissuṇitvā kaivalakappaṃ bārāṇasiṃ āhiṇḍantau na addasa pavattamaṃsaṃ| atha khau sau purisau yaina suppiyā upāsikā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā suppiyaṃ upāsikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘natthayyai pavattamaṃsaṃ| māghātau aj‍jā’’ti| atha khau suppiyāya upāsikāya aitadahausi – ‘‘tassa khau gilānassa bhikkhunau paṭicchādanīyaṃ alabhantassa ābādhau vā abhivaḍḍhissati, kālaṅkiriyā  vā bhavissati| na khau maitaṃ patirūpaṃ yāhaṃ paṭissuṇitvā na harāpaiyya’’nti| pautthanikaṃ gahaitvā ūrumaṃsaṃ uk‍kantitvā dāsiyā adāsi – ‘‘handa, jai, imaṃ maṃsaṃ sampādaitvā amukasmiṃ vihārai bhikkhu gilānau, tassa daj‍jāhi| yau ca maṃ pucchati, ‘gilānā’ti paṭivaidaihī’’ti| uttarāsaṅgaina ūruṃ vaiṭhaitvā auvarakaṃ pavisitvā mañ‍cakai nipaj‍ji| atha khau suppiyau upāsakau gharaṃ gantvā dāsiṃ pucchi – ‘‘kahaṃ suppiyā’’ti? ‘‘aisāyya auvarakai nipan‍nā’’ti| atha khau suppiyau upāsakau yaina suppiyā upāsikā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā suppiyaṃ upāsikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa nipan‍nāsī’’ti? ‘‘gilānāmhī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ tai ābādhau’’ti? atha khau suppiyā upāsikā suppiyassa upāsakassa aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| atha khau suppiyau upāsakau – acchariyaṃ vata bhau! abbhutaṃ vata bhau! yāva saddhāyaṃ suppiyā pasan‍nā, yatra hi nāma attanaupi maṃsāni paric‍cattāni! kimpimāya kiṃ panimāya (sī॰ syā॰) añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍ci  adaiyyaṃ bhavissatīti – haṭṭhau udaggau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau suppiyau upāsakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya bhattaṃ, saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau suppiyau upāsakau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau suppiyau upāsakau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina suppiyassa  upāsakassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi, saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau suppiyau upāsakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau suppiyaṃ upāsakaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kahaṃ suppiyā’’ti? ‘‘gilānā bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi āgacchatū’’ti| ‘‘na bhagavā ussahatī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi pariggahaitvāpi ānaithā’’ti| atha khau suppiyau upāsakau suppiyaṃ upāsikaṃ pariggahaitvā  ānaisi| tassā, saha dassanaina bhagavatau, tāva mahāvaṇau ruḷahau ahausi, succhavilaumajātau| atha khau suppiyau ca upāsakau suppiyā ca upāsikā – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau! abbhutaṃ vata bhau! tathāgatassa mahiddhikatā  mahānubhāvatā, yatra hi nāma saha dassanaina bhagavatau tāva mahāvaṇau ruḷahau bhavissati, succhavilaumajātau’’ti – haṭṭhā udaggā buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| atha khau bhagavā suppiyañ‍ca upāsakaṃ suppiyañ‍ca upāsikaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū

paṭipucchi – ‘‘kau, bhikkhavai, suppiyaṃ upāsikaṃ maṃsaṃ viñ‍ñāpaisī’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, suppiyaṃ upāsikaṃ maṃsaṃ viñ‍ñāpaisi’’nti| ‘‘āhariyittha bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘āhariyittha bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘paribhuñ‍ji tvaṃ bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘paribhuñ‍jāmahaṃ bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘paṭivaikkhi tvaṃ bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ bhagavā paṭivaikkhi’’nti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, appaṭivaikkhitvā maṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jissasi| manussamaṃsaṃ khau tayā, maughapurisa, paribhuttaṃ| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, manussā saddhā pasan‍nā, taihi attanaupi maṃsāni paric‍cattāni| na, bhikkhavai, manussamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, appaṭivaikkhitvā maṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau  paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

manussamaṃsapaṭikkhaipakathā niṭṭhitā|

169. hatthimaṃsādipaṭikkhaipakathā

281. taina khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñau hatthī maranti| manussā dubbhikkhai hatthimaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍janti, bhikkhūnaṃ piṇḍāya carantānaṃ hatthimaṃsaṃ dainti| bhikkhū hatthimaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍janti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā hatthimaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jissanti| rājaṅgaṃ hatthī, sacai rājā jānaiyya, na naisaṃ attamanau assā’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, hatthimaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñau assā maranti| manussā dubbhikkhai assamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍janti, bhikkhūnaṃ piṇḍāya carantānaṃ assamaṃsaṃ dainti| bhikkhū assamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍janti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā assamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jissanti| rājaṅgaṃ assā, sacai rājā jānaiyya, na naisaṃ attamanau assā’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, assamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina manussā dubbhikkhai sunakhamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍janti, bhikkhūnaṃ piṇḍāya carantānaṃ sunakhamaṃsaṃ dainti| bhikkhū sunakhamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍janti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā sunakhamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jissanti, jaigucchau  sunakhau paṭikūlau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, sunakhamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina manussā dubbhikkhai ahimaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍janti, bhikkhūnaṃ piṇḍāya carantānaṃ ahimaṃsaṃ dainti| bhikkhū ahimaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍janti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā ahimaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jissanti, jaigucchau ahi paṭikūlau’’ti| supassaupi suphassau (sī॰) nāgarājā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau supassau nāgarājā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘santi, bhantai, nāgā assaddhā appasan‍nā| tai appamattakaihipi bhikkhū vihaiṭhaiyyuṃ| sādhu, bhantai, ayyā ahimaṃsaṃ na paribhuñ‍jaiyyu’’nti| atha khau bhagavā supassaṃ nāgarājānaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi…pai॰… padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ  katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, ahimaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina luddakā sīhaṃ hantvā sīhamaṃsaṃ maṃsaṃ (ka॰) paribhuñ‍janti, bhikkhūnaṃ piṇḍāya  carantānaṃ sīhamaṃsaṃ dainti| bhikkhū sīhamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jitvā arañ‍ñai viharanti| sīhā sīhamaṃsagandhaina bhikkhū paripātainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, sīhamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina luddakā byagghaṃ hantvā…pai॰… dīpiṃ hantvā…pai॰… acchaṃ hantvā…pai॰… taracchaṃ hantvā taracchamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍janti, bhikkhūnaṃ piṇḍāya carantānaṃ  taracchamaṃsaṃ dainti| bhikkhū taracchamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jitvā arañ‍ñai viharanti| taracchā taracchamaṃsagandhaina bhikkhū paripātainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, taracchamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

hatthimaṃsādipaṭikkhaipakathā niṭṭhitā|

suppiyabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau dutiyau|

170. yāgumadhugauḷakānujānanā

282. atha khau bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina andhakavindaṃ taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi, mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ, aḍḍhatailasaihi bhikkhusataihi| taina khau pana samayaina jānapadā manussā bahuṃ lauṇampi, tailampi, taṇḍulampi, khādanīyampi sakaṭaisu āraupaitvā buddhappamukhassa bhikkhusaṅghassa piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhā haunti – yadā paṭipāṭiṃ labhissāma tadā bhattaṃ karissāmāti, pañ‍camattāni ca vighāsādasatāni| atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina andhakavindaṃ tadavasari| atha khau añ‍ñatarassa brāhmaṇassa paṭipāṭiṃ alabhantassa aitadahausi – ‘‘atītāni adhikāni (sī॰ syā॰) khau mai dvai māsāni buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ anubandhantassa ‘yadā paṭipāṭiṃ labhissāmi tadā bhattaṃ karissāmī’ti, na ca mai paṭipāṭi labbhati, ahañ‍camhi aikattakau aikatau (sī॰ syā॰), bahu ca mai gharāvāsatthau hāyati| yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhattaggaṃ aulaukaiyyaṃ; yaṃ bhattaggai nāssa, taṃ paṭiyādaiyya’’nti| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau bhattaggaṃ aulaukaintau dvai nāddasa – yāguñ‍ca  madhugauḷakañ‍ca| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha mai, bhau ānanda, paṭipāṭiṃ alabhantassa aitadahausi ‘atītāni khau mai  dvai māsāni buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ anubandhantassa, yadā paṭipāṭiṃ labhissāmi tadā bhattaṃ karissāmīti| na ca mai paṭipāṭi labbhati, ahañ‍camhi aikattakau, bahu ca mai gharāvāsatthau hāyati| yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhattaggaṃ aulaukaiyyaṃ; yaṃ bhattaggai nāssa, taṃ paṭiyādaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhau ānanda, bhattaggaṃ aulaukaintau dvai nāddasaṃ – yāguñ‍ca madhugauḷakañ‍ca| sacāhaṃ, bhau ānanda, paṭiyādaiyyaṃ yāguñ‍ca madhugauḷakañ‍ca, paṭiggaṇhaiyya mai bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti? ‘‘taina hi, brāhmaṇa, bhagavantaṃ paṭipucchissāmī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| taina hānanda, paṭiyādaitūti| taina hi, brāhmaṇa, paṭiyādaihīti| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pahūtaṃ yāguñ‍ca madhugauḷakañ‍ca paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau upanāmaisi – paṭiggaṇhātu mai bhavaṃ gautamau yāguñ‍ca madhugauḷakañ‍cāti| taina hi, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhūnaṃ daihīti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na paṭiggaṇhanti| paṭiggaṇhatha, bhikkhavai, paribhuñ‍jathāti| atha khau sau brāhmaṇau buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pahūtāya yāguyā ca madhugauḷakaina ca sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā bhagavantaṃ dhautahatthaṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ  bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘dasayimai, brāhmaṇa, ānisaṃsā yāguyā| katamai dasa? yāguṃ daintau āyuṃ daiti, vaṇṇaṃ daiti, sukhaṃ daiti, balaṃ daiti, paṭibhānaṃ daiti, yāgu pītā khuddaṃ khudaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) paṭihanati, pipāsaṃ vinaiti, vātaṃ anulaumaiti, vatthiṃ saudhaiti, āmāvasaisaṃ pācaiti – imai khau, brāhmaṇa, dasānisaṃsā yāguyā’’ti pacchimā pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā a॰ ni॰ 5.207|

a॰ ni॰ 4.58-59 thaukaṃ visadisaṃ yau sañ‍ñatānaṃ paradattabhaujinaṃ|

kālaina sak‍kac‍ca dadāti yāguṃ|

dasassa ṭhānāni anuppavaicchati|

āyuñ‍ca vaṇṇañ‍ca sukhaṃ balañ‍ca||

paṭibhānamassa  upajāyatai tatau|

khuddaṃ pipāsañ‍ca byapanaiti vātaṃ|

saudhaiti vatthiṃ pariṇāmaiti bhuttaṃ|

bhaisaj‍jamaitaṃ sugataina vaṇṇitaṃ||

tasmā hi yāguṃ alamaiva dātuṃ|

nic‍caṃ manussaina sukhatthikaina|

dibbāni  vā patthayatā sukhāni|

manussasaubhagyatamicchatā vāti||

atha  khau bhagavā taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ imāhi gāthāhi anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai  dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yāguñ‍ca madhugauḷakañ‍cā’’ti|

yāgumadhugauḷakānujānanā niṭṭhitā|

171. taruṇapasan‍namahāmattavatthu

283. assausuṃ khau manussā bhagavatā kira yāgu anuñ‍ñātā madhugauḷakañ‍cāti| tai kālassaiva, bhauj‍jayāguṃ paṭiyādainti madhugauḷakañ‍ca| bhikkhū kālassaiva bhauj‍jayāguyā dhātā madhugauḷakaina ca bhattaggai na cittarūpaṃ paribhuñ‍janti| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraina taruṇapasan‍naina mahāmattaina svātanāya buddhappamukhau bhikkhusaṅghau nimantitau hauti| atha khau tassa taruṇapasan‍nassa mahāmattassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ aḍḍhatailasan‍naṃ bhikkhusatānaṃ aḍḍhatailasāni maṃsapātisatāni paṭiyādaiyyaṃ, aikamaikassa bhikkhunau aikamaikaṃ maṃsapātiṃ upanāmaiyya’’nti| atha khau sau taruṇapasan‍nau mahāmattau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā aḍḍhatailasāni ca maṃsapātisatāni, bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina tassa taruṇapasan‍nassa mahāmattassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi, saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau sau taruṇapasan‍nau mahāmattau bhattaggai bhikkhū parivisati| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘thaukaṃ, āvusau, daihi; thaukaṃ, āvusau, daihī’’ti| ‘‘mā khau tumhai, bhantai, – ‘ayaṃ taruṇapasan‍nau mahāmattau’ti  – thaukaṃ thaukaṃ paṭiggaṇhatha| bahuṃ mai khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyattaṃ, aḍḍhatailasāni ca maṃsapātisatāni| aikamaikassa bhikkhunau aikamaikaṃ maṃsapātiṃ upanāmaissāmīti| paṭiggaṇhatha, bhantai, yāvadattha’’nti| ‘‘na khau mayaṃ, āvusau, aitaṃkāraṇā thaukaṃ thaukaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma, api ca mayaṃ kālassaiva bhauj‍jayāguyā dhātā madhugauḷakaina ca| taina mayaṃ thaukaṃ thaukaṃ paṭiggaṇhāmā’’ti| atha khau sau taruṇapasan‍nau mahāmattau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhadantā mayā nimantitā añ‍ñassa bhauj‍jayāguṃ paribhuñ‍jissanti, na cāhaṃ paṭibalau yāvadatthaṃ dātu’’nti  kupitau anattamanau āsādanāpaikkhau bhikkhūnaṃ pattai pūraintau agamāsi – bhuñ‍jatha vā haratha vāti| atha khau sau taruṇapasan‍nau mahāmattau buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina  khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau taṃ taruṇapasan‍naṃ mahāmattaṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau tassa taruṇapasan‍nassa mahāmattassa acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau ahudaiva kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, ahu vippaṭisārau – ‘‘alābhā vata mai, na vata mai lābhā; dul‍laddhaṃ vata mai, na vata mai suladdhaṃ; yauhaṃ kupitau anattamanau āsādanāpaikkhau bhikkhūnaṃ  pattai pūraintau agamāsiṃ – ‘bhuñ‍jatha vā haratha vā’ti| kiṃ nu khau mayā bahuṃ pasutaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ vā apuñ‍ñaṃ vā’’ti? atha khau sau taruṇapasan‍nau mahāmattau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau taruṇapasan‍nau mahāmattau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau ahudaiva kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, ahu vippaṭisārau ‘alābhā vata mai, na vata mai lābhā; dul‍laddhaṃ vata mai, na vata mai suladdhaṃ; yauhaṃ kupitau anattamanau āsādanāpaikkhau bhikkhūnaṃ pattai pūraintau agamāsiṃ – bhuñ‍jatha vā haratha vāti| kiṃ nu khau mayā bahuṃ pasutaṃ, puñ‍ñaṃ vā apuñ‍ñaṃ vā’ti| kiṃ nu khau mayā, bhantai, bahuṃ pasutaṃ, puñ‍ñaṃ vā apuñ‍ñaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘yadaggaina tayā, āvusau, svātanāya buddhappamukhau bhikkhusaṅghau nimantitau tadaggaina tai bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasutaṃ| yadaggaina tai  aikamaikaina bhikkhunā aikamaikaṃ sitthaṃ paṭiggahitaṃ tadaggaina tai bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasutaṃ, saggā tai āraddhā’’ti| atha khau sau taruṇapasan‍nau mahāmattau – ‘‘lābhā kira mai, suladdhaṃ kira mai, bahuṃ kira mayā puñ‍ñaṃ pasutaṃ, saggā kira mai āraddhā’’ti – haṭṭhau udaggau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū  paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū añ‍ñatra nimantitā añ‍ñassa bhauj‍jayāguṃ paribhuñ‍jantī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā añ‍ñatra nimantitā añ‍ñassa bhauj‍jayāguṃ paribhuñ‍jissanti| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi  – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatra nimantitaina añ‍ñassa bhauj‍jayāgu paribhuñ‍jitabbā| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau’’ti|

taruṇapasan‍namahāmattavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

172. bailaṭṭhakac‍cānavatthu

284. atha  khau bhagavā andhakavindai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina rājagahaṃ taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi, mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ, aḍḍhatailasaihi bhikkhusataihi| taina khau pana samayaina bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau rājagahā andhakavindaṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau hauti, pañ‍camattaihi sakaṭasataihi, sabbaihaiva guḷakumbhapūraihi| addasā khau bhagavā bailaṭṭhaṃ kac‍cānaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ, disvāna maggā auk‍kamma añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisīdi| atha khau bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, aikamaikassa bhikkhunau aikamaikaṃ guḷakumbhaṃ dātu’’nti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, kac‍cāna, aikaṃyaiva guḷakumbhaṃ  āharā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā aikaṃyaiva guḷakumbhaṃ ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ābhatau āhaṭau (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰), bhantai, guḷakumbhau; kathāhaṃ, bhantai, paṭipaj‍jāmī’’ti? ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, kac‍cāna, bhikkhūnaṃ guḷaṃ daihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā  bhikkhūnaṃ guḷaṃ datvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘din‍nau, bhantai, bhikkhūnaṃ guḷau, bahu cāyaṃ guḷau avasiṭṭhau| kathāhaṃ, bhantai, paṭipaj‍jāmī’’ti? ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, kac‍cāna, bhikkhūnaṃ guḷaṃ yāvadatthaṃ daihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā bhikkhūnaṃ guḷaṃ yāvadatthaṃ datvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘din‍nau, bhantai, bhikkhūnaṃ guḷau yāvadatthau, bahu cāyaṃ guḷau avasiṭṭhau| kathāhaṃ, bhantai, paṭipaj‍jāmī’’ti? ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, kac‍cāna, bhikkhū guḷaihi santappaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā bhikkhū guḷaihi santappaisi| aikac‍cai bhikkhū pattaipi pūraisuṃ parissāvanānipi thavikāyaupi pūraisuṃ| atha khau bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau bhikkhū guḷaihi santappaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘santappitā, bhantai, bhikkhū guḷaihi, bahu cāyaṃ guḷau avasiṭṭhau| kathāhaṃ, bhantai, paṭipaj‍jāmī’’ti? ‘‘taina hi  tvaṃ, kac‍cāna, vighāsādānaṃ guḷaṃ daihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā vighāsādānaṃ guḷaṃ datvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘din‍nau, bhantai, vighāsādānaṃ guḷau, bahu cāyaṃ guḷau avasiṭṭhau| kathāhaṃ, bhantai, paṭipaj‍jāmī’’ti? ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, kac‍cāna, vighāsādānaṃ guḷaṃ yāvadatthaṃ daihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā vighāsādānaṃ guḷaṃ yāvadatthaṃ datvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘din‍nau, bhantai, vighāsādānaṃ guḷau yāvadatthau, bahu cāyaṃ guḷau avasiṭṭhau| kathāhaṃ, bhantai, paṭipaj‍jāmī’’ti? ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, kac‍cāna, vighāsādai guḷaihi santappaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti  khau bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā vighāsādai guḷaihi santappaisi| aikac‍cai vighāsādā kaulambaipi ghaṭaipi pūraisuṃ, piṭakānipi ucchaṅgaipi pūraisuṃ| atha khau bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau vighāsādai guḷaihi santappaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘santappitā, bhantai, vighāsādā guḷaihi, bahu cāyaṃ guḷau avasiṭṭhau| kathāhaṃ, bhantai, paṭipaj‍jāmī’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ taṃ, kac‍cāna, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya yassa sau guḷau paribhuttau sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchaiyya, añ‍ñatra tathāgatassa vā tathāgatasāvakassa vā| taina hi tvaṃ, kac‍cāna, taṃ guḷaṃ appaharitai vā chaḍḍaihi, appāṇakai vā udakai aupilāpaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā taṃ guḷaṃ appāṇakai udakai  aupilāpaiti| atha khau sau guḷau udakai pakkhittau cic‍ciṭāyati ciṭiciṭāyati padhūpāyati  sandhūpāyati (sī॰ syā॰) sampadhūpāyati| saiyyathāpi nāma phālau divasaṃsantattau udakai pakkhittau cic‍ciṭāyati ciṭiciṭāyati padhūpāyati sampadhūpāyati, aivamaiva sau guḷau udakai pakkhittau cic‍ciṭāyati ciṭiciṭāyati padhūpāyati sampadhūpāyati|

atha khau bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau saṃviggau laumahaṭṭhajātau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nassa khau bailaṭṭhassa kac‍cānassa bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – ‘‘dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ, kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā bhagavā añ‍ñāsi bailaṭṭhaṃ kac‍cānaṃ kal‍lacittaṃ, muducittaṃ, vinīvaraṇacittaṃ, udaggacittaṃ, pasan‍nacittaṃ, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā, taṃ pakāsaisi…pai॰… aivamaiva bailaṭṭhassa  kac‍cānassa tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti| atha khau bailaṭṭhau kac‍cānau diṭṭhadhammau pattadhammau viditadhammau pariyaugāḷhadhammau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya…pai॰…  aivamaivaṃ khau bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dhammañ‍ca, bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇagata’’nti|

atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina rājagahaṃ tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina rājagahai guḷau ussan‍nau hauti| bhikkhū – gilānassaiva bhagavatā guḷau anuñ‍ñātau, nau agilānassāti – kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā  guḷaṃ na bhuñ‍janti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānassa guḷaṃ, agilānassa guḷaudakanti|

bailaṭṭhakac‍cānavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

173. pāṭaligāmavatthu

285. itau paraṃ mahāva॰ 286-287 ‘tiṇṇā maidhāvinau janā’ti pāṭhau dī॰ ni॰ 2.148; udā॰ 76 ādayau atha  khau bhagavā rājagahai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina pāṭaligāmau taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi, mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ, aḍḍhatailasaihi bhikkhusataihi| atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina pāṭaligāmau tadavasari| assausuṃ khau pāṭaligāmikā upāsakā – ‘‘bhagavā kira pāṭaligāmaṃ anuppattau’’ti| atha khau pāṭaligāmikā upāsakā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau pāṭaligāmikai upāsakai bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi, samādapaisi, samuttaijaisi, sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau pāṭaligāmikā upāsakā bhagavatā dhammiyā  kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘adhivāsaitu nau, bhantai, bhagavā āvasathāgāraṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau pāṭaligāmikā upāsakā bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ  katvā yaina āvasathāgāraṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā sabbasanthariṃ āvasathāgāraṃ santharitvā, āsanāni pañ‍ñapaitvā, udakamaṇikaṃ patiṭṭhāpaitvā, tailapadīpaṃ āraupaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau pāṭaligāmikā upāsakā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sabbasantharisanthataṃ, bhantai, āvasathāgāraṃ| āsanāni pañ‍ñattāni| udakamaṇikau patiṭṭhāpitau| tailapadīpau āraupitau| yassadāni, bhantai, bhagavā kālaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina yaina āvasathāgāraṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pādai pakkhālaitvā āvasathāgāraṃ pavisitvā majjhimaṃ thambhaṃ nissāya puratthābhimukhau nisīdi| bhikkhusaṅghaupi khau pādai pakkhālaitvā āvasathāgāraṃ pavisitvā pacchimaṃ bhittiṃ nissāya puratthābhimukhau nisīdi, bhagavantaṃyaiva purakkhatvā| pāṭaligāmikāpi khau upāsakā pādai pakkhālaitvā āvasathāgāraṃ pavisitvā puratthimaṃ bhittiṃ nissāya pacchimābhimukhā  nisīdiṃsu, bhagavantaṃyaiva purakkhatvā| atha khau bhagavā pāṭaligāmikai upāsakai āmantaisi –

 dī॰ ni॰ 3.316; a॰ ni॰ 5.213 ādayau, gahapatayau, ādīnavā dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, gahapatayau, dussīlau sīlavipan‍nau pamādādhikaraṇaṃ mahatiṃ bhaugajāniṃ nigacchati| ayaṃ paṭhamau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā| puna caparaṃ, gahapatayau, dussīlassa sīlavipan‍nassa pāpakau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati| ayaṃ dutiyau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā| puna caparaṃ, gahapatayau, dussīlau sīlavipan‍nau yañ‍ñadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamati, yadi khattiyaparisaṃ, yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ, yadi gahapatiparisaṃ, yadi samaṇaparisaṃ, avisāradau upasaṅkamati maṅkubhūtau| ayaṃ tatiyau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā| puna caparaṃ, gahapatayau, dussīlau sīlavipan‍nau sammūḷhau kālaṃkarauti| ayaṃ catutthau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā| puna caparaṃ, gahapatayau, dussīlau sīlavipan‍nau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ pañ‍camau ādīnavau dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā| imai khau, gahapatayau, pañ‍ca ādīnavā dussīlassa sīlavipattiyā|

dī॰ ni॰ 3.316; a॰ ni॰ 5.213 ādayau ‘‘pañ‍cimai, gahapatayau, ānisaṃsā sīlavatau sīlasampadāya| katamai  pañ‍ca? idha, gahapatayau, sīlavā sīlasampan‍nau appamādādhikaraṇaṃ mahantaṃ bhaugakkhandhaṃ adhigacchati| ayaṃ paṭhamau ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya| puna caparaṃ, gahapatayau, sīlavatau sīlasampan‍nassa kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati| ayaṃ dutiyau ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya| puna caparaṃ, gahapatayau, sīlavā sīlasampan‍nau yañ‍ñadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamati, yadi khattiyaparisaṃ, yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ, yadi gahapatiparisaṃ, yadi samaṇaparisaṃ, visāradau upasaṅkamati amaṅkubhūtau| ayaṃ tatiyau ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya| puna caparaṃ, gahapatayau, sīlavā sīlasampan‍nau asammūḷhau kālaṃkarauti| ayaṃ catutthau ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya| puna caparaṃ, gahapatayau, sīlavā sīlasampan‍nau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ pañ‍camau ānisaṃsau sīlavatau sīlasampadāya| imai khau, gahapatayau, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā sīlavatau sīlasampadāyāti|

atha khau bhagavā pāṭaligāmikai upāsakai bahudaiva rattiṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uyyaujaisi  – ‘‘abhik‍kantā khau, gahapatayau, ratti| yassadāni tumhai kālaṃ mañ‍ñathā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti, khau pāṭaligāmikā upāsakā bhagavatau  paṭissuṇitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kamiṃsu| atha khau bhagavā acirapak‍kantaisu pāṭaligāmikaisu upāsakaisu suñ‍ñāgāraṃ pāvisi|

pāṭaligāmavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

174. sunidhavassakāravatthu

286. taina  khau pana samayaina sunidhavassakārā magadhamahāmattā pāṭaligāmai nagaraṃ māpainti vaj‍jīnaṃ paṭibāhāya| addasā khau bhagavā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sambahulā daivatāyau pāṭaligāmai vatthūni pariggaṇhantiyau| yasmiṃ padaisai mahaisakkhā daivatā vatthūni pariggaṇhanti, mahaisakkhānaṃ tattha rājūnaṃ rājamahāmattānaṃ cittāni namanti nivaisanāni māpaituṃ| yasmiṃ padaisai majjhimā daivatā vatthūni pariggaṇhanti, majjhimānaṃ tattha rājūnaṃ rājamahāmattānaṃ cittāni namanti nivaisanāni māpaituṃ| yasmiṃ padaisai nīcā daivatā vatthūni pariggaṇhanti, nīcānaṃ tattha rājūnaṃ rājamahāmattānaṃ cittāni namanti nivaisanāni māpaituṃ| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kai nu khau tai, ānanda, pāṭaligāmai nagaraṃ māpaintī’’ti? ‘‘sunidhavassakārā, bhantai, magadhamahāmattā pāṭaligāmai nagaraṃ māpainti vaj‍jīnaṃ paṭibāhāyā’’ti| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, daivaihi tāvatiṃsaihi saddhiṃ mantaitvā, aivamaiva khau, ānanda, sunidhavassakārā magadhamahāmattā pāṭaligāmai nagaraṃ māpainti vaj‍jīnaṃ paṭibāhāya| idhāhaṃ, ānanda, rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya addasaṃ dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sambahulā daivatāyau pāṭaligāmai vatthūni pariggaṇhantiyau| yasmiṃ padaisai mahaisakkhā daivatā vatthūni pariggaṇhanti, mahaisakkhānaṃ tattha rājūnaṃ rājamahāmattānaṃ cittāni namanti nivaisanāni māpaituṃ| yasmiṃ  padaisai majjhimā daivatā vatthūni pariggaṇhanti, majjhimānaṃ tattha rājūnaṃ rājamahāmattānaṃ cittāni namanti nivaisanāni māpaituṃ| yasmiṃ padaisai nīcā daivatā vatthūni pariggaṇhanti, nīcānaṃ tattha rājūnaṃ rājamahāmattānaṃ cittāni namanti nivaisanāni māpaituṃ| yāvatā, ānanda, ariyaṃ āyatanaṃ, yāvatā vaṇippathau, idaṃ agganagaraṃ bhavissati pāṭaliputtaṃ puṭabhaidanaṃ| pāṭaliputtassa khau, ānanda, tayau antarāyā  bhavissanti – aggitau vā udakatau vā abbhantaratau vā mithubhaidāti|

atha khau sunidhavassakārā magadhamahāmattā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā  saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu, sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sunidhavassakārā magadhamahāmattā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘adhivāsaitu nau bhavaṃ gautamau aj‍jatanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau sunidhavassakārā magadhamahāmattā bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā pak‍kamiṃsu| atha khau sunidhavassakārā magadhamahāmattā paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisuṃ – ‘‘kālau, bhau gautama, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina sunidhavassakārānaṃ magadhamahāmattānaṃ parivaisanā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau sunidhavassakārā magadhamahāmattā buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau sunidhavassakārai magadhamahāmattai bhagavā imāhi gāthāhi anumaudi –

‘‘yasmiṃ padaisai kappaiti, vāsaṃ paṇḍitajātiyau|

sīlavantaittha bhaujaitvā, sañ‍ñatai brahmacārayau brahmacārinau (syā॰)||

‘‘yā tattha daivatā āsuṃ, tāsaṃ dakkhiṇamādisai|

tā pūjitā pūjayanti, mānitā mānayanti naṃ||

‘‘tatau  naṃ anukampanti, mātā puttaṃva aurasaṃ|

daivatānukampitau pausau, sadā bhadrāni passatī’’ti||

atha khau bhagavā sunidhavassakārai magadhamahāmattai imāhi gāthāhi anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| taina khau pana samayaina sunidhavassakārā magadhamahāmattā bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhā haunti, ‘‘yainaj‍ja samaṇau gautamau dvāraina nikkhamissati, taṃ gautamadvāraṃ nāma bhavissati; yaina titthaina gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ uttarissati, taṃ gautamatitthaṃ nāma bhavissatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā yaina dvāraina nikkhami, taṃ gautamadvāraṃ nāma ahausi| atha khau bhagavā yaina gaṅgā nadī tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina gaṅgā nadī pūrā hauti samatittikā kākapaiyyā| manussā añ‍ñai nāvaṃ pariyaisanti, añ‍ñai uḷumpaṃ pariyaisanti, añ‍ñai kul‍laṃ bandhanti aurā pāraṃ gantukāmā| addasā khau bhagavā tai manussai añ‍ñai nāvaṃ pariyaisantai, añ‍ñai uḷumpaṃ pariyaisantai, añ‍ñai kul‍laṃ bandhantai aurā pāraṃ gantukāmai, disvāna saiyyathāpi  nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva khau gaṅgāya nadiyā aurimatīrai antarahitau pārimatīrai pac‍cuṭṭhāsi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yai taranti aṇṇavaṃ saraṃ|

saituṃ katvāna visaj‍ja pal‍lalāni|

kul‍lañhi janau bandhati|

tiṇṇā maidhāvinau janā’’ti||

sunidhavassakāravatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

175. kauṭigāmai sac‍cakathā

287. atha khau bhagavā yaina kauṭigāmau tainupasaṅkami| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā kauṭigāmai viharati| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – dī॰ ni॰ 2.155 ‘‘catun‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasac‍cānaṃ ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍ca| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa, bhikkhavai, ariyasac‍cassa ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍ca| dukkhasamudayassa ariyasac‍cassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhassa ariyasac‍cassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa  ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍ca| tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ  anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ, dukkhasamudayaṃ dukkhasamudayau (syā॰) ariyasac‍caṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ, dukkhaniraudhaṃ dukkhaniraudhau (syā॰) ariyasac‍caṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ, ucchin‍nā bhavataṇhā, khīṇā bhavanaitti, natthidāni punabbhavau’’ti|

catun‍naṃ  ariyasac‍cānaṃ, yathābhūtaṃ adassanā|

saṃsitaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ, tāsu tāsvaiva jātisu||

tāni  aitāni diṭṭhāni, bhavanaitti samūhatā|

ucchin‍naṃ mūlaṃ dukkhassa, natthidāni punabbhavauti||

kauṭigāmai sac‍cakathā niṭṭhitā|

176. ambapālīvatthu

288. dī॰ ni॰ 2.161 ādayau assausi khau ambapālī gaṇikā – bhagavā kira kauṭigāmaṃ anuppattauti| atha khau ambapālī gaṇikā bhadrāni bhadrāni yānāni yaujāpaitvā bhadraṃ bhadraṃ yānaṃ abhiruhitvā bhadraihi bhadraihi yānaihi vaisāliyā niyyāsi bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi, yānaina gantvā, yānā pac‍caurauhitvā, pattikāva yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau ambapāliṃ gaṇikaṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya  sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau ambapālī gaṇikā bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau ambapālī gaṇikā bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

ambapālīvatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

177. licchavīvatthu

289. dī॰ ni॰ 2.161 ādayau assausuṃ khau vaisālikā licchavī – bhagavā kira kauṭigāmaṃ anuppattauti| atha khau vaisālikā licchavī bhadrāni bhadrāni yānāni yaujāpaitvā bhadraṃ bhadraṃ yānaṃ abhiruhitvā bhadraihi bhadraihi yānaihi vaisāliyā niyyāsuṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| appaikac‍cai licchavī nīlā haunti nīlavaṇṇā nīlavatthā nīlālaṅkārā, appaikac‍cai licchavī pītā haunti pītavaṇṇā pītavatthā pītālaṅkārā, appaikac‍cai licchavī lauhitā haunti lauhitavaṇṇā lauhitavatthā lauhitālaṅkārā, appaikac‍cai licchavī audātā haunti audātavaṇṇā audātavatthā audātālaṅkārā| atha khau ambapālī gaṇikā daharānaṃ daharānaṃ licchavīnaṃ īsāya īsaṃ yugaina yugaṃ cak‍kaina cak‍kaṃ akkhaina akkhaṃ paṭivaṭṭaisi paṭivattaisi (ka॰)| atha  khau tai licchavī ambapāliṃ gaṇikaṃ  aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa, jai ambapāli, daharānaṃ daharānaṃ amhākaṃ daharānaṃ daharānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) licchavīnaṃ īsāya īsaṃ yugaina yugaṃ cak‍kaina cak‍kaṃ akkhaina akkhaṃ paṭivaṭṭaisī’’ti? ‘‘tathā hi pana  mayā, ayyaputtā, svātanāya buddhappamukhau bhikkhusaṅghau nimantitau’’ti| ‘‘daihi, jai ambapāli, amhākaṃ aitaṃ bhattaṃ satasahassainā’’ti| ‘‘sacaipi mai, ayyaputtā, vaisāliṃ sāhāraṃ daj‍jaiyyātha, naiva daj‍jāhaṃ taṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau tai licchavī aṅguliṃ phauṭaisuṃ – ‘‘jitamhā vata, bhau, ambakāya, parājitamha vata, bhau, ambakāyā’’ti| atha khau tai licchavī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| addasā khau bhagavā tai licchavī dūratauva āgacchantai, disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yaihi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi daivā tāvatiṃsā adiṭṭhapubbā, aulaukaitha, bhikkhavai, licchavīparisaṃ; apalaukaitha, bhikkhavai, licchavīparisaṃ; upasaṃharatha, bhikkhavai, licchavīparisaṃ tāvatiṃsaparisa’’nti| atha khau tai licchavī yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi, yānaina gantvā yānā pac‍caurauhitvā pattikāva yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai licchavī bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau tai licchavī, bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘adhivāsaitu nau, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| ‘‘adhivuṭṭhaumhi, licchavī, svātanāya ambapāliyā gaṇikāya bhatta’’nti| atha khau tai licchavī aṅguliṃ phauṭaisuṃ – ‘‘jitamha vata, bhau, ambakāya, parājitamha vata, bhau, ambakāyā’’ti| atha khau tai licchavī bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kamiṃsu|

atha khau bhagavā kauṭigāmai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā mahāparinibbānasuttai anusandhi añ‍ñathā āgatau yaina nātikā nādikā (sī॰ syā॰) tainupasaṅkami| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā nātikai viharati giñ‍jakāvasathai| atha khau ambapālī gaṇikā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina sakai ārāmai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ  nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina ambapāliyā gaṇikāya parivaisanā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau ambapālī gaṇikā buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau ambapālī gaṇikā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imāhaṃ, bhantai, ambavanaṃ buddhappamukhassa bhikkhusaṅghassa dammī’’ti| paṭiggahaisi bhagavā ārāmaṃ| atha khau bhagavā ambapāliṃ gaṇikaṃ dhammiyā kathāya  sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina mahāvanaṃ tainupasaṅkami| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ|

licchavīvatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

licchavibhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau tatiyau|

178. sīhasaināpativatthu

290. a॰ ni॰ 8.12 ādayau taina  khau pana samayaina abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī sandhāgārai santhāgārai (sī॰ syā॰) san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| taina khau pana samayaina sīhau saināpati nigaṇṭhasāvakau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau sīhassa saināpatissa aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bhavissati, tathā himai abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī santhāgārai san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamaiyyaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti| atha khau sīhau saināpati yaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, sīha, kiriyavādau samānau akiriyavādaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissasi? samaṇau hi, sīha, gautamau akiriyavādau akiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’ti| atha khau sīhassa saināpatissa yau ahausi gamikābhisaṅkhārau bhagavantaṃ dassanāya, sau  paṭippassambhi| dutiyampi khau abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī sandhāgārai san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikapariyānaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| dutiyampi khau sīhassa saināpatissa aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bhavissati, tathā himai abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī sandhāgārai san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamaiyyaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti| dutiyampi khau sīhau saināpati yaina  nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, sīha, kiriyavādau samānau akiriyavādaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissasi? samaṇau hi, sīha, gautamau akiriyavādau akiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’ti| dutiyampi khau sīhassa saināpatissa yau ahausi gamikābhisaṅkhārau bhagavantaṃ dassanāya, sau paṭippassambhi| tatiyampi khau abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā  licchavī sandhāgārai san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| tatiyampi khau sīhassa saināpatissa aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bhavissati, tathā himai abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī sandhāgārai san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| kiñhi mai karissanti nigaṇṭhā apalaukitā vā anapalaukitā vā? yaṃnūnāhaṃ anapalaukaitvāva nigaṇṭhai taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamaiyyaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti|

atha khau sīhau saināpati pañ‍cahi rathasataihi divā divassa vaisāliyā niyyāsi bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi, yānaina gantvā yānā pac‍caurauhitvā pattikauva yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sīhau saināpati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ mai taṃ, bhantai, ‘akiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau akiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’ti| yai tai, bhantai, aivamāhaṃsu ‘akiriyavādau samaṇau  gautamau, akiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’ti| kac‍ci tai, bhantai, bhagavatau vuttavādinau, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraunti, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau  gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchati? anabbhakkhātukāmā hi mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavanta’’nti|

291. ‘‘atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – akiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau, akiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti| atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – kiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau kiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti| atthi, sīha, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ucchaidavādau samaṇau gautamau ucchaidāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti| atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – jaigucchī samaṇau gautamau, jaigucchitāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti| atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – vainayikau samaṇau gautamau, vinayāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti| atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – tapassī samaṇau gautamau, tapassitāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina  ca sāvakai vinaitīti| atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – apagabbhau samaṇau gautamau, apagabbhatāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti| atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – assatthau samaṇau gautamau, assāsāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti|

292. ‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – akiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau, akiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti? ahañhi, sīha, akiriyaṃ vadāmi kāyaduc‍caritassa vacīduc‍caritassa manauduc‍caritassa; anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ akiriyaṃ vadāmi| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – akiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau, akiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti|

‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – kiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau, kiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti? ahañhi, sīha, kiriyaṃ vadāmi kāyasucaritassa vacīsucaritassa manausucaritassa, anaikavihitānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ kiriyaṃ vadāmi| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – kiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau, kiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti|

‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ucchaidavādau samaṇau gautamau, ucchaidāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti? ahañhi, sīha, ucchaidaṃ vadāmi rāgassa dausassa mauhassa; anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ucchaidaṃ vadāmi| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ucchaidavādau samaṇau gautamau ucchaidāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti|

‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – jaigucchī samaṇau gautamau, jaigucchitāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti? ahañhi, sīha, jigucchāmi kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina; anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā jigucchāmi| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina  sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – jaigucchī samaṇau gautamau, jaigucchitāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti|

‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – vainayikau samaṇau gautamau, vinayāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti? ahañhi, sīha, vinayāya dhammaṃ daisaimi rāgassa dausassa mauhassa; anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ  dhammānaṃ vinayāya dhammaṃ daisaimi| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – vainayikau samaṇau gautamau, vinayāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti|

‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – tapassī samaṇau gautamau, tapassitāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti? tapanīyāhaṃ, sīha, pāpakai akusalai dhammai vadāmi – kāyaduc‍caritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ| yassa khau, sīha, tapanīyā pāpakā akusalā dhammā  pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṅkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, tamahaṃ tapassīti vadāmi| tathāgatassa khau, sīha, tapanīyā pāpakā akusalā dhammā pahīnā ucchīn‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya ‘‘tapassī samaṇau gautamau  tapassitāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’ti|

‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – apagabbhau samaṇau gautamau apagabbhatāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti? yassa khau, sīha, āyatiṃ gabbhasaiyyā punabbhavābhinibbatti pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, tamahaṃ apagabbhauti vadāmi| tathāgatassa khau, sīha, āyatiṃ gabbhasaiyyā punabbhavābhinibbatti pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – apagabbhau samaṇau gautamau, apagabbhatāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti|

‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – assatthau samaṇau gautamau assāsāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitīti? ahañhi, sīha, assatthau  paramaina assāsaina, assāsāya dhammaṃ daisaimi, taina ca sāvakai vinaimi| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā  vadamānau vadaiyya – assatthau samaṇau gautamau assāsāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’ti|

293. aivaṃ  vuttai sīhau saināpati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| ‘‘anuvic‍cakāraṃ anuvij‍jakāraṃ (ka॰) khau, sīha, karauhi; anuvic‍cakārau tumhādisānaṃ ñātamanussānaṃ sādhu hautī’’ti| ‘‘imināpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau bhiyyausaumattāya attamanau abhiraddhau, yaṃ maṃ bhagavā aivamāha – ‘anuvic‍cakāraṃ khau, sīha, karauhi; anuvic‍cakārau tumhādisānaṃ ñātamanussānaṃ sādhu hautī’ti| mamañhi, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyā sāvakaṃ labhitvā kaivalakappaṃ vaisāliṃ paṭākaṃ pariharaiyyuṃ – ‘sīhau khau amhākaṃ saināpati sāvakattaṃ upagatau’ti| atha ca pana maṃ bhagavā aivamāha – ‘anuvic‍cakāraṃ khau, sīha, karauhi; anuvic‍cakārau tumhādisānaṃ ñātamanussānaṃ sādhu hautī’ti| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, dutiyampi bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā  dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| ‘‘dīgharattaṃ khau tai, sīha, nigaṇṭhānaṃ aupānabhūtaṃ kulaṃ, yaina naisaṃ upagatānaṃ piṇḍakaṃ dātabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyāsī’’ti| ‘‘imināpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau bhiyyausaumattāya attamanau abhiraddhau, yaṃ maṃ bhagavā aivamāha – ‘dīgharattaṃ khau tai, sīha, nigaṇṭhānaṃ aupānabhūtaṃ kulaṃ, yaina naisaṃ upagatānaṃ piṇḍakaṃ dātabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyāsī’ti| sutaṃ mai taṃ, bhantai, samaṇau gautamau aivamāha – ‘mayhamaiva dānaṃ dātabbaṃ, na añ‍ñaisaṃ dānaṃ dātabbaṃ; mayhamaiva sāvakānaṃ dānaṃ dātabbaṃ, na añ‍ñaisaṃ sāvakānaṃ dānaṃ dātabbaṃ; mayhamaiva din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ, na añ‍ñaisaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ; mayhamaiva sāvakānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ, na añ‍ñaisaṃ sāvakānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphala’nti| atha ca pana maṃ bhagavā nigaṇṭhaisupi dānai samādapaiti| api ca, bhantai, mayamaittha kālaṃ jānissāma| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, tatiyampi bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

atha khau bhagavā sīhassa saināpatissa anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ…pai॰…  aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha  khau sīhau saināpati bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

294. atha  khau sīhau saināpati añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha, bhaṇai, pavattamaṃsaṃ jānāhī’’ti| atha khau sīhau saināpati tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina sīhassa saināpatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā nigaṇṭhā vaisāliyaṃ rathikāya rathikaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ bāhā paggayha kandanti – ‘‘aj‍ja sīhaina saināpatinā thūlaṃ pasuṃ vadhitvā samaṇassa gautamassa bhattaṃ kataṃ, taṃ samaṇau gautamau jānaṃ  uddissakataṃ maṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jati paṭic‍cakamma’’nti| atha khau añ‍ñatarau purisau yaina sīhau saināpati tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā sīhassa saināpatissa upakaṇṇakai āraucaisi ‘‘yagghai, bhantai, jānaiyyāsi, aitai sambahulā nigaṇṭhā vaisāliyaṃ rathikāya rathikaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ bāhā paggayha kandanti – ‘aj‍ja sīhaina saināpatinā thūlaṃ pasuṃ vadhitvā samaṇassa gautamassa bhattaṃ kataṃ, taṃ samaṇau gautamau jānaṃ uddissakataṃ maṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jati paṭic‍cakamma’’’nti| ‘‘alaṃ ayyau, dīgharattampi tai āyasmantā avaṇṇakāmā buddhassa, avaṇṇakāmā dhammassa, avaṇṇakāmā saṅghassa; na ca pana tai āyasmantā jiridanti taṃ bhagavantaṃ asatā tucchā musā abhūtaina abbhācikkhantā; na ca mayaṃ jīvitahaitupi sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyyāmā’’ti| atha khau sīhau saināpati buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā  sampavāraitvā bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau sīhaṃ saināpatiṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, jā naṃ uddissakataṃ  maṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau  paribhuñ‍jaiyya āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tikauṭiparisuddhaṃ macchamaṃsaṃ – adiṭṭhaṃ assutaṃ aparisaṅkita’’nti|

sīhasaināpativatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

179. kappiyabhūmianujānanā

295. taina  khau pana samayaina vaisālī subhikkhā hauti susassā sulabhapiṇḍā, sukarā uñchaina paggahaina yāpaituṃ| atha khau bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘yāni tāni mayā bhikkhūnaṃ anuñ‍ñātāni dubbhikkhai dussassai dul‍labhapiṇḍai antau vuṭṭhaṃ antau pak‍kaṃ sāmaṃ pak‍kaṃ uggahitapaṭiggahitakaṃ tatau nīhaṭaṃ puraibhattaṃ paṭiggahitaṃ vanaṭṭhaṃ paukkharaṭṭhaṃ, aj‍jāpi nu khau tāni bhikkhū paribhuñ‍jantī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘yāni tāni, ānanda, mayā bhikkhūnaṃ anuñ‍ñātāni dubbhikkhai dussassai dul‍labhapiṇḍai antau vuṭṭhaṃ antau pak‍kaṃ sāmaṃ pak‍kaṃ uggahitapaṭiggahitakaṃ tatau nīhaṭaṃ puraibhattaṃ paṭiggahitaṃ vanaṭṭhaṃ paukkharaṭṭhaṃ, aj‍jāpi nu khau tāni bhikkhū paribhuñ‍jantī’’ti? ‘‘paribhuñ‍janti bhagavā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yāni tāni, bhikkhavai, mayā bhikkhūnaṃ anuñ‍ñātāni dubbhikkhai dussassai dul‍labhapiṇḍai antau vuṭṭhaṃ antau pak‍kaṃ sāmaṃ pak‍kaṃ uggahitapaṭiggahitakaṃ tatau nīhaṭaṃ puraibhattaṃ paṭiggahitaṃ vanaṭṭhaṃ paukkharaṭṭhaṃ, tānāhaṃ aj‍jataggai paṭikkhipāmi| na, bhikkhavai, antau vuṭṭhaṃ antau pak‍kaṃ sāmaṃ pak‍kaṃ  uggahitapaṭiggahitakaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, tatau nīhaṭaṃ puraibhattaṃ paṭiggahitaṃ vanaṭṭhaṃ paukkharaṭṭhaṃ bhuttāvinā pavāritaina anatirittaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina jānapadā manussā bahuṃ lauṇampi, tailampi, taṇḍulampi, khādanīyampi sakaṭaisu āraupaitvā bahārāmakauṭṭhakai sakaṭaparivaṭṭaṃ karitvā acchanti – yadā paṭipāṭiṃ labhissāma, tadā bhattaṃ karissāmāti| mahā ca maighau  uggatau hauti| atha khau tai manussā yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha, bhantai ānanda, bahuṃ lauṇampi, tailampi, taṇḍulampi, khādanīyampi sakaṭaisu āraupitā tiṭṭhanti, mahā ca maighau uggatau; kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai ānanda, paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘taina hānanda, saṅghau pac‍cantimaṃ vihāraṃ kappiyabhūmiṃ samman‍nitvā tattha vāsaitu, yaṃ saṅghau ākaṅkhati vihāraṃ vā aḍḍhayaugaṃ vā pāsādaṃ vā hammiyaṃ vā guhaṃ vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ vihāraṃ kappiyabhūmiṃ samman‍naiyya, aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ vihāraṃ kappiyabhūmiṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa vihārassa kappiyabhūmiyā sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau vihārau kappiyabhūmi| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina manussā tatthaiva sammutiyā sammatikāya (syā॰) kappiyabhūmiyā yāguyau pacanti, bhattāni pacanti, sūpāni sampādainti, maṃsāni kauṭṭainti, kaṭṭhāni phālainti| assausi khau bhagavā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ kākauravasaddaṃ, sutvāna āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau sau, ānanda, uc‍cāsaddau mahāsaddau kākauravasaddau’’ti? ‘‘aitarahi, bhantai, manussā tatthaiva sammutiyā kappiyabhūmiyā yāguyau pacanti, bhattāni pacanti, sūpāni sampādainti, maṃsāni kauṭṭainti, kaṭṭhāni phālainti| sau aisau, bhagavā, uc‍cāsaddau mahāsaddau kākauravasaddau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, sammuti sammatikā (syā॰) kappiyabhūmi paribhuñ‍jitabbā| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tissau kappiyabhūmiyau – ussāvanantikaṃ gaunisādikaṃ gahapati’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā yasaujau gilānau hauti| tassatthāya bhaisaj‍jāni āhariyanti| tāni bhikkhū bahi vāsainti| uk‍kapiṇḍikāpi  khādanti, caurāpi haranti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sammutiṃ kappiyabhūmiṃ paribhuñ‍jituṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, catassau kappiyabhūmiyau – ussāvanantikaṃ gaunisādikaṃ gahapatiṃ sammutinti|

kappiyabhūmianujānanā niṭṭhitā|

sīhabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau catutthau|

180. maiṇḍakagahapativatthu

296. taina  khau pana samayaina bhaddiyanagarai maiṇḍakau gahapati paṭivasati| tassa aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau hauti – sīsaṃ nahāyitvā dhañ‍ñāgāraṃ sammaj‍jāpaitvā bahidvārai nisīdati, antalikkhā dhañ‍ñassa dhārā aupatitvā dhañ‍ñāgāraṃ pūraiti| bhariyāya aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau hauti – aikaṃyaiva āḷhakathālikaṃ upanisīditvā aikañ‍ca sūpabhiñ‍janakaṃ sūpabhiñ‍jarakaṃ (sī॰) dāsakammakarapaurisaṃ bhattaina parivisati, na tāva taṃ khiyyati khīyati (sī॰ syā॰) yāva sā na vuṭṭhāti| puttassa aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau hauti – aikaṃyaiva sahassathavikaṃ gahaitvā dāsakammakarapaurisassa chamāsikaṃ vaitanaṃ daiti, na tāva taṃ khiyyati yāvassa hatthagatā| suṇisāya aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau hauti – aikaṃyaiva catudauṇikaṃ piṭakaṃ upanisīditvā dāsakammakarapaurisassa chamāsikaṃ bhattaṃ daiti, na tāva taṃ khiyyati yāva sā na vuṭṭhāti| dāsassa aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau hauti – aikaina naṅgalaina kasantassa satta sītāyau gacchanti|

assausi khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau – ‘‘amhākaṃ  kira vijitai bhaddiyanagarai maiṇḍakau gahapati paṭivasati| tassa aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau – sīsaṃ nahāyitvā dhañ‍ñāgāraṃ sammaj‍jāpaitvā bahidvārai nisīdati, antalikkhā dhañ‍ñassa dhārā aupatitvā dhañ‍ñāgāraṃ pūraiti| bhariyāya aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau – aikaṃyaiva āḷhakathālikaṃ upanisīditvā aikañ‍ca sūpabhiñ‍janakaṃ dāsakammakarapaurisaṃ bhattaina parivisati, na tāva taṃ khiyyati yāva sā na vuṭṭhāti| puttassa aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau – aikaṃyaiva sahassathavikaṃ gahaitvā dāsakammakarapaurisassa chamāsikaṃ vaitanaṃ daiti, na tāva taṃ khiyyati yāvassa hatthagatā| suṇisāya aivarūpau  iddhānubhāvau – aikaṃyaiva catudauṇikaṃ piṭakaṃ upanisīditvā dāsakammakarapaurisassa chamāsikaṃ bhattaṃ daiti, na tāva taṃ khiyyati yāva sā na vuṭṭhāti| dāsassa aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau – aikaina naṅgalaina kasantassa satta sītāyau gacchantī’’ti| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau añ‍ñataraṃ sabbatthakaṃ mahāmattaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘amhākaṃ kira, bhaṇai, vijitai bhaddiyanagarai maiṇḍakau gahapati paṭivasati| tassa aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau  – sīsaṃ nahāyitvā dhañ‍ñāgāraṃ sammaj‍jāpaitvā bahidvārai nisīdati, antalikkhā dhañ‍ñassa dhārā aupatitvā dhañ‍ñāgāraṃ pūraiti| bhariyāya…pai॰… puttassa… suṇisāya… dāsassa aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau, aikaina naṅgalaina kasantassa satta sītāyau gacchantīti| gaccha, bhaṇai, jānāhi| yathā mayā sāmaṃ diṭṭhau, aivaṃ tava diṭṭhau bhavissatī’’ti|

297. aivaṃ, daivāti  khau sau mahāmattau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa paṭissuṇitvā caturaṅginiyā saināya yaina bhaddiyaṃ taina pāyāsi| anupubbaina yaina bhaddiyaṃ yaina maiṇḍakau gahapati tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maiṇḍakaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahañhi, gahapati, rañ‍ñā āṇattau ‘amhākaṃ kira, bhaṇai, vijitai bhaddiyanagarai maiṇḍakau gahapati paṭivasati, tassa aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau, sīsaṃ nahāyitvā…pai॰… bhariyāya… puttassa… suṇisāya… dāsassa aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau, aikaina naṅgalaina kasantassa satta sītāyau gacchantī’ti, gaccha, bhaṇai, jānāhi| yathā mayā sāmaṃ diṭṭhau, aivaṃ tava diṭṭhau bhavissatī’ti| passāma tai, gahapati, iddhānubhāva’’nti| atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati sīsaṃ nahāyitvā dhañ‍ñāgāraṃ sammaj‍jāpaitvā bahidvārai nisīdi, antalikkhā dhañ‍ñassa dhārā aupatitvā dhañ‍ñāgāraṃ pūraisi| ‘‘diṭṭhau tai, gahapati, iddhānubhāvau| bhariyāya tai iddhānubhāvaṃ  passissāmā’’ti| atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati bhariyaṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘taina hi caturaṅginiṃ sainaṃ bhattaina parivisā’’ti| atha khau maiṇḍakassa gahapatissa bhariyā aikaṃyaiva āḷhakathālikaṃ upanisīditvā aikañ‍ca sūpabhiñ‍janakaṃ caturaṅginiṃ sainaṃ bhattaina parivisi, na tāva taṃ khiyyati, yāva sā na vuṭṭhāti| ‘‘diṭṭhau tai, gahapati, bhariyāyapi iddhānubhāvau| puttassa tai iddhānubhāvaṃ passissāmā’’ti| atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati puttaṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘taina hi caturaṅginiyā saināya chamāsikaṃ vaitanaṃ daihī’’ti| atha khau maiṇḍakassa gahapatissa puttau aikaṃyaiva sahassathavikaṃ gahaitvā caturaṅginiyā saināya chamāsikaṃ vaitanaṃ adāsi, na tāva taṃ khiyyati, yāvassa hatthagatā| ‘‘diṭṭhau tai, gahapati, puttassapi iddhānubhāvau| suṇisāya tai iddhānubhāvaṃ passissāmā’’ti| atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati suṇisaṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘taina hi caturaṅginiyā saināya chamāsikaṃ bhattaṃ daihī’’ti| atha khau maiṇḍakassa gahapatissa suṇisā aikaṃyaiva catudauṇikaṃ piṭakaṃ upanisīditvā caturaṅginiyā saināya chamāsikaṃ bhattaṃ adāsi, na tāva taṃ khiyyati yāva sā na vuṭṭhāti| ‘‘diṭṭhau tai, gahapati, suṇisāyapi iddhānubhāvau| dāsassa tai iddhānubhāvaṃ passissāmā’’ti| ‘‘mayhaṃ khau, sāmi, dāsassa iddhānubhāvau khaittai passitabbau’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, gahapati, diṭṭhau tai dāsassapi iddhānubhāvau’’ti| atha khau sau mahāmattau caturaṅginiyā saināya punadaiva rājagahaṃ pac‍cāgañchi| yaina rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi|

298. atha  khau bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina bhaddiyaṃ taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhatailasaihi bhikkhusataihi| atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ  caramānau yaina  bhaddiyaṃ tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā bhaddiyai viharati jātiyā vanai| assausi khau maiṇḍakau gahapati – ‘‘samaṇau khalu bhau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau bhaddiyaṃ anuppattau bhaddiyai viharati jātiyā vanai| taṃ khau pana bhagavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ bhagavāti (ka॰)| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti| atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati bhadrāni bhadrāni yānāni yaujāpaitvā bhadraṃ bhadraṃ yānaṃ abhiruhitvā bhadraihi bhadraihi yānaihi  bhaddiyā niyyāsi bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| addasaṃsu khau sambahulā titthiyā maiṇḍakaṃ gahapatiṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ, disvāna maiṇḍakaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kahaṃ tvaṃ, gahapati, gacchasī’’ti? ‘‘gacchāmahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ idaṃ padaṃ sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu natthi samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāyā’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, gahapati, kiriyavādau samānau akiriyavādaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissasi? samaṇau hi, gahapati, gautamau akiriyavādau akiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’ti| atha khau maiṇḍakassa gahapatissa  aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ, khau sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bhavissati, yathayimai titthiyā usūyantī’’ti| yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi, yānaina gantvā yānā pac‍caurauhitvā pattikauva yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nassa khau maiṇḍakassa gahapatissa bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ…pai॰… aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰…  upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gataṃ| adhivāsaitu ca mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina maiṇḍakassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau maiṇḍakassa gahapatissa bhariyā ca puttau ca suṇisā ca dāsau ca yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| taisaṃ bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ…pai॰…  aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… aitai mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāma dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakai nau bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitai saraṇaṃ  gatai’’ti| atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau maiṇḍakau gahapati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yāva, bhantai, bhagavā bhaddiyai viharati tāva ahaṃ buddhappamukhassa bhikkhusaṅghassa dhuvabhattainā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā maiṇḍakaṃ gahapatiṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

maiṇḍakagahapativattha niṭṭhitaṃ|

181. pañ‍cagaurasādianujānanā

299. atha khau bhagavā bhaddiyai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā maiṇḍakaṃ gahapatiṃ anāpucchā yaina aṅguttarāpau taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhatailasaihi bhikkhusataihi| assausi khau  maiṇḍakau gahapati – ‘‘bhagavā kira yaina aṅguttarāpau taina cārikaṃ pak‍kantau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhatailasaihi bhikkhusataihī’’ti| atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati dāsai ca kammakarai ca āṇāpaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhaṇai, bahuṃ lauṇampi, tailampi, taṇḍulampi, khādanīyampi sakaṭaisu āraupaitvā āgacchatha, aḍḍhatailasāni ca gaupālakasatāni aḍḍhatailasāni ca dhainusatāni ādāya āgacchantu, yattha bhagavantaṃ passissāma tattha taruṇaina dhāruṇhaina (sī॰ syā॰) khīraina bhaujaissāmā’’ti| atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati  bhagavantaṃ antarāmaggai kantārai sambhāvaisi| atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau maiṇḍakau gahapati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā  pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina maiṇḍakassa gahapatissa parivaisanā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati aḍḍhatailasāni gaupālakasatāni āṇāpaisi  – ‘‘tainahi, bhaṇai, aikamaikaṃ dhainuṃ gahaitvā aikamaikassa bhikkhunau upatiṭṭhatha taruṇaina khīraina bhaujaissāmā’’ti| atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi, taruṇaina ca khīraina| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā khīraṃ na paṭiggaṇhanti| paṭiggaṇhatha, bhikkhavai, paribhuñ‍jathāti| atha khau maiṇḍakau gahapati buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā taruṇaina ca khīraina bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau maiṇḍakau gahapati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘santi, bhantai, maggā kantārā, appaudakā appabhakkhā, na sukarā apāthaiyyaina gantuṃ| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pāthaiyyaṃ anujānātū’’ti| atha khau bhagavā maiṇḍakaṃ gahapatiṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca gaurasai – khīraṃ, dadhiṃ, tak‍kaṃ, navanītaṃ, sappiṃ| santi, bhikkhavai, maggā kantārā appaudakā appabhakkhā, na sukarā apāthaiyyaina gantuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pāthaiyyaṃ pariyaisituṃ taṇḍulau taṇḍulatthikaina, muggau muggatthikaina, māsau māsatthikaina, lauṇaṃ lauṇatthikaina, guḷau guḷatthikaina, tailaṃ tailatthikaina, sappi sappitthikaina| santi, bhikkhavai, manussā, saddhā pasan‍nā, tai kappiyakārakānaṃ hatthai hirañ‍ñaṃ upanikkhipanti – ‘iminā ayyassa yaṃ kappiyaṃ taṃ daithā’ti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yaṃ tatau kappiyaṃ taṃ sādituṃ; na tvaivāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, kainaci pariyāyaina jātarūparajataṃ sāditabbaṃ pariyaisitabbanti vadāmī’’ti|

pañ‍cagaurasādianujānanā niṭṭhitā|

182. kaiṇiyajaṭilavatthu

300. ma॰ ni॰ 2.396 ādayau; su॰ ni॰ sailasuttampi passitabbaṃ atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina āpaṇaṃ tadavasari| assausi khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau – ‘‘samaṇau khalu bhau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau āpaṇaṃ anuppattau, taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau…pai॰… sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti| atha khau kaiṇiyassa  jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ samaṇassa gautamassa harāpaiyya’’nti| atha khau kaiṇiyassa jaṭilassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yaipi khau tai  brāhmaṇānaṃ ayaṃ pāṭhau dī॰ ni॰ 1.285, 526, 536; ma॰ ni॰ 2.427; a॰ ni॰ 5.191-192 ādayau pubbakā isayau mantānaṃ kattārau mantānaṃ pavattārau, yaisamidaṃ aitarahi brāhmaṇā paurāṇaṃ mantapadaṃ gītaṃ pavuttaṃ samihitaṃ, tadanugāyanti tadanubhāsanti, bhāsitamanubhāsanti, vācitamanuvācainti, saiyyathidaṃ – aṭṭhakau vāmakau vāmadaivau vaissāmittau yamataggi yamadaggi (ka॰) aṅgīrasau bhāradvājau vāsaiṭṭhau kassapau bhagu ayaṃ pāṭhau dī॰ ni॰ 1.285, 526, 536; ma॰ ni॰ 2.427; a॰ ni॰ 5.191-192 ādayau, rattūparatā viratā vikālabhaujanā, tai aivarūpāni pānāni sādiyiṃsu| samaṇaupi gautamau rattūparatau viratau vikālabhaujanā, arahati samaṇaupi gautamau aivarūpāni pānāni sādiyitu’’nti pahūtaṃ pānaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā kājaihi gāhāpaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi; sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭiggaṇhātu mai bhavaṃ gautamau pāna’’nti| taina hi, kaiṇiya, bhikkhūnaṃ daihīti| atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhikkhūnaṃ daiti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na paṭiggaṇhanti| paṭiggaṇhatha, bhikkhavai, paribhuñ‍jathāti| atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pahūtaihi pānaihi sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā bhagavantaṃ dhautahatthaṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau  kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai  bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| mahā khau, kaiṇiya, bhikkhusaṅghau aḍḍhatailasāni bhikkhusatāni, tvañ‍ca brāhmaṇaisu abhippasan‍nauti| dutiyampi khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi khau, bhau gautama, mahā bhikkhusaṅghau aḍḍhatailasāni bhikkhusatāni, ahañ‍ca brāhmaṇaisu abhippasan‍nau, adhivāsaitu mai bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| mahā khau, kaiṇiya, bhikkhusaṅghau aḍḍhatailasāni bhikkhusatāni, tvañ‍ca brāhmaṇaisu abhippasan‍nauti| tatiyampi khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi khau, bhau gautama, mahā bhikkhusaṅghau aḍḍhatailasāni bhikkhusatāni, ahañ‍ca brāhmaṇaisu abhippasan‍nau, adhivāsaitu bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā
