tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha pānāni – ambapānaṃ jambupānaṃ caucapānaṃ maucapānaṃ madhūkapānaṃ madhupānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) muddikapānaṃ sālūkapānaṃ phārusakapānaṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ phalarasaṃ ṭhapaitvā dhañ‍ñaphalarasaṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ pattarasaṃ ṭhapaitvā  ḍākarasaṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ puppharasaṃ ṭhapaitvā madhūkapuppharasaṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ucchurasa’’nti|

atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau tassā rattiyā  ac‍cayaina sakai assamai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhau gautama, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina kaiṇiyassa jaṭilassa assamau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau kaiṇiyau jaṭilau buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ bhagavā imāhi gāthāhi anumaudi –

‘‘aggihuttamukhā yañ‍ñā, sāvittī chandasau mukhaṃ|

rājā mukhaṃ manussānaṃ, nadīnaṃ sāgarau mukhaṃ||

‘‘nakkhattānaṃ mukhaṃ candau, ādic‍cau tapataṃ mukhaṃ|

puñ‍ñaṃ ākaṅkhamānānaṃ saṅghau, vai yajataṃ mukha’’nti||

atha khau bhagavā kaiṇiyaṃ jaṭilaṃ imāhi gāthāhi anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

kaiṇiyajaṭilavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

183. raujamal‍lavatthu

301. atha  khau bhagavā āpaṇai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina kusinārā taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhatailasaihi bhikkhusataihi| assausuṃ khau kausinārakā mal‍lā – ‘‘bhagavā kira kusināraṃ āgacchati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhatailasaihi bhikkhusataihī’’ti| tai saṅgaraṃ saṅkaraṃ (ka॰) akaṃsu – ‘‘yau bhagavatau pac‍cuggamanaṃ na  karissati, pañ‍casatānissa daṇḍau’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina raujau mal‍lau āyasmatau ānandassa sahāyau hauti| atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina kusinārā tadavasari| atha khau kausinārakā mal‍lā bhagavatau pac‍cuggamanaṃ akaṃsu| atha khau raujau mal‍lau  bhagavatau pac‍cuggamanaṃ karitvā yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau raujaṃ mal‍laṃ āyasmā ānandau aitadavauca – ‘‘uḷāraṃ khau tai idaṃ, āvusau rauja, yaṃ tvaṃ bhagavatau pac‍cuggamanaṃ akāsī’’ti| ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai ānanda, bahukatau buddhai vā dhammai vā saṅghai vā; api ca ñātīhi saṅgarau katau – ‘yau bhagavatau pac‍cuggamanaṃ na karissati, pañ‍casatānissa daṇḍau’’’ti; sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai ānanda, ñātīnaṃ daṇḍabhayā aivāhaṃ bhagavatau pac‍cuggamanaṃ akāsinti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau anattamanau ahausi’ kathañhi nāma raujau mal‍lau aivaṃ vakkhatī’ti? atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, raujau mal‍lau abhiñ‍ñātau ñātamanussau| mahatthikau khau pana aivarūpānaṃ ñātamanussānaṃ imasmiṃ dhammavinayai pasādau| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā tathā  karautu, yathā raujau mal‍lau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai pasīdaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘na khau taṃ, ānanda, duk‍karaṃ tathāgataina, yathā raujau mal‍lau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai pasīdaiyyā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā raujaṃ mal‍laṃ maittaina cittaina pharitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi| atha khau raujau mal‍lau bhagavatau maittaina cittaina phuṭṭhau, saiyyathāpi nāma gāviṃ taruṇavacchau, aivamaiva, vihāraina  vihāraṃ parivaiṇaina parivaiṇaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhū pucchati – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau, bhantai, aitarahi sau bhagavā viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, dassanakāmā hi mayaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti| ‘‘aisāvusau rauja, vihārau  saṃvutadvārau, taina appasaddau upasaṅkamitvā ataramānau āḷindaṃ pavisitvā uk‍kāsitvā aggaḷaṃ ākauṭaihi, vivarissati tai bhagavā dvāra’’nti| atha khau raujau mal‍lau yaina sau vihārau saṃvutadvārau, taina appasaddau upasaṅkamitvā ataramānau āḷindaṃ pavisitvā uk‍kāsitvā aggaḷaṃ ākauṭaisi| vivari bhagavā dvāraṃ| atha khau raujau mal‍lau vihāraṃ pavisitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nassa khau raujassa mal‍lassa bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ…pai॰…  aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu, bhantai, ayyā mamañ‍ñaiva paṭiggaṇhaiyyuṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ, nau añ‍ñaisa’’nti| ‘‘yaisaṃ khau, rauja, saikkhaina ñāṇaina saikkhaina dassanaina dhammau diṭṭhau saiyyathāpi tayā, taisampi aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau nūna ayyā amhākañ‍ñaiva paṭiggaṇhaiyyuṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ, nau añ‍ñaisa’nti| taina hi, rauja, tava caiva paṭiggahissanti añ‍ñaisañ‍cā’’ti|

302. taina  khau pana samayaina kusinārāyaṃ paṇītānaṃ bhattānaṃ bhattapaṭipāṭi aṭṭhitā hauti| atha khau raujassa mal‍lassa paṭipāṭiṃ alabhantassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhattaggaṃ aulaukaiyyaṃ, yaṃ bhattaggai nāssa, taṃ paṭiyādaiyya’’nti| atha khau raujau mal‍lau bhattaggaṃ aulaukaintau dvai nāddasa – ḍākañ‍ca piṭṭhakhādanīyañ‍ca| atha khau raujau mal‍lau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘idha mai, bhantai ānanda, paṭipāṭiṃ alabhantassa aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhattaggaṃ aulaukaiyyaṃ, yaṃ bhattaggai nāssa, taṃ paṭiyādaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai ānanda, bhattaggaṃ aulaukaintau dvai nāddasaṃ – ḍākañ‍ca piṭṭhakhādanīyañ‍ca| sacāhaṃ, bhantai ānanda, paṭiyādaiyyaṃ ḍākañ‍ca piṭṭhakhādanīyañ‍ca, paṭiggaṇhaiyya mai bhagavā’’ti? ‘‘taina hi, rauja, bhagavantaṃ paṭipucchissāmī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘taina hānanda, paṭiyādaitū’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, rauja, paṭiyādaihī’’ti| atha khau raujau mal‍lau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina  pahūtaṃ ḍākañ‍ca piṭṭhakhādanīyañ‍ca paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau upanāmaisi ‘‘paṭiggaṇhātu mai, bhantai, bhagavā ḍākañ‍ca piṭṭhakhādanīyañ‍cā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, rauja, bhikkhūnaṃ daihī’’ti| atha khau raujau mal‍lau bhikkhūnaṃ daiti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na paṭiggaṇhanti| ‘‘paṭiggaṇhatha, bhikkhavai, paribhuñ‍jathā’’ti| atha khau raujau mal‍lau buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pahūtaihi ḍākaihi ca piṭṭhakhādanīyaihi ca sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā bhagavantaṃ dhautahatthaṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau raujaṃ mal‍laṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sabbañ‍ca ḍākaṃ sabbañ‍ca piṭṭhakhādanīya’’nti|

raujamal‍lavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

184. vuḍḍhapabbajitavatthu

303. atha  khau bhagavā kusinārāyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina ātumā taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhatailasaihi bhikkhusataihi| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau vuḍḍhapabbajitau ātumāyaṃ paṭivasati nahāpitapubbau| tassa dvai dārakā haunti, mañ‍jukā paṭibhānaiyyakā, dakkhā pariyaudātasippā sakai ācariyakai nahāpitakammai| assausi khau sau vuḍḍhapabbajitau – ‘‘bhagavā kira ātumaṃ āgacchati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhatailasaihi  bhikkhusataihī’’ti| atha khau sau vuḍḍhapabbajitau tai dārakai aitadavauca – ‘‘bhagavā kira, tātā, ātumaṃ āgacchati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhatailasaihi bhikkhusataihi| gacchatha tumhai, tātā, khurabhaṇḍaṃ ādāya nāḷiyāvāpakaina anugharakaṃ anugharakaṃ āhiṇḍatha, lauṇampi, tailampi, taṇḍulampi, khādanīyampi saṃharatha, bhagavatau āgatassa yāgupānaṃ karissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, tātā’’ti khau tai dārakā tassa vuḍḍhapabbajitassa paṭissuṇitvā khurabhaṇḍaṃ ādāya nāḷiyāvāpakaina anugharakaṃ anugharakaṃ āhiṇḍanti, lauṇampi, tailampi, taṇḍulampi, khādanīyampi saṃharantā| manussā tai dārakai mañ‍jukai paṭibhānaiyyakai passitvā yaipi na kārāpaitukāmā taipi kārāpainti, kārāpaitvāpi bahuṃ dainti| atha khau tai dārakā bahuṃ lauṇampi, tailampi, taṇḍulampi, khādanīyampi saṃhariṃsu|

atha  khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina  ātumā tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā ātumāyaṃ viharati bhusāgārai| atha khau sau vuḍḍhapabbajitau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pahūtaṃ yāguṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau upanāmaisi – ‘‘paṭiggaṇhātu mai, bhantai, bhagavā yāgu’’nti| jānantāpi  tathāgatā pucchanti…pai॰… sāvakānaṃ vā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmāti| atha khau bhagavā taṃ vuḍḍhapabbajitaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kutāyaṃ, bhikkhu yāgū’’ti? atha khau sau vuḍḍhapabbajitau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā, ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, maughapurisa, ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, pabbajitau akappiyai samādapaissasi samādapaisi (ka॰)| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi ‘na, bhikkhavai, pabbajitaina akappiyai samādapaitabbaṃ, yau samādapaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, nahāpitapubbaina khurabhaṇḍaṃ pariharitabbaṃ| yau pariharaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā ātumāyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina sāvatthi  taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina sāvatthi tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ bahuṃ phalakhādanīyaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau bhagavatā phalakhādanīyaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kiṃ ananuñ‍ñāta’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ phalakhādanīya’’nti|

304. taina  khau pana samayaina saṅghikāni bījāni puggalikāya bhūmiyā raupiyanti, puggalikāni bījāni saṅghikāya bhūmiyā raupiyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| saṅghikāni, bhikkhavai, bījāni puggalikāya bhūmiyā raupitāni bhāgaṃ datvā paribhuñ‍jitabbāni| puggalikāni bījāni saṅghikāya bhūmiyā raupitāni bhāgaṃ datvā paribhuñ‍jitabbānīti|

vuḍḍhapabbajitavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

185. catumahāpadaisakathā

305. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhūnaṃ kismiñ‍ci kismiñ‍ci ṭhānai kuk‍kuc‍caṃ uppaj‍jati – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kiṃ ananuñ‍ñāta’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā ‘idaṃ na kappatī’ti appaṭikkhittaṃ tañ‍cai  akappiyaṃ anulaumaiti, kappiyaṃ paṭibāhati, taṃ vau na kappati| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā ‘idaṃ na kappatī’ti appaṭikkhittaṃ  tañ‍cai kappiyaṃ anulaumaiti, akappiyaṃ paṭibāhati, taṃ vau kappati| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā ‘idaṃ kappatī’ti ananuñ‍ñātaṃ tañ‍cai akappiyaṃ anulaumaiti, kappiyaṃ paṭibāhati, taṃ vau  na kappati| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā ‘idaṃ kappatī’ti ananuñ‍ñātaṃ, tañ‍cai kappiyaṃ anulaumaiti, akappiyaṃ paṭibāhati, taṃ vau kappatī’’ti|

atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kappati nu khau yāvakālikaina yāmakālikaṃ, na nu khau kappati? kappati nu khau yāvakālikaina sattāhakālikaṃ, na nu khau kappati? kappati nu khau yāvakālikaina yāvajīvikaṃ, na nu khau kappati? kappati nu khau yāmakālikaina sattāhakālikaṃ, na nu khau kappati? kappati nu khau yāmakālikaina yāvajīvikaṃ, na nu khau kappati? kappati nu khau sattāhakālikaina yāvajīvikaṃ, na nu khau kappatī’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘yāvakālikaina, bhikkhavai, yāmakālikaṃ, tadahu paṭiggahitaṃ kālai kappati, vikālai na kappati| yāvakālikaina, bhikkhavai, sattāhakālikaṃ, tadahu paṭiggahitaṃ kālai kappati, vikālai na kappati| yāvakālikaina, bhikkhavai, yāvajīvikaṃ, tadahu paṭiggahitaṃ kālai kappati, vikālai na kappati| yāmakālikaina, bhikkhavai, sattāhakālikaṃ, tadahu paṭiggahitaṃ yāmai kappati, yāmātik‍kantai na kappati| yāmakālikaina, bhikkhavai, yāvajīvikaṃ, tadahu paṭiggahitaṃ yāmai kappati, yāmātik‍kantai  na kappati| sattāhakālikaina, bhikkhavai, yāvajīvikaṃ paṭiggahitaṃ, sattāhaṃ kappati, sattāhātik‍kantai na kappatī’’ti|

catumahāpadaisakathā niṭṭhitā|

bhaisaj‍jakkhandhakau chaṭṭhau|

186. tassuddānaṃ

sāradikai  vikālaipi, vasaṃ mūlai piṭṭhaihi ca|

kasāvaihi paṇṇaṃ phalaṃ, jatu lauṇaṃ chakaṇañ‍ca||

cuṇṇaṃ cālini maṃsañ‍ca, añ‍janaṃ upapisanī upapiṃ sanī (sī॰), upapiṃ sanaṃ (syā॰)|

añ‍janī uc‍cāpārutā, salākā salākaṭhāniṃ salākaudhanī (sī॰ syā॰)||

thavikaṃsabaddhakaṃ  suttaṃ, muddhanitailanatthu ca|

natthukaraṇī dhūmañ‍ca, naittañ‍cāpidhanatthavi||

tailapākaisu maj‍jañ‍ca, atikkhittaṃ abbhañ‍janaṃ|

tumbaṃ saidaṃ sambhārañ‍ca, mahā bhaṅgaudakaṃ tathā||

dakakauṭṭhaṃ lauhitañ‍ca, visāṇaṃ pādabbhañ‍janaṃ|

paj‍jaṃ satthaṃ kasāvañ‍ca, tilakak‍kaṃ kabaḷikaṃ||

cauḷaṃ  sāsapakuṭṭañ‍ca, dhūma sakkharikāya ca|

vaṇatailaṃ vikāsikaṃ, vikaṭañ‍ca paṭiggahaṃ||

gūthaṃ karauntau lauḷiñ‍ca, khāraṃ muttaharītakaṃ|

gandhā viraicanañ‍caiva, acchākaṭaṃ kaṭākaṭaṃ||

paṭicchādani  pabbhārā, ārāma sattāhaina ca|

guḷaṃ muggaṃ sauvīrañ‍ca, sāmaṃpākā punāpacai||

punānuñ‍ñāsi dubbhikkhai, phalañ‍ca tilakhādanī|

puraibhattaṃ kāyaḍāhau, nibbattañ‍ca bhagandalaṃ||

vatthikammañ‍ca suppiñ‍ca, manussamaṃsamaiva ca|

hatthiassā sunakhau ca, ahi sīhañ‍ca dīpikaṃ hatthiassasunakhāhi, sīhabyagghañ‍ca dīpikaṃ (sī॰)||

acchataracchamaṃsañ‍ca, paṭipāṭi ca yāgu ca|

taruṇaṃ añ‍ñatra guḷaṃ, sunidhāvasathāgāraṃ||

gaṅgā kauṭisac‍cakathā, ambapālī ca licchavī|

uddissa kataṃ subhikkhaṃ, punadaiva paṭikkhipi||

maighau yasau maiṇḍakau, ca gaurasaṃ pāthaiyyakaina ca|

kaiṇi ambau jambu cauca, maucamadhumuddikasālukaṃ||

phārusakā ḍākapiṭṭhaṃ, ātumāyaṃ nahāpitau|

sāvatthiyaṃ phalaṃ bījaṃ, kismiṃ ṭhānai ca kālikaiti||

imamhi khandhakai vatthū aikasataṃ chavatthu|

bhaisaj‍jakkhandhakau niṭṭhitau|



7. kathinakkhandhakau

187. kathinānujānanā

306. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina tiṃsamattā pāvaiyyakā pāṭhaiyyakā (sī॰ syā॰) bhikkhū, sabbai ārañ‍ñikā sabbai piṇḍapātikā sabbai paṃsukūlikā sabbai taicīvarikā sāvatthiṃ āgacchantā bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upakaṭṭhāya vassūpanāyikāya nāsakkhiṃsu sāvatthiyaṃ vassūpanāyikaṃ sambhāvaituṃ; antarāmaggai sākaitai vassaṃ upagacchiṃsu| tai uk‍kaṇṭhitarūpā vassaṃ vasiṃsu – āsan‍naiva nau bhagavā viharati itau chasu yaujanaisu, na ca mayaṃ labhāma bhagavantaṃ dassanāyāti| atha khau tai bhikkhū vassaṃvuṭṭhā, taimāsac‍cayaina katāya pavāraṇāya, daivai vassantai, udakasaṅgahai udakacikkhal‍lai aukapuṇṇaihi cīvaraihi kilantarūpā yaina sāvatthi jaitavanaṃ anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmau, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| āciṇṇaṃ khau panaitaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ āgantukaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ paṭisammaudituṃ| atha khau bhagavā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhikkhavai, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍ci samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā phāsukaṃ vassaṃ vasittha, na ca piṇḍakaina kilamitthā’’ti? ‘‘khamanīyaṃ, bhagavā; yāpanīyaṃ, bhagavā; samaggā ca mayaṃ, bhantai, sammaudamānā avivadamānā vassaṃ vasimhā, na ca piṇḍakaina kilamimhā| idha mayaṃ, bhantai, tiṃsamattā pāvaiyyakā bhikkhū sāvatthiṃ āgacchantā bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upakaṭṭhāya vassūpanāyikāya nāsakkhimhā sāvatthiyaṃ vassūpanāyikaṃ sambhāvaituṃ, antarāmaggai sākaitai vassaṃ upagacchimhā| tai mayaṃ, bhantai, uk‍kaṇṭhitarūpā vassaṃ vasimhā  – ‘āsan‍naiva nau bhagavā viharati itau chasu yaujanaisu, na ca mayaṃ labhāma bhagavantaṃ dassanāyā’ti| atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, vassaṃvuṭṭhā, taimāsac‍cayaina katāya pavāraṇāya, daivai vassantai, udakasaṅgahai udakacikkhal‍lai aukapuṇṇaihi cīvaraihi kilantarūpā addhānaṃ āgatāti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ  nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vassaṃvuṭṭhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ  kathinaṃ kaṭhinaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) attharituṃ| atthatakathinānaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca kappissanti – anāmantacārau, asamādānacārau, gaṇabhaujanaṃ, yāvadatthacīvaraṃ, yau ca tattha cīvaruppādau sau naisaṃ bhavissatīti| atthatakathinānaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, imāni pañ‍ca kappissanti| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, kathinaṃ attharitabbaṃ| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

307. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| idaṃ saṅghassa kathinadussaṃ uppan‍naṃ| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau imaṃ kathinadussaṃ itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau dadaiyya kathinaṃ attharituṃ| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| idaṃ saṅghassa kathinadussaṃ uppan‍naṃ| saṅghau imaṃ kathinadussaṃ itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau daiti kathinaṃ  attharituṃ| yassāyasmatau khamati imassa kathinadussassa itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau dānaṃ kathinaṃ attharituṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍naṃ idaṃ saṅghaina kathinadussaṃ itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau kathinaṃ attharituṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

308. ‘‘aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, aivaṃ anatthataṃ| kathañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, anatthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ? na ul‍likhitamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na dhauvanamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na cīvaravicāraṇamattaina na gaṇṭusakaraṇamattaina (ka॰) atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na chaidanamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na bandhanamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na auvaṭṭiyakaraṇamattaina na auvaṭṭaiyyakaraṇamattaina (sī॰ syā॰), na auvadaiyyakaraṇamattaina (ka॰) atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na kaṇḍusakaraṇamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na daḷhīkammakaraṇamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na anuvātakaraṇamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na paribhaṇḍakaraṇamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na auvaddhaiyyakaraṇamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na kambalamaddanamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na nimittakataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na parikathākataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na kuk‍kukataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na san‍nidhikataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na nissaggiyaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na akappakataina atthataṃ  hauti kathinaṃ, na añ‍ñatra saṅghāṭiyā atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na  añ‍ñatra uttarāsaṅgaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na añ‍ñatra antaravāsakaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na añ‍ñatra pañ‍cakaina vā atiraikapañ‍cakaina vā tadahaiva sañchin‍naina samaṇḍalīkataina  atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na añ‍ñatra puggalassa atthārā atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ; sammā caiva atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, tañ‍cai nissīmaṭṭhau anumaudati, aivampi anatthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, anatthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ|

309. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ? ahataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, ahatakappaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, pilautikāya atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, paṃsukūlaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, pāpaṇikaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, animittakataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, aparikathākataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, akuk‍kukataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, asan‍nidhikataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, anissaggiyaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, kappakataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, saṅghāṭiyā atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, uttarāsaṅgaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, antaravāsakaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, pañ‍cakaina vā atiraikapañ‍cakaina vā tadahaiva sañchin‍naina samaṇḍalīkataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, puggalassa atthārā atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ; sammā cai atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, tañ‍cai sīmaṭṭhau anumaudati, aivampi atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ|

310. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, ubbhataṃ hauti kathinaṃ? aṭṭhimā, bhikkhavai, mātikā kathinassa ubbhārāya – pak‍kamanantikā, niṭṭhānantikā, san‍niṭṭhānantikā, nāsanantikā, savanantikā, āsāvacchaidikā, sīmātik‍kantikā, sahubbhārā’’ti saubbhārāti (ka॰)|

kathinānujānanā niṭṭhitā|

188. ādāyasattakaṃ

311. bhikkhu atthatakathinau katacīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati – ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau pak‍kamanantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘naivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati – ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| sau katacīvarau suṇāti ‘‘ubbhataṃ  kira tasmiṃ āvāsai kathina’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau savanantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati – ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| sau katacīvarau – ‘‘pac‍caissaṃ pac‍caissa’’nti – bahiddhā kathinuddhāraṃ vītināmaiti| tassa bhikkhunau sīmātik‍kantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati – ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| sau katacīvarau – ‘‘pac‍caissaṃ pac‍caissa’’nti – sambhuṇāti kathinuddhāraṃ| tassa bhikkhunau saha bhikkhūhi kathinuddhārau|

ādāyasattakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ …dutiyaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ (ka॰)|

189. samādāyasattakaṃ

312. bhikkhu atthatakathinau katacīvaraṃ samādāya pak‍kamati ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau pak‍kamanantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ samādāya pak‍kamati| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ samādāya pak‍kamati| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ samādāya pak‍kamati| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ samādāya pak‍kamati – ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| sau katacīvarau suṇāti  – ‘‘ubbhataṃ kira tasmiṃ āvāsai kathina’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau savanantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ samādāya pak‍kamati – ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| sau katacīvarau – ‘‘pac‍caissaṃ pac‍caissa’’nti – bahiddhā kathinuddhāraṃ vītināmaiti| tassa bhikkhunau sīmātik‍kantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ samādāya pak‍kamati – ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| sau katacīvarau – ‘‘pac‍caissaṃ pac‍caissa’’nti – sambhuṇāti kathinuddhāraṃ| tassa bhikkhunau saha bhikkhūhi kathinuddhārau|

samādāyasattakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

190. ādāyachak‍kaṃ

313. bhikkhu  atthatakathinau vippakatacīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau vippakatacīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘naivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau vippakatacīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassaṃ taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau vippakatacīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvaraṃ  kāraiti| sau katacīvarau suṇāti – ‘‘ubbhataṃ kira tasmiṃ āvāsai kathina’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau savanantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau vippakatacīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| sau katacīvarau ‘‘pac‍caissaṃ pac‍caissa’’nti bahiddhā kathinuddhāraṃ vītināmaiti| tassa bhikkhunau sīmātik‍kantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau vippakatacīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| sau katacīvarau ‘‘pac‍caissaṃ pac‍caissa’’nti sambhuṇāti kathinuddhāraṃ| tassa bhikkhunau saha bhikkhūhi kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau vippakatacīvaraṃ samādāya pak‍kamati| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ  hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

192. ādāyapan‍narasakaṃ

315. bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘naivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti –

‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ  cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

tikaṃ|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘naivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

tikaṃ|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati anadhiṭṭhitaina; naivassa hauti ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti, na panassa hauti ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati anadhiṭṭhitaina; naivassa hauti ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti, na panassa hauti ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘naivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati anadhiṭṭhitaina; naivassa hauti ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti, na panassa hauti ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

vippakatasamādāyapan‍narasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

ādāyabhāṇavārau|

195. anāsādauḷasakaṃ

318. bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| anāsāya labhati, āsāya na labhati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| anāsāya labhati, āsāya na labhati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘naivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| anāsāya labhati, āsāya na labhati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| tassa sā cīvarāsā upacchij‍jati| tassa bhikkhunau āsāvacchaidikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| anāsāya labhati, āsāya na labhati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| anāsāya labhati, āsāya na labhati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘naivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| anāsāya labhati, āsāya na labhati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bahisīmagatassa  aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| tassa sā cīvarāsā upacchij‍jati| tassa bhikkhunau āsāvacchaidikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati anadhiṭṭhitaina; naivassa hauti – ‘‘pac‍caissanti, na panassa hauti – ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| anāsāya labhati, āsāya na  labhati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati anadhiṭṭhitaina; naivassa hauti – ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti, na panassa hauti – ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| anāsāya labhati, āsāya na labhati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘naivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

anāsādauḷasakaṃ anāsādvādasakaṃ (sī॰) niṭṭhitaṃ|

196. āsādauḷasakaṃ

319. bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau  bahisīmagatau  taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| āsāya labhati, anāsāya na labhati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| āsāya labhati, anāsāya na labhati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘naivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| āsāya labhati, anāsāya na labhati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| tassa sā cīvarāsā upacchij‍jati| tassa bhikkhunau āsāvacchaidikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau suṇāti – ‘‘ubbhataṃ kira  tasmiṃ āvāsai kathina’’nti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘yatau tasmiṃ āvāsai ubbhataṃ kathinaṃ, idhaivimaṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| āsāya labhati, anāsāya na labhati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau suṇāti – ‘‘ubbhataṃ kira tasmiṃ āvāsai kathina’’nti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘yatau tasmiṃ āvāsai ubbhataṃ kathinaṃ, idhaivimaṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| āsāya labhati, anāsāya na labhati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘naivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau suṇāti – ‘‘ubbhataṃ kira tasmiṃ āvāsai kathina’’nti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘yatau tasmiṃ āvāsai ubbhataṃ kathinaṃ, idhaivimaṃ cīvarāsaṃ  payirupāsissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| āsāya labhati, anāsāya na labhati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau suṇāti – ‘‘ubbhataṃ kira tasmiṃ āvāsai kathina’’nti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘yatau tasmiṃ āvāsai ubbhataṃ kathinaṃ, idhaivimaṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| tassa sā cīvarāsā upacchij‍jati| tassa  bhikkhunau āsāvacchaidikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| āsāya labhati, anāsāya na labhati| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| sau katacīvarau suṇāti – ‘‘ubbhataṃ kira tasmiṃ āvāsai kathina’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau savanantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| tassa sā cīvarāsā upacchij‍jati| tassa bhikkhunau āsāvacchaidikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| āsāya labhati, anāsāya na labhati| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| sau katacīvarau ‘‘pac‍caissaṃ pac‍caissa’’nti – bahiddhā kathinuddhāraṃ vītināmaiti| tassa bhikkhunau sīmātik‍kantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau cīvarāsāya pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvarāsaṃ payirupāsati| āsāya labhati, anāsāya na labhati| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| sau  katacīvarau ‘‘pac‍caissaṃ pac‍caissa’’nti – sambhuṇāti kathinuddhāraṃ| tassa bhikkhunau saha bhikkhūhi kathinuddhārau|

āsādauḷasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

197. karaṇīyadauḷasakaṃ

karaṇīyadauḷasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

198. apavilāyananavakaṃ

321. bhikkhu  atthatakathinau disaṃgamikau pak‍kamati cīvarapaṭivīsaṃ apavilāyamānau apavinayamānau (sī॰), apacinayamānau (ka॰)| tamainaṃ disaṅgataṃ bhikkhū pucchanti – ‘‘kahaṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, vassaṃvuṭṭhau, kattha ca tai cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘amukasmiṃ āvāsai vassaṃvuṭṭhaumhi| tattha ca mai cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti| tai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘gacchāvusau, taṃ cīvaraṃ āhara, mayaṃ tai idha cīvaraṃ karissāmā’’ti| sau taṃ āvāsaṃ gantvā bhikkhū pucchati – ‘‘kahaṃ mai, āvusau, cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti? tai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘ayaṃ tai, āvusau, cīvarapaṭivīsau; kahaṃ gamissasī’’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘amukaṃ nāma amukañ‍ca (ka॰) āvāsaṃ gamissāmi, tattha mai bhikkhū cīvaraṃ karissantī’’ti| tai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau, mā agamāsi| mayaṃ tai idha cīvaraṃ karissāmā’’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau disaṃgamikau pak‍kamati…pai॰… ‘‘naivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau disaṃgamikau pak‍kamati…pai॰… ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

322. bhikkhu atthatakathinau disaṃgamikau pak‍kamati cīvarapaṭivīsaṃ apavilāyamānau| tamainaṃ disaṃgataṃ bhikkhū pucchanti – ‘‘kahaṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, vassaṃvuṭṭhau, kattha ca tai cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘amukasmiṃ āvāsai vassaṃvuṭṭhaumhi, tattha ca mai cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti| tai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘gacchāvusau, taṃ cīvaraṃ āhara, mayaṃ tai idha cīvaraṃ karissāmā’’ti| sau taṃ āvāsaṃ gantvā bhikkhū pucchati – ‘‘kahaṃ mai, āvusau, cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti? tai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘ayaṃ tai, āvusau, cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ ādāya taṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tamainaṃ antarāmaggai bhikkhū pucchanti – ‘‘āvusau, kahaṃ gamissasī’’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi, tattha mai bhikkhū cīvaraṃ karissantī’’ti| tai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau, mā agamāsi, mayaṃ tai idha cīvaraṃ karissāmā’’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa  bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau disaṃgamikau pak‍kamati cīvarapaṭivīsaṃ apavilāyamānau| tamainaṃ disaṃgataṃ bhikkhū pucchanti – ‘‘kahaṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, vassaṃvuṭṭhau, kattha ca tai cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘amukasmiṃ āvāsai vassaṃvuṭṭhaumhi, tattha ca mai cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti| tai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘gacchāvusau, taṃ cīvaraṃ āhara, mayaṃ tai idha cīvaraṃ karissāmā’’ti| sau taṃ āvāsaṃ gantvā bhikkhū pucchati – ‘‘kahaṃ mai, āvusau, cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti? tai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘ayaṃ tai, āvusau, cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ ādāya taṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tamainaṃ antarāmaggai bhikkhū  pucchanti – ‘‘āvusau, kahaṃ gamissasī’’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi, tattha mai bhikkhū cīvaraṃ karissantī’’ti| tai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau, mā agamāsi, mayaṃ tai idha cīvaraṃ karissāmā’’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘naivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau disaṃgamikau pak‍kamati…pai॰… ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

323. bhikkhu  atthatakathinau disaṃgamikau pak‍kamati cīvarapaṭivīsaṃ apavilāyamānau| tamainaṃ disaṃgataṃ bhikkhū pucchanti – ‘‘kahaṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, vassaṃvuṭṭhau, kattha ca tai cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘amukasmiṃ āvāsai vassaṃvuṭṭhaumhi, tattha ca mai cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti| tai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘gacchāvusau, taṃ cīvaraṃ āhara, mayaṃ tai idha cīvaraṃ karissāmā’’ti| sau taṃ āvāsaṃ gantvā bhikkhū pucchati – ‘‘kahaṃ mai, āvusau, cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti? tai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘ayaṃ tai, āvusau, cīvarapaṭivīsau’’ti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ ādāya taṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tassa taṃ āvāsaṃ gatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau disaṃgamikau pak‍kamati…pai॰… ‘‘naivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau disaṃgamikau pak‍kamati…pai॰… ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

apavilāyananavakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

199. phāsuvihārapañ‍cakaṃ

324. bhikkhu atthatakathinau phāsuvihārikau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati – ‘‘amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati vasissāmi, nau cai mai phāsu bhavissati, amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati vasissāmi, nau cai mai phāsu bhavissati, amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati  vasissāmi, nau cai mai phāsu bhavissati, pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ  cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa bhikkhunau niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau phāsuvihārikau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati – ‘‘amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati vasissāmi, nau cai mai phāsu bhavissati, amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati vasissāmi, nau cai mai phāsu bhavissati, amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati vasissāmi, nau cai mai phāsu bhavissati, pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘naivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bhikkhunau san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau phāsuvihārikau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati – ‘‘amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati vasissāmi, nau cai mai phāsu bhavissati, amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati vasissāmi, nau cai mai phāsu bhavissati, amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati vasissāmi, nau  cai mai phāsu bhavissati, pac‍caissa’’nti| tassa bahisīmagatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘idhaivimaṃ cīvaraṃ kāraissaṃ, na pac‍caissa’’nti| sau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| tassa taṃ cīvaraṃ kayiramānaṃ nassati| tassa bhikkhunau nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu atthatakathinau phāsuvihārikau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati – ‘‘amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati vasissāmi, nau cai mai phāsu bhavissati, amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati vasissāmi, nau cai mai phāsu bhavissati, amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati vasissāmi, nau cai mai phāsu bhavissati, pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| sau katacīvarau – ‘‘pac‍caissaṃ pac‍caissa’’nti bahiddhā kathinuddhāraṃ vītināmaiti| tassa bhikkhunau sīmātik‍kantikau  kathinuddhārau|

bhikkhu  atthatakathinau phāsuvihārikau cīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati – ‘‘amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati vasissāmi, nau cai mai phāsu bhavissati, amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati vasissāmi, nau cai mai phāsu bhavissati, amukaṃ nāma āvāsaṃ gamissāmi; tattha mai phāsu bhavissati vasissāmi, nau cai  mai phāsu bhavissati, pac‍caissa’’nti| sau bahisīmagatau taṃ cīvaraṃ kāraiti| sau katacīvarau ‘‘pac‍caissaṃ pac‍caissa’’nti sambhuṇāti kathinuddhāraṃ| tassa bhikkhunau saha bhikkhūhi kathinuddhārau|

phāsuvihārapañ‍cakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

200. palibaudhāpalibaudhakathā

325. dvaimai, bhikkhavai, kathinassa palibaudhā, dvai apalibaudhā| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dvai kathinassa palibaudhā? āvāsapalibaudhau ca cīvarapalibaudhau ca| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, āvāsapalibaudhau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vasati vā tasmiṃ āvāsai, sāpaikkhau vā pak‍kamati ‘‘pac‍caissa’’nti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, āvāsapalibaudhau hauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, cīvarapalibaudhau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau cīvaraṃ akataṃ vā hauti vippakataṃ vā, cīvarāsā vā anupacchin‍nā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cīvarapalibaudhau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, dvai kathinassa palibaudhā|

katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dvai kathinassa apalibaudhā? āvāsaapalibaudhau ca cīvaraapalibaudhau ca| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, āvāsaapalibaudhau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pak‍kamati tamhā āvāsā cattaina vantaina muttaina anapaikkhau anapaikkhaina (ka॰) ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, āvāsaapalibaudhau hauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, cīvaraapalibaudhau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau cīvaraṃ kataṃ vā hauti, naṭṭhaṃ vā vinaṭṭhaṃ vā daḍḍhaṃ  vā, cīvarāsā vā upacchin‍nā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cīvaraapalibaudhau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, dvai kathinassa apalibaudhāti|

palibaudhāpalibaudhakathā niṭṭhitā|

kathinakkhandhakau niṭṭhitau sattamau|

201. tassuddānaṃ

tiṃsa  pāvaiyyakā bhikkhū, sākaituk‍kaṇṭhitā vasuṃ|

vassaṃvuṭṭhaukapuṇṇaihi, agamuṃ jinadassanaṃ||

idaṃ vatthu kathinassa, kappissanti ca pañ‍cakā|

anāmantā asamācārā, tathaiva gaṇabhaujanaṃ||

yāvadatthañ‍ca uppādau, atthatānaṃ bhavissati|

ñatti aivatthatañ‍caiva, aivañ‍caiva anatthataṃ||

ul‍likhi dhauvanā caiva, vicāraṇañ‍ca chaidanaṃ|

bandhanau vaṭṭi kaṇḍusa, daḷhīkammānuvātikā||

paribhaṇḍaṃ  auvaddhaiyyaṃ, maddanā nimittaṃ kathā|

kuk‍ku san‍nidhi nissaggi, na kappañ‍ñatra tai tayau||

añ‍ñatra pañ‍cātiraikai, sañchin‍naina samaṇḍalī|

nāñ‍ñatra puggalā sammā, nissīmaṭṭhaunumaudati||

kathinānatthataṃ hauti, aivaṃ buddhaina daisitaṃ|

ahatākappapilauti, paṃsu pāpaṇikāya ca||

animittāparikathā, akuk‍ku ca asan‍nidhi|

anissaggi kappakatai, tathā ticīvaraina ca||

pañ‍cakai vātiraikai vā, chin‍nai samaṇḍalīkatai|

puggalassatthārā  sammā, sīmaṭṭhau anumaudati||

aivaṃ  kathinattharaṇaṃ, ubbhārassaṭṭhamātikā|

pak‍kamananti niṭṭhānaṃ, san‍niṭṭhānañ‍ca nāsanaṃ||

savanaṃ  āsāvacchaidi, sīmā sahubbhāraṭṭhamī|

katacīvaramādāya, ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti gacchati||

tassa taṃ kathinuddhārā,ai hauti pak‍kamanantikau|

ādāya cīvaraṃ yāti, nissīmai idaṃ cintayi||

‘‘kāraissaṃ na pac‍caissa’’nti, niṭṭhānai kathinuddhārau|

ādāya nissīmaṃ naiva, ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti mānasau||

tassa taṃ kathinuddhārau, san‍niṭṭhānantikau bhavai|

ādāya cīvaraṃ yāti, nissīmai idaṃ cintayi||

‘‘kāraissaṃ na pac‍caissa’’nti, kayiraṃ tassa nassati|

tassa taṃ kathinuddhārau, bhavati nāsanantikau||

ādāya yāti ‘‘pac‍caissaṃ’’, bahi kāraiti cīvaraṃ|

katacīvarau suṇāti, ubbhataṃ kathinaṃ tahiṃ||

tassa taṃ kathinuddhārau, bhavati savanantikau|

ādāya yāti ‘‘pac‍caissaṃ’’, bahi kāraiti cīvaraṃ||

katacīvarau bahiddhā, nāmaiti kathinuddhāraṃ|

tassa taṃ kathinuddhārau, sīmātik‍kantikau bhavai||

ādāya yāti ‘‘pac‍caissaṃ’’, bahi kāraiti cīvaraṃ|

katacīvarau  pac‍caissaṃ, sambhauti kathinuddhāraṃ||

tassa  taṃ kathinuddhārau, saha bhikkhūhi jāyati|

ādāya ca samādāya, satta-sattavidhā gati||

pak‍kamanantikā natthi, chak‍kai vippakatai chaṭṭhai vippakatā (sī॰), chac‍cā vippakathā (ka॰) gati|

ādāya nissīmagataṃ, kāraissaṃ iti jāyati||

niṭṭhānaṃ san‍niṭṭhānañ‍ca, nāsanañ‍ca imai tayau|

ādāya ‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti, bahisīmai karaumiti||

niṭṭhānaṃ san‍niṭṭhānampi, nāsanampi idaṃ tayau|

anadhiṭṭhitaina naivassa, haiṭṭhā tīṇi nayāvidhi||

ādāya  yāti pac‍caissaṃ, bahisīmai karaumiti|

‘‘na pac‍caissa’’nti kāraiti, niṭṭhānai kathinuddhārau||

san‍niṭṭhānaṃ nāsanañ‍ca, savanasīmātik‍kamā|

saha bhikkhūhi jāyaitha, aivaṃ pan‍narasaṃ gati||

samādāya vippakatā, samādāya punā tathā|

imai tai caturau vārā, sabbai pan‍narasavidhi||

anāsāya ca āsāya, karaṇīyau ca tai tayau|

nayatau taṃ vijānaiyya, tayau dvādasa dvādasa||

apavilānā navaittha apavilāyamānaiva (syā॰), apavinā nava caittha (sī॰), phāsu pañ‍cavidhā tahiṃ|

palibaudhāpalibaudhā, uddānaṃ nayatau katanti||

imamhi khandhakai vatthū dauḷasakapaiyyālamukhāni aikasataṃ aṭṭhārasa|

kathinakkhandhakau niṭṭhitau|



8. cīvarakkhandhakau

202. jīvakavatthu

326. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina vaisālī iddhā caiva hauti phitā phītā (bahūsu) ca bahujanā ca ākiṇṇamanussā ca subhikkhā ca; satta ca pāsādasahassāni satta ca pāsādasatāni satta ca pāsādā; satta ca kūṭāgārasahassāni satta ca kūṭāgārasatāni satta ca kūṭāgārāni; satta ca ārāmasahassāni satta ca ārāmasatāni satta ca ārāmā; satta ca paukkharaṇīsahassāni satta ca paukkharaṇīsatāni satta ca paukkharaṇiyau; ambapālī ca gaṇikā abhirūpā hauti dassanīyā pāsādikā paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatā, padakkhiṇā padakkhā (syā॰) nac‍cai ca gītai ca vāditai ca, abhisaṭā atthikānaṃ atthikānaṃ manussānaṃ pañ‍ñāsāya ca rattiṃ gacchati; tāya ca vaisālī bhiyyausaumattāya upasaubhati| atha khau rājagahakau naigamau vaisāliṃ agamāsi kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| addasā khau rājagahakau naigamau vaisāliṃ iddhañ‍caiva phitañ‍ca bahujanañ‍ca ākiṇṇamanussañ‍ca subhikkhañ‍ca; satta ca pāsādasahassāni satta ca pāsādasatāni satta ca pāsādai; satta ca kūṭāgārasahassāni satta ca kūṭāgārasatāni satta ca kūṭāgārāni; satta ca ārāmasahassāni satta ca ārāmasatāni satta ca ārāmai; satta ca paukkharaṇīsahassāni satta ca paukkharaṇīsatāni satta ca paukkharaṇiyau; ambapāliñ‍ca gaṇikaṃ abhirūpaṃ dassanīyaṃ pāsādikaṃ paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgataṃ, padakkhiṇaṃ padakkhaṃ (syā॰) nac‍cai ca gītai ca vāditai ca, abhisaṭaṃ atthikānaṃ atthikānaṃ manussānaṃ pañ‍ñāsāya ca rattiṃ gacchantiṃ, tāya ca vaisāliṃ bhiyyausaumattāya upasaubhantiṃ|

327. atha khau rājagahakau naigamau vaisāliyaṃ taṃ karaṇīyaṃ tīraitvā punadaiva rājagahaṃ pac‍cāgañchi| yaina rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ  sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘vaisālī, daiva, iddhā caiva phitā ca bahujanā ca ākiṇṇamanussā ca subhikkhā ca; satta ca pāsādasahassāni…pai॰… tāya ca vaisālī bhiyyausaumattāya upasaubhati| sādhu, daiva, mayampi gaṇikaṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmā’’ti vuṭṭhāpaiyyāma (ka॰)| ‘‘taina hi, bhaṇai, tādisiṃ kumāriṃ jānātha yaṃ tumhai gaṇikaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiyyāthā’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina rājagahai sālavatī nāma  kumārī abhirūpā hauti dassanīyā pāsādikā paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatā| atha khau rājagahakau naigamau sālavatiṃ kumāriṃ gaṇikaṃ  vuṭṭhāpaisi| atha khau sālavatī gaṇikā nacirassaiva padakkhiṇā ahausi nac‍cai ca gītai ca vāditai ca, abhisaṭā atthikānaṃ atthikānaṃ manussānaṃ paṭisataina ca rattiṃ gacchati| atha khau sālavatī gaṇikā nacirassaiva gabbhinī ahausi| atha khau sālavatiyā gaṇikāya aitadahausi – ‘‘itthī khau gabbhinī purisānaṃ amanāpā| sacai maṃ kauci jānissati sālavatī gaṇikā gabbhinīti, sabbau mai sak‍kārau bhañ‍jissati  parihāyissati (sī॰ syā॰)| yaṃnūnāhaṃ gilānaṃ paṭivaidaiyya’’nti| atha khau sālavatī gaṇikā dauvārikaṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘mā, bhaṇai dauvārika, kauci purisau pāvisi| yau ca maṃ pucchati, ‘gilānā’ti paṭivaidaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, ayyai’’ti khau sau dauvārikau sālavatiyā gaṇikāya pac‍cassausi| atha khau sālavatī gaṇikā tassa gabbhassa paripākamanvāya puttaṃ vijāyi| atha khau sālavatī gaṇikā dāsiṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘handa, jai, imaṃ dārakaṃ kattarasuppai pakkhipitvā nīharitvā saṅkārakūṭai chaḍḍaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, ayyai’’ti khau sā dāsī sālavatiyā gaṇikāya paṭissutvā taṃ dārakaṃ kattarasuppai pakkhipitvā nīharitvā saṅkārakūṭai chaḍḍaisi|

328. taina khau pana samayaina abhayau nāma rājakumārau kālassaiva rājupaṭṭhānaṃ gacchantau addasa taṃ dārakaṃ kākaihi samparikiṇṇaṃ, disvāna manussai pucchi – ‘‘kiṃ aitaṃ, bhaṇai, kākaihi samparikiṇṇa’’nti? ‘‘dārakau, daivā’’ti| ‘‘jīvati, bhaṇai’’ti? ‘‘jīvati, daivā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhaṇai, taṃ dārakaṃ amhākaṃ antaipuraṃ naitvā dhātīnaṃ daitha pausaitu’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau tai manussā abhayassa rājakumārassa paṭissutvā taṃ  dārakaṃ abhayassa rājakumārassa antaipuraṃ naitvā dhātīnaṃ adaṃsu – ‘‘pausaithā’’ti| tassa jīvatīti ‘jīvakau’ti nāmaṃ akaṃsu| kumāraina pausāpitauti ‘kaumārabhac‍cau’ti nāmaṃ akaṃsu| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau nacirassaiva viñ‍ñutaṃ pāpuṇi| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau yaina abhayau rājakumārau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā abhayaṃ rājakumāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kā mai, daiva, mātā, kau pitā’’ti? ‘‘ahampi khau tai, bhaṇai jīvaka, mātaraṃ na jānāmi; api cāhaṃ tai pitā; mayāsi mayāpi (ka॰) pausāpitau’’ti| atha khau jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa aitadahausi – ‘‘imāni  khau rājakulāni na sukarāni asippaina upajīvituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ sippaṃ sikkhaiyya’’nti|

329. taina khau pana samayaina tak‍kasilāyaṃ tak‍kasīlāyaṃ (ka॰) disāpāmaukkhau vaij‍jau paṭivasati| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau abhayaṃ rājakumāraṃ anāpucchā yaina tak‍kasilā taina  pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina yaina tak‍kasilā, yaina vaij‍jau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ vaij‍jaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, ācariya, sippaṃ sikkhitu’’nti| ‘‘taina hi, bhaṇai  jīvaka, sikkhassū’’ti| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau bahuñ‍ca gaṇhāti lahuñ‍ca gaṇhāti suṭṭhu ca upadhāraiti, gahitañ‍cassa na sammussati na pamussati (sī॰ syā॰)| atha khau jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa sattan‍naṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina aitadahausi – ‘‘ahaṃ, khau bahuñ‍ca gaṇhāmi lahuñ‍ca gaṇhāmi suṭṭhu ca upadhāraimi, gahitañ‍ca mai na sammussati, satta ca mai vassāni adhīyantassa, nayimassa sippassa antau pañ‍ñāyati| kadā imassa sippassa antau pañ‍ñāyissatī’’ti| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau yaina sau vaij‍jau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ vaij‍jaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, ācariya, bahuñ‍ca gaṇhāmi lahuñ‍ca gaṇhāmi suṭṭhu ca upadhāraimi, gahitañ‍ca mai na sammussati, satta ca mai vassāni adhīyantassa, nayimassa sippassa antau pañ‍ñāyati| kadā imassa sippassa antau pañ‍ñāyissatī’’ti? ‘‘taina hi, bhaṇai jīvaka, khaṇittiṃ ādāya tak‍kasilāya samantā yaujanaṃ āhiṇḍitvā yaṃ kiñ‍ci abhaisaj‍jaṃ passaiyyāsi taṃ āharā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, ācariyā’’ti khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau tassa vaij‍jassa paṭissutvā khaṇittiṃ ādāya tak‍kasilāya  samantā yaujanaṃ āhiṇḍantau na kiñ‍ci abhaisaj‍jaṃ addasa| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau yaina sau vaij‍jau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ vaij‍jaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āhiṇḍantaumhi, ācariya, tak‍kasilāya samantā yaujanaṃ, na kiñ‍ci āhiṇṭantau na kiñ‍ci (ka॰) abhaisaj‍jaṃ addasa’’nti| ‘‘susikkhitausi, bhaṇai jīvaka| alaṃ tai aittakaṃ jīvikāyā’’ti jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa parittaṃ pāthaiyyaṃ pādāsi| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau taṃ parittaṃ pāthaiyyaṃ ādāya yaina rājagahaṃ taina pak‍kāmi| atha khau jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa taṃ parittaṃ pāthaiyyaṃ antarāmaggai sākaitai parikkhayaṃ agamāsi| atha khau jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa aitadahausi – ‘‘imai khau maggā kantārā appaudakā appabhakkhā, na sukarā apāthaiyyaina gantuṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ pāthaiyyaṃ pariyaisaiyya’’nti|

jīvakavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

203. saiṭṭhibhariyāvatthu

330. taina  khau pana samayaina sākaitai saiṭṭhibhariyāya sattavassikau sīsābādhau hauti| bahū mahantā mahantā disāpāmaukkhā vaij‍jā āgantvā nāsakkhiṃsu araugaṃ kātuṃ| bahuṃ hirañ‍ñaṃ ādāya agamaṃsu| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau sākaitaṃ pavisitvā manussai pucchi – ‘‘kau, bhaṇai, gilānau, kaṃ tikicchāmī’’ti? ‘‘aitissā, ācariya, saiṭṭhibhariyāya sattavassikau  sīsābādhau; gaccha, ācariya, saiṭṭhibhariyaṃ tikicchāhī’’ti| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau yaina saiṭṭhissa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā dauvārikaṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha, bhaṇai dauvārika, saiṭṭhibhariyāya pāvada – ‘vaij‍jau, ayyai, āgatau, sau taṃ daṭṭhukāmau’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, ācariyā’’ti khau sau dauvārikau jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa paṭissutvā yaina saiṭṭhibhariyā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā saiṭṭhibhariyaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vaij‍jau, ayyai, āgatau; sau taṃ daṭṭhukāmau’’ti| ‘‘kīdisau, bhaṇai dauvārika, vaij‍jau’’ti? ‘‘daharakau, ayyai’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, bhaṇai dauvārika, kiṃ mai daharakau vaij‍jau karissati? bahū mahantā mahantā disāpāmaukkhā vaij‍jā āgantvā  nāsakkhiṃsu araugaṃ kātuṃ| bahuṃ hirañ‍ñaṃ ādāya agamaṃsū’’ti| atha khau sau dauvārikau yaina jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saiṭṭhibhariyā, ācariya, aivamāha – ‘alaṃ, bhaṇai dauvārika, kiṃ mai daharakau vaij‍jau karissati? bahū mahantā mahantā disāpāmaukkhā vaij‍jā āgantvā nāsakkhiṃsu araugaṃ kātuṃ| bahuṃ hirañ‍ñaṃ ādāya agamaṃsū’’’ti| ‘‘gaccha, bhaṇai dauvārika, saiṭṭhibhariyāya pāvada – ‘vaij‍jau, ayyai, aivamāha – mā kira, ayyai, purai kiñ‍ci adāsi| yadā araugā ahausi tadā yaṃ icchaiyyāsi taṃ daj‍jaiyyāsī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, ācariyā’’ti khau sau dauvārikau jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa paṭissutvā yaina saiṭṭhibhariyā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā saiṭṭhibhariyaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vaij‍jau, ayyai, aivamāha – ‘mā kira, ayyai, purai kiñ‍ci adāsi| yadā araugā ahausi tadā yaṃ icchaiyyāsi taṃ daj‍jaiyyāsī’’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhaṇai dauvārika, vaij‍jau āgacchatū’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, ayyai’’ti khau sau dauvārikau saiṭṭhibhariyāya paṭissutvā yaina jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saiṭṭhibhariyā taṃ, ācariya, pak‍kausatī’’ti|

atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau yaina saiṭṭhibhariyā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā saiṭṭhibhariyāya vikāraṃ sal‍lakkhaitvā saiṭṭhibhariyaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pasataina, ayyai, sappinā atthau’’ti| atha khau saiṭṭhibhariyā jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa pasataṃ sappiṃ dāpaisi| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau taṃ pasataṃ sappiṃ nānābhaisaj‍jaihi nippacitvā saiṭṭhibhariyaṃ mañ‍cakai uttānaṃ nipātaitvā nipaj‍jāpaitvā (sī॰ syā॰) natthutau adāsi| atha khau taṃ sappiṃ natthutau din‍naṃ mukhatau uggañchi| atha khau saiṭṭhibhariyā paṭiggahai niṭṭhubhitvā dāsiṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘handa, jai, imaṃ sappiṃ picunā gaṇhāhī’’ti| atha khau jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa aitadahausi – ‘‘acchariyaṃ acchariyaṃ vata bhau (syā॰) yāva lūkhāyaṃ gharaṇī, yatra hi nāma imaṃ chaḍḍanīyadhammaṃ sappiṃ picunā gāhāpaissati| bahukāni  ca mai mahagghāni mahagghāni mahagghāni (sī॰ syā॰) bhaisaj‍jāni upagatāni| kimpi māyaṃ kiñ‍ci kañ‍ci (syā॰) daiyyadhammaṃ dassatī’’ti| atha khau saiṭṭhibhariyā jīvakassa  kaumārabhac‍cassa vikāraṃ sal‍lakkhaitvā jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, ācariya, vimanausī’’ti? idha mai aitadahausi – ‘‘acchariyaṃ yāva lūkhāyaṃ dharaṇī, yatra hi nāma imaṃ chaḍḍanīyadhammaṃ sappiṃ picunā gāhāpaissati| bahukāni ca mai mahagghāni saj‍jāni upagatāni| kimpi māyaṃ kiñ‍ci daiyyadhammaṃ dassatī’’ti| ‘‘mayaṃ  khau, ācariya, āgārikā nāma upajānāmaitassa saṃyamassa| varamaitaṃ sappi dāsānaṃ vā kammakarānaṃ vā pādabbhañ‍janaṃ vā padīpakaraṇai vā āsittaṃ| mā khau tvaṃ, ācariya, vimanau ahausi| na tai daiyyadhammau hāyissatī’’ti| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau saiṭṭhibhariyāya sattavassikaṃ sīsābādhaṃ aikainaiva natthukammaina apakaḍḍhi| atha khau saiṭṭhibhariyā araugā samānā jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa cattāri sahassāni pādāsi| puttau – mātā mai araugā ṭhitāti cattāri sahassāni pādāsi| suṇisā – sassu mai araugā ṭhitāti cattāri sahassāni pādāsi| saiṭṭhi gahapati – bhariyā mai araugā ṭhitāti cattāri sahassāni pādāsi dāsañ‍ca dāsiñ‍ca assarathañ‍ca|

atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau tāni sauḷasasahassāni ādāya dāsañ‍ca dāsiñ‍ca assarathañ‍ca yaina rājagahaṃ taina pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina yaina rājagahaṃ yaina abhayau rājakumārau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā abhayaṃ rājakumāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ mai, daiva, paṭhamakammaṃ sauḷasasahassāni dāsau ca dāsī ca assarathau ca| paṭiggaṇhātu mai daivau pausāvanika’’nti| ‘‘alaṃ, bhaṇai jīvaka; tuyhamaiva hautu| amhākañ‍ñaiva antaipurai nivaisanaṃ māpaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau abhayassa rājakumārassa paṭissutvā abhayassa rājakumārassa antaipurai nivaisanaṃ māpaisi|

saiṭṭhibhariyāvatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

204. bimbisārarājavatthu

331. taina  khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa bhagandalābādhau hauti| sāṭakā lauhitaina makkhiyanti| daiviyau disvā uppaṇḍainti – ‘‘utunī dāni daivau, pupphaṃ daivassa uppan‍naṃ, na ciraṃ nacirassaiva (syā॰) daivau vijāyissatī’’ti| taina rājā maṅku hauti| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau abhayaṃ rājakumāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mayhaṃ khau, bhaṇai abhaya, tādisau ābādhau, sāṭakā lauhitaina makkhiyanti, daiviyau maṃ disvā uppaṇḍainti – ‘utunī dāni daivau, pupphaṃ daivassa uppan‍naṃ, na ciraṃ daivau vijāyissatī’ti| iṅgha, bhaṇai abhaya, tādisaṃ vaij‍jaṃ jānāhi yau maṃ tikicchaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘ayaṃ, daiva, amhākaṃ jīvakau vaij‍jau taruṇau bhadrakau| sau daivaṃ tikicchissatī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhaṇai abhaya, jīvakaṃ  vaij‍jaṃ āṇāpaihi; sau maṃ tikicchissatī’’ti| atha khau abhayau rājakumārau jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha, bhaṇai jīvaka, rājānaṃ tikicchāhī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau abhayassa rājakumārassa paṭissutvā nakhaina bhaisaj‍jaṃ ādāya yaina rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ābādhaṃ tai, daiva, passāmā’’ti passāmīti (syā॰)| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa bhagandalābādhaṃ aikainaiva ālaipaina apakaḍḍhi| atha khau rājā māgadhau  sainiyau bimbisārau araugau samānau pañ‍ca itthisatāni sabbālaṅkāraṃ bhūsāpaitvā aumuñ‍cāpaitvā puñ‍jaṃ kārāpaitvā jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitaṃ, bhaṇai jīvaka, pañ‍can‍naṃ itthisatānaṃ sabbālaṅkāraṃ tuyhaṃ hautū’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, daiva, adhikāraṃ mai daivau saratū’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhaṇai jīvaka, maṃ upaṭṭhaha, itthāgārañ‍ca, buddhappamukhañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅgha’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa pac‍cassausi|

bimbisārarājavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

205. rājagahasaiṭṭhivatthu

332. taina khau pana samayaina rājagahakassa saiṭṭhissa sattavassikau sīsābādhau hauti| bahū mahantā mahantā disāpāmaukkhā vaij‍jā āgantvā nāsakkhiṃsu araugaṃ kātuṃ| bahuṃ hirañ‍ñaṃ ādāya agamaṃsu| api ca, vaij‍jaihi pac‍cakkhātau hauti| aikac‍cai vaij‍jā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘pañ‍camaṃ divasaṃ saiṭṭhi gahapati kālaṃ karissatī’’ti| aikac‍cai vaij‍jā aivamāhaṃsu  – ‘‘sattamaṃ divasaṃ saiṭṭhi gahapati kālaṃ karissatī’’ti| atha  khau rājagahakassa naigamassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau saiṭṭhi gahapati bahūpakārau rañ‍ñau caiva naigamassa ca| api ca, vaij‍jaihi pac‍cakkhātau| aikac‍cai vaij‍jā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘pañ‍camaṃ divasaṃ saiṭṭhi gahapati kālaṃ karissatī’ti| aikac‍cai vaij‍jā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘sattamaṃ divasaṃ saiṭṭhi gahapati kālaṃ karissatī’ti| ayañ‍ca rañ‍ñau jīvakau vaij‍jau taruṇau bhadrakau| yaṃnūna mayaṃ rājānaṃ jīvakaṃ vaij‍jaṃ yācaiyyāma saiṭṭhiṃ gahapatiṃ  tikicchitu’’nti| atha khau rājagahakau naigamau yaina rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, daiva, saiṭṭhi gahapati bahūpakārau daivassa caiva naigamassa ca; api ca, vaij‍jaihi pac‍cakkhātau| aikac‍cai vaij‍jā aivamāhaṃsu – pañ‍camaṃ divasaṃ saiṭṭhi gahapati kālaṃ karissatīti| aikac‍cai vaij‍jā aivamāhaṃsu – sattamaṃ divasaṃ saiṭṭhi gahapati kālaṃ karissatīti| sādhu daivau jīvakaṃ vaij‍jaṃ āṇāpaitu saiṭṭhiṃ gahapatiṃ tikicchitu’’nti|

atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha, bhaṇai jīvaka, saiṭṭhiṃ gahapatiṃ tikicchāhī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa paṭissutvā yaina saiṭṭhi gahapati tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā saiṭṭhissa gahapatissa vikāraṃ sal‍lakkhaitvā saiṭṭhiṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai tvaṃ, gahapati, araugau bhavaiyyāsi sacāhaṃ taṃ gahapati araugāpaiyyaṃ (sī॰), sacāhaṃ taṃ gahapati araugaṃ karaiyyaṃ (syā॰) kiṃ mai assa daiyyadhammau’’ti? ‘‘sabbaṃ sāpataiyyañ‍ca tai, ācariya, hautu, ahañ‍ca tai dāsau’’ti| ‘‘sakkhissasi pana tvaṃ, gahapati, aikaina passaina sattamāsai nipaj‍jitu’’nti? ‘‘sak‍kaumahaṃ, ācariya, aikaina passaina sattamāsai nipaj‍jitu’’nti| ‘‘sakkhissasi pana tvaṃ, gahapati, dutiyaina passaina sattamāsai nipaj‍jitu’’nti? ‘‘sak‍kaumahaṃ, ācariya, dutiyaina passaina sattamāsai nipaj‍jitu’’nti| ‘‘sakkhissasi pana tvaṃ, gahapati, uttānau sattamāsai nipaj‍jitu’’nti? ‘‘sak‍kaumahaṃ, ācariya, uttānau sattamāsai nipaj‍jitu’’nti|

atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau saiṭṭhiṃ gahapatiṃ mañ‍cakai nipātaitvā nipaj‍jāpaitvā (sī॰ syā॰) mañ‍cakai mañ‍cakaina (sī॰) sambandhitvā sīsacchaviṃ uppāṭaitvā phālaitvā (sī॰) sibbiniṃ vināmaitvā dvai  pāṇakai nīharitvā mahājanassa dassaisi – ‘‘passathayyai passaisyātha (sī॰), passatha (syā॰), passathayyau (ka॰), imai dvai pāṇakai, aikaṃ khuddakaṃ aikaṃ mahal‍lakaṃ| yai tai ācariyā aivamāhaṃsu – pañ‍camaṃ divasaṃ saiṭṭhi gahapati kālaṃ karissatīti – taihāyaṃ mahal‍lakau pāṇakau diṭṭhau| pañ‍camaṃ divasaṃ saiṭṭhissa gahapatissa matthaluṅgaṃ pariyādiyissati| matthaluṅgassa pariyādānā saiṭṭhi gahapati kālaṃ karissati| sudiṭṭhau taihi ācariyaihi| yai tai ācariyā aivamāhaṃsu – sattamaṃ divasaṃ  saiṭṭhi gahapati kālaṃ karissatīti – taihāyaṃ khuddakau pāṇakau diṭṭhau| sattamaṃ divasaṃ saiṭṭhissa gahapatissa matthaluṅgaṃ pariyādiyissati| matthaluṅgassa pariyādānā saiṭṭhi gahapati kālaṃ karissati| sudiṭṭhau taihi ācariyaihī’’ti| sibbiniṃ sampaṭipāṭaitvā sīsacchaviṃ sibbitvā ālaipaṃ adāsi| atha khau saiṭṭhi gahapati sattāhassa ac‍cayaina jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nāhaṃ, ācariya, sak‍kaumi aikaina passaina sattamāsai nipaj‍jitu’’nti| ‘‘nanu mai tvaṃ, gahapati, paṭissuṇi – sak‍kaumahaṃ, ācariya, aikaina passaina sattamāsai  nipaj‍jitu’’nti? ‘‘sac‍cāhaṃ, ācariya, paṭissuṇiṃ, apāhaṃ marissāmi, nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi aikaina passaina sattamāsai nipaj‍jitu’’nti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, gahapati, dutiyaina passaina sattamāsai nipaj‍jāhī’’ti| atha khau saiṭṭhi gahapati sattāhassa ac‍cayaina jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘nāhaṃ, ācariya, sak‍kaumi dutiyaina passaina sattamāsai nipaj‍jitu’’nti| ‘‘nanu mai tvaṃ, gahapati, paṭissuṇi – sak‍kaumahaṃ, ācariya, dutiyaina passaina sattamāsai nipaj‍jitu’’nti? ‘‘sac‍cāhaṃ, ācariya, paṭissuṇiṃ, apāhaṃ marissāmi, nāhaṃ, ācariya, sak‍kaumi dutiyaina passaina sattamāsai nipaj‍jitu’’nti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, gahapati, uttānau sattamāsai nipaj‍jāhī’’ti| atha khau saiṭṭhi gahapati sattāhassa ac‍cayaina jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nāhaṃ, ācariya, sak‍kaumi uttānau sattamāsai nipaj‍jitu’’nti| ‘‘nanu mai tvaṃ, gahapati, paṭissuṇi – sak‍kaumahaṃ, ācariya, uttānau sattamāsai nipaj‍jitu’’nti? ‘‘sac‍cāhaṃ, ācariya, paṭissuṇiṃ, apāhaṃ marissāmi, nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi uttānau sattamāsai nipaj‍jitu’’nti| ‘‘ahaṃ cai taṃ, gahapati, na vadaiyyaṃ, aittakampi tvaṃ na nipaj‍jaiyyāsi, api ca paṭikac‍caiva mayā ñātau – tīhi sattāhaihi saiṭṭhi gahapati araugau bhavissatīti| uṭṭhaihi, gahapati, araugausi| jānāsi kiṃ mai daiyyadhammau’’ti? ‘‘sabbaṃ sāpataiyyañ‍ca tai, ācariya, hautu, ahañ‍ca tai dāsau’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, gahapati, mā mai  tvaṃ sabbaṃ sāpataiyyaṃ adāsi, mā ca mai dāsau| rañ‍ñau satasahassaṃ daihi, mayhaṃ satasahassa’’nti| atha khau saiṭṭhi gahapati araugau samānau rañ‍ñau satasahassaṃ adāsi, jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa satasahassaṃ|

rājagahasaiṭṭhivatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

206. saiṭṭhiputtavatthu

333. taina khau pana samayaina bārāṇasaiyyakassa saiṭṭhiputtassa maukkhacikāya kīḷantassa antagaṇṭhābādhau hauti, yaina yāgupi pītā na sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, bhattampi bhuttaṃ na sammā  pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, uc‍cāraupi passāvaupi na paguṇau| sau taina kisau hauti lūkhau dubbaṇṇau uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau| atha khau bārāṇasaiyyakassa saiṭṭhissa aitadahausi – ‘‘mayhaṃ khau puttassa tādisau ābādhau, yaina yāgupi pītā na sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, bhattampi bhuttaṃ na sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, uc‍cāraupi passāvaupi na paguṇau| sau taina kisau lūkhau dubbaṇṇau uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ rājagahaṃ gantvā rājānaṃ jīvakaṃ vaij‍jaṃ yācaiyyaṃ puttaṃ mai tikicchitu’’nti| atha khau bārāṇasaiyyakau saiṭṭhi rājagahaṃ gantvā yaina rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ sainiyaṃ bimbisāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mayhaṃ khau, daiva, puttassa tādisau ābādhau, yaina yāgupi pītā na sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, bhattampi bhuttaṃ na sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, uc‍cāraupi passāvaupi na paguṇau| sau taina kisau lūkhau  dubbaṇṇau uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau| sādhu daivau jīvakaṃ vaij‍jaṃ  āṇāpaitu puttaṃ mai tikicchitu’’nti|

atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha, bhaṇai jīvaka, bārāṇasiṃ gantvā bārāṇasaiyyakaṃ saiṭṭhiputtaṃ tikicchāhī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa paṭissutvā bārāṇasiṃ gantvā yaina bārāṇasaiyyakau saiṭṭhiputtau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bārāṇasaiyyakassa saiṭṭhiputtassa vikāraṃ sal‍lakkhaitvā janaṃ ussāraitvā  tiraukaraṇiyaṃ parikkhipitvā thambhai ubbandhitvā upanibandhitvā (sī॰ syā॰) bhariyaṃ puratau ṭhapaitvā udaracchaviṃ uppāṭaitvā antagaṇṭhiṃ nīharitvā bhariyāya dassaisi – ‘‘passa tai sāmikassa ābādhaṃ, iminā yāgupi pītā na sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, bhattampi bhuttaṃ na sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, uc‍cāraupi passāvaupi na paguṇau; imināyaṃ kisau lūkhau dubbaṇṇau uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātau dhamanisanthatagattau’’ti| antagaṇṭhiṃ vinivaiṭhaitvā antāni paṭipavaisaitvā udaracchaviṃ sibbitvā ālaipaṃ adāsi| atha khau bārāṇasaiyyakau saiṭṭhiputtau nacirassaiva araugau ahausi| atha khau bārāṇasaiyyakau saiṭṭhi ‘puttau mai araugau ṭhitau’ti araugāpitauti (sī॰) jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa sauḷasasahassāni pādāsi| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau tāni sauḷasasahassāni ādāya punadaiva rājagahaṃ pac‍cāgañchi|

saiṭṭhiputtavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

207. paj‍jautarājavatthu

334. taina  khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñau uj‍jainiyaṃ rañ‍ñau (syā॰) paj‍jautassa paṇḍuraugābādhau hauti| bahū mahantā mahantā disāpāmaukkhā vaij‍jā āgantvā nāsakkhiṃsu araugaṃ kātuṃ| bahuṃ hirañ‍ñaṃ ādāya agamaṃsu| atha khau rājā paj‍jautau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘mayhaṃ khau tādisau ābādhau, sādhu daivau jīvakaṃ vaij‍jaṃ āṇāpaitu, sau maṃ tikicchissatī’’ti| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha, bhaṇai jīvaka; uj‍jainiṃ gantvā rājānaṃ paj‍jautaṃ tikicchāhī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa paṭissutvā uj‍jainiṃ gantvā yaina rājā paj‍jautau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rañ‍ñau paj‍jautassa vikāraṃ sal‍lakkhaitvā rājānaṃ paj‍jautaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sappiṃ daihi idaṃ padadvayaṃ sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu natthi, sappiṃ daiva, nippacissāmi| taṃ daivau pivissatī’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, bhaṇai jīvaka, yaṃ tai sak‍kā vinā sappinā araugaṃ kātuṃ taṃ karauhi| jaigucchaṃ mai sappi, paṭikūla’’nti| atha khau jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa aitadahausi  – ‘‘imassa khau rañ‍ñau tādisau ābādhau, na sak‍kā vinā sappinā araugaṃ kātuṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ sappiṃ nippacaiyyaṃ  kasāvavaṇṇaṃ kasāvagandhaṃ kasāvarasa’’nti| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau nānābhaisaj‍jaihi sappiṃ nippaci kasāvavaṇṇaṃ kasāvagandhaṃ kasāvarasaṃ| atha khau jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa aitadahausi – ‘‘imassa khau rañ‍ñau sappi pītaṃ pariṇāmaintaṃ uddaikaṃ dassati| caṇḍauyaṃ rājā ghātāpaiyyāpi maṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ paṭikac‍caiva āpucchaiyya’’nti| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau yaina rājā paj‍jautau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ paj‍jautaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mayaṃ khau, daiva, vaij‍jā nāma tādisaina muhuttaina mūlāni uddharāma bhaisaj‍jāni saṃharāma| sādhu daivau vāhanāgāraisu ca dvāraisu ca āṇāpaitu – yaina vāhanaina jīvakau icchati taina vāhanaina gacchatu, yaina dvāraina icchati taina dvāraina gacchatu, yaṃ kālaṃ icchati taṃ kālaṃ gacchatu, yaṃ kālaṃ icchati taṃ kālaṃ pavisatū’’ti| atha khau rājā paj‍jautau vāhanāgāraisu ca dvāraisu ca āṇāpaisi – ‘‘yaina vāhanaina jīvakau icchati taina vāhanaina gacchatu, yaina dvāraina icchati taina dvāraina gacchatu, yaṃ kālaṃ icchati taṃ kālaṃ gacchatu, yaṃ kālaṃ icchati taṃ kālaṃ pavisatū’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñau paj‍jautassa bhaddavatikā nāma hatthinikā pañ‍ñāsayaujanikā hauti| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau rañ‍ñau paj‍jautassa sappiṃ taṃ sappiṃ (syā॰) upanāmaisi  – ‘‘kasāvaṃ daivau pivatū’’ti| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau rājānaṃ paj‍jautaṃ sappiṃ pāyaitvā hatthisālaṃ gantvā bhaddavatikāya hatthinikāya nagaramhā nippati|

atha khau rañ‍ñau paj‍jautassa taṃ sappi pītaṃ pariṇāmaintaṃ uddaikaṃ adāsi| atha khau rājā paj‍jautau manussai aitadavauca – ‘‘duṭṭhaina, bhaṇai, jīvakaina sappiṃ pāyitaumhi| taina hi, bhaṇai, jīvakaṃ vaij‍jaṃ vicinathā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddavatikāya, daiva, hatthinikāya nagaramhā nippatitau’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñau paj‍jautassa kākau nāma dāsau saṭṭhiyaujanikau hauti, amanussaina paṭic‍ca jātau| atha khau rājā paj‍jautau kākaṃ dāsaṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha, bhaṇai kāka, jīvakaṃ vaij‍jaṃ  nivattaihi – rājā taṃ, ācariya, nivattāpaitīti| aitai khau, bhaṇai kāka, vaij‍jā nāma bahumāyā| mā cassa kiñ‍ci paṭiggahaisī’’ti|

atha khau kākau dāsau jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ antarāmaggai kausambiyaṃ sambhāvaisi

pātarāsaṃ karauntaṃ| atha khau kākau dāsau jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘rājā taṃ, ācariya, nivattāpaitī’’ti| ‘‘āgamaihi, bhaṇai kāka, yāva bhuñ‍jāma bhuñ‍jāmi (sī॰ syā॰)| handa, bhaṇai kāka, bhuñ‍jassū’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, ācariya, rañ‍ñāmhi āṇattau – aitai khau, bhaṇai kāka, vaij‍jā nāma bahumāyā, mā cassa kiñ‍ci paṭiggahaisī’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau nakhaina bhaisaj‍jaṃ aulumpaitvā āmalakañ‍ca khādati pānīyañ‍ca pivati| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau kākaṃ dāsaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘handa, bhaṇai kāka, āmalakañ‍ca khāda pānīyañ‍ca pivassū’’ti| atha khau kākau dāsau – ayaṃ khau vaij‍jau āmalakañ‍ca  khādati pānīyañ‍ca pivati, na arahati kiñ‍ci pāpakaṃ hautunti – upaḍḍhāmalakañ‍ca khādi pānīyañ‍ca apāyi| tassa taṃ upaḍḍhāmalakaṃ khāditaṃ tatthaiva nicchāraisi| atha khau kākau dāsau jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi mai, ācariya, jīvita’’nti? ‘‘mā, bhaṇai kāka, bhāyi, tvaṃ caiva araugau bhavissasi rājā ca| caṇḍau sau rājā ghātāpaiyyāpi maṃ, taināhaṃ na nivattāmī’’ti bhaddavatikaṃ hatthinikaṃ kākassa niyyādaitvā yaina rājagahaṃ taina pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina yaina rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘suṭṭhu, bhaṇai jīvaka, akāsi yampi na nivattau, caṇḍau sau rājā ghātāpaiyyāpi ta’’nti| atha khau rājā paj‍jautau araugau samānau jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘āgacchatu jīvakau, varaṃ dassāmī’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, ayyau daiva (syā॰), adhikāraṃ mai daivau saratū’’ti|

paj‍jautarājavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

208. sivaiyyakadussayugakathā

335. taina  khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñau paj‍jautassa sivaiyyakaṃ dussayugaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti – bahūnaṃ bahun‍naṃ (sī॰ syā॰) dussānaṃ bahūnaṃ dussayugānaṃ bahūnaṃ dussayugasatānaṃ bahūnaṃ dussayugasahassānaṃ bahūnaṃ dussayugasatasahassānaṃ  aggañ‍ca saiṭṭhañ‍ca maukkhañ‍ca uttamañ‍ca pavarañ‍ca| atha khau rājā paj‍jautau taṃ sivaiyyakaṃ dussayugaṃ jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa pāhaisi| atha khau jīvakassa  kaumārabhac‍cassa aitadahausi – ‘‘idaṃ khau mai sivaiyyakaṃ dussayugaṃ rañ‍ñā paj‍jautaina pahitaṃ – bahūnaṃ dussānaṃ bahūnaṃ dussayugānaṃ bahūnaṃ dussayugasatānaṃ bahūnaṃ dussayugasahassānaṃ bahūnaṃ dussayugasatasahassānaṃ aggañ‍ca saiṭṭhañ‍ca maukkhañ‍ca uttamañ‍ca pavarañ‍ca| nayidaṃ añ‍ñau kauci pac‍cārahati añ‍ñatra taina bhagavatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina, rañ‍ñā vā māgadhaina sainiyaina bimbisārainā’’ti|

sivaiyyakadussayugakathā niṭṭhitā|

209. samattiṃsaviraicanakathā

336. taina khau pana samayaina bhagavatau kāyau dausābhisan‍nau hauti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘dausābhisan‍nau  khau, ānanda, tathāgatassa kāyau| icchati tathāgatau viraicanaṃ pātu’’nti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘dausābhisan‍nau khau, āvusau jīvaka, tathāgatassa kāyau| icchati tathāgatau viraicanaṃ pātu’’nti| ‘‘taina hi, bhantai ānanda, bhagavatau kāyaṃ katipāhaṃ sinaihaithā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau kāyaṃ katipāhaṃ sinaihaitvā yaina jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘siniddhau khau, āvusau jīvaka, tathāgatassa kāyau| yassa dāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa aitadahausi – ‘‘na khau maitaṃ patirūpaṃ yauhaṃ bhagavatau auḷārikaṃ viraicanaṃ dadaiyya’’nti| tīṇi uppalahatthāni nānābhaisaj‍jaihi paribhāvaitvā yaina  bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃ uppalahatthaṃ bhagavatau upanāmaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā paṭhamaṃ uppalahatthaṃ upasiṅghatu| idaṃ bhagavantaṃ dasakkhattuṃ viraicaissatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ uppalahatthaṃ bhagavatau upanāmaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dutiyaṃ uppalahatthaṃ upasiṅghatu| idaṃ bhagavantaṃ dasakkhattuṃ viraicaissatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ uppalahatthaṃ bhagavatau upanāmaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhantai, bhagavā tatiyaṃ uppalahatthaṃ upasiṅghatu| idaṃ bhagavantaṃ dasakkhattuṃ viraicaissatī’’ti| aivaṃ bhagavatau samattiṃsāya viraicanaṃ bhavissatīti| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau bhagavatau samattiṃsāya viraicanaṃ datvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa bahi dvārakauṭṭhakā nikkhantassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mayā khau bhagavatau samattiṃsāya viraicanaṃ din‍naṃ| dausābhisan‍nau tathāgatassa kāyau| na bhagavantaṃ samattiṃsakkhattuṃ viraicaissati, aikūnattiṃsakkhattuṃ bhagavantaṃ viraicaissati| api ca, bhagavā virittau nahāyissati| nahātaṃ bhagavantaṃ sakiṃ viraicaissati| aivaṃ bhagavatau samattiṃsāya viraicanaṃ bhavissatī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘idhānanda, jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa bahi dvārakauṭṭhakā nikkhantassa aitadahausi – ‘mayā khau bhagavatau samattiṃsāya viraicanaṃ din‍naṃ| dausābhisan‍nau tathāgatassa kāyau| na bhagavantaṃ samatiṃsakkhattuṃ viraicaissati, aikūnatiṃsakkhattuṃ bhagavantaṃ viraicaissati| api ca, bhagavā virittau nahāyissati| nahātaṃ bhagavantaṃ sakiṃ viraicaissati| aivaṃ bhagavatau samattiṃsāya viraicanaṃ bhavissatī’ti| taina hānanda, uṇhaudakaṃ paṭiyādaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā uṇhaudakaṃ  paṭiyādaisi|

atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘virittau, bhantai, bhagavā’’ti? ‘‘virittaumhi, jīvakā’’ti| idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, bahi dvārakauṭṭhakā nikkhantassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mayā khau bhagavatau samattiṃsāya viraicanaṃ din‍naṃ| dausābhisan‍nau tathāgatassa kāyau| na bhagavantaṃ samattiṃsakkhattuṃ viraicaissati, aikūnattiṃsakkhattuṃ bhagavantaṃ viraicaissati| api ca, bhagavā virittau nahāyissati| nahātaṃ bhagavantaṃ sakiṃ viraicaissati| aivaṃ bhagavatau samattiṃsāya viraicanaṃ bhavissatī’’ti| nahāyatu, bhantai, bhagavā, nahāyatu sugatauti| atha khau bhagavā uṇhaudakaṃ nahāyi| nahātaṃ bhagavantaṃ sakiṃ viraicaisi| aivaṃ bhagavatau samattiṃsāya viraicanaṃ ahausi| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘yāva, bhantai, bhagavatau kāyau pakatattau hauti, alaṃ ahaṃ tāva yūsapiṇṭapātaināti (sī॰), alaṃ yūsapiṇṭakaināti (syā॰) yūsapiṇḍapātainā’’ti|

samattiṃsaviraicanakathā niṭṭhitā|

210. varayācanākathā

337. atha  khau bhagavatau kāyau nacirassaiva pakatattau ahausi| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau taṃ sivaiyyakaṃ dussayugaṃ ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aikāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ varaṃ yācāmī’’ti| ‘‘atik‍kantavarā khau, jīvaka, tathāgatā’’ti| ‘‘yañ‍ca, bhantai, kappati yañ‍ca anavaj‍ja’’nti| ‘‘vadaihi, jīvakā’’ti| ‘‘bhagavā, bhantai, paṃsukūlikau, bhikkhusaṅghau ca| idaṃ mai, bhantai, sivaiyyakaṃ dussayugaṃ rañ‍ñā paj‍jautaina pahitaṃ – bahūnaṃ dussānaṃ bahūnaṃ dussayugānaṃ bahūnaṃ dussayugasatānaṃ bahūnaṃ dussayugasahassānaṃ bahūnaṃ dussayugasatasahassānaṃ aggañ‍ca saiṭṭhañ‍ca maukkhañ‍ca uttamañ‍ca pavarañ‍ca| paṭiggaṇhātu mai, bhantai, bhagavā sivaiyyakaṃ dussayugaṃ; bhikkhusaṅghassa ca gahapaticīvaraṃ anujānātū’’ti| paṭiggahaisi bhagavā sivaiyyakaṃ dussayugaṃ| atha khau bhagavā jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gahapaticīvaraṃ| yau icchati, paṃsukūlikau hautu| yau icchati, gahapaticīvaraṃ sādiyatu| itarītarainapāhaṃ pahaṃ (sī॰), cāhaṃ (syā॰), bhikkhavai, santuṭṭhiṃ vaṇṇaimī’’ti|

assausuṃ khau rājagahai manussā – ‘‘bhagavatā kira  bhikkhūnaṃ gahapaticīvaraṃ  anuñ‍ñāta’’nti| tai ca manussā haṭṭhā ahaisuṃ udaggā ‘‘idāni khau mayaṃ dānāni dassāma puñ‍ñāni karissāma, yatau bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ gahapaticīvaraṃ anuñ‍ñāta’’nti| aikāhainaiva rājagahai bahūni cīvarasahassāni uppaj‍jiṃsu|

assausuṃ  khau jānapadā manussā – ‘‘bhagavatā kira bhikkhūnaṃ gahapaticīvaraṃ anuñ‍ñāta’’nti| tai ca manussā haṭṭhā ahaisuṃ udaggā – ‘‘idāni khau mayaṃ dānāni dassāma puñ‍ñāni karissāma, yatau bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ gahapaticīvaraṃ anuñ‍ñāta’’nti| janapadaipi aikāhainaiva bahūni cīvarasahassāni uppaj‍jiṃsu|

taina  khau pana samayaina saṅghassa pāvārau uppan‍nau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pāvāranti|

kausaiyyapāvārau uppan‍nau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kausaiyyapāvāranti|

kaujavaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kaujavanti|

varayācanākathā niṭṭhitā|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

211. kambalānujānanādikathā

338. taina khau pana samayaina kāsirājā jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa aḍḍhakāsikaṃ kambalaṃ pāhaisi upaḍḍhakāsinaṃ khamamānaṃ| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau taṃ aḍḍhakāsikaṃ kambalaṃ ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ mai, bhantai, aḍḍhakāsikau kambalau kāsirañ‍ñā pahitau upaḍḍhakāsinaṃ khamamānau| paṭiggaṇhātu mai, bhantai, bhagavā kambalaṃ, yaṃ mamassa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| paṭiggahaisi bhagavā kambalaṃ| atha khau bhagavā jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi…pai॰… padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kambala’’nti|

339. taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa uc‍cāvacāni cīvarāni uppan‍nāni haunti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau bhagavatā cīvaraṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kiṃ ananuñ‍ñāta’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cha cīvarāni – khaumaṃ kappāsikaṃ kausaiyyaṃ kambalaṃ sāṇaṃ bhaṅganti|
