340. taina khau pana samayaina yai tai bhikkhū gahapaticīvaraṃ  sādiyanti tai kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā  paṃsukūlaṃ na sādiyanti – aikaṃyaiva bhagavatā cīvaraṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, na dvaiti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gahapaticīvaraṃ sādiyantaina paṃsukūlampi sādiyituṃ; tadubhayainapāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, santuṭṭhiṃ vaṇṇaimīti|

kambalānujānanādikathā niṭṭhitā|

212. paṃsukūlapariyaisanakathā

341. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū kausalaisu janapadai  addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū susānaṃ auk‍kamiṃsu paṃsukūlāya, aikac‍cai bhikkhū nāgamaisuṃ| yai tai bhikkhū susānaṃ auk‍kamiṃsu paṃsukūlāya tai paṃsukūlāni labhiṃsu| yai tai bhikkhū nāgamaisuṃ tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘amhākampi, āvusau, bhāgaṃ daithā’’ti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na mayaṃ, āvusau, tumhākaṃ bhāgaṃ dassāma| kissa tumhai nāgamitthā’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, nāgamaintānaṃ nākāmā bhāgaṃ dātunti|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū kausalaisu janapadai addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū susānaṃ auk‍kamiṃsu paṃsukūlāya, aikac‍cai bhikkhū āgamaisuṃ| yai tai bhikkhū susānaṃ auk‍kamiṃsu paṃsukūlāya tai paṃsukūlāni labhiṃsu| yai tai bhikkhū āgamaisuṃ tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘amhākampi, āvusau, bhāgaṃ daithā’’ti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na mayaṃ, āvusau, tumhākaṃ bhāgaṃ dassāma| kissa tumhai na auk‍kamitthā’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āgamaintānaṃ akāmā bhāgaṃ dātunti|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū kausalaisu janapadai addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū paṭhamaṃ susānaṃ auk‍kamiṃsu paṃsukūlāya, aikac‍cai bhikkhū pacchā auk‍kamiṃsu| yai tai bhikkhū paṭhamaṃ susānaṃ auk‍kamiṃsu paṃsukūlāya tai paṃsukūlāni labhiṃsu| yai tai bhikkhū  pacchā auk‍kamiṃsu tai na labhiṃsu| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘amhākampi, āvusau, bhāgaṃ daithā’’ti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na mayaṃ, āvusau, tumhākaṃ bhāgaṃ dassāma| kissa tumhai pacchā auk‍kamitthā’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pacchā auk‍kantānaṃ nākāmā bhāgaṃ dātunti|

taina  khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū kausalaisu janapadai addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| tai sadisā susānaṃ auk‍kamiṃsu paṃsukūlāya| aikac‍cai bhikkhū paṃsukūlāni labhiṃsu, aikac‍cai bhikkhū na labhiṃsu| yai tai bhikkhū na labhiṃsu, tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘amhākampi, āvusau, bhāgaṃ daithā’’ti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na mayaṃ, āvusau, tumhākaṃ bhāgaṃ dassāma| kissa tumhai na labhitthā’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sadisānaṃ auk‍kantānaṃ akāmā bhāgaṃ dātunti|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū kausalaisu janapadai addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| tai katikaṃ katvā susānaṃ auk‍kamiṃsu paṃsukūlāya| aikac‍cai bhikkhū paṃsukūlāni labhiṃsu, aikac‍cai bhikkhū na labhiṃsu| yai tai bhikkhū na labhiṃsu tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘amhākampi, āvusau, bhāgaṃ daithā’’ti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na mayaṃ, āvusau, tumhākaṃ bhāgaṃ dassāma| kissa tumhai na labhitthā’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, katikaṃ katvā auk‍kantānaṃ akāmā bhāgaṃ dātunti|

paṃsukūlapariyaisanakathā niṭṭhitā|

213. cīvarapaṭiggāhakasammutikathā

342. taina khau pana samayaina manussā cīvaraṃ ādāya ārāmaṃ āgacchanti| tai paṭiggāhakaṃ alabhamānā paṭiharanti| cīvaraṃ parittaṃ  uppaj‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvarapaṭiggāhakaṃ samman‍nituṃ – yau na chandāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na dausāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, gahitāgahitañ‍ca jānaiyya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau; yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvarapaṭiggāhakaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvarapaṭiggāhakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau  khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau cīvarapaṭiggāhakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu cīvarapaṭiggāhakau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina cīvarapaṭiggāhakā bhikkhū cīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitvā tatthaiva ujjhitvā pak‍kamanti| cīvaraṃ nassati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvaranidahakaṃ samman‍nituṃ – yau na chandāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na dausāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, nihitānihitañ‍ca jānaiyya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau; yācitvā byattaina  bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvaranidahakaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti| ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvaranidahakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau cīvaranidahakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu cīvaranidahakau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

cīvarapaṭiggāhakasammutikathā niṭṭhitā|

214. bhaṇḍāgārasammutiādikathā

343. taina khau pana samayaina cīvaranidahakau bhikkhu maṇḍapaipi rukkhamūlaipi nibbakausaipi cīvaraṃ nidahati, undūraihipi upacikāhipi khaj‍janti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhaṇḍāgāraṃ samman‍nituṃ, yaṃ saṅghau ākaṅkhati vihāraṃ vā aḍḍhayaugaṃ vā pāsādaṃ vā hammiyaṃ vā guhaṃ vā| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ vihāraṃ bhaṇḍāgāraṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ vihāraṃ bhaṇḍāgāraṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa vihārassa bhaṇḍāgārassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa  nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau vihārau bhaṇḍāgāraṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa bhaṇḍāgārai cīvaraṃ aguttaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ bhikkhuṃ bhaṇḍāgārikaṃ samman‍nituṃ – yau na chandāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na dausāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, guttāguttañ‍ca jānaiyya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau; yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ bhaṇḍāgārikaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ bhaṇḍāgārikaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau bhaṇḍāgārikassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu bhaṇḍāgārikau| khamati  saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhaṇḍāgārikaṃ vuṭṭhāpainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, bhaṇḍāgārikau vuṭṭhāpaitabbau| yau vuṭṭhāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina  khau pana samayaina saṅghassa bhaṇḍāgārai cīvaraṃ ussan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sammukhībhūtaina saṅghaina bhājaitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghau cīvaraṃ bhājaintau kaulāhalaṃ akāsi| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvarabhājakaṃ samman‍nituṃ – yau na chandāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na dausāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, bhājitābhājitañ‍ca jānaiyya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau; yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvarabhājakaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ cīvarabhājakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau cīvarabhājakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu cīvarabhājakau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

atha khau cīvarabhājakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau cīvaraṃ bhājaitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ uc‍cinitvā tulayitvā vaṇṇāvaṇṇaṃ katvā bhikkhū gaṇaitvā vaggaṃ bandhitvā cīvarapaṭivīsaṃ ṭhapaitunti|

atha khau cīvarabhājakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau sāmaṇairānaṃ cīvarapaṭivīsau dātabbau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sāmaṇairānaṃ upaḍḍhapaṭivīsaṃ  dātunti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sakaina bhāgaina uttaritukāmau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uttarantassa sakaṃ bhāgaṃ dātunti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu atiraikabhāgaina uttaritukāmau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, anukkhaipai din‍nai atiraikabhāgaṃ dātunti|

atha khau cīvarabhājakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau cīvarapaṭivīsau dātabbau, āgatapaṭipāṭiyā āgatāgatapaṭipāṭiyā (ka॰) nu khau udāhu yathāvuḍḍha’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vikalakai tausaitvā kusapātaṃ kātunti|

bhaṇḍāgārasammutiādikathā niṭṭhitā|

215. cīvararajanakathā

344. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū chakaṇainapi paṇḍumattikāyapi  cīvaraṃ rajanti| cīvaraṃ dubbaṇṇaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cha rajanāni – mūlarajanaṃ, khandharajanaṃ, tacarajanaṃ, pattarajanaṃ, puppharajanaṃ, phalarajananti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū sītudakāya sītundikāya (sī॰), sītūdakāya (syā॰) cīvaraṃ rajanti| cīvaraṃ duggandhaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, rajanaṃ pacituṃ cul‍laṃ rajanakumbhinti| rajanaṃ uttariyati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uttarāḷumpaṃ uttarāḷupaṃ (yaujanā), uttarāḷupaṃ (syā॰) bandhitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū na jānanti rajanaṃ pak‍kaṃ vā apak‍kaṃ vā| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakai vā nakhapiṭṭhikāya vā thaivakaṃ  dātunti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū rajanaṃ auraupaintā kumbhiṃ āviñchanti āviñ‍janti (sī॰), āvaṭṭanti (syā॰)| kumbhī bhij‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, rajanuḷuṅkaṃ rajanāḷuṅkaṃ (yaujanā) daṇḍakathālakanti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhūnaṃ rajanabhājanaṃ na saṃvij‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, rajanakaulambaṃ rajanaghaṭanti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū pātiyāpi pattaipi cīvaraṃ aumaddanti| cīvaraṃ paribhij‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, rajanadauṇikanti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū chamāya cīvaraṃ pattharanti| cīvaraṃ paṃsukitaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tiṇasanthārakanti|

tiṇasanthārakau upacikāhi khaj‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cīvaravaṃsaṃ cīvararaj‍junti|

majjhaina laggainti| rajanaṃ ubhatau galati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kaṇṇai bandhitunti|

kaṇṇau  jīrati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kaṇṇasuttakanti|

rajanaṃ aikatau galati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, samparivattakaṃ samparivattakaṃ rajaituṃ, na ca acchin‍nai thaivai pak‍kamitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina cīvaraṃ patthin‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakai ausāraitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina cīvaraṃ pharusaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pāṇinā ākauṭaitunti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū acchin‍nakāni cīvarāni dhārainti dantakāsāvāni| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi nāma saiyyathāpi (?) gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, acchin‍nakāni cīvarāni dhāraitabbāni| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

cīvararajanakathā niṭṭhitā|

216. chin‍nakacīvarānujānanā

345. atha  khau bhagavā rājagahai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina dakkhiṇāgiri taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| addasā khau bhagavā magadhakhaittaṃ acchibaddhaṃ ac‍cibaddhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), acchibandhaṃ (ka॰) pāḷibaddhaṃ mariyādabaddhaṃ siṅghāṭakabaddhaṃ, disvāna āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘passasi nau tvaṃ, ānanda, magadhakhaittaṃ acchibaddhaṃ pāḷibaddhaṃ mariyādabaddhaṃ siṅghāṭakabaddha’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘ussahasi tvaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhūnaṃ aivarūpāni cīvarāni saṃvidahitu’’nti? ‘‘ussahāmi, bhagavā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā dakkhiṇāgirismiṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā punadaiva rājagahaṃ pac‍cāgañchi| atha khau āyasmā ānandau sambahulānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ cīvarāni saṃvidahitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘passatu mai passatha tumhai (ka॰), bhantai, bhagavā cīvarāni saṃvidahitānī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai  dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, ānandau; mahāpañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, ānandau; yatra hi nāma mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānissati, kusimpi nāma karissati, aḍḍhakusimpi nāma karissati, maṇḍalampi nāma karissati, aḍḍhamaṇḍalampi nāma karissati, vivaṭṭampi nāma karissati, anuvivaṭṭampi nāma karissati, gīvaiyyakampi nāma karissati, jaṅghaiyyakampi nāma karissati, bāhantampi nāma karissati, chin‍nakaṃ bhavissati, satthalūkhaṃ samaṇasāruppaṃ pac‍catthikānañ‍ca anabhicchitaṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, chin‍nakaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ chin‍nakaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ chin‍nakaṃ antaravāsaka’’nti|

chin‍nakacīvarānujānanā niṭṭhitā|

217. ticīvarānujānanā

346. atha khau bhagavā rājagahai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina vaisālī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| addasa khau bhagavā antarā ca rājagahaṃ antarā ca vaisāliṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau sambahulai bhikkhū cīvaraihi ubbhaṇḍitai ubbhaṇḍīkatai (syā॰) sīsaipi cīvarabhisiṃ karitvā khandhaipi cīvarabhisiṃ karitvā kaṭiyāpi cīvarabhisiṃ karitvā āgacchantai, disvāna bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘atilahuṃ khau imai maughapurisā cīvarai bāhul‍lāya āvattā| yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ cīvarai sīmaṃ bandhaiyyaṃ, mariyādaṃ ṭhapaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina vaisālī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati gautamakai caitiyai| taina khau pana samayaina  bhagavā sītāsu haimantikāsu rattīsu antaraṭṭhakāsu himapātasamayai rattiṃ ajjhaukāsai aikacīvarau nisīdi| na bhagavantaṃ  sītaṃ ahausi| nikkhantai paṭhamai yāmai sītaṃ bhagavantaṃ ahausi| dutiyaṃ bhagavā cīvaraṃ pārupi| na bhagavantaṃ sītaṃ ahausi| nikkhantai majjhimai yāmai sītaṃ bhagavantaṃ ahausi| tatiyaṃ bhagavā cīvaraṃ pārupi| na bhagavantaṃ sītaṃ ahausi| nikkhantai pacchimai yāmai uddhastai aruṇai nandimukhiyā rattiyā sītaṃ bhagavantaṃ ahausi| catutthaṃ bhagavā cīvaraṃ pārupi| na bhagavantaṃ sītaṃ ahausi| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘yaipi khau tai kulaputtā imasmiṃ dhammavinayai sītālukā sītabhīrukā taipi sak‍kaunti ticīvaraina yāpaituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ cīvarai sīmaṃ bandhaiyyaṃ, mariyādaṃ ṭhapaiyyaṃ, ticīvaraṃ anujānaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, antarā ca rājagahaṃ antarā ca vaisāliṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau addasaṃ sambahulai bhikkhū  cīvaraihi ubbhaṇḍitai sīsaipi cīvarabhisiṃ karitvā khandhaipi cīvarabhisiṃ karitvā kaṭiyāpi cīvarabhisiṃ karitvā āgacchantai, disvāna mai aitadahausi – ‘atilahuṃ khau imai maughapurisā cīvarai bāhul‍lāya āvattā| yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ cīvarai sīmaṃ bandhaiyyaṃ, mariyādaṃ ṭhapaiyya’nti| idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sītāsu haimantikāsu rattīsu antaraṭṭhakāsu himapātasamayai rattiṃ ajjhaukāsai aikacīvarau nisīdiṃ| na maṃ sītaṃ ahausi| nikkhantai paṭhamai yāmai sītaṃ maṃ ahausi| dutiyāhaṃ cīvaraṃ pārupiṃ| na maṃ sītaṃ  ahausi| nikkhantai majjhimai yāmai sītaṃ maṃ ahausi| tatiyāhaṃ cīvaraṃ pārupiṃ| na maṃ sītaṃ ahausi| nikkhantai pacchimai yāmai uddhastai aruṇai nandimukhiyā rattiyā sītaṃ maṃ ahausi| catutthāhaṃ cīvaraṃ pārupiṃ| na maṃ sītaṃ ahausi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘‘yaipi khau tai kulaputtā imasmiṃ dhammavinayai sītālukā sītabhīrukā taipi sak‍kaunti ticīvaraina yāpaituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ cīvarai sīmaṃ bandhaiyyaṃ, mariyādaṃ ṭhapaiyyaṃ, ticīvaraṃ anujānaiyya’nti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ticīvaraṃ – diguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ, aikac‍ciyaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ, aikac‍ciyaṃ antaravāsaka’’nti|

ticīvarānujānanā niṭṭhitā|

218. atiraikacīvarakathā

347. pārā॰ 461 taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhagavatā ticīvaraṃ anuñ‍ñātanti añ‍ñainaiva ticīvaraina gāmaṃ pavisanti, añ‍ñaina ticīvaraina ārāmai acchanti, añ‍ñaina ticīvaraina  nahānaṃ autaranti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū atiraikacīvaraṃ dhāraissantī’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, atiraikacīvaraṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| yau dhāraiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau’’ti|

pārā॰ 461 taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau ānandassa atiraikacīvaraṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| āyasmā ca ānandau taṃ cīvaraṃ āyasmatau sāriputtassa dātukāmau hauti| āyasmā ca  sāriputtau sākaitai viharati| atha khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘na atiraikacīvaraṃ dhāraitabba’nti| idañ‍ca mai atiraikacīvaraṃ uppan‍naṃ| ahañ‍cimaṃ cīvaraṃ āyasmatau sāriputtassa dātukāmau| āyasmā ca sāriputtau sākaitai viharati| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘kīvaciraṃ panānanda, sāriputtau āgacchissatī’’ti? ‘‘navamaṃ vā, bhagavā, divasaṃ, dasamaṃ vā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dasāhaparamaṃ atiraikacīvaraṃ dhāraitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhūnaṃ atiraikacīvaraṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau amhaihi atiraikacīvarai paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, atiraikacīvaraṃ vikappaitunti|

348. atha khau bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina bārāṇasī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina bārāṇasī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati isipatanai migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau antaravāsakau chiddau hauti| atha khau tassa bhikkhunau aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā ticīvaraṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ – diguṇā saṅghāṭi, aikac‍ciyau uttarāsaṅgau, aikac‍ciyau antaravāsakau| ayañ‍ca mai antaravāsakau chiddau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ aggaḷaṃ acchupaiyyaṃ, samantatau dupaṭṭaṃ bhavissati, majjhai aikac‍ciya’’nti| atha khau sau bhikkhu aggaḷaṃ acchupaisi| addasā khau bhagavā saināsanacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantau taṃ bhikkhuṃ aggaḷaṃ acchupaintaṃ acchupantaṃ (ka॰), disvāna yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, karausī’’ti? ‘‘aggaḷaṃ, bhagavā, acchupaimī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu; sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, aggaḷaṃ acchupaisī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ahatānaṃ dussānaṃ ahatakappānaṃ diguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ, aikac‍ciyaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ, aikac‍ciyaṃ antaravāsakaṃ; utuddhaṭānaṃ dussānaṃ catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ, diguṇaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ, diguṇaṃ  antaravāsakaṃ; paṃsukūlai yāvadatthaṃ; pāpaṇikai ussāhau karaṇīyau| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aggaḷaṃ tun‍naṃ auvaṭṭikaṃ kaṇḍusakaṃ daḷhīkamma’’nti|

atiraikacīvarakathā niṭṭhitā|

219. visākhāvatthu

349. atha khau bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina sāvatthi taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina sāvatthi tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau visākhā migāramātā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau visākhaṃ migāramātaraṃ bhagavā  dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau visākhā migāramātā, bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā, bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau visākhā migāramātā bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

taina khau pana samayaina tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina cātuddīpikau mahāmaighau pāvassi| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yathā, bhikkhavai, jaitavanai vassati aivaṃ catūsu dīpaisu vassati| auvassāpaitha, bhikkhavai, kāyaṃ| ayaṃ pacchimakau cātuddīpikau mahāmaighau’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā  nikkhittacīvarā kāyaṃ auvassāpainti| atha khau visākhā migāramātā paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā dāsiṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha, jai| ārāmaṃ gantvā kālaṃ āraucaihi – kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, ayyai’’ti khau sā dāsī visākhāya migāramātuyā paṭissuṇitvā ārāmaṃ gantvā addasa bhikkhū nikkhittacīvarai kāyaṃ auvassāpaintai, disvāna ‘natthi ārāmai bhikkhū, ājīvakā kāyaṃ auvassāpaintī’ti yaina visākhā migāramātā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā visākhaṃ migāramātaraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘natthayyai, ārāmai bhikkhū, ājīvakā kāyaṃ auvassāpaintī’’ti| atha khau visākhāya migāramātuyā  paṇḍitāya viyattāya maidhāviniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau ayyā nikkhittacīvarā kāyaṃ auvassāpainti| sāyaṃ  bālā mañ‍ñittha – natthi ārāmai bhikkhū, ājīvakā kāyaṃ auvassāpaintī’’ti, puna dāsiṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha, jai| ārāmaṃ gantvā kālaṃ āraucaihi – kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau tai bhikkhū gattāni sītiṃ karitvā sītīkaritvā (syā॰) kal‍lakāyā cīvarāni gahaitvā yathāvihāraṃ pavisiṃsu| atha khau sā dāsī ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhū apassantī ‘natthi ārāmai bhikkhū, suñ‍ñau ārāmau’ti yaina visākhā migāramātā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā visākhaṃ migāramātaraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘natthayyai, ārāmai bhikkhū, suñ‍ñau ārāmau’’ti| atha khau visākhāya migāramātuyā paṇḍitāya viyattāya maidhāviniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau ayyā gattāni sītiṃ karitvā kal‍lakāyā cīvarāni gahaitvā yathāvihāraṃ paviṭṭhā| sāyaṃ bālā mañ‍ñittha – natthi ārāmai bhikkhū, suñ‍ñau ārāmau’’ti, puna dāsiṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha, jai| ārāmaṃ gantvā kālaṃ āraucaihi – kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti|

350. atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘sandahatha san‍nahatha (sī॰ syā॰), bhikkhavai, pattacīvaraṃ; kālau bhattassā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau sammiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – jaitavanai antarahitau visākhāya migāramātuyā kauṭṭhakai  pāturahausi| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau visākhā migāramātā – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau! abbhutaṃ vata bhau! tathāgatassa mahiddhikatā mahānubhāvatā, yatra hi nāma jaṇṇukamattaisupi aughaisu pavattamānaisu, kaṭimattaisupi aughaisu pavattamānaisu, na hi nāma  aikabhikkhussapi pavattamānaisu na aikabhikkhussapi (?) pādā vā cīvarāni vā al‍lāni bhavissantī’’ti – haṭṭhā udaggā buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau visākhā migāramātā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aṭṭhāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ varāni yācāmī’’ti| ‘‘atik‍kantavarā khau, visākhai, tathāgatā’’ti| ‘‘yāni ca, bhantai, kappiyāni  yāni ca anavaj‍jānī’’ti| ‘‘vadaihi, visākhai’’ti| ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, saṅghassa yāvajīvaṃ vassikasāṭikaṃ dātuṃ, āgantukabhattaṃ dātuṃ, gamikabhattaṃ dātuṃ, gilānabhattaṃ dātuṃ, gilānupaṭṭhākabhattaṃ dātuṃ, gilānabhaisaj‍jaṃ dātuṃ, dhuvayāguṃ dātuṃ, bhikkhunisaṅghassa  udakasāṭikaṃ dātu’’nti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, visākhai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānā tathāgataṃ aṭṭha varāni yācasī’’ti?

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, dāsiṃ āṇāpaisiṃ – ‘gaccha, jai| ārāmaṃ gantvā kālaṃ āraucaihi – kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’’nti| atha khau sā, bhantai, dāsī ārāmaṃ gantvā addasa bhikkhū nikkhittacīvarai kāyaṃ auvassāpaintai, disvāna  ‘‘natthi ārāmai bhikkhū, ājīvakā kāyaṃ auvassāpaintī’’ti yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘natthayyai, ārāmai bhikkhū, ājīvakā kāyaṃ auvassāpaintī’’ti| asuci, bhantai, naggiyaṃ jaigucchaṃ paṭikūlaṃ| imāhaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānā icchāmi saṅghassa yāvajīvaṃ vassikasāṭikaṃ dātuṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, āgantukau bhikkhu na vīthikusalau na gaucarakusalau kilantau piṇḍāya carati| sau mai āgantukabhattaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā vīthikusalau gaucarakusalau akilantau piṇḍāya carissati| imāhaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānā icchāmi saṅghassa yāvajīvaṃ āgantukabhattaṃ dātuṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, gamikau bhikkhu attanau bhattaṃ pariyaisamānau satthā vā vihāyissati, yattha vā vāsaṃ gantukāmau bhavissati tattha vikālai upagacchissati, kilantau addhānaṃ gamissati| sau mai gamikabhattaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā satthā na vihāyissati, yattha vāsaṃ gantukāmau bhavissati tattha kālai upagacchissati, akilantau addhānaṃ gamissati| imāhaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānā icchāmi saṅghassa yāvajīvaṃ gamikabhattaṃ dātuṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, gilānassa bhikkhunau sappāyāni bhaujanāni alabhantassa ābādhau vā abhivaḍḍhissati, kālaṃkiriyā vā bhavissati| tassa mai gilānabhattaṃ bhuttassa ābādhau  na abhivaḍḍhissati, kālaṃkiriyā na bhavissati| imāhaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānā icchāmi saṅghassa yāvajīvaṃ gilānabhattaṃ dātuṃ| ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhantai, gilānupaṭṭhākau bhikkhu attanau bhattaṃ pariyaisamānau gilānassa ussūrai bhattaṃ nīharissati, bhattacchaidaṃ karissati| sau mai gilānupaṭṭhākabhattaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā gilānassa  kālaina bhattaṃ nīharissati, bhattacchaidaṃ na karissati| imāhaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānā icchāmi saṅghassa yāvajīvaṃ gilānupaṭṭhākabhattaṃ dātuṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, gilānassa bhikkhunau sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni alabhantassa ābādhau vā abhivaḍḍhissati, kālaṃkiriyā vā bhavissati| tassa mai gilānabhaisaj‍jaṃ paribhuttassa ābādhau na abhivaḍḍhissati, kālaṃkiriyā na bhavissati| imāhaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānā icchāmi saṅghassa yāvajīvaṃ gilānabhaisaj‍jaṃ dātuṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā andhakavindai dasānisaṃsai sampassamānaina yāgu anuñ‍ñātā| tyāhaṃ, bhantai, ānisaṃsai sampassamānā icchāmi saṅghassa yāvajīvaṃ dhuvayāguṃ dātuṃ|

‘‘idha, bhantai, bhikkhuniyau aciravatiyā nadiyā vaisiyāhi saddhiṃ naggā aikatitthai nahāyanti| tā, bhantai, vaisiyā bhikkhuniyau uppaṇḍaisuṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khau nāma tumhākaṃ, ayyai, daharānaṃ daharānaṃ daharānaṃ (sī॰) brahmacariyaṃ ciṇṇaina, nanu nāma kāmā paribhuñ‍jitabbā; yadā jiṇṇā bhavissatha tadā brahmacariyaṃ carissatha| aivaṃ tumhākaṃ ubhau atthā pariggahitā bhavissantī’ti| tā, bhantai, bhikkhuniyau vaisiyāhi uppaṇḍiyamānā maṅkū ahaisuṃ| asuci, bhantai, mātugāmassa naggiyaṃ jaigucchaṃ paṭikūlaṃ| imāhaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānā icchāmi bhikkhunisaṅghassa  yāvajīvaṃ udakasāṭikaṃ dātu’’nti|

351. ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, visākhai, ānisaṃsaṃ sampassamānā tathāgataṃ aṭṭha varāni yācasī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhantai, disāsu vassaṃvuṭṭhā bhikkhū sāvatthiṃ āgacchissanti bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| tai bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchissanti – ‘itthan‍nāmau, bhantai, bhikkhu kālaṅkatau, tassa kā gati kau abhisamparāyau’ti? taṃ bhagavā byākarissati sautāpattiphalai vā sakadāgāmiphalai vā anāgāmiphalai vā arahattai vā| tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchissāmi – ‘āgatapubbā nu khau, bhantai, taina ayyaina sāvatthī’ti? sacai mai  vakkhanti – ‘āgatapubbā taina bhikkhunā sāvatthī’ti niṭṭhamaittha  gacchissāmi – nissaṃsayaṃ mai paribhuttaṃ taina ayyaina vassikasāṭikā vā āgantukabhattaṃ vā gamikabhattaṃ vā gilānabhattaṃ vā gilānupaṭṭhākabhattaṃ vā gilānabhaisaj‍jaṃ vā dhuvayāgu vāti| tassā mai tadanussarantiyā pāmuj‍jaṃ jāyissati, pamuditāya pīti jāyissati, pītimanāya kāyau passambhissati, passaddhakāyā sukhaṃ  vaidiyissāmi, sukhiniyā cittaṃ samādhiyissati| sā mai bhavissati indriyabhāvanā balabhāvanā baujjhaṅgabhāvanā| imāhaṃ, bhantai, ānisaṃsaṃ sampassamānā tathāgataṃ aṭṭha varāni yācāmī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, visākhai; sādhu khau tvaṃ, visākhai, imaṃ ānisaṃsaṃ sampassamānā tathāgataṃ aṭṭha varāni yācasi| anujānāmi tai, visākhai, aṭṭha varānī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā visākhaṃ migāramātaraṃ imāhi  gāthāhi anumaudi –

‘‘yā an‍napānaṃ dadatippamauditā|

sīlūpapan‍nā sugatassa sāvikā|

dadāti dānaṃ abhibhuyya maccharaṃ|

sauvaggikaṃ saukanudaṃ sukhāvahaṃ||

‘‘dibbaṃ sā labhatai āyuṃ dibbaṃ balaṃ sā labhatai ca āyuṃ (sī॰ syā॰)|

āgamma maggaṃ virajaṃ anaṅgaṇaṃ|

sā puñ‍ñakāmā sukhinī anāmayā|

saggamhi kāyamhi ciraṃ pamaudatī’’ti||

352. atha khau bhagavā visākhaṃ migāramātaraṃ imāhi gāthāhi anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vassikasāṭikaṃ, āgantukabhattaṃ, gamikabhattaṃ, gilānabhattaṃ, gilānupaṭṭhākabhattaṃ, gilānabhaisaj‍jaṃ, dhuvayāguṃ, bhikkhunisaṅghassa udakasāṭika’’nti|

visākhāvatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

visākhābhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

220. nisīdanādianujānanā

353. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū paṇītāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā muṭṭhassatī asampajānā niddaṃ auk‍kamanti| taisaṃ muṭṭhassatīnaṃ asampajānānaṃ niddaṃ auk‍kamantānaṃ supinantaina asuci muc‍cati, saināsanaṃ asucinā makkhiyati| atha khau bhagavā āyasmatā ānandaina pacchāsamaṇaina saināsanacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantau addasa saināsanaṃ  asucinā makkhitaṃ, disvāna  āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ aitaṃ, ānanda, saināsanaṃ makkhita’’nti? ‘‘aitarahi, bhantai, bhikkhū paṇītāni bhaujanāni  bhuñ‍jitvā muṭṭhassatī asampajānā niddaṃ auk‍kamanti| taisaṃ muṭṭhassatīnaṃ asampajānānaṃ niddaṃ auk‍kamantānaṃ supinantaina asuci muc‍cati; tayidaṃ, bhagavā, saināsanaṃ asucinā makkhita’’nti| ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, ānanda, aivamaitaṃ, ānanda| muc‍cati hi, ānanda, muṭṭhassatīnaṃ asampajānānaṃ niddaṃ auk‍kamantānaṃ supinantaina asuci| yai tai, ānanda, bhikkhū upaṭṭhitassatī sampajānā niddaṃ auk‍kamanti, taisaṃ asuci na muc‍cati| yaipi tai, ānanda, puthuj‍janā kāmaisu vītarāgā, taisampi asuci na muc‍cati| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, ānanda, anavakāsau yaṃ arahatau asuci muc‍caiyyā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ānandaina pacchāsamaṇaina saināsanacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantau addasaṃ saināsanaṃ asucinā makkhitaṃ, disvāna ānandaṃ āmantaisiṃ ‘kiṃ aitaṃ, ānanda, saināsanaṃ makkhita’nti? ‘aitarahi, bhantai, bhikkhū paṇītāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā muṭṭhassatī asampajānā niddaṃ auk‍kamanti| taisaṃ muṭṭhassatīnaṃ asampajānānaṃ niddaṃ auk‍kamantānaṃ supinantaina asuci muc‍cati; tayidaṃ, bhagavā, saināsanaṃ asucinā makkhita’nti| ‘aivamaitaṃ, ānanda, aivamaitaṃ, ānanda, muc‍cati hi, ānanda, muṭṭhassatīnaṃ asampajānānaṃ niddaṃ auk‍kamantānaṃ supinantaina  asuci| yai tai, ānanda, bhikkhū upaṭṭhitassatī sampajānā niddaṃ auk‍kamanti, taisaṃ asuci na muc‍cati| yaipi tai, ānanda, puthuj‍janā kāmaisu vītarāgā taisampi asuci na muc‍cati| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, ānanda, anavakāsau yaṃ arahatau asuci muc‍caiyyā’’’ti|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa niddaṃ auk‍kamatau – dukkhaṃ supati, dukkhaṃ paṭibujjhati, pāpakaṃ supinaṃ passati, daivatā na rakkhanti, asuci muc‍cati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa niddaṃ auk‍kamatau|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā upaṭṭhitassatissa sampajānassa niddaṃ auk‍kamatau – sukhaṃ supati, sukhaṃ paṭibujjhati, na pāpakaṃ supinaṃ passati, daivatā rakkhanti, asuci na muc‍cati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā upaṭṭhitassatissa sampajānassa niddaṃ auk‍kamatau|

‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kāyaguttiyā cīvaraguttiyā saināsanaguttiyā nisīdana’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina atikhuddakaṃ nisīdanaṃ na sabbaṃ saināsanaṃ saṃgaupaiti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yāvamahantaṃ pac‍cattharaṇaṃ ākaṅkhati tāvamahantaṃ pac‍cattharaṇaṃ kātunti|

354. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau ānandassa upajjhāyassa āyasmatau bailaṭṭhasīsassa thul‍lakacchābādhau hauti| tassa lasikāya cīvarāni kāyai lagganti| tāni bhikkhū udakaina taimaitvā taimaitvā apakaḍḍhanti| addasā khau bhagavā saināsanacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantau tai  bhikkhū tāni cīvarāni udakaina taimaitvā taimaitvā apakaḍḍhantai, disvāna yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami, upaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ imassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ābādhau’’ti? ‘‘imassa, bhantai, āyasmatau  thul‍lakacchābādhau| lasikāya cīvarāni kāyai lagganti| tāni mayaṃ udakaina taimaitvā taimaitvā apakaḍḍhāmā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yassa kaṇḍu vā piḷakā vā assāvau vā thul‍lakacchu vā ābādhau kaṇḍuppaṭicchādi’’nti|

355. atha khau visākhā migāramātā mukhapuñchanacauḷaṃ mukhapuñ‍janacauḷaṃ (ka॰) ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau visākhā migāramātā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭiggaṇhātu mai, bhantai, bhagavā mukhapuñchanacauḷaṃ, yaṃ mamassa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| paṭiggahaisi bhagavā mukhapuñchanacauḷaṃ| atha khau bhagavā visākhaṃ migāramātaraṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau visākhā migāramātā bhagavatā dhammiyā  kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mukhapuñchanacauḷaka’’nti mukhapuñchanacaulanti (syā॰)|

356. taina  khau pana samayaina raujau mal‍lau āyasmatau ānandassa sahāyau hauti| raujassa mal‍lassa khaumapilautikā āyasmatau ānandassa hatthai nikkhittā hauti| āyasmatau ca ānandassa khaumapilautikāya atthau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa vissāsaṃ gahaituṃ – sandiṭṭhau ca hauti, sambhattau ca, ālapitau  ca, jīvati ca, jānāti ca, gahitai mai attamanau bhavissatīti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, imaihi pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa vissāsaṃ gahaitunti|

357. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhūnaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ hauti ticīvaraṃ| atthau ca hauti parissāvanaihipi thavikāhipi| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, parikkhāracauḷakanti|

nisīdanādianujānanā niṭṭhitā|

221. pacchimavikappanupagacīvarādikathā

358. atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘yāni tāni bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātāni ticīvaranti vā vassikasāṭikāti vā nisīdananti vā pac‍cattharaṇanti vā kaṇḍuppaṭicchādīti  vā mukhapuñchanacauḷanti vā parikkhāracauḷanti vā, sabbāni tāni adhiṭṭhātabbāni nu khau, udāhu, vikappaitabbānī’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ticīvaraṃ adhiṭṭhātuṃ na vikappaituṃ; vassikasāṭikaṃ vassānaṃ cātumāsaṃ adhiṭṭhātuṃ, tatau paraṃ vikappaituṃ; nisīdanaṃ adhiṭṭhātuṃ na vikappaituṃ; pac‍cattharaṇaṃ adhiṭṭhātuṃ  na vikappaituṃ; kaṇḍuppaṭicchādiṃ yāvaābādhā adhiṭṭhātuṃ tatau paraṃ vikappaituṃ; mukhapuñchanacauḷaṃ adhiṭṭhātuṃ na vikappaituṃ; parikkhāracauḷaṃ adhiṭṭhātuṃ na vikappaitunti|

atha  khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kittakaṃ pacchimaṃ nu khau cīvaraṃ vikappaitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āyāmaina aṭṭhaṅgulaṃ sugataṅgulaina caturaṅgulavitthataṃ pacchimaṃ cīvaraṃ vikappaitunti|

359. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau mahākassapassa paṃsukūlakatau garukau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, suttalūkhaṃ kātunti| vikaṇṇau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vikaṇṇaṃ uddharitunti| suttā aukiriyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, anuvātaṃ paribhaṇḍaṃ āraupaitunti|

taina  khau pana samayaina saṅghāṭiyā pattā luj‍janti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhapadakaṃ kātunti|

360. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau ticīvarai kayiramānai sabbaṃ chin‍nakaṃ nappahauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dvai chin‍nakāni aikaṃ acchin‍nakanti|

dvai chin‍nakāni aikaṃ acchin‍nakaṃ nappahauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dvai acchin‍nakāni aikaṃ chin‍nakanti|

dvai acchin‍nakāni  aikaṃ chin‍nakaṃ nappahauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, anvādhikampi āraupaituṃ, na ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ acchin‍nakaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

361. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau bahuṃ cīvaraṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| sau ca taṃ cīvaraṃ mātāpitūnaṃ dātukāmau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| mātāpitarauti mātāpitūnaṃ khau (sī॰) khau, bhikkhavai, dadamānai vadamānau (ka॰), vadamānai (?) kiṃ vadaiyyāma? anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mātāpitūnaṃ dātuṃ| na ca, bhikkhavai, saddhādaiyyaṃ vinipātaitabbaṃ| yau vinipātaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

362. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu andhavanai cīvaraṃ nikkhipitvā santaruttaraina gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| caurā taṃ cīvaraṃ avahariṃsu| sau bhikkhu duc‍cauḷau hauti lūkhacīvarau| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, āvusau, duc‍cauḷau lūkhacīvarausī’’ti? ‘‘idhāhaṃ sau ahaṃ (katthaci), āvusau, andhavanai cīvaraṃ nikkhipitvā santaruttaraina gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| caurā taṃ cīvaraṃ avahariṃsu| taināhaṃ duc‍cauḷau lūkhacīvarau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, santaruttaraina gāmau pavisitabbau| yau pavisaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ānandau assatiyā santaruttaraina gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘nanu, āvusau ānanda, bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ – ‘na santaruttaraina gāmau pavisitabbau’ti? kissa tvaṃ, āvusau ānanda, santaruttaraina  gāmaṃ paviṭṭhau’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ – ‘na santaruttaraina gāmau pavisitabbau’ti| api cāhaṃ assatiyā paviṭṭhau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, pac‍cayā saṅghāṭiyā nikkhaipāya – gilānau vā hauti, vassikasaṅkaitaṃ vā hauti, nadīpāraṃ gantuṃ vā hauti, aggaḷaguttivihārau vā hauti, atthatakathinaṃ vā hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca pac‍cayā saṅghāṭiyā nikkhaipāya|

pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, pac‍cayā uttarāsaṅgassa nikkhaipāya…pai॰… antaravāsakassa nikkhaipāya – gilānau vā hauti, vassikasaṅkaitaṃ vā hauti, nadīpāraṃ gantuṃ vā hauti, aggaḷaguttivihārau vā hauti, atthatakathinaṃ vā hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca pac‍cayā uttarāsaṅgassa antaravāsakassa nikkhaipāya|

pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, pac‍cayā vassikasāṭikāya nikkhaipāya – gilānau vā hauti, nissīmaṃ gantuṃ vā hauti, nadīpāraṃ gantuṃ vā hauti, aggaḷaguttivihārau vā hauti, vassikasāṭikā akatā vā hauti vippakatā  vā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca pac‍cayā vassikasāṭikāya nikkhaipāyāti|

pacchimavikappanupagacīvarādikathā niṭṭhitā|

222. saṅghikacīvaruppādakathā

363. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu aikau vassaṃ vasi| tattha manussā saṅghassa daimāti cīvarāni adaṃsu| atha khau tassa bhikkhunau aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘catuvaggau pacchimau saṅghau’ti| ahañ‍camhi aikakau| imai ca manussā  saṅghassa daimāti cīvarāni adaṃsu| yaṃnūnāhaṃ imāni saṅghikāni cīvarāni sāvatthiṃ haraiyya’’nti| atha khau sau bhikkhu tāni cīvarāni ādāya sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘tuyhaiva, bhikkhu, tāni cīvarāni yāva kathinassa ubbhārāyā’’ti| idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aikau vassaṃ vasati| tattha manussā saṅghassa daimāti cīvarāni dainti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tassaiva tāni cīvarāni yāva kathinassa ubbhārāyāti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu utukālaṃ aikau vasi| tattha manussā saṅghassa daimāti cīvarāni adaṃsu| atha khau tassa bhikkhunau aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ ‘catuvaggau pacchimau saṅghau’ti| ahañ‍camhi aikakau| imai ca manussā saṅghassa daimāti cīvarāni adaṃsu| yaṃnūnāhaṃ imāni saṅghikāni cīvarāni sāvatthiṃ haraiyya’’nti| atha khau sau bhikkhu tāni cīvarāni ādāya sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sammukhībhūtaina saṅghaina bhājaituṃ| idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu utukālaṃ aikau vasati| tattha manussā saṅghassa daimāti cīvarāni dainti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, taina bhikkhunā tāni cīvarāni adhiṭṭhātuṃ – ‘‘mayhimāni cīvarānī’’ti| tassa cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taṃ cīvaraṃ anadhiṭṭhitai añ‍ñau bhikkhu āgacchati, samakau dātabbau bhāgau| taihi cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi taṃ cīvaraṃ bhājiyamānai, apātitai kusai, añ‍ñau bhikkhu āgacchati, samakau dātabbau bhāgau| taihi cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi taṃ cīvaraṃ bhājiyamānai, pātitai kusai, añ‍ñau bhikkhu āgacchati, nākāmā dātabbau bhāgauti|

taina khau pana samayaina dvai bhātikā thairā, āyasmā ca isidāsau āyasmā ca isibhaṭau, sāvatthiyaṃ vassaṃvuṭṭhā añ‍ñataraṃ gāmakāvāsaṃ agamaṃsu| manussā cirassāpi thairā āgatāti sacīvarāni bhattāni  adaṃsu| āvāsikā bhikkhū thairai pucchiṃsu – ‘‘imāni, bhantai, saṅghikāni cīvarāni thairai āgamma uppan‍nāni, sādiyissanti thairā bhāga’’nti| thairā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yathā khau mayaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāma, tumhākaṃyaiva tāni cīvarāni yāva kathinassa ubbhārāyā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina tayau bhikkhū rājagahai vassaṃ vasanti| tattha manussā saṅghassa

daimāti cīvarāni dainti| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ  ‘catuvaggau pacchimau saṅghau’ti| mayañ‍camhā tayau janā| imai ca manussā saṅghassa daimāti cīvarāni  dainti| kathaṃ nu khau amhaihi paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā thairā, āyasmā ca nilavāsī āyasmā ca sāṇavāsī āyasmā ca gautakau āyasmā ca bhagu āyasmā ca phaḷikasantānau, pāṭaliputtai viharanti kuk‍kuṭārāmai| atha khau tai bhikkhū pāṭaliputtaṃ gantvā thairai pucchiṃsu| thairā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yathā khau mayaṃ āvusau bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāma, tumhākaṃyaiva tāni cīvarāni yāva kathinassa ubbhārāyā’’ti|

saṅghikacīvaruppādakathā niṭṭhitā|

223. upanandasakyaputtavatthu

364. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau sāvatthiyaṃ vassaṃvuṭṭhau añ‍ñataraṃ gāmakāvāsaṃ agamāsi| tattha ca bhikkhū cīvaraṃ bhājaitukāmā san‍nipatiṃsu| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘imāni khau, āvusau, saṅghikāni cīvarāni bhājiyissanti, sādiyissasi bhāga’’nti? ‘‘āmāvusau, sādiyissāmī’’ti| tatau cīvarabhāgaṃ gahaitvā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ agamāsi| tatthapi bhikkhū cīvaraṃ bhājaitukāmā san‍nipatiṃsu| taipi aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘imāni khau, āvusau, saṅghikāni cīvarāni bhājiyissanti, sādiyissasi bhāga’’nti? ‘‘āmāvusau, sādiyissāmī’’ti| tataupi cīvarabhāgaṃ gahaitvā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ agamāsi| tatthapi bhikkhū cīvaraṃ bhājaitukāmā san‍nipatiṃsu| taipi aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘imāni khau, āvusau, saṅghikāni cīvarāni bhājiyissanti, sādiyissasi bhāga’’nti? ‘‘āmāvusau, sādiyissāmī’’ti| tataupi cīvarabhāgaṃ gahaitvā mahantaṃ cīvarabhaṇḍikaṃ ādāya punadaiva sāvatthiṃ pac‍cāgañchi| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘mahāpuñ‍ñausi tvaṃ, āvusau upananda, bahuṃ tai cīvaraṃ uppan‍na’’nti| ‘‘kutau mai, āvusau, puñ‍ñaṃ? idhāhaṃ, āvusau, sāvatthiyaṃ vassaṃvuṭṭhau añ‍ñataraṃ gāmakāvāsaṃ agamāsiṃ| tattha bhikkhū cīvaraṃ bhājaitukāmā san‍nipatiṃsu| tai maṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘imāni khau, āvusau, saṅghikāni cīvarāni bhājiyissanti, sādiyissasi bhāga’nti? ‘āmāvusau, sādiyissāmī’ti| tatau cīvarabhāgaṃ gahaitvā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ agamāsiṃ| tatthapi bhikkhū cīvaraṃ bhājaitukāmā san‍nipatiṃsu| taipi maṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘imāni khau, āvusau, saṅghikāni cīvarāni bhājiyissanti, sādiyissasi bhāga’’’nti? ‘āmāvusau, sādiyissāmī’ti| tataupi cīvarabhāgaṃ gahaitvā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ  agamāsiṃ| tatthapi bhikkhū cīvaraṃ bhājaitukāmā san‍nipatiṃsu| taipi maṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘imāni khau, āvusau, saṅghikāni cīvarāni bhājiyissanti, sādiyissasi bhāga’nti? ‘āmāvusau, sādiyissāmī’ti| tataupi cīvarabhāgaṃ aggahaisiṃ| aivaṃ mai bahuṃ cīvaraṃ uppan‍nanti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, āvusau upananda, añ‍ñatra vassaṃvuṭṭhau añ‍ñatra cīvarabhāgaṃ sādiyī’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau añ‍ñatra vassaṃvuṭṭhau añ‍ñatra cīvarabhāgaṃ sādiyissatī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, añ‍ñatra vassaṃvuṭṭhau añ‍ñatra cīvarabhāgaṃ sādiyī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, añ‍ñatra vassaṃvuṭṭhau añ‍ñatra  cīvarabhāgaṃ sādiyissasi| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ  kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatra vassaṃvuṭṭhaina añ‍ñatra cīvarabhāgau sāditabbau| yau sādiyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau aikau dvīsu āvāsaisu vassaṃ vasi – ‘‘aivaṃ mai bahuṃ cīvaraṃ uppaj‍jissatī’’ti| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau āyasmatau upanandassa sakyaputtassa cīvarapaṭivīsau dātabbau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| daitha, bhikkhavai, maughapurisassa aikādhippāyaṃ| idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aikau dvīsu āvāsaisu vassaṃ vasati – ‘‘aivaṃ mai bahuṃ cīvaraṃ uppaj‍jissatī’’ti| sacai amutra upaḍḍhaṃ amutra upaḍḍhaṃ vasati, amutra upaḍḍhau amutra upaḍḍhau cīvarapaṭivīsau dātabbau| yattha vā pana bahutaraṃ vasati, tatau cīvarapaṭivīsau dātabbauti|

upanandasakyaputtavatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

224. gilānavatthukathā

365. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau kucchivikārābādhau hauti| sau sakai muttakarīsai palipan‍nau saiti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmatā ānandaina pacchāsamaṇaina saināsanacārikaṃ āhiṇḍantau yaina tassa bhikkhunau vihārau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ sakai muttakarīsai palipan‍naṃ sayamānaṃ, disvāna yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ tai, bhikkhu, ābādhau’’ti? ‘‘kucchivikārau mai, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘atthi  pana tai, bhikkhu, upaṭṭhākau’’ti? ‘‘natthi, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘kissa taṃ bhikkhū na upaṭṭhaintī’’ti? ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, bhikkhūnaṃ akārakau; taina maṃ bhikkhū na upaṭṭhaintī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘gacchānanda, udakaṃ āhara, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ nahāpaissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā udakaṃ āhari| bhagavā udakaṃ āsiñ‍ci| āyasmā ānandau paridhauvi| bhagavā sīsatau aggahaisi| āyasmā ānandau pādatau uc‍cāraitvā mañ‍cakai nipātaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, amukasmiṃ vihārai bhikkhu gilānau’’ti? ‘‘atthi, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ābādhau’’ti? ‘‘tassa, bhantai, āyasmatau kucchivikārābādhau’’ti| ‘‘atthi pana, bhikkhavai, tassa bhikkhunau upaṭṭhākau’’ti? ‘‘natthi, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘kissa taṃ bhikkhū na upaṭṭhaintī’’ti? ‘‘aisau, bhantai, bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ akārakau; taina taṃ bhikkhū na upaṭṭhaintī’’ti| ‘‘natthi vau, bhikkhavai, mātā, natthi pitā, yai vau upaṭṭhahaiyyuṃ| tumhai cai, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ na upaṭṭhahissatha, atha kau carahi upaṭṭhahissati? yau, bhikkhavai, maṃ upaṭṭhahaiyya sau gilānaṃ upaṭṭhahaiyya| sacai upajjhāyau hauti, upajjhāyaina yāvajīvaṃ upaṭṭhātabbau; vuṭṭhānamassa āgamaitabbaṃ| sacai ācariyau hauti, ācariyaina yāvajīvaṃ upaṭṭhātabbau; vuṭṭhānamassa āgamaitabbaṃ| sacai saddhivihārikau hauti, saddhivihārikaina yāvajīvaṃ upaṭṭhātabbau; vuṭṭhānamassa āgamaitabbaṃ| sacai antaivāsikau hauti, antaivāsikaina yāvajīvaṃ upaṭṭhātabbau; vuṭṭhānamassa āgamaitabbaṃ| sacai samānupajjhāyakau hauti, samānupajjhāyakaina yāvajīvaṃ upaṭṭhātabbau; vuṭṭhānamassa āgamaitabbaṃ| sacai samānācariyakau hauti, samānācariyakaina yāvajīvaṃ upaṭṭhātabbau; vuṭṭhānamassa āgamaitabbaṃ| sacai na hauti upajjhāyau vā ācariyau vā saddhivihārikau vā antaivāsikau vā samānupajjhāyakau vā samānācariyakau vā saṅghaina upaṭṭhātabbau| nau cai upaṭṭhahaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa’’|

366. pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānau dūpaṭṭhau hauti – asappāyakārī hauti, sappāyai mattaṃ na jānāti, bhaisaj‍jaṃ na paṭisaivitā hauti, atthakāmassa gilānupaṭṭhākassa yathābhūtaṃ ābādhaṃ nāvikattā hauti ‘abhik‍kamantaṃ vā abhik‍kamatīti, paṭik‍kamantaṃ vā paṭik‍kamatīti, ṭhitaṃ vā ṭhitau’ti, uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ anadhivāsakajātikau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānau dūpaṭṭhau hauti|

pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānau sūpaṭṭhau hauti – sappāyakārī hauti, sappāyai mattaṃ jānāti, bhaisaj‍jaṃ paṭisaivitā hauti, atthakāmassa gilānupaṭṭhākassa yathābhūtaṃ ābādhaṃ āvikattā hauti ‘abhik‍kamantaṃ vā abhik‍kamatīti, paṭik‍kamantaṃ vā paṭik‍kamatīti, ṭhitaṃ  vā ṭhitau’ti, uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātikau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānau sūpaṭṭhau hauti|

pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānupaṭṭhākau nālaṃ gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ – na paṭibalau hauti bhaisaj‍jaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ, sappāyāsappāyaṃ na jānāti, asappāyaṃ upanāmaiti sappāyaṃ apanāmaiti, āmisantarau gilānaṃ upaṭṭhāti nau maittacittau, jaigucchī hauti uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ  vā khaiḷaṃ vā vantaṃ vā nīhātuṃ, na paṭibalau hauti gilānaṃ kālaina kālaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaituṃ samādapaituṃ samuttaijaituṃ sampahaṃsaituṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānupaṭṭhākau nālaṃ gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ|

pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānupaṭṭhākau alaṃ gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ – paṭibalau hauti bhaisaj‍jaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ, sappāyāsappāyaṃ jānāti, asappāyaṃ apanāmaiti sappāyaṃ upanāmaiti, maittacittau gilānaṃ upaṭṭhāti nau āmisantarau, ajaigucchī hauti uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā vantaṃ vā nīhātuṃ, paṭibalau hauti gilānaṃ kālaina kālaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaituṃ samādapaituṃ samuttaijaituṃ sampahaṃsaituṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau gilānupaṭṭhākau alaṃ gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātunti|

gilānavatthukathā niṭṭhitā|

225. matasantakakathā

367. taina khau pana samayaina dvai bhikkhū kausalaisu janapadai addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| tai añ‍ñataraṃ āvāsaṃ upagacchiṃsu| tattha  añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā khau, āvusau, gilānupaṭṭhānaṃ vaṇṇitaṃ| handa, mayaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ upaṭṭhahaimā’’ti| tai taṃ upaṭṭhahiṃsu| sau taihi upaṭṭhahiyamānau kālamakāsi| atha khau tai bhikkhū tassa bhikkhunau pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘bhikkhussa, bhikkhavai, kālaṅkatai saṅghau sāmī pattacīvarai, apica gilānupaṭṭhākā bahūpakārā| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṅghaina ticīvarañ‍ca  pattañ‍ca gilānupaṭṭhākānaṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbaṃ| taina gilānupaṭṭhākaina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘itthan‍nāmau, bhantai, bhikkhu kālaṅkatau| idaṃ tassa ticīvarañ‍ca pattau cā’’’ti| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu kālaṅkatau| idaṃ tassa ticīvarañ‍ca pattau ca| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau imaṃ ticīvarañ‍ca pattañ‍ca gilānupaṭṭhākānaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu kālaṅkatau| idaṃ tassa ticīvarañ‍ca pattau ca| saṅghau imaṃ ticīvarañ‍ca pattañ‍ca gilānupaṭṭhākānaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati imassa ticīvarassa ca pattassa ca gilānupaṭṭhākānaṃ dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍naṃ  idaṃ saṅghaina ticīvarañ‍ca pattau ca gilānupaṭṭhākānaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

368. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau sāmaṇairau kālaṅkatau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| sāmaṇairassa, bhikkhavai, kālaṅkatai saṅghau sāmī pattacīvarai, api ca gilānupaṭṭhākā bahūpakārā| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṅghaina cīvarañ‍ca pattañ‍ca gilānupaṭṭhākānaṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbaṃ| taina gilānupaṭṭhākaina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘itthan‍nāmau, bhantai, sāmaṇairau kālaṅkatau, idaṃ tassa cīvarañ‍ca pattau cā’’ti| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau sāmaṇairau kālaṅkatau| idaṃ tassa cīvarañ‍ca pattau ca| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau imaṃ cīvarañ‍ca pattañ‍ca gilānupaṭṭhākānaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau sāmaṇairau kālaṅkatau| idaṃ tassa cīvarañ‍ca pattau ca| saṅghau imaṃ cīvarañ‍ca pattañ‍ca gilānupaṭṭhākānaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati imassa cīvarassa ca pattassa ca gilānupaṭṭhākānaṃ dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍naṃ idaṃ saṅghaina cīvarañ‍ca pattau ca gilānupaṭṭhākānaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

369. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu ca sāmaṇairau ca gilānaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃsu| sau taihi upaṭṭhahiyamānau kālamakāsi| atha khau tassa gilānupaṭṭhākassa bhikkhunau aitadahausi  – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau gilānupaṭṭhākassa  sāmaṇairassa cīvarapaṭivīsau dātabbau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānupaṭṭhākassa sāmaṇairassa samakaṃ paṭivīsaṃ dātunti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bahubhaṇḍau bahuparikkhārau kālaṅkatau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhussa, bhikkhavai, kālaṅkatai saṅghau sāmī pattacīvarai, api ca gilānupaṭṭhākā bahūpakārā| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṅghaina ticīvarañ‍ca pattañ‍ca  gilānupaṭṭhākānaṃ dātuṃ| yaṃ tattha lahubhaṇḍaṃ lahuparikkhāraṃ taṃ sammukhībhūtaina saṅghaina bhājaituṃ| yaṃ tattha garubhaṇḍaṃ garuparikkhāraṃ taṃ āgatānāgatassa cātuddisassa saṅghassa avissaj‍jikaṃ avaibhaṅgikanti|

matasantakakathā niṭṭhitā|

226. naggiyapaṭikkhaipakathā

370. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu naggau hutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhagavā, bhantai, anaikapariyāyaina appicchassa santuṭṭhassa sal‍laikhassa dhutassa pāsādikassa apacayassa vīriyārambhassa vaṇṇavādī| idaṃ, bhantai, naggiyaṃ anaikapariyāyaina appicchatāya santuṭṭhitāya sal‍laikhāya dhutatāya dhutattāya (ka॰) pāsādikatāya apacayāya vīriyārambhāya saṃvattati| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ naggiyaṃ anujānātū’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, maughapurisa, ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, naggiyaṃ titthiyasamādānaṃ samādiyissasi| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰…’’ vigarahitvā dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, naggiyaṃ  titthiyasamādānaṃ samādiyitabbaṃ| yau samādiyaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

naggiyapaṭikkhaipakathā niṭṭhitā|

227. kusacīrādipaṭikkhaipakathā

371. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kusacīraṃ nivāsaitvā…pai॰… vākacīraṃ  nivāsaitvā…pai॰… phalakacīraṃ nivāsaitvā…pai॰… kaisakambalaṃ nivāsaitvā…pai॰… vāḷakambalaṃ nivāsaitvā…pai॰… ulūkapakkhaṃ nivāsaitvā…pai॰… ajinakkhipaṃ nivāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhagavā, bhantai, anaikapariyāyaina appicchassa santuṭṭhassa sal‍laikhassa dhutassa pāsādikassa apacayassa vīriyārambhassa vaṇṇavādī| idaṃ, bhantai, ajinakkhipaṃ anaikapariyāyaina appicchatāya santuṭṭhitāya sal‍laikhāya dhutatāya pāsādikatāya apacayāya vīriyārambhāya saṃvattati| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ ajinakkhipaṃ anujānātū’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, maughapurisa, ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ  akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, ajinakkhipaṃ titthiyadhajaṃ dhāraissasi| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, ajinakkhipaṃ titthiyadhajaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu ak‍kanāḷaṃ nivāsaitvā…pai॰… pautthakaṃ nivāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhagavā, bhantai, anaikapariyāyaina appicchassa santuṭṭhassa sal‍laikhassa dhutassa pāsādikassa apacayassa vīriyārambhassa, vaṇṇavādī| ayaṃ, bhantai, pautthakau anaikapariyāyaina appicchatāya  santuṭṭhitāya sal‍laikhāya dhutatāya pāsādikatāya apacayāya vīriyārambhāya saṃvattati| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pautthakaṃ anujānātū’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, maughapurisa, ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, pautthakaṃ nivāsaissasi| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pautthakau nivāsaitabbau| yau nivāsaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

kusacīrādipaṭikkhaipakathā niṭṭhitā|

228. sabbanīlakādipaṭikkhaipakathā

372. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sabbanīlakāni cīvarāni dhārainti…pai॰… sabbapītakāni cīvarāni dhārainti…pai॰… sabbalauhitakāni cīvarāni dhārainti…pai॰… sabbamañ‍jiṭṭhakāni sabbamañ‍jaiṭṭhakāni (sī॰ syā॰) cīvarāni dhārainti…pai॰… sabbakaṇhāni cīvarāni dhārainti  …pai॰… sabbamahāraṅgarattāni cīvarāni dhārainti…pai॰… sabbamahānāmarattāni cīvarāni dhārainti…pai॰… acchin‍nadasāni cīvarāni dhārainti…pai॰… dīghadasāni cīvarāni dhārainti…pai॰… pupphadasāni cīvarāni dhārainti…pai॰… phaṇadasāni phaladasāni (ka॰) cīvarāni dhārainti…pai॰… kañ‍cukaṃ dhārainti…pai॰… tirīṭakaṃ dhārainti…pai॰… vaiṭhanaṃ dhārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā vaiṭhanaṃ dhāraissanti, saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| na, bhikkhavai, sabbanīlakāni cīvarāni dhāraitabbāni, na sabbapītakāni cīvarāni dhāraitabbāni, na sabbalauhitakāni cīvarāni dhāraitabbāni, na sabbamañ‍jiṭṭhakāni cīvarāni dhāraitabbāni, na sabbakaṇhāni cīvarāni dhāraitabbāni, na sabbamahāraṅgarattāni cīvarāni dhāraitabbāni, na  sabbamahānāmarattāni cīvarāni dhāraitabbāni, na acchin‍nadasāni cīvarāni dhāraitabbāni, na dīghadasāni cīvarāni dhāraitabbāni, na pupphadasāni cīvarāni dhāraitabbāni, na phaṇadasāni cīvarāni dhāraitabbāni, na kañ‍cukaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ, na tirīṭakaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ, na vaiṭhanaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti|

sabbanīlakādipaṭikkhaipakathā niṭṭhitā|

229. vassaṃvuṭṭhānaṃ anuppan‍nacīvarakathā

373. taina khau pana samayaina vassaṃvuṭṭhā bhikkhū anuppan‍nai cīvarai  pak‍kamantipi, vibbhamantipi, kālampi karaunti, sāmaṇairāpi paṭijānanti, sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakāpi paṭijānanti, antimavatthuṃ ajjhāpan‍nakāpi paṭijānanti, ummattakāpi paṭijānanti, khittacittāpi paṭijānanti, vaidanāṭṭāpi paṭijānanti, āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakāpi paṭijānanti, āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhittakāpi paṭijānanti, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhittakāpi paṭijānanti, paṇḍakāpi paṭijānanti, thaiyyasaṃvāsakāpi paṭijānanti, titthiyapak‍kantakāpi paṭijānanti, tiracchānagatāpi paṭijānanti, mātughātakāpi paṭijānanti, pitughātakāpi paṭijānanti, arahantaghātakāpi paṭijānanti, bhikkhunidūsakāpi paṭijānanti, saṅghabhaidakāpi paṭijānanti, lauhituppādakāpi paṭijānanti, ubhataubyañ‍janakāpi paṭijānanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

374. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, vassaṃvuṭṭhau bhikkhu anuppan‍nai cīvarai pak‍kamati, santai patirūpai gāhakai dātabbaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, vassaṃvuṭṭhau bhikkhu anuppan‍nai cīvarai vibbhamati, kālaṃ karauti, sāmaṇairau paṭijānāti, sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakau paṭijānāti, antimavatthuṃ ajjhāpan‍nakau paṭijānāti, saṅghau sāmī|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, vassaṃvuṭṭhau bhikkhu anuppan‍nai cīvarai ummattakau paṭijānāti, khittacittau paṭijānāti, vaidanāṭṭau paṭijānāti, āpattiyā  adassanai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti, āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti, santai patirūpai gāhakai dātabbaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, vassaṃvuṭṭhau bhikkhu anuppan‍nai cīvarai paṇḍakau paṭijānāti, thaiyyasaṃvāsakau paṭijānāti, titthiyapak‍kantakau paṭijānāti, tiracchānagatau paṭijānāti, mātughātakau paṭijānāti, pitughātakau paṭijānāti, arahantaghātakau paṭijānāti, bhikkhunidūsakau paṭijānāti, saṅghabhaidakau paṭijānāti, lauhituppādakau paṭijānāti, ubhataubyañ‍janakau paṭijānāti, saṅghau sāmī|

375. idha pana, bhikkhavai, vassaṃvuṭṭhau bhikkhu uppan‍nai cīvarai abhājitai pak‍kamati, santai patirūpai gāhakai dātabbaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, vassaṃvuṭṭhau bhikkhu uppan‍nai cīvarai abhājitai vibbhamati, kālaṃ karauti, sāmaṇairau paṭijānāti, sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakau paṭijānāti, antimavatthuṃ ajjhāpan‍nakau paṭijānāti, saṅghau sāmī|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, vassaṃvuṭṭhau bhikkhu uppan‍nai cīvarai abhājitai ummattakau paṭijānāti| khittacittau paṭijānāti, vaidanāṭṭau paṭijānāti, āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti, āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti, santai patirūpai gāhakai dātabbaṃ|

idha  pana, bhikkhavai, vassaṃvuṭṭhau bhikkhu uppan‍nai cīvarai abhājitai paṇḍakau paṭijānāti, thaiyyasaṃvāsakau paṭijānāti, titthiyapak‍kantakau paṭijānāti, tiracchānagatau paṭijānāti, mātughātakau paṭijānāti, pitughātakau paṭijānāti, arahantaghātakau paṭijānāti, bhikkhunidūsakau paṭijānāti, saṅghabhaidakau paṭijānāti, lauhituppādakau paṭijānāti, ubhataubyañ‍janakau paṭijānāti, saṅghau sāmī|

vassaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ anuppan‍nacīvarakathā niṭṭhitā|

230. saṅghai bhin‍nai cīvaruppādakathā

376. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, vassaṃvuṭṭhānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ anuppan‍nai cīvarai saṅghau bhij‍jati| tattha manussā aikasmiṃ pakkhai udakaṃ dainti, aikasmiṃ pakkhai cīvaraṃ dainti – saṅghassa daimāti| saṅghassaivaitaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, vassaṃvuṭṭhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anuppan‍nai cīvarai saṅghau bhij‍jati| tattha manussā aikasmiṃ pakkhai udakaṃ dainti, tasmiṃyaiva pakkhai cīvaraṃ dainti – saṅghassa  daimāti| saṅghassaivaitaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, vassaṃvuṭṭhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anuppan‍nai cīvarai saṅghau bhij‍jati| tattha manussā aikasmiṃ pakkhai udakaṃ dainti, aikasmiṃ pakkhai cīvaraṃ dainti – pakkhassa daimāti| pakkhassaivaitaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, vassaṃvuṭṭhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anuppan‍nai cīvarai saṅghau bhij‍jati| tattha manussā aikasmiṃ pakkhai udakaṃ dainti, tasmiṃyaiva pakkhai cīvaraṃ dainti – pakkhassa daimāti| pakkhassaivaitaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, vassaṃvuṭṭhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ uppan‍nai cīvarai abhājitai saṅghau bhij‍jati| sabbaisaṃ samakaṃ bhājaitabbanti|

saṅghai bhin‍nai cīvaruppādakathā niṭṭhitā|

231. duggahitasuggahitādikathā

377. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā raivatau añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau hatthai āyasmatau sāriputtassa cīvaraṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ thairassa daihī’’ti| atha khau sau bhikkhu antarāmaggai āyasmatau raivatassa vissāsā taṃ cīvaraṃ aggahaisi| atha khau āyasmā raivatau āyasmatā sāriputtaina samāgantvā pucchi – ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, thairassa cīvaraṃ pāhaisiṃ| sampattaṃ taṃ cīvara’’nti? ‘‘nāhaṃ taṃ, āvusau, cīvaraṃ passāmī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā raivatau taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, āyasmatau hatthai thairassa cīvaraṃ pāhaisiṃ| kahaṃ taṃ cīvara’’nti? ‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, āyasmatau vissāsā taṃ cīvaraṃ aggahaisi’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

378. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhikkhussa hatthai cīvaraṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ itthan‍nāmassa daihī’’ti| sau antarāmaggai yau pahiṇati tassa vissāsā gaṇhāti| suggahitaṃ| yassa pahiyyati tassa vissāsā gaṇhāti| duggahitaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhikkhussa hatthai cīvaraṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ itthan‍nāmassa daihī’’ti| sau antarāmaggai yassa pahiyyati tassa vissāsā gaṇhāti| duggahitaṃ| yau pahiṇati tassa vissāsā gaṇhāti| suggahitaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhikkhussa hatthai cīvaraṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ itthan‍nāmassa daihī’’ti| sau antarāmaggai suṇāti – yau pahiṇati sau kālaṅkatauti| tassa matakacīvaraṃ adhiṭṭhāti| svādhiṭṭhitaṃ| yassa pahiyyati tassa vissāsā gaṇhāti| duggahitaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhikkhussa hatthai cīvaraṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ itthan‍nāmassa daihī’’ti| sau antarāmaggai suṇāti – yassa pahiyyati sau kālaṅkatauti| tassa matakacīvaraṃ adhiṭṭhāti| dvādhiṭṭhitaṃ| yau pahiṇati tassa vissāsā gaṇhāti| suggahitaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhikkhussa hatthai cīvaraṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ itthan‍nāmassa daihī’’ti| sau  antarāmaggai suṇāti – ubhau kālaṅkatāti| yau pahiṇati tassa matakacīvaraṃ  adhiṭṭhāti| svādhiṭṭhitaṃ| yassa pahiyyati  tassa matakacīvaraṃ adhiṭṭhāti| dvādhiṭṭhitaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhikkhussa hatthai cīvaraṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ itthan‍nāmassa dammī’’ti| sau antarāmaggai yau pahiṇati tassa vissāsā gaṇhāti| duggahitaṃ| yassa pahiyyati tassa vissāsā gaṇhāti| suggahitaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhikkhussa hatthai cīvaraṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ itthan‍nāmassa dammī’’ti| sau antarāmaggai yassa pahiyyati tassa vissāsā gaṇhāti| suggahitaṃ| yau pahiṇati tassa vissāsā gaṇhāti| duggahitaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhikkhussa hatthai cīvaraṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ itthan‍nāmassa dammī’’ti| sau antarāmaggai suṇāti – ‘‘yau pahiṇati sau  kālaṅkatau’’ti| tassa matakacīvaraṃ adhiṭṭhāti| dvādhiṭṭhitaṃ| yassa pahiyyati tassa vissāsā gaṇhāti| suggahitaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhikkhussa hatthai cīvaraṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ itthan‍nāmassa dammī’’ti| sau antarāmaggai suṇāti – ‘‘yassa pahiyyati sau kālaṅkatau’’ti| tassa matakacīvaraṃ adhiṭṭhāti| svādhiṭṭhitaṃ| yau pahiṇati tassa vissāsā gaṇhāti| duggahitaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhikkhussa hatthai cīvaraṃ pahiṇati – ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ itthan‍nāmassa dammī’’ti| sau antarāmaggai suṇāti ‘‘ubhau kālaṅkatā’’ti| yau pahiṇati tassa matakacīvaraṃ adhiṭṭhāti| dvādhiṭṭhitaṃ| yassa pahiyyati tassa matakacīvaraṃ adhiṭṭhāti| svādhiṭṭhitaṃ|

duggahitasuggahitādikathā niṭṭhitā|

232. aṭṭhacīvaramātikā

379. aṭṭhimā, bhikkhavai, mātikā cīvarassa uppādāya – sīmāya daiti, katikāya daiti, bhikkhāpañ‍ñattiyā daiti, saṅghassa daiti, ubhatausaṅghassa daiti, vassaṃvuṭṭhasaṅghassa daiti, ādissa daiti, puggalassa daiti|

sīmāya daiti – yāvatikā bhikkhū antausīmagatā taihi bhājaitabbaṃ| katikāya daiti – sambahulā āvāsā samānalābhā haunti aikasmiṃ āvāsai din‍nai sabbattha din‍naṃ hauti| bhikkhāpañ‍ñattiyā daiti, yattha saṅghassa dhuvakārā kariyyanti, tattha daiti| saṅghassa daiti, sammukhībhūtaina saṅghaina bhājaitabbaṃ| ubhatausaṅghassa daiti, bahukāpi bhikkhū haunti, aikā bhikkhunī hauti, upaḍḍhaṃ dātabbaṃ, bahukāpi bhikkhuniyau haunti, aikau bhikkhu hauti, upaḍḍhaṃ dātabbaṃ| vassaṃvuṭṭhasaṅghassa daiti, yāvatikā bhikkhū tasmiṃ āvāsai vassaṃvuṭṭhā, taihi bhājaitabbaṃ| ādissa daiti, yāguyā vā bhattai vā khādanīyai vā cīvarai vā saināsanai vā bhaisaj‍jai vā| puggalassa daiti, ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ itthan‍nāmassa dammī’’ti|

aṭṭhacīvaramātikā niṭṭhitā|

cīvarakkhandhakau aṭṭhamau|

233. tassuddānaṃ

rājagahakau  naigamau, disvā vaisāliyaṃ gaṇiṃ|

puna rājagahaṃ gantvā, rañ‍ñau taṃ paṭivaidayi||

puttau  sālavatikāya, abhayassa hi atrajau|

jīvatīti kumāraina, saṅkhātau jīvakau iti||

sau hi tak‍kasīlaṃ gantvā, uggahaitvā mahābhisau|

sattavassikaābādhaṃ, natthukammaina nāsayi||

rañ‍ñau  bhagandalābādhaṃ, ālaipaina apākaḍḍhi|

mamañ‍ca itthāgārañ‍ca, buddhasaṅghaṃ cupaṭṭhahi||

rājagahakau ca saiṭṭhi, antagaṇṭhi tikicchitaṃ|

paj‍jautassa mahāraugaṃ, ghatapānaina nāsayi||

adhikārañ‍ca sivaiyyaṃ, abhisan‍naṃ sinaihati|

tīhi uppalahatthaihi, samattiṃsaviraicanaṃ||

pakatattaṃ varaṃ yāci, sivaiyyañ‍ca paṭiggahi|

cīvarañ‍ca gihidānaṃ, anuñ‍ñāsi tathāgatau||

rājagahai janapadai bahuṃ, uppaj‍ji cīvaraṃ|

pāvārau kausiyañ‍caiva, kaujavau aḍḍhakāsikaṃ||

uc‍cāvacā ca santuṭṭhi, nāgamaisāgamaisuṃ ca|

paṭhamaṃ pacchā sadisā, katikā ca paṭiharuṃ||

bhaṇḍāgāraṃ aguttañ‍ca, vuṭṭhāpainti tathaiva ca|

ussan‍naṃ kaulāhalañ‍ca, kathaṃ bhājai kathaṃ dadai||

sakātiraikabhāgaina, paṭivīsau kathaṃ dadai|

chakaṇaina  sītudakā sītundī ca (sī॰), sītuṇhi ca (katthaci), uttaritu na jānarai||

āraupaintā bhājanañ‍ca, pātiyā ca chamāya ca|

upacikāmajjhai jīranti, aikatau patthin‍naina ca||

pharusācchin‍nacchibandhā, addasāsi ubbhaṇḍitai|

vīmaṃsitvā sakyamuni, anuñ‍ñāsi ticīvaraṃ||

añ‍ñaina  atiraikaina, uppaj‍ji chiddamaiva ca|

cātuddīpau varaṃ yāci, dātuṃ vassikasāṭikaṃ||

āgantugamigilānaṃ, upaṭṭhākañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jaṃ|

dhuvaṃ udakasāṭiñ‍ca, paṇītaṃ atikhuddakaṃ||

thul‍lakacchumukhaṃ khaumaṃ, paripuṇṇaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ|

pacchimaṃ katau garukau, vikaṇṇau suttamaukiri||

luj‍janti  nappahaunti, ca anvādhikaṃ bahūni ca|

andhavanai assatiyā, aikau vassaṃ utumhi ca||

dvai bhātukā rājagahai, upanandau puna dvisu|

kucchivikārau gilānau, ubhau caiva gilānakā gilāyanā (ka॰)||

naggā kusā vākacīraṃ, phalakau kaisakambalaṃ|

vāḷaulūkapakkhañ‍ca, ajinaṃ ak‍kanāḷakaṃ||

pautthakaṃ nīlapītañ‍ca, lauhitaṃ mañ‍jiṭṭhaina ca|

kaṇhā mahāraṅganāma, acchin‍nadasikā tathā||

dīghapupphaphaṇadasā, kañ‍cutirīṭavaiṭhanaṃ|

anuppan‍nai pak‍kamati, saṅghau bhij‍jati tāvadai||

pakkhai dadanti saṅghassa, āyasmā raivatau pahi|

vissāsagāhādhiṭṭhāti, aṭṭha cīvaramātikāti||

imamhi khandhakai vatthū chan‍navuti|

cīvarakkhandhakau niṭṭhitau|



9. campaiyyakkhandhakau

234. kassapagauttabhikkhuvatthu

380. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā campāyaṃ viharati gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai| taina khau pana samayaina kāsīsu janapadai vāsabhagāmau nāma hauti| tattha kassapagauttau nāma bhikkhu āvāsikau hauti tantibaddhau ussuk‍kaṃ āpan‍nau – kinti anāgatā ca paisalā bhikkhū āgacchaiyyuṃ, āgatā ca paisalā bhikkhū phāsu viharaiyyuṃ, ayañ‍ca āvāsau vuddhiṃ viruḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū kāsīsu cārikaṃ caramānā yaina vāsabhagāmau tadavasaruṃ| addasā khau kassapagauttau bhikkhu tai bhikkhū dūratauva āgacchantai, disvāna āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ pādakathalikaṃ upanikkhipi, pac‍cuggantvā pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaisi, pānīyaina āpucchi, nahānai ussuk‍kaṃ akāsi, ussuk‍kampi akāsi yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ| atha khau taisaṃ āgantukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘bhaddakau khau ayaṃ, āvusau, āvāsikau bhikkhu nahānai ussuk‍kaṃ karauti, ussuk‍kampi karauti yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ| handa, mayaṃ, āvusau, idhaiva vāsabhagāmai nivāsaṃ kappaimā’’ti| atha khau tai āgantukā bhikkhū tatthaiva vāsabhagāmai nivāsaṃ kappaisuṃ|

atha khau kassapagauttassa bhikkhunau aitadahausi – ‘‘yau khau imaisaṃ  āgantukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ āgantukakilamathau sau paṭippassaddhau| yaipimai gaucarai appakatañ‍ñunau taidānimai gaucarai pakatañ‍ñunau| duk‍karaṃ khau pana parakulaisu yāvajīvaṃ ussuk‍kaṃ kātuṃ, viñ‍ñatti ca manussānaṃ amanāpā| yaṃnūnāhaṃ na ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyyaṃ yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmi’’nti| sau na ussuk‍kaṃ akāsi yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ| atha khau taisaṃ āgantukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ  aitadahausi – ‘‘pubbai khvāyaṃ, āvusau, āvāsikau bhikkhu nahānai ussuk‍kaṃ akāsi, ussuk‍kampi akāsi yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ| saudānāyaṃ na ussuk‍kaṃ karauti yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ| duṭṭhaudānāyaṃ, āvusau, āvāsikau bhikkhu| handa, mayaṃ, āvusau, āvāsikaṃ  imaṃ āvāsikaṃ (syā॰) bhikkhuṃ ukkhipāmā’’ti| atha khau tai  āgantukā bhikkhū san‍nipatitvā kassapagauttaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pubbai khau tvaṃ, āvusau, nahānai ussuk‍kaṃ karausi, ussuk‍kampi karausi yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ| saudāni tvaṃ na ussuk‍kaṃ karausi yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ| āpattiṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, āpan‍nau| passasaitaṃ āpatti’’nti? ‘‘natthi mai, āvusau, āpatti, yamahaṃ passaiyya’’nti| atha khau tai āgantukā bhikkhū kassapagauttaṃ bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā adassanai ukkhipiṃsu|

atha khau kassapagauttassa bhikkhunau aitadahausi – ‘‘ahaṃ khau aitaṃ na jānāmi ‘āpatti vā aisā anāpatti vā, āpan‍nau camhi vamhi (?) anāpan‍nau vā, ukkhittau camhi  anukkhittau vā, dhammikaina vā adhammikaina vā, kuppaina vā akuppaina vā, ṭhānārahaina vā aṭṭhānārahaina vā’| yaṃnūnāhaṃ campaṃ gantvā bhagavantaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyya’’nti| atha khau kassapagauttau bhikkhu saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina campā taina pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina yaina campā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| āciṇṇaṃ khau panaitaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ āgantukaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ paṭisammaudituṃ| atha khau bhagavā kassapagauttaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhikkhu, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍ci appakilamathaina addhānaṃ āgatau, kutau ca tvaṃ, bhikkhu, āgacchasī’’ti? ‘‘khamanīyaṃ, bhagavā; yāpanīyaṃ, bhagavā; appakilamathaina cāhaṃ, bhantai, addhānaṃ āgatau| atthi, bhantai, kāsīsu janapadai vāsabhagāmau nāma| tatthāhaṃ, bhagavā, āvāsikau tantibaddhau ussuk‍kaṃ āpan‍nau – ‘kinti anāgatā ca paisalā bhikkhū āgacchaiyyuṃ, āgatā ca paisalā bhikkhū phāsu viharaiyyuṃ, ayañ‍ca āvāsau vuddhiṃ viruḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyyā’ti| atha khau, bhantai, sambahulā bhikkhū kāsīsu cārikaṃ caramānā yaina vāsabhagāmau tadavasaruṃ| addasaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, tai bhikkhū dūratauva āgacchantai, disvāna āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisiṃ, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ pādakathalikaṃ upanikkhipiṃ, pac‍cuggantvā pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaisiṃ, pānīyaina apucchiṃ, nahānai ussuk‍kaṃ akāsiṃ, ussuk‍kampi akāsiṃ yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ| atha khau taisaṃ, bhantai, āgantukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘bhaddakau khau  ayaṃ āvusau āvāsikau bhikkhu nahānai ussuk‍kaṃ karauti, ussuk‍kampi karauti yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ| handa, mayaṃ, āvusau, idhaiva vāsabhagāmai nivāsaṃ kappaimā’ti| atha khau  tai, bhantai, āgantukā bhikkhū tatthaiva vāsabhagāmai nivāsaṃ kappaisuṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘yau khau imaisaṃ āgantukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ āgantukakilamathau sau paṭippassaddhau| yaipimai gaucarai appakatañ‍ñunau taidānimai gaucarai pakatañ‍ñunau| duk‍karaṃ khau pana parakulaisu  yāvajīvaṃ ussuk‍kaṃ kātuṃ, viñ‍ñatti ca manussānaṃ amanāpā| yaṃnūnāhaṃ na ussuk‍kaṃ karaiyyaṃ yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmi’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, na ussuk‍kaṃ akāsiṃ yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ| atha khau taisaṃ, bhantai, āgantukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘pubbai khvāyaṃ, āvusau, āvāsikau bhikkhu nahānai ussuk‍kaṃ karauti, ussuk‍kampi karauti yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ| saudānāyaṃ na ussuk‍kaṃ karauti yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ| duṭṭhaudānāyaṃ, āvusau, āvāsikau bhikkhu| handa, mayaṃ, āvusau, āvāsikaṃ bhikkhuṃ ukkhipāmā’ti| atha khau tai, bhantai, āgantukā bhikkhū san‍nipatitvā maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘pubbai khau tvaṃ, āvusau, nahānai ussuk‍kaṃ karausi, ussuk‍kampi karausi yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ| saudāni tvaṃ na ussuk‍kaṃ karausi yāguyā khādanīyai bhattasmiṃ| āpattiṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, āpan‍nau| passasaitaṃ āpatti’nti? ‘natthi mai, āvusau, āpatti yamahaṃ passaiyya’nti| atha khau tai, bhantai, āgantukā bhikkhū maṃ āpattiyā adassanai ukkhipiṃsu| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau aitaṃ na jānāmi ‘āpatti vā aisā anāpatti vā, āpan‍nau camhi anāpan‍nau vā, ukkhittau camhi anukkhittau vā, dhammikaina vā adhammikaina vā, kuppaina vā akuppaina vā, ṭhānārahaina vā aṭṭhānārahaina vā’| yaṃnūnāhaṃ campaṃ gantvā bhagavantaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyya’nti| tatau ahaṃ, bhagavā, āgacchāmī’’ti| ‘‘anāpatti aisā, bhikkhu, naisā āpatti| anāpan‍nausi, nasi āpan‍nau| anukkhittausi, nasi ukkhittau| adhammikaināsi kammaina ukkhittau kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina| gaccha tvaṃ, bhikkhu, tatthaiva vāsabhagāmai nivāsaṃ kappaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau kassapagauttau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissuṇitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina vāsabhagāmau taina pak‍kāmi|

381. atha  khau taisaṃ āgantukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ ahudaiva kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, ahu vippaṭisārau – ‘‘alābhā vata nau, na vata nau lābhā, dul‍laddhaṃ vata nau, na vata nau suladdhaṃ, yai mayaṃ suddhaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpattikaṃ avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai ukkhipimhā| handa, mayaṃ, āvusau, campaṃ gantvā bhagavatau santikai ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau daisaimā’’ti| atha khau tai āgantukā bhikkhū saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina campā taina pak‍kamiṃsu| anupubbaina  yaina campā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| āciṇṇaṃ khau panaitaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ āgantukaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ paṭisammaudituṃ| atha khau bhagavā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhikkhavai, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍cittha appakilamathaina addhānaṃ āgatā, kutau ca tumhai, bhikkhavai, āgacchathā’’ti? ‘‘khamanīyaṃ, bhagavā; yāpanīyaṃ, bhagavā; appakilamathaina ca mayaṃ, bhantai, addhānaṃ āgatā| atthi, bhantai, kāsīsu janapadai vāsabhagāmau nāma| tatau mayaṃ, bhagavā, āgacchāmā’’ti| ‘‘tumhai, bhikkhavai, āvāsikaṃ bhikkhuṃ ukkhipitthā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘kismiṃ, bhikkhavai, vatthusmiṃ kāraṇai’’ti? ‘‘avatthusmiṃ, bhagavā, akāraṇai’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, maughapurisā, ananulaumikaṃ  appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, suddhaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpattikaṃ avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai ukkhipissatha| naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰…’’ vigarahitvā dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, suddhau bhikkhu anāpattikau avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai ukkhipitabbau| yau ukkhipaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

atha khau tai bhikkhū uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘ac‍cayau nau, bhantai, ac‍cagamā yathābālai yathāmūḷhai  yathāakusalai, yai mayaṃ suddhaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpattikaṃ avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai ukkhipimhā| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, bhagavā ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhātu āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā’’ti| ‘‘taggha, tumhai, bhikkhavai, ac‍cayau ac‍cagamā yathābālai yathāmūḷhai yathāakusalai, yai tumhai suddhaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpattikaṃ avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai ukkhipittha| yatau ca khau tumhai, bhikkhavai, ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarautha, taṃ vau mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma| vuddhihaisā, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai yau ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarauti, āyatiṃ āyatiṃ ca (sī॰) saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti|

kassapagauttabhikkhuvatthu niṭṭhitaṃ|

235. adhammaina vaggādikammakathā

382. taina khau pana samayaina campāyaṃ bhikkhū aivarūpāni kammāni karaunti – adhammaina vaggakammaṃ karaunti, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ karaunti; dhammaina vaggakammaṃ karaunti, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ karaunti; dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ karaunti; aikaupi aikaṃ ukkhipati, aikaupi dvai ukkhipati, aikaupi sambahulai ukkhipati, aikaupi saṅghaṃ ukkhipati; dvaipi aikaṃ ukkhipanti, dvaipi dvai ukkhipanti, dvaipi sambahulai ukkhipanti, dvaipi saṅghaṃ ukkhipanti; sambahulāpi  aikaṃ ukkhipanti; sambahulāpi dvai ukkhipanti, sambahulāpi sambahulai ukkhipanti, sambahulāpi saṅghaṃ ukkhipanti; saṅghaupi saṅghaṃ ukkhipati| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma campāyaṃ bhikkhū aivarūpāni kammāni karissanti – adhammaina vaggakammaṃ karissanti, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ karissanti, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ karissanti, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ karissanti, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ karissanti, aikaupi aikaṃ ukkhipissati, aikaupi dvai ukkhipissati, aikaupi sambahulai ukkhipissati, aikaupi saṅghaṃ ukkhipissati, dvaipi aikaṃ ukkhipissanti, dvaipi dvai ukkhipissanti, dvaipi sambahulai ukkhipissanti, dvaipi saṅghaṃ ukkhipissanti, sambahulāpi aikaṃ ukkhipissanti, sambahulāpi dvai ukkhipissanti, sambahulāpi sambahulai ukkhipissanti, sambahulāpi saṅghaṃ ukkhipissanti, saṅghaupi saṅghaṃ ukkhipissatī’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, campāyaṃ bhikkhū aivarūpāni kammāni karaunti – adhammaina  vaggakammaṃ karaunti…pai॰… saṅghaupi saṅghaṃ ukkhipatī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ maughapurisānaṃ ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā aivarūpāni kammāni karissanti – adhammaina vaggakammaṃ karissanti…pai॰… saṅghaupi saṅghaṃ ukkhipissati| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi –
