383. ‘‘adhammaina  cai, bhikkhavai, vaggakammaṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ, adhammaina adhammaina cai bhikkhavai (syā॰) samaggakammaṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; aikaupi aikaṃ ukkhipati akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ, aikaupi dvai ukkhipati akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ, aikaupi sambahulai ukkhipati akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ, aikaupi saṅghaṃ ukkhipati akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; dvaipi aikaṃ ukkhipanti akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ, dvaipi dvai ukkhipanti akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ, dvaipi sambahulai ukkhipanti akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ, dvaipi  saṅghaṃ ukkhipanti akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; sambahulāpi aikaṃ ukkhipanti akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ, sambahulāpi dvai ukkhipanti akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ, sambahulāpi sambahulai ukkhipanti akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ, sambahulāpi saṅghaṃ ukkhipanti akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; saṅghaupi saṅghaṃ ukkhipati akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ|

384. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, kammāni – adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammaina samaggakammaṃ| tatra, bhikkhavai, yadidaṃ adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, idaṃ, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ adhammattā vaggattā kuppaṃ aṭṭhānārahaṃ; na, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaṃ kammaṃ kātabbaṃ, na ca mayā aivarūpaṃ kammaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ| tatra, bhikkhavai, yadidaṃ adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, idaṃ, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ adhammattā kuppaṃ aṭṭhānārahaṃ; na, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaṃ kammaṃ kātabbaṃ, na ca mayā aivarūpaṃ kammaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ| tatra, bhikkhavai, yadidaṃ dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, idaṃ, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ vaggattā kuppaṃ aṭṭhānārahaṃ; na, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaṃ kammaṃ kātabbaṃ, na ca mayā aivarūpaṃ kammaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ| tatra, bhikkhavai, yadidaṃ dhammaina samaggakammaṃ, idaṃ, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ dhammattā samaggattā akuppaṃ ṭhānārahaṃ; aivarūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kātabbaṃ, aivarūpañ‍ca mayā kammaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaṃ kammaṃ karissāma yadidaṃ dhammaina samagganti – aivañhi  vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti|

adhammaina vaggādikammakathā niṭṭhitā|

236. ñattivipan‍nakammādikathā

385. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū aivarūpāni kammāni karaunti – adhammaina vaggakammaṃ karaunti, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ karaunti; dhammaina vaggakammaṃ  karaunti, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ karaunti, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ karaunti; ñattivipan‍nampi kammaṃ karaunti anussāvanasampan‍naṃ, anussāvanavipan‍nampi kammaṃ karaunti ñattisampan‍naṃ, ñattivipan‍nampi  anussāvanavipan‍nampi kammaṃ karaunti; añ‍ñatrāpi dhammā kammaṃ karaunti, añ‍ñatrāpi vinayā kammaṃ karaunti, añ‍ñatrāpi satthusāsanā kammaṃ karaunti; paṭikuṭṭhakatampi kammaṃ karaunti adhammikaṃ kuppaṃ aṭṭhānārahaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū aivarūpāni kammāni karissanti – adhammaina vaggakammaṃ karissanti, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ karissanti; dhammaina vaggakammaṃ karissanti, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ karissanti, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ karissanti; ñattivipan‍nampi kammaṃ karissanti anussāvanasampan‍naṃ, anussāvanavipan‍nampi kammaṃ karissanti ñattisampan‍naṃ, ñattivipan‍nampi anussāvanavipan‍nampi kammaṃ karissanti; añ‍ñatrāpi dhammā kammaṃ karissanti, añ‍ñatrāpi vinayā kammaṃ karissanti, añ‍ñatrāpi satthusāsanā kammaṃ karissanti; paṭikuṭṭhakatampi kammaṃ karissanti adhammikaṃ kuppaṃ aṭṭhānāraha’’nti| atha khau tai bhikkhū  bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū aivarūpāni kammāni karaunti – adhammaina vaggakammaṃ karaunti…pai॰… paṭikuṭṭhakatampi kammaṃ karaunti adhammikaṃ kuppaṃ aṭṭhānāraha’’nti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… vigarahitvā dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

386. ‘‘adhammaina cai, bhikkhavai, vaggakammaṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; adhammaina samaggakammaṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; dhammaina vaggakammaṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| ñattivipan‍nañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ anussāvanasampan‍naṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; anussāvanavipan‍nañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ ñattisampan‍naṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; ñattivipan‍nañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ anussāvanavipan‍naṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; añ‍ñatrāpi dhammā kammaṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; añ‍ñatrāpi vinayā kammaṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; añ‍ñatrāpi satthusāsanā kammaṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ; paṭikuṭṭhakatañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ adhammikaṃ kuppaṃ aṭṭhānārahaṃ akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ|

387. chayimāni, bhikkhavai, kammāni – adhammakammaṃ, vaggakammaṃ, samaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina samaggakammaṃ|

katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, adhammakammaṃ? ñattidutiyai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai aikāya ñattiyā kammaṃ karauti, na ca kammavācaṃ anussāvaiti – adhammakammaṃ| ñattidutiyai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai dvīhi ñattīhi kammaṃ karauti, na ca kammavācaṃ anussāvaiti – adhammakammaṃ| ñattidutiyai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai aikāya kammavācāya kammaṃ karauti, na ca ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti  – adhammakammaṃ| ñattidutiyai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai dvīhi kammavācāhi kammaṃ karauti, na ca ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti – adhammakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai aikāya ñattiyā kammaṃ karauti, na ca kammavācaṃ anussāvaiti – adhammakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai dvīhi ñattīhi kammaṃ karauti, na ca kammavācaṃ anussāvaiti – adhammakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai tīhi ñattīhi kammaṃ karauti, na ca kammavācaṃ anussāvaiti – adhammakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai catūhi ñattīhi kammaṃ karauti, na ca kammavācaṃ anussāvaiti – adhammakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai aikāya kammavācāya kammaṃ karauti, na ca ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti – adhammakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai dvīhi kammavācāhi  kammaṃ karauti, na ca ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti – adhammakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai tīhi kammavācāhi kammaṃ karauti, na ca ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti – adhammakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai catūhi kammavācāhi kammaṃ karauti, na ca ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti – adhammakammaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhammakammaṃ|

katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vaggakammaṃ? ñattidutiyai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai anāgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti – vaggakammaṃ| ñattidutiyai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti – vaggakammaṃ| ñattidutiyai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti – vaggakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā  tai anāgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti – vaggakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā  paṭik‍kausanti – vaggakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti – vaggakammaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vaggakammaṃ|

katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, samaggakammaṃ? ñattidutiyai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā, tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā na paṭik‍kausanti – samaggakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā, tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā na paṭik‍kausanti – samaggakammaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, samaggakammaṃ|

katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ? ñattidutiyai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai paṭhamaṃ kammavācaṃ anussāvaiti, pacchā ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti, yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai anāgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ| ñattidutiyai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai paṭhamaṃ kammavācaṃ anussāvaiti, pacchā ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti, yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ| ñattidutiyai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai paṭhamaṃ kammavācaṃ  anussāvaiti, pacchā ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti, yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā  paṭik‍kausanti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai paṭhamaṃ kammavācaṃ anussāvaiti, pacchā ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti, yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai anāgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai paṭhamaṃ kammavācaṃ anussāvaiti, pacchā ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti, yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai paṭhamaṃ kammavācaṃ anussāvaiti, pacchā ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti, yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ|

katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ? ñattidutiyai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai paṭhamaṃ kammavācaṃ anussāvaiti, pacchā ñattiṃ  ṭhapaiti, yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā, tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā na paṭik‍kausanti – dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai paṭhamaṃ kammavācaṃ anussāvaiti, pacchā ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti, yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā na paṭik‍kausanti – dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ|

katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhammaina samaggakammaṃ? ñattidutiyai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai paṭhamaṃ ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti, pacchā aikāya kammavācāya kammaṃ karauti, yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā na paṭik‍kausanti – dhammaina samaggakammaṃ| ñatticatutthai cai, bhikkhavai, kammai paṭhamaṃ ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti, pacchā tīhi kammavācāhi kammaṃ karauti, yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā, tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā na paṭik‍kausanti, dhammaina samaggakammaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammaina samaggakammaṃ|

ñattivipan‍nakammādikathā niṭṭhitā|

237. catuvaggakaraṇādikathā

388. pañ‍ca  saṅghā – catuvaggau bhikkhusaṅghau pañ‍cavaggau bhikkhusaṅghau, dasavaggau bhikkhusaṅghau, vīsativaggau bhikkhusaṅghau, atiraikavīsativaggau bhikkhusaṅghau| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ catuvaggau bhikkhusaṅghau, ṭhapaitvā tīṇi kammāni – upasampadaṃ pavāraṇaṃ abbhānaṃ, dhammaina samaggau  sabbakammaisu kammappattau| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ pañ‍cavaggau bhikkhusaṅghau, ṭhapaitvā dvai kammāni – majjhimaisu janapadaisu upasampadaṃ abbhānaṃ, dhammaina samaggau sabbakammaisu kammappattau| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ dasavaggau bhikkhusaṅghau, ṭhapaitvā aikaṃ kammaṃ – abbhānaṃ, dhammaina samaggau sabbakammaisu kammappattau| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ vīsativaggau bhikkhusaṅghau dhammaina samaggau sabbakammaisu  kammappattau| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ atiraikavīsativaggau bhikkhusaṅghau  dhammaina samaggau sabbakammaisu kammappattau|

389. catuvaggakaraṇañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ bhikkhunicatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya – akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| catuvaggakaraṇañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ sikkhamānacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ…pai॰…| sāmaṇairacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| sāmaṇairicatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| antimavatthuṃ ajjhāpan‍nakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhittakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhittakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| paṇḍakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| thaiyyasaṃvāsakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| titthiyapak‍kantakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| tiracchānagatacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| mātughātakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| pitughātakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| arahantaghātakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| bhikkhunidūsakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| saṅghabhaidakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| lauhituppādakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| ubhataubyañ‍janakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| nānāsaṃvāsakacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| nānāsīmāya ṭhitacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| iddhiyā vaihāsai ṭhitacatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ  na ca karaṇīyaṃ| yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti, taṃcatutthau kammaṃ karaiyya … akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ|

catuvaraṇaṃ|

390. pañ‍cavaggakaraṇañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ bhikkhunipañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| pañ‍cavaggakaraṇañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ sikkhamānapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya…pai॰…| sāmaṇairapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… sāmaṇairipañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya … sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… antimavatthuṃ ajjhāpan‍nakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhittakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhittakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… paṇḍakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… thaiyyasaṃvāsakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… titthiyapak‍kantakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… tiracchānagatapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… mātughātakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… pitughātakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… arahantaghātakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… bhikkhunidūsakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… saṅghabhaidakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… lauhituppādakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… ubhataubyañ‍janakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… nānāsaṃvāsakapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… nānāsīmāya ṭhitapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… iddhiyā vaihāsai ṭhitapañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya… yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti, taṃpañ‍camau kammaṃ karaiyya – akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ|

pañ‍cavaggakaraṇaṃ|

391. dasavaggakaraṇañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ bhikkhunidasamau kammaṃ karaiyya, akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| dasavaggakaraṇañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ sikkhamānadasamau kammaṃ karaiyya, akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ…pai॰…| dasavaggakaraṇañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti, taṃdasamau kammaṃ karaiyya – akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ|

dasavaggakaraṇaṃ|

392. vīsativaggakaraṇañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ bhikkhunivīsau kammaṃ karaiyya – akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| vīsativaggakaraṇañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ sikkhamānavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya  …pai॰… sāmaṇairavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… sāmaṇairivīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… antimavatthuṃ ajjhāpan‍nakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhittakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhittakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… paṇḍakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… thaiyyasaṃvāsakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… titthiyapak‍kantakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… tiracchānagatavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… mātughātakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… pitughātakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… arahantaghātakavīsau  kammaṃ karaiyya… bhikkhunidūsakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… saṅghabhaidakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… lauhituppādakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… ubhataubyañ‍janakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… nānāsaṃvāsakavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… nānāsīmāya ṭhitavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… iddhiyā vaihāsai ṭhitavīsau kammaṃ karaiyya… yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti, taṃvīsau kammaṃ karaiyya – akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ|

vīsativaggakaraṇaṃ|

catuvaggakaraṇādikathā niṭṭhitā|

238. pārivāsikādikathā

393. pārivāsikacatutthau  cai, bhikkhavai, parivāsaṃ dadaiyya, mūlāya paṭikassaiyya, mānattaṃ dadaiyya, taṃvīsau abbhaiyya – akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| mūlāya paṭikassanārahacatutthau cai, bhikkhavai, parivāsaṃ dadaiyya, mūlāya paṭikassaiyya, mānattaṃ dadaiyya, taṃvīsau abbhaiyya – akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| mānattārahacatutthau cai, bhikkhavai, parivāsaṃ dadaiyya, mūlāya paṭikassaiyya, mānattaṃ dadaiyya, taṃvīsau abbhaiyya  – akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| mānattacārikacatutthau cai, bhikkhavai, parivāsaṃ dadaiyya, mūlāya paṭikassaiyya, mānattaṃ dadaiyya, taṃvīsau abbhaiyya – akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ| abbhānārahacatutthau cai, bhikkhavai, parivāsaṃ dadaiyya, mūlāya paṭikassaiyya, mānattaṃ dadaiyya, taṃvīsau abbhaiyya – akammaṃ na ca karaṇīyaṃ|

394. aikac‍cassa, bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai paṭik‍kausanā ruhati, aikac‍cassa na ruhati| kassa ca, bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai paṭik‍kausanā na ruhati? bhikkhuniyā, bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai paṭik‍kausanā na ruhati| sikkhamānāya, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sāmaṇairassa, bhikkhavai… sāmaṇairiyā, bhikkhavai… sikkhāpac‍cakkhātakassa bhikkhavai… antimavatthuṃ ajjhāpan‍nakassa, bhikkhavai  … ummattakassa, bhikkhavai… khittacittassa, bhikkhavai… vaidanāṭṭassa, bhikkhavai… āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakassa, bhikkhavai… āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhittakassa, bhikkhavai… pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai  ukkhittakassa, bhikkhavai… paṇḍakassa, bhikkhavai… thaiyyasaṃvāsakassa, bhikkhavai… titthiyapak‍kantakassa, bhikkhavai … tiracchānagatassa bhikkhavai… mātughātakassa, bhikkhavai… pitughātakassa, bhikkhavai… arahantaghātakassa, bhikkhavai… bhikkhunidūsakassa, bhikkhavai… saṅghabhaidakassa, bhikkhavai… lauhituppādakassa, bhikkhavai… ubhataubyañ‍janakassa, bhikkhavai… nānāsaṃvāsakassa, bhikkhavai… nānāsīmāya ṭhitassa, bhikkhavai… iddhiyā vaihāsai ṭhitassa, bhikkhavai, yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti, tassa ca tassa (syā॰), bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai paṭik‍kausanā na ruhati| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai paṭik‍kausanā na ruhati|

kassa ca, bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai paṭik‍kausanā ruhati? bhikkhussa, bhikkhavai, pakatattassa

samānasaṃvāsakassa samānasīmāya ṭhitassa antamasau ānantarikassāpi anantarikassāpi (syā॰) bhikkhunau viñ‍ñāpaintassa saṅghamajjhai paṭik‍kausanā ruhati| imassa khau, bhikkhavai, saṅghamajjhai paṭik‍kausanā ruhati|

pārivāsikādikathā niṭṭhitā|

239. dvainissāraṇādikathā

395. dvaimā, bhikkhavai, nissāraṇā| atthi, bhikkhavai, puggalau appattau nissāraṇaṃ| tañ‍cai saṅghau nissāraiti, aikac‍cau sunissāritau, aikac‍cau dun‍nissāritau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau appattau nissāraṇaṃ, tañ‍cai saṅghau nissāraiti – dun‍nissāritau? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu suddhau hauti anāpattikau| tañ‍cai saṅghau nissāraiti – dun‍nissāritau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau appattau nissāraṇaṃ, tañ‍cai saṅghau nissāraiti – dun‍nissāritau|

katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau appattau nissāraṇaṃ, tañ‍cai saṅghau nissāraiti – sunissāritau? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau, gihisaṃsaṭṭhau  viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi, tañ‍cai saṅghau nissāraiti – sunissāritau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau appattau nissāraṇaṃ, tañ‍cai saṅghau nissāraiti – sunissāritau|

396. dvaimā, bhikkhavai, ausāraṇā| atthi, bhikkhavai, puggalau appattau ausāraṇaṃ tañ‍cai saṅghau ausāraiti, aikac‍cau sausāritau, aikac‍cau  dausāritau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau appattau ausāraṇaṃ, tañ‍cai saṅghau ausāraiti – dausāritau? paṇḍakau, bhikkhavai, appattau ausāraṇaṃ, tañ‍cai saṅghau ausāraiti – dausāritau| thaiyyasaṃvāsakau, bhikkhavai, appattau ausāraṇaṃ, tañ‍cai saṅghau ausāraiti – dausāritau| titthiyapak‍kantakau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… tiracchānagatau, bhikkhavai… mātughātakau, bhikkhavai… pitughātakau, bhikkhavai… arahantaghātakau, bhikkhavai… bhikkhunidūsakau, bhikkhavai… saṅghabhaidakau, bhikkhavai… lauhituppādakau, bhikkhavai… ubhataubyañ‍janakau, bhikkhavai, appattau, ausāraṇaṃ, tañ‍cai saṅghau ausāraiti – dausāritau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau appattau ausāraṇaṃ, tañ‍cai saṅghau ausāraiti – dausāritau| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, puggalā appattā ausāraṇaṃ, tai cai saṅghau ausāraiti – dausāritā|

katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau appattau ausāraṇaṃ, tañ‍cai saṅghau ausāraiti – sausāritau? hatthacchin‍nau, bhikkhavai, appattau ausāraṇaṃ, tañ‍cai saṅghau ausāraiti, sausāritau| pādacchin‍nau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… hatthapādacchin‍nau, bhikkhavai… kaṇṇacchin‍nau, bhikkhavai… nāsacchin‍nau, bhikkhavai… kaṇṇanāsacchin‍nau, bhikkhavai… aṅgulicchin‍nau, bhikkhavai… aḷacchin‍nau, bhikkhavai… kaṇḍaracchin‍nau, bhikkhavai… phaṇahatthakau, bhikkhavai… khuj‍jau, bhikkhavai… vāmanau, bhikkhavai… galagaṇḍī, bhikkhavai… lakkhaṇāhatau, bhikkhavai… kasāhatau, bhikkhavai… likhitakau, bhikkhavai… sīpadikau, bhikkhavai… pāparaugī, bhikkhavai… parisadūsakau, bhikkhavai… kāṇau, bhikkhavai… kuṇī, bhikkhavai… khañ‍jau, bhikkhavai… pakkhahatau, bhikkhavai… chin‍niriyāpathau, bhikkhavai… jarādubbalau, bhikkhavai… andhau, bhikkhavai… mūgau, bhikkhavai… badhirau, bhikkhavai… andhamūgau, bhikkhavai… andhabadhirau, bhikkhavai… mūgabadhirau, bhikkhavai… andhamūgabadhirau, bhikkhavai, appattau ausāraṇaṃ, tañ‍cai saṅghau ausāraiti – sausāritau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau appattau ausāraṇaṃ, tañ‍cai saṅghau ausāraiti – sausāritau| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, puggalā appattā ausāraṇaṃ, tai cai saṅghau ausāraiti – sausāritā|

dvainissāraṇādikathā niṭṭhitā|

vāsabhagāmabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau paṭhamau|

240. adhammakammādikathā

397. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa na hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, āpan‍nau, passasaitaṃ āpatti’’nti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘natthi mai, āvusau, āpatti, yamahaṃ passaiyya’’nti| taṃ  saṅghau āpattiyā adassanai ukkhipati – adhammakammaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa na hauti āpatti paṭikātabbā| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, āpan‍nau, paṭikarauhi  taṃ āpatti’’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘natthi mai, āvusau, āpatti, yamayaṃ paṭikaraiyya’’nti| taṃ saṅghau āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhipati – adhammakammaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa na hauti pāpikā diṭṭhi paṭinissaj‍jaitā| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘pāpikā tai, āvusau, diṭṭhi, paṭinissaj‍jaitaṃ pāpikaṃ diṭṭhi’’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘natthi mai, āvusau, pāpikā diṭṭhi, yamahaṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya’’nti| taṃ saṅghau pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhipati – adhammakammaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa na hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā, na hauti āpatti paṭikātabbā| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, āpan‍nau, passasaitaṃ āpatti? paṭikarauhi taṃ āpatti’’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘natthi mai, āvusau, āpatti, yamahaṃ passaiyyaṃ| natthi mai, āvusau, āpatti, yamahaṃ paṭikaraiyya’’nti| taṃ saṅghau adassanai vā appaṭikammai vā ukkhipati – adhammakammaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa na hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā, na hauti pāpikā diṭṭhi paṭinissaj‍jaitā| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, āpan‍nau, passasaitaṃ āpattiṃ? pāpikā tai diṭṭhi, paṭinissaj‍jaitaṃ pāpikaṃ diṭṭhi’’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘natthi mai, āvusau, āpatti, yamahaṃ passaiyyaṃ; natthi mai, āvusau, pāpikā diṭṭhi, yamahaṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya’’nti| taṃ saṅghau adassanai vā appaṭinissaggai  vā ukkhipati – adhammakammaṃ|

idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa na hauti āpatti paṭikātabbā, na hauti pāpikā diṭṭhi paṭinissaj‍jaitā| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, āpan‍nau, paṭikarauhi taṃ āpattiṃ; pāpikā tai diṭṭhi, paṭinissaj‍jaitaṃ pāpikaṃ diṭṭhi’’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘natthi mai, āvusau, āpatti, yamahaṃ paṭikaraiyyaṃ| natthi mai, āvusau, pāpikā diṭṭhi, yamahaṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya’’nti| taṃ saṅghau appaṭikammai vā appaṭinissaggai vā ukkhipati – adhammakammaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa na hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā, na hauti āpatti paṭikātabbā, na hauti pāpikā diṭṭhi paṭinissaj‍jaitā| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā  aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, āpan‍nau, passasaitaṃ āpattiṃ? paṭikarauhi taṃ āpattiṃ; pāpikā tai diṭṭhi, paṭinissaj‍jaitaṃ pāpikaṃ diṭṭhi’’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘natthi mai, āvusau, āpatti, yamahaṃ passaiyyaṃ| natthi mai, āvusau, āpatti, yamahaṃ paṭikaraiyyaṃ| natthi mai, āvusau, pāpikā diṭṭhi, yamahaṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya’’nti| taṃ saṅghau adassanai vā appaṭikammai vā appaṭinissaggai vā ukkhipati – adhammakammaṃ|

398. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā| tamainaṃ caudaiti| saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, āpan‍nau, passasaitaṃ āpatti’’nti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘āmāvusau, passāmī’’ti| taṃ saṅghau āpattiyā adassanai ukkhipati – adhammakammaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa hauti āpatti paṭikātabbā| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, āpan‍nau, paṭikarauhi taṃ āpatti’’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘āmāvusau, paṭikarissāmī’’ti| taṃ saṅghau āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhipati – adhammakammaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa hauti pāpikā diṭṭhi paṭinissaj‍jaitā| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘pāpikā tai, āvusau, diṭṭhi; paṭinissaj‍jaitaṃ pāpikaṃ diṭṭhi’’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘āmāvusau, paṭinissaj‍jissāmī’’ti| taṃ saṅghau pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhipati – adhammakammaṃ|

idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā, hauti āpatti paṭikātabbā…pai॰… hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā, hauti pāpikā diṭṭhi paṭinissaj‍jaitā…pai॰… hauti āpatti paṭikātabbā, hauti pāpikā diṭṭhi paṭinissaj‍jaitā…pai॰… hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā, hauti āpatti paṭikātabbā, hauti pāpikā diṭṭhi paṭinissaj‍jaitā| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, āpan‍nau, passasaitaṃ āpattiṃ? paṭikarauhi taṃ āpattiṃ; pāpikā tai diṭṭhi, paṭinissaj‍jaitaṃ pāpikaṃ diṭṭhi’’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘āmāvusau, passāmi, āma paṭikarissāmi, āma paṭinissaj‍jissāmī’’ti| taṃ saṅghau adassanai vā appaṭikammai vā appaṭinissaggai vā ukkhipati – adhammakammaṃ|

399. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, āpan‍nau, passasaitaṃ āpatti’’nti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘natthi mai, āvusau, āpatti, yamahaṃ passaiyya’’nti| taṃ  saṅghau āpattiyā adassanai ukkhipati – dhammakammaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa hauti āpatti paṭikātabbā| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, āpan‍nau, paṭikarauhi taṃ āpatti’’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘natthi mai, āvusau, āpatti, yamahaṃ paṭikaraiyya’’nti| taṃ saṅghau āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhipati – dhammakammaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa hauti pāpikā diṭṭhi paṭinissaj‍jaitā| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘pāpikā tai, āvusau, diṭṭhi, paṭinissaj‍jaitaṃ pāpikaṃ diṭṭhi’’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘natthi mai, āvusau, pāpikā diṭṭhi, yamahaṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya’’nti| taṃ saṅghau pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhipati – dhammakammaṃ|

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā, hauti āpatti paṭikātabbā…pai॰…

hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā, hauti pāpikā diṭṭhi paṭinissaj‍jaitā…pai॰… hauti āpatti paṭikātabbā, hauti pāpikā diṭṭhi paṭinissaj‍jaitā …pai॰… hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā, hauti āpatti paṭikātabbā, hauti pāpikā diṭṭhi  paṭinissaj‍jaitā| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā – ‘‘āpattiṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, āpan‍nau, passasaitaṃ  āpattiṃ? paṭikarauhi taṃ āpattiṃ| pāpikā tai diṭṭhi, paṭinissaj‍jaitaṃ pāpikaṃ diṭṭhi’’nti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘natthi mai, āvusau, āpatti, yamahaṃ passaiyyaṃ| natthi mai, āvusau, āpatti yamahaṃ paṭikaraiyyaṃ| natthi mai, āvusau, pāpikā diṭṭhi, yamahaṃ paṭinissaj‍jaiyya’’nti| taṃ saṅghau adassanai vā appaṭikammai vā appaṭinissaggai vā ukkhipati – dhammakammanti|

adhammakammādikathā niṭṭhitā|

241. upālipucchākathā

400. atha khau āyasmā upāli yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā upāli bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yau nu khau, bhantai, samaggau saṅghau sammukhākaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ asammukhā karauti, dhammakammaṃ nu khau taṃ, bhantai, vinayakamma’’nti? ‘‘adhammakammaṃ taṃ, upāli, avinayakamma’’nti| ‘‘yau nu khau, bhantai, samaggau saṅghau paṭipucchākaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ appaṭipucchā karauti…pai॰… paṭiñ‍ñāyakaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ apaṭiñ‍ñāya karauti… sativinayārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti… amūḷhavinayārahassa tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti… tassapāpiyasikākammārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti… taj‍janīyakammārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti … niyassakammārahassa  pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti… pabbājanīyakammārahassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti… paṭisāraṇīyakammārahassa ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti… ukkhaipanīyakammārahassa parivāsaṃ daiti… parivāsārahaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati… mūlāyapaṭikassanārahassa mānattaṃ daiti… mānattārahaṃ abbhaiti… abbhānārahaṃ upasampādaiti, dhammakammaṃ nu khau taṃ, bhantai, vinayakamma’’nti? ‘‘adhammakammaṃ taṃ, upāli, avinayakammaṃ’’|

‘‘yau khau, upāli, samaggau saṅghau sammukhākaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ asammukhā karauti, aivaṃ khau, upāli, adhammakammaṃ hauti avinayakammaṃ, aivañ‍ca pana saṅghau sātisārau hauti| yau khau, upāli, samaggau saṅghau paṭipucchākaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ appaṭipucchā karauti…pai॰… paṭiñ‍ñāyakaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ  apaṭiñ‍ñāya karauti… sativinayārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti… amūḷhavinayārahassa tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti… tassapāpiyasikākammārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti… taj‍janīyakammārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti… niyassakammārahassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti… pabbājanīyakammārahassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti… paṭisāraṇīyakammārahassa  ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti… ukkhaipanīyakammārahassa parivāsaṃ daiti… parivāsārahaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati… mūlāyapaṭikassanārahassa mānattaṃ daiti… mānattārahaṃ abbhaiti… abbhānārahaṃ upasampādaiti, aivaṃ khau, upāli, adhammakammaṃ hauti avinayakammaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana saṅghau sātisārau hautī’’ti|

401. ‘‘yau  nu khau, bhantai, samaggau saṅghau sammukhākaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ sammukhā karauti, dhammakammaṃ nu khau taṃ, bhantai, vinayakamma’’nti? ‘‘dhammakammaṃ taṃ, upāli, vinayakamma’’nti| ‘‘yau nu khau, bhantai, samaggau saṅghau paṭipucchākaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ paṭipucchā karauti…pai॰… paṭiñ‍ñāyakaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ paṭiñ‍ñāya karauti… sativinayārahassa sativinayaṃ daiti… amūḷhavinayārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti… tassapāpiyasikākammārahassa tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti… taj‍janīyakammārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti… niyassakammārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti… pabbājanīyakammārahassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti… paṭisāraṇīyakammārahassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti… ukkhaipanīyakammārahassa ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti… parivāsārahassa parivāsaṃ daiti mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati… mānattārahassa mānattaṃ daiti… abbhānārahaṃ abbhaiti… upasampadārahaṃ upasampādaiti, dhammakammaṃ nu khau taṃ, bhantai, vinayakamma’’nti? ‘‘dhammakammaṃ taṃ, upāli, vinayakammaṃ|

‘‘yau khau, upāli, samaggau saṅghau sammukhākaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ sammukhā karauti, aivaṃ khau, upāli, dhammakammaṃ hauti vinayakammaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana saṅghau anatisārau hauti| yau khau, upāli, samaggau saṅghau paṭipucchākaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ paṭipucchā karauti… paṭiñ‍ñāyakaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ paṭiñ‍ñāya karauti… sativinayārahassa sativinayaṃ daiti… amūḷhavinayārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti… tassapāpiyasikākammārahassa tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti… taj‍janīyakammārahassa  taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti… niyassakammārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti… pabbājanīyakammārahassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti … paṭisāraṇīyakammārahassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti… ukkhaipanīyakammārahassa ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti… parivāsārahassa parivāsaṃ daiti… mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati… mānattārahassa mānattaṃ daiti… abbhānārahaṃ abbhaiti… upasampadārahaṃ upasampādaiti, aivaṃ khau, upāli, dhammakammaṃ hauti vinayakammaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana saṅghau anatisārau hautī’’ti|

402. ‘‘yau nu khau, bhantai, samaggau saṅghau sativinayārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti, amūḷhavinayārahassa  sativinayaṃ daiti, dhammakammaṃ nu khau taṃ, bhantai, vinayakamma’’nti? ‘‘adhammakammaṃ taṃ, upāli, avinayakamma’’nti| ‘‘yau nu khau, bhantai, samaggau saṅghau amūḷhavinayārahassa tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti, tassapāpiyasikākammārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti…pai॰… tassapāpiyasikākammārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ  karauti, taj‍janīyakammārahassa tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti… taj‍janīyakammārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti, niyassakammārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti… niyassakammārahassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti, pabbājanīyakammārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti… pabbājanīyakammārahassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti, paṭisāraṇīyakammārahassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti… paṭisāraṇīyakammārahassa ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti, ukkhaipanīyakammārahassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti… ukkhaipanīyakammārahassa parivāsaṃ daiti, parivāsārahassa  ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti… parivāsārahaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahassa parivāsaṃ daiti… mūlāyapaṭikassanārahassa mānattaṃ daiti, mānattārahaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati… mānattārahaṃ abbhaiti, abbhānārahassa mānattaṃ daiti… abbhānārahaṃ upasampādaiti, upasampadārahaṃ abbhaiti, dhammakammaṃ nu khau taṃ, bhantai, vinayakamma’’nti? ‘‘adhammakammaṃ taṃ, upāli, avinayakamma’’nti|

‘‘yau khau, upāli, samaggau saṅghau sativinayārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti, amūḷhavinayārahassa sativinayaṃ daiti, aivaṃ khau, upāli, adhammakammaṃ hauti avinayakammaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana saṅghau sātisārau hauti| yau khau, upāli, samaggau saṅghau amūḷhavinayārahassa tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti, tassapāpiyasikākammārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti…pai॰… tassapāpiyasikākammārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti, taj‍janīyakammārahassa tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti… taj‍janīyakammārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti, niyassakammārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti… niyassakammārahassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti, pabbājanīyakammārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti… pabbājanīyakammārahassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti, paṭisāraṇīyakammārahassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti… paṭisāraṇīyakammārahassa ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti, ukkhaipanīyakammārahassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti… ukkhaipanīyakammārahassa parivāsaṃ daiti, parivāsārahassa ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti… parivāsārahaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahassa parivāsaṃ  daiti… mūlāyapaṭikassanārahassa mānattaṃ daiti, mānattārahaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati – mānattārahaṃ abbhaiti, abbhānārahassa mānattaṃ daiti… abbhānārahaṃ upasampādaiti, upasampadārahaṃ abbhaiti, aivaṃ khau, upāli, adhammakammaṃ hauti avinayakammaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana saṅghau sātisārau hautī’’ti|

403. ‘‘yau  nu khau, bhantai, samaggau saṅghau sativinayārahassa sativinayaṃ daiti, amūḷhavinayārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti, dhammakammaṃ nu khau taṃ, bhantai, vinayakamma’’nti? ‘‘dhammakammaṃ taṃ, upāli, vinayakamma’’nti| ‘‘yau nu khau, bhantai, samaggau saṅghau amūḷhavinayārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti…pai॰… tassapāpiyasikākammārahassa tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti…pai॰… taj‍janīyakammārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti…pai॰… niyassakammārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti…pai॰… pabbājanīyakammārahassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti…pai॰… paṭisāraṇīyakammārahassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti…pai॰… ukkhaipanīyakammārahassa ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti…pai॰… parivāsārahassa parivāsaṃ daiti…pai॰… mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati…pai॰… mānattārahassa mānattaṃ daiti…pai॰… abbhānārahaṃ abbhaiti, upasampadārahaṃ upasampādaiti, dhammakammaṃ nu khau taṃ, bhantai, vinayakamma’’nti? ‘‘dhammakammaṃ taṃ, upāli, vinayakammaṃ’’|

‘‘yau khau, upāli, samaggau saṅghau sativinayārahassa sativinayaṃ daiti, amūḷhavinayārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti, aivaṃ khau, upāli, dhammakammaṃ hauti vinayakammaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana saṅghau anatisārau hauti| yau khau, upāli, samaggau saṅghau amūḷhavinayārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti …pai॰… tassapāpiyasikākammārahassa tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti…pai॰… taj‍janīyakammārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti…pai॰… niyassakammārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti…pai॰… pabbājanīyakammārahassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti…pai॰… paṭisāraṇīyakammārahassa  paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti…pai॰… ukkhaipanīyakammārahassa ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti…pai॰… parivāsārahassa parivāsaṃ daiti…pai॰… mūlāya paṭikassanārahaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati…pai॰… mānattārahassa mānattaṃ daiti…pai॰… abbhānārahaṃ abbhaiti, upasampadārahaṃ upasampādaiti, aivaṃ khau, upāli, dhammakammaṃ hauti vinayakammaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana saṅghau anatisārau hautī’’ti|

404. atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – yau khau, bhikkhavai, samaggau saṅghau sativinayārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti, aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, adhammakammaṃ hauti avinayakammaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana saṅghau sātisārau hauti| yau khau, bhikkhavai, samaggau saṅghau sativinayārahassa tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti…pai॰… sativinayārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti… sativinayārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti… sativinayārahassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti… sativinayārahassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti… sativinayārahassa ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti  … sativinayārahassa parivāsaṃ daiti… sativinayārahaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati… sativinayārahassa mānattaṃ daiti… sativinayārahaṃ abbhaiti… sativinayārahaṃ upasampādaiti, aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, adhammakammaṃ hauti avinayakammaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana saṅghau sātisārau hauti|

405. yau khau, bhikkhavai, samaggau saṅghau amūḷhavinayārahassa tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti, aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, adhammakammaṃ hauti avinayakammaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana saṅghau sātisārau hauti| yau khau, bhikkhavai, samaggau saṅghau amūḷhavinayārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti…pai॰… amūḷhavinayārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti… amūḷhavinayārahassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti… amūḷhavinayārahassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti… amūḷhavinayārahassa ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti… amūḷhavinayārahassa parivāsaṃ daiti… amūḷhavinayārahaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati… amūḷhavinayārahassa mānattaṃ daiti… amūḷhavinayārahaṃ abbhaiti… amūḷhavinayārahaṃ upasampādaiti… amūḷhavinayārahassa sativinayaṃ daiti, aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, adhammakammaṃ hauti avinayakammaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana saṅghau sātisārau hauti|

406. yau  khau, bhikkhavai, samaggau saṅghau tassapāpiyasikākammārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti…pai॰… taj‍janīyakammārahassa… niyassakammārahassa… pabbājanīyakammārahassa… paṭisāraṇīyakammārahassa… ukkhaipanīyakammārahassa… parivāsārahaṃ… mūlāyapaṭikassanārahassa… mānattārahaṃ… abbhānārahaṃ… upasampadārahassa sativinayaṃ daiti, aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, adhammakammaṃ hauti avinayakammaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana saṅghau sātisārau hauti| yau khau, bhikkhavai, samaggau saṅghau upasampadārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti…pai॰… upasampadārahassa tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti… upasampadārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti… upasampadārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti… upasampadārahassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti… upasampadārahassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti… upasampadārahassa ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti… upasampadārahassa parivāsaṃ daiti… upasampadārahaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati… upasampadārahassa mānattaṃ daiti… upasampadārahaṃ abbhaiti, aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, adhammakammaṃ hauti avinayakammaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana saṅghau  sātisārau hautīti|

upālipucchākathā niṭṭhitā|

upālipucchābhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau dutiyau|

242. taj‍janīyakammakathā

407. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina samaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau adhammaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ  karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammaina vaggā| sau  tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau dhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau dhammapatirūpakaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā|

408. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti  – adhammaina samaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau adhammaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau dhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau  dhammapatirūpakaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau dhammapatirūpakaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā|

409. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau  kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| handassa  mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau dhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau dhammapatirūpakaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau dhammapatirūpakaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina samaggā|

410. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau kalahakārakau  vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau dhammapatirūpakaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammapatirūpakaina  samaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau dhammapatirūpakaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ  aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina samaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau adhammaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammaina vaggā|

411. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ  aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa  taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau dhammapatirūpakaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina samaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau adhammaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau dhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā|

taj‍janīyakammakathā niṭṭhitā|

243. niyassakammakathā

412. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau  anapadānau, gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu bālau abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau, gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ niyassakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa niyassakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina niyassakammakatau adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ niyassakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa niyassakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina samaggā…pai॰… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā…pai॰…|

yathā haiṭṭhā, tathā cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ|

niyassakammakathā niṭṭhitā|

244. pabbājanīyakammakathā

413. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kuladūsakau hauti pāpasamācārau| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu kuladūsakau pāpasamācārau| handassa mayaṃ pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammakatau adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina samaggā…pai॰… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā…pai॰…|

cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ|

pabbājanīyakammakatā niṭṭhitā|

245. paṭisāraṇīyakammakathā

414. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gihī ak‍kausati paribhāsati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu gihī ak‍kausati paribhāsati| handassa mayaṃ paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina paṭisāraṇīyakammakatau adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ  karaumā’’ti| tai tassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina samaggā…pai॰… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā…pai॰…|

cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ|

paṭisāraṇīyakammakathā niṭṭhitā|

246. adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammakathā

415. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ passituṃ| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ passituṃ| handassa mayaṃ āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammakatau adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina samaggā…pai॰… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina  vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā…pai॰…|

cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ|

adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammakathā niṭṭhitā|

247. appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammakathā

416. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ paṭikātuṃ| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ paṭikātuṃ| handassa mayaṃ āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammakatau adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina samaggā…pai॰… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā…pai॰…|

cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ|

appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammakathā niṭṭhitā|

248. appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammakathā

417. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na icchati pāpikaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭinissaj‍jituṃ| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu na icchati pāpikaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭinissaj‍jituṃ| handassa mayaṃ pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammakatau adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina samaggā…pai॰… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā…pai॰…|

cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ|

appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammakathā niṭṭhitā|

249. taj‍janīyakammapaṭippassaddhikathā

418. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ adhammaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina samaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti  – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ dhammaina vaggaihi| handassa  mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ dhammapatirūpakaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā|

419. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina samaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ adhammaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ dhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ dhammapatirūpakaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ dhammapatirūpakaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina vaggā|

420. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ dhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ dhammapatirūpakaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ dhammapatirūpakaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina samaggā|

421. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa  kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ dhammapatirūpakaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ  hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ dhammapatirūpakaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina samaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ adhammaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammaina vaggā|

422. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ dhammapatirūpakaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ  paṭippassaddhaṃ adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina samaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ adhammaina samaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammaina vaggā| sau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ  dhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā|

taj‍janīyakammapaṭippassaddhikathā niṭṭhitā|

250. niyassakammapaṭippassaddhikathā

423. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghaina niyassakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, niyassassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina niyassakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, niyassassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ niyassakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa niyassakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina niyassakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ niyassakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa niyassakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina samaggā…pai॰… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā…pai॰…|

cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ|

niyassakammapaṭippassaddhikathā niṭṭhitā|

251. pabbājanīyakammapaṭippassaddhikathā

424. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, pabbājanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, pabbājanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina samaggā…pai॰… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā…pai॰…|

cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ|

pabbājanīyakammapaṭippassaddhikathā niṭṭhitā|

253. adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammapaṭippassaddhikathā

426. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghaina āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, āpattiyā  adassanai ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ  paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina samaggā…pai॰… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā…pai॰…|

cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ|

adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammapaṭippassaddhikathā niṭṭhitā|

254. appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammapaṭippassaddhikathā

427. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghaina āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ – adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa  āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina samaggā…pai॰… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā…pai॰…|

cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ|

appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammapaṭippassaddhikathā niṭṭhitā|

255. appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammapaṭippassaddhikathā

428. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghaina pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai  ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa  pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina vaggā| sau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ gacchati| tatthapi bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘imassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau saṅghaina pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ – adhammaina vaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti… adhammaina samaggā…pai॰… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā…pai॰…|

cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ|

appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammapaṭippassaddhikathā niṭṭhitā|

256. taj‍janīyakammavivādakathā

429. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘adhammaina  vaggakamma’’nti, yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau|

430. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina samaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘adhammaina samaggakamma’’nti, yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau|

431. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti  – dhammaina vaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘dhammaina vaggakamma’’nti, yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau|

432. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa  taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakamma’’nti, yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau|

433. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti…pai॰…  saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaunti – dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakamma’’nti, yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau|

taj‍janīyakammavivādakathā niṭṭhitā|

257. niyassakammavivādakathā

434. idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau, gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu bālau abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau, gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi| handassa mayaṃ niyassakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa niyassakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā…pai॰… adhammaina samaggā… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakamma’’nti, yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu  – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau| imai pañ‍ca vārā saṃkhittā|

niyassakammavivādakathā niṭṭhitā|

258. pabbājanīyakammavivādakathā

435. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kuladūsakau hauti pāpasamācārau| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu kuladūsakau pāpasamācārau| handassa mayaṃ pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā…pai॰… adhammaina samaggā… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakamma’’nti yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau| imai pañ‍ca vārā saṃkhittā|

pabbājanīyakammavivādakathā niṭṭhitā|

259. paṭisāraṇīyakammavivādakathā

436. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gihī ak‍kausati paribhāsati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu gihī ak‍kausati  paribhāsati| handassa mayaṃ paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ  karaumā’’ti| tai  tassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā…pai॰… adhammaina samaggā… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakamma’’nti, yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ  duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau| imai pañ‍ca vārā saṃkhittā|

paṭisāraṇīyakammavivādakathā niṭṭhitā|

260. adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammavivādakathā

437. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ passituṃ| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ passituṃ| handassa mayaṃ āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā…pai॰… adhammaina samaggā… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai  tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakamma’’nti, yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau| imai pañ‍ca vārā saṃkhittā|

adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammavivādakathā niṭṭhitā|

261. appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammavivādakathā

438. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ paṭikātuṃ| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ paṭikātuṃ| handassa mayaṃ āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā…pai॰… adhammaina samaggā… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina  vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ  kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakamma’’nti; yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau| imai pañ‍ca vārā saṃkhittā|

appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammavivādakathā niṭṭhitā|

262. appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammavivādakathā

439. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na icchati pāpikaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭinissaj‍jituṃ| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu na icchati pāpikaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭinissaj‍jituṃ| handassa mayaṃ pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai  ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti| tai tassa pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaunti – adhammaina vaggā…pai॰… adhammaina samaggā… dhammaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā… dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakamma’’nti, yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau| imai pañ‍ca vārā saṃkhittā|

appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammavivādakathā niṭṭhitā|

263. taj‍janīyakammapaṭippassaddhikathā

440. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina vaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ  kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ  puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakamma’’nti, yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu  – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau|

441. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – adhammaina samaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘adhammaina samaggakamma’’nti, yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau|

442. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammaina vaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘dhammaina vaggakamma’’nti yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau|

443. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ  paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammapatirūpakaina vaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakamma’’nti, yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau|

444. idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| handassa mayaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaimā’’ti| tai tassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhainti – dhammapatirūpakaina samaggā| tatraṭṭhau saṅghau vivadati – ‘‘adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakammaṃ, akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti| tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘dhammapatirūpakaina samaggakamma’’nti, yai ca tai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘akataṃ kammaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ puna kātabbaṃ kamma’’nti, imai tattha bhikkhū dhammavādinau|

taj‍janīyakammapaṭippassaddhikathā niṭṭhitā|

264. niyassakammapaṭippassaddhikathā
